==================== FILE: approval.py ====================
"""Project-local uploader attestations; not independent identity or legal clearance."""
import time
from production import asset_matches
BASIS='Uploader declares that this file has been reviewed for its intended use and may be reused. This is a user attestation, not independent copyright clearance or face recognition.'
AUTO_POLICY_VERSION=1
AUTO_BASIS='Automatic media-use approval requested by the user. This is uploader-authorized reuse approval, not independent copyright clearance, face recognition, or editorial scene approval.'
def approve_asset(p,a,method='bulk_user_attestation'):
 if not str(a.get('source','')).strip():
  from document_plan import source_register
  links=list(dict.fromkeys(str(r.get('youtube_url') or '').strip() for r in source_register(p) if asset_matches(a,str(r.get('source_file') or '')) and str(r.get('youtube_url') or '').strip()))
  a['source']=links[0] if len(links)==1 else 'User-provided file: '+str(a.get('original_name') or a['name'])+'; original publisher not recorded.'
 if a.get('rights','unreviewed')=='unreviewed':a['rights']='reviewed_other'
 a['verified']=True
 basis=AUTO_BASIS if method.startswith(('automatic_','user_requested_','derived_')) else BASIS
 if basis not in a.get('notes',''):a['notes']=(a.get('notes','')+'\n'+basis).strip()
 a['approval_record']={'basis':basis,'method':method,'at':time.time(),'active':True,'independent_verification':False}
 # Existing person identifications are preserved; no names are invented from captions or filenames.
 return a

def auto_approve_upload(p,a):
 if (p.get('media_approval_policy') or {}).get('auto_upload',True):
  approve_asset(p,a,'automatic_local_upload_user_instruction')
  a['approval_record']['basis']=AUTO_BASIS
 return a


def ensure_automatic_policy(p):
 """One-time migration for existing projects; do not repeatedly undo explicit later revocations."""
 if p.get('automatic_media_policy_version')==AUTO_POLICY_VERSION:return False
 for a in p['assets']:
  approve_asset(p,a,'user_requested_automatic_existing_uploads')
  a['approval_record']['basis']=AUTO_BASIS
 p['automatic_media_policy_version']=AUTO_POLICY_VERSION
 p['media_approval_policy']={'auto_upload':True,'basis':AUTO_BASIS,'at':time.time(),'scope':'local_uploads_and_frames_from_approved_local_uploads'}
 p.setdefault('media_approval_history',[]).append({'action':'automatic_approval_requested','count':len(p['assets']),'at':time.time(),'basis':AUTO_BASIS})
 return True

==================== END FILE: approval.py ====================


==================== FILE: automatic.py ====================
"""Automatic picture conform. Document claims never become face identifications.
A visually reviewed assembly is reused only while its documents AND local media remain unchanged.
Frame decoding verifies availability, not semantic identity or copyright.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import copy,hashlib,json,math

def file_sha256(path,chunk=1024*1024):
 """Hash a file by streaming it. Uploads may be 8 GB; reading one into memory to hash it
 would either swap the machine or fail outright, and it is only ever compared, not stored."""
 digest=hashlib.sha256()
 with open(path,'rb') as handle:
  for block in iter(lambda:handle.read(chunk),b''):digest.update(block)
 return digest.hexdigest()

def fingerprint(p,root):
 root=Path(root)
 media=[(a['id'],a['path'],(root/a['path']).stat().st_size,(root/a['path']).stat().st_mtime_ns) for a in p['assets'] if a['kind']=='video' and (root/a['path']).is_file()]
 if p.get('portable_from_project'):
  media=[(a['id'],a['path'],(root/a['path']).stat().st_size,file_sha256(root/a['path'])) for a in p['assets'] if a['kind']=='video' and (root/a['path']).is_file()]
 data=[p.get('production_script'),p.get('srt'),p.get('srt_block_map'),p.get('source_bindings'),media,p.get('picture_policy'),p.get('repetition_policy'),p.get('transition_policy')]
 return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(data,sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()

# A beat survives a rebuild only when the decision behind its picture is unchanged.
# Everything that could make the same picture mean something different is in this list.
SCENE_IDENTITY_FIELDS = ['srt_blocks', 'frame_in', 'frame_out', 'asset_id', 'in_s',
                         'transition', 'transition_handle_frames', 'narration', 'person',
                         'context_only', 'focus']


def scene_identity(scene):
    """The part of a scene a rebuild must reproduce for its approval to still stand."""
    return {field: scene.get(field) for field in SCENE_IDENTITY_FIELDS}


def carry_previous_review(p, scenes):
    """Keep approvals on beats whose picture did not change, drop them on beats that did.

    A rebuild starts every scene unapproved, which is right for new pictures and wrong for
    the ninety-nine beats an editor already reviewed. This compares the rebuilt scene with
    the previously saved one on the fields that decide what the picture is; a match
    carries the approval and its record forward, a difference leaves the beat unapproved
    so it is reviewed again. It never approves anything a human did not approve before.
    """
    previous = {str(s.get('plan_row_id') or s.get('srt_blocks')): s for s in p.get('scenes') or []}
    kept, reset = 0, 0
    for scene in scenes:
        key = str(scene.get('plan_row_id') or scene.get('srt_blocks'))
        old = previous.get(key)
        if old and old.get('approved') and scene_identity(old) == scene_identity(scene):
            scene['approved'] = True
            if old.get('approval_record'):
                scene['approval_record'] = dict(old['approval_record'], carried_from_rebuild=True)
            kept += 1
        else:
            scene['approved'] = False
            reset += 1
    return {'carried': kept, 'requiring_review': reset}


def restore_reviewed(p,root):
 record=p.get('automatic_timeline') or {}
 if not record.get('scenes') or record.get('input_fingerprint')!=fingerprint(p,root):return False
 staged=copy.deepcopy(record['scenes'])
 carry_previous_review(p,staged)
 p['scenes']=staged;p['allow_archive_reuse']=True
 p['script_application']={'recognized_instructions':len(p['srt']['blocks']),'placed_scenes':len(p['scenes']),'issues':[],'mode':'reviewed_mp4_only_assembly','note':'Automatic restoration of frame-reviewed cuts; input fingerprint matched. Unavailable exact evidence is recorded separately, never fabricated.'}
 return True

def native_rows(p,rows):
 from production import matching_assets
 out=copy.deepcopy(rows)
 for row in out:
  row['transition_out']='cut';row['transition_handle_frames']=0;row['transition_handle_ms']=0
  if str(row.get('visual_status','')).upper() in ('PHOTOGRAPH','STILL_EXTRACTED'):continue  # already a prepared still
  row['visual_status']='VIDEO';row['picture_treatment']='Native MP4 action; no extracted still, loop, frozen tail or imposed camera effect.'
  row['import_blockers']=[n for n in row.get('import_blockers',[]) if 'transition' not in str(n).lower()]
  a=matching_assets(p,row.get('source_file',''))
  if len(a)==1 and a[0]['kind']=='video':
   duration=(round(row['timeline_out_ms']*.03)-round(row['timeline_in_ms']*.03))/30
   if duration>a[0]['duration']:continue
   original=row['source_in_ms']/1000;inside=max(0,min(original,a[0]['duration']-duration-.07));row['source_in_ms']=round(inside*1000);row['source_out_ms']=round((inside+duration)*1000)
   if abs(inside-original)>.001:row.setdefault('import_notes',[]).append('Nominal source IN was outside playable coverage; bounded to this same uploaded MP4. This is not semantic verification.')
 return out

def inspect_interval(root,asset,inside,frames,fps=30,cap=None):
 from conform import capture
 import cv2,numpy as np
 owned=cap is None
 cap=capture(Path(root)/asset['path']) if owned else cap;samples=[]
 try:
  source_fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
  if not math.isfinite(source_fps) or source_fps<=0:raise ValueError('Source has no valid frame rate.')
  for offset in [0,max(0,(frames-1)/fps/2),max(0,(frames-1)/fps)]:
   t=inside+offset
   # Seek by frame number, not timestamp: on a long programme timestamp seeking walks
   # from the previous position and cost ~46 ms per seek even on a 3-minute source.
   cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,max(0,int(round(t*source_fps))))
   ok,f=cap.read()
   if not ok:raise ValueError(f'Cannot decode {asset["original_name"]} at {t:.3f}s.')
   gray=cv2.cvtColor(f,cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY);black=float(np.mean(gray<20));samples.append({'time':t,'black_fraction':black,'luma_mean':float(gray.mean())})
   if black>.99:raise ValueError(f'Black picture in {asset["original_name"]} at {t:.3f}s.')
 finally:
  if owned:cap.release()
 return samples

def still_kind(asset):
 if not asset:return ''
 if asset.get('kind')=='image':return 'photograph'
 return 'extracted frame' if (asset.get('derived_from') or {}).get('kind')=='image' else ''

def inspect_still(root,asset):
 """A still must decode and must not be a black frame. It is not a moving interval."""
 from PIL import Image,ImageOps,ImageStat
 path=Path(root)/asset['path']
 if not path.is_file():raise ValueError('The picture file is missing from disk.')
 with Image.open(path) as im:
  im=ImageOps.exif_transpose(im).convert('L')
  if min(im.size)<64:raise ValueError('The picture is too small to fill a frame.')
  mean=ImageStat.Stat(im).mean[0];size=im.size
 if mean<3:raise ValueError('The picture is almost black; choose a usable image.')
 return {'kind':still_kind(asset),'size':size,'luma_mean':round(mean,2)}

def validate_assembly(p,root):
 assets={a['id']:a for a in p['assets']};report={'scope':'decoded IN/middle/LAST frame availability and still decode; not face recognition','scenes':[],'errors':[]}
 from conform import capture
 decoders={}
 try:
  for i,s in enumerate(p['scenes'],1):
   a=assets.get(s.get('asset_id'))
   if not a:report['errors'].append(f'Scene {i}: no media assigned.');continue
   kind=still_kind(a)
   try:
    if kind:
     report['scenes'].append({'scene':i,'still':inspect_still(root,a)})
     continue
    if a['kind']!='video':report['errors'].append(f'Scene {i}: no native MP4 interval.');continue
    # One decoder per source file, reused for every beat taken from it: opening a long
    # file once per beat is pure overhead on a programme with hundreds of beats.
    if a['id'] not in decoders:decoders[a['id']]=capture(Path(root)/a['path'])
    report['scenes'].append({'scene':i,'samples':inspect_interval(root,a,s['in_s'],s['frame_out']-s['frame_in'],cap=decoders[a['id']])})
   except Exception as e:report['errors'].append(f'Scene {i}: '+str(e))
 finally:
  for cap in decoders.values():
   try:cap.release()
   except Exception:pass
 return report

# Coalesce sequential uploads into one assembly after all mapped sources are present.
import threading,time
_QUEUE_LOCK=threading.Lock()
_PENDING=set()
def monitor_render(studio,pid,status):
 """Keep automatic_status in sync while the continuous master is encoded."""
 def watch():
  terminal={'done','cancelled','error'}
  for _ in range(5400):
   try:
    with studio.app.test_client() as client:
     r=client.get('/api/projects/'+pid).get_json() or {}
     state=r.get('render') or {}
    st=state.get('status') or 'running'
    status('rendering',state.get('detail') or ('Continuous master render: '+str(state.get('progress',0))+'%'))
    if st in terminal:
     status('done','Continuous master ready. Program monitor can play the rendered MP4.' if st=='done' else 'Render finished: '+str(state.get('stage') or st))
     return
   except Exception:
    pass
   time.sleep(2)
 threading.Thread(target=watch,daemon=True,name='automatic-render-monitor-'+pid).start()
def _plan_coverage(p):
 """Which SRT blocks the block map never places, and where the plan stops."""
 blocks=(p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks') or []
 rows=(p.get('srt_block_map') or {}).get('beats') or []
 if not blocks or not rows:return []
 covered={n for row in rows for n in row.get('srt_blocks') or []}
 return [b['number'] for b in blocks if b['number'] not in covered]


def _plan_does_not_cover_srt(p):
 return bool(_plan_coverage(p))


def _coverage_detail(p):
 missing=_plan_coverage(p)
 plan_s=((p.get('srt_block_map') or {}).get('beats') or [{}])[-1].get('timeline_out_ms',0)/1000
 srt_s=((p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks') or [{}])[-1].get('end_ms',0)/1000
 return (str(len(missing))+' SRT block(s) are not placed by this block map: it covers '+str(round(plan_s,1))+'s of a '
         +str(round(srt_s,1))+'s narration (first unplaced block '+str(missing[0])+'). A map written for a different '
         'runtime needs its rows extended, or download a fresh template for this SRT. Nothing is left silently unplanned.')


def automatic_readiness(p):
 """Why automatic assembly will or will not run, in words an editor can act on.

 The app used to fail this silently: with inputs present but no plan, or with the SRT
 missing, nothing happened and nothing said why. This is the single place that answers
 the question, used by the status line and by maybe_queue.
 """
 have_srt=bool(p.get('srt'))
 have_voice=bool(p.get('voiceover'))
 have_media=bool(p.get('assets'))
 have_script=bool(p.get('production_script'))
 have_map=bool(p.get('srt_block_map'))
 missing=[]
 if not have_media:missing.append('footage')
 if not have_srt:missing.append('SRT')
 # Narration is recorded, never required: a programme may be carried by its text alone.
 if have_map:
  plan='block map'
 elif have_script:
  plan='production script'
 else:
  plan=''
 state={'inputs':{'srt':have_srt,'voiceover':have_voice,'assets':have_media,'production_script':have_script,'srt_block_map':have_map},
         'has_plan':bool(plan),'plan':plan,'missing':missing,
         'auto_enabled':bool(p.get('auto_assemble_uploads')),
         'manual_edits':bool(p.get('manual_scene_edits'))}
 if not have_srt:
  state.update(status='blocked',detail='Automatic assembly is waiting for the narration SRT, which supplies every beat timing.')
 elif not have_media:
  state.update(status='blocked',detail='Automatic assembly is waiting for footage. Upload the source videos (and photographs if the picture policy allows them).')
 elif have_map and _plan_does_not_cover_srt(p):
  state.update(status='blocked',detail=_coverage_detail(p))
 elif not plan:
  state.update(status='needs_plan',detail='All required inputs are present, but there is no plan saying which picture goes where. Upload a block map (download the template first), or a production script with per-scene instructions. Without one, nothing is placed - the app will not choose pictures by guesswork.')
 elif p.get('scenes'):
  state.update(status='built',detail=str(len(p['scenes']))+' picture beats are built. Edit, approve and render.')
 elif state['manual_edits']:
  state.update(status='manual',detail='This production has manual scene edits, so automatic assembly will not overwrite them. Use Build storyboard to rebuild deliberately.')
 elif not state['auto_enabled']:
  state.update(status='paused',detail='Automatic assembly is switched off for this production. Use Build storyboard to assemble now.')
 else:
  state.update(status='ready',detail='Every input is present and a plan is defined. Automatic assembly will run; if it has not started, use Build storyboard.')
 return state


def maybe_queue(studio,p):
 state=automatic_readiness(p)
 pid=p['id'];root=studio.folder(pid)
 def publish(payload):
  studio.write(root/'automatic_status.json',dict(payload,at=time.time()))
 def status_only(state_,detail):
  publish({'status':state_,'detail':detail})
 if state['status']!='ready' and not (state['status']=='built' and state['auto_enabled'] and not state['manual_edits']):
  # Say why, loudly, instead of sitting idle.
  publish({'status':state['status'],'detail':state['detail'],'missing':state['missing'],'plan':state['plan']})
  return
 from document_plan import rows_for
 from production import matching_assets
 from reviewed_library import queue_ready
 try:
  ready=queue_ready(p,root)
  if not ready:
   rows,_=rows_for(p)
   ready=bool(rows) and all(len(matching_assets(p,r.get('source_file','')))==1 for r in rows)
 except (ValueError,OSError) as e:
  publish({'status':'blocked','detail':'The plan could not be read: '+str(e)[:300]})
  return
 if not ready:
  publish({'status':'blocked','detail':'A plan exists but some beats name a source file that does not match exactly one upload. Resolve those filenames, then build.'})
  return
 render_status=root/'render_status.json'
 def render_busy():
  if not render_status.is_file():return False
  try:return json.loads(render_status.read_text()).get('status') in ['running','queued']
  except (OSError,ValueError):return False
 if p.get('scenes'):
  current=(p.get('render') or {}).get('output') and (p.get('render') or {}).get('current')
  if render_busy() or current:return
  with _QUEUE_LOCK:
   if pid in _PENDING:return
   _PENDING.add(pid)
  def render_only():
   try:
    status_only('rendering','Storyboard is ready. Starting the continuous MP4 master automatically.')
    with studio.app.test_client() as client:
     result=client.post('/api/projects/'+pid+'/render',json={'final':False})
     if result.status_code>=400:
      problem=result.get_json() or {};raise RuntimeError(problem.get('error') or '; '.join(problem.get('errors',[])[:3]) or 'Automatic rendering failed.')
     monitor_render(studio,pid,status_only)
     status_only('done','Continuous master rendering is active. The Program monitor will switch to the rendered MP4 when ready.')
   except Exception as e:status_only('error',str(e))
   finally:
    with _QUEUE_LOCK:_PENDING.discard(pid)
  threading.Thread(target=render_only,daemon=True,name='automatic-render-'+pid).start()
  return
 with _QUEUE_LOCK:
  if pid in _PENDING:return
  _PENDING.add(pid)
 def status(state_,detail):status_only(state_,detail)
 def work():
  try:
   status('working','All inputs are ready. Allowing the machine time to study every source before assembly.')
   time.sleep(1.5);status('studying','Decoding every source MP4 and checking the production plan.')
   with studio.app.test_client() as client:
    result=client.post('/api/projects/'+pid+'/storyboard',json={'replace':True})
    if result.status_code>=400:raise RuntimeError((result.get_json() or {}).get('error','Automatic assembly failed.'))
    count=len((result.get_json() or {}).get('scenes',[]))
    status('storyboard_ready',str(count)+' clean-cut picture slots are ready. Starting continuous MP4 render automatically.')
    time.sleep(.5);status('rendering','Building the continuous MP4 master for smooth Program-monitor playback.')
    rendered=client.post('/api/projects/'+pid+'/render',json={'final':False})
    if rendered.status_code>=400:raise RuntimeError((rendered.get_json() or {}).get('error','Automatic rendering failed.'))
    monitor_render(studio,pid,status)
    status('done','Automatic storyboard and continuous-master rendering are active. The Program monitor will switch to the rendered master when it is ready.')
  except Exception as e:status('error',str(e))
  finally:
   with _QUEUE_LOCK:_PENDING.discard(pid)
 status('queued','Automatic source study and storyboard assembly will begin shortly.')
 threading.Thread(target=work,daemon=True,name='automatic-assembly-'+pid).start()

def full_source_analysis(p,root):
 """Decode every uploaded video before conforming. Scene/black detection is not identity recognition."""
 import subprocess,re,concurrent.futures,imageio_ffmpeg
 root=Path(root);dest=root/'reviews/Full_source_analysis.json';old=json.loads(dest.read_text()) if dest.exists() else {};cached={x['asset_id']:x for x in old.get('sources',[])};ff=imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()
 def scan(a):
  path=root/a['path'];st=path.stat();key=[st.st_size,st.st_mtime_ns];prev=cached.get(a['id'])
  if prev and prev.get('file_signature')==key and prev.get('complete_decode_success'):return prev
  # The budget scales with the picture instead of capping at a fixed 600 s. A scan runs at
  # roughly 50x realtime on two cores, so a ten-minute file takes about 12 s; the allowance
  # is deliberately loose (6 s of decoding per minute of footage, minimum 10 minutes) and
  # exists only to stop a pathological file hanging the whole assembly forever. A fixed
  # ceiling would have failed a long enough file on a slow enough disk with no explanation.
  budget=max(600,int(float(a.get('duration') or 0)*6)+60)
  try:
   r=subprocess.run([ff,'-hide_banner','-threads','2','-i',str(path),'-map','0:v:0','-vf',"scale=320:-2,blackdetect=d=0.05:pic_th=0.99:pix_th=0.05,select='gt(scene,0.32)',showinfo",'-an','-f','null','-'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=budget)
  except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   raise RuntimeError('Source study timed out after %d seconds on %s. The file may be on slow storage; retry, or re-encode it at a lower bitrate.'%(budget,a['original_name']))
  if r.returncode:raise RuntimeError('Full source decode failed: '+a['original_name']+' '+r.stderr[-400:])
  record={'asset_id':a['id'],'file':a['original_name'],'file_signature':key,'sha256':file_sha256(path),'duration_s':a['duration'],'complete_decode_success':True,'scene_change_times_s':[float(x) for x in re.findall(r'pts_time:\s*([\d.]+)',r.stderr)],'black_intervals':[dict(start=float(x),end=float(y)) for x,y in re.findall(r'black_start:([\d.]+) black_end:([\d.]+)',r.stderr)],'scope':'Every video frame decoded; shot changes/black intervals indexed. Semantic identity is NOT inferred by this scan.'}
  return record
 videos=[a for a in p['assets'] if a['kind']=='video'];results=[]
 def checkpoint():
  dest.write_text(json.dumps({'sources':results,'complete':False,'source_count':len(videos),'before_timeline_creation':True},indent=2))
 with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=3) as pool:
  # Each file reports for itself. pool.map raises on the first failure and would discard
  # the completed scans of every other file, so a single unreadable upload cancelled the
  # whole study and lost the work already done.
  futures={pool.submit(scan,a):a for a in videos}
  failures=[]
  try:
   for done in concurrent.futures.as_completed(futures):
    try:
     record=done.result()
    except Exception as e:
     failures.append(str(e)[:400]);continue
    results.append(record);checkpoint()
    print('Full video analysis:',len(results),'/',len(videos),record['file'],flush=True)
  finally:
   # The scans that did succeed are written first, whatever happens next. Studying sources
   # is the most expensive part of assembly on long footage, and one unreadable upload used
   # to throw the finished studies away and start over on the retry.
   checkpoint()
  if failures:raise RuntimeError(failures[0]+('' if len(failures)==1 else ' (%d sources failed.)'%len(failures)))
 report={'sources':results,'complete':len(results)==len(videos),'source_count':len(videos),'before_timeline_creation':True};dest.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2));return report

# Beats are laid on whole output frames, so where an editor split one continuous take
# the two half-frames can appear to overlap by up to a frame. Anything larger is real
# reuse of the same source interval.
CONTINUATION_TOLERANCE = 1.5 / 30


def repetition_errors(p):
 """Pictures that genuinely repeat, never pictures that merely continue.

 The measurement is the source interval each beat actually plays. Two beats from the
 same file are a repeat only when those intervals genuinely overlap. Splitting one take
 across consecutive beats - the normal way to cut a long take into beats - produces
 intervals that touch or overlap by under a frame after frame rounding, and that is the
 same continuous shot, not a repeat. Adjacent beats holding the same photograph are the
 still equivalent. Everything else is reported.
 """
 policy=p.get('repetition_policy') or {};window=int(policy.get('cooldown_slots',0))
 if not window:return []
 errors=[];recent=[];assets={a['id']:a for a in p['assets']};tolerance=CONTINUATION_TOLERANCE
 for i,s in enumerate(p['scenes'],1):
  a=assets.get(s.get('asset_id'));m=s.get('automatic_match') or {}
  if not a:continue  # Empty timeline slots have no played source interval to repeat.
  group=m.get('footage_group') or s.get('footage_group') or (a or {}).get('footage_group')
  source=m.get('original_source_file') or ((a or {}).get('original_full_source') or {}).get('file') or (a or {}).get('original_name')
  duration=(s['frame_out']-s['frame_in'])/30
  start=m.get('original_source_in_s',((a or {}).get('original_full_source') or {}).get('source_in_s',0)+s.get('in_s',0));end=start+duration
  still=a is not None and a.get('kind')=='image'
  previous=recent[-1] if recent else None
  continues=bool(previous and previous[0]==i-1 and previous[2]==source and
                 (abs(previous[4]-start)<=tolerance or (still and previous[6]==a['id'])))
  if (s.get('plan_row') or {}).get('requested_treatment')=='ARCHIVAL REUSE':
   recent.append((i,group,source,start,end,m.get('perceptual_hashes') or [],a['id'] if a else None))
   continue
  if continues:
   recent.append((i,group,source,start,end,m.get('perceptual_hashes') or [],a['id'] if a else None))
   continue
  for j,g,src,inside,outside,prior_hashes,twin in recent[-window:]:
   if group and group==g:
    errors.append(f'Scene {i}: repeats the same shot as scene {j} inside the {window}-slot cooldown.')
    break
   overlap=min(end,outside)-max(start,inside)
   if source is not None and src==source and overlap>tolerance:
    errors.append(f'Scene {i}: replays source footage from scene {j} inside the {window}-slot cooldown '
                  f'(the intervals overlap by {overlap:.2f}s).')
    break
   hs=m.get('perceptual_hashes') or []
   if hs and prior_hashes and min((int(x,16)^int(y,16)).bit_count() for x in hs for y in prior_hashes)<=int(policy.get('visual_hash_max_distance',4)):
    errors.append(f'Scene {i}: visually matching source footage recurs from scene {j} inside the {window}-slot cooldown.')
    break
  recent.append((i,group,source,start,end,m.get('perceptual_hashes') or [],a['id'] if a else None))
 return errors

def source_capacity_errors(p):
 """A plan cannot demand more picture from a file than the file holds.

 Reusing one interval across many beats would pass the cooldown check while making the
 same shot repeat over and over, so the arithmetic is stated plainly before a long
 render. Photographs are exempt: holding one across beats is a legitimate choice.
 """
 assets={a['id']:a for a in p['assets']};demand={}
 for i,s in enumerate(p['scenes'],1):
  a=assets.get(s.get('asset_id'))
  if not a or a.get('kind')!='video' or (s.get('plan_row') or {}).get('requested_treatment')=='ARCHIVAL REUSE':continue
  handle=(s.get('transition_handle_frames') or 0)/30 if s.get('transition')=='dissolve' else 0
  record=demand.setdefault(a['id'],{'name':a.get('original_name') or a.get('name'),'needed':0.0,'beats':0,'duration':float(a.get('duration') or 0)})
  record['needed']+=(s['frame_out']-s['frame_in'])/30+handle;record['beats']+=1
 errors=[]
 for record in demand.values():
  if record['duration'] and record['needed']>record['duration']+1e-6:
   errors.append(str(record['beats'])+' picture beats take '+str(round(record['needed'],3))+'s from '+str(record['name'])+', which is only '+str(round(record['duration'],3))+'s long. Fit cannot come from repeating the same footage; use more sources or shorter beats.')
 return errors

def link_frame_csv(payload,p):
 """Link the supplied frame-cut CSV to actual SRT blocks; preserve planned frame columns."""
 import csv,io
 try:rows=list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(payload.decode('utf-8-sig'))))
 except (UnicodeError,csv.Error):return payload
 if not rows or not {'scene','media_filename','timeline_in_ms','timeline_out_ms_excl'}<=set(rows[0]):return payload
 blocks=(p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks',[])
 if not blocks:raise ValueError('The frame-cut CSV needs the original SRT to resolve block references; no block numbers will be guessed.')
 fields=list(rows[0]);fields += [k for k in ['srt_blocks','import_notes'] if k not in fields]
 for r in rows:
  start,end=int(r['timeline_in_ms']),int(r['timeline_out_ms_excl']);r['srt_blocks']=json.dumps([b['number'] for b in blocks if b['start_ms']<end and b['end_ms']>start]);r['import_notes']='Provided frame columns are a planning grid; explicit millisecond timing is conformed to the project frame rate. A supplied verified YES/NO flag does not independently verify identity or source content.'
 out=io.StringIO();w=csv.DictWriter(out,fieldnames=fields);w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows);return out.getvalue().encode()

{
  "version": 1,
  "purpose": "Reusable editorial standards, not copied case decisions",
  "verification": "User-attested identity/context, with source recorded; never automatic factual or face verification",
  "named_people": "Use matching registered names; unverified wide context requires explicit disclosure and no substituted identity",
  "review_export": "Requires assignments, verification, physical ranges, valid handles, focus, brief and narration timing",
  "final_export": "Additionally requires per-scene editorial approval and rights/reuse notes",
  "audio": "One continuous original voiceover; source-video audio muted in this initial workspace",
  "captions": "Clean MP4, original separate SRT; no burn-in",
  "motion": "Distinct deterministic proposals; review each scene; native action for live video",
  "transitions": "Clean cuts by default; optional 4-frame dissolve pairs with genuine continuation handles; no adjacent dissolve pairs",
  "repetition": "No overlapping selected video intervals, including handles; different reports may contain identical underlying footage, not globally certified",
  "framing": "Unstretched foreground and blurred background; prepared image canvas protects headroom",
  "scope": "Local trusted-user workspace; no multi-user authentication or cloud storage built in",
  "approval_invalidation": "Brief, identity-register or narration changes reset scene approval; asset edits reset affected scene approvals."
}

==================== FILE: conform.py ====================
"""Local media conform: explicit source intervals and extracted-frame previews, not semantic video AI."""
import copy,hashlib,math,threading
from pathlib import Path
import cv2
from production import asset_matches,matching_assets
_SAMPLE_LOCK=threading.RLock()
def capture(path):
 if hasattr(cv2,'CAP_PROP_N_THREADS'):
  cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(path),cv2.CAP_FFMPEG,[cv2.CAP_PROP_N_THREADS,2])
  if cap.isOpened():return cap
  cap.release()
 return cv2.VideoCapture(str(path))

def stills(p,root,rows):
 """Prepare every still beat for assembly.

 Two cases, both real:
 - the beat names an uploaded photograph, which is used as-is for its whole length;
 - the beat asks for a STILL moment inside an uploaded video, which is extracted once
   at exactly the requested time and registered as its own approved asset.

 Nothing is cropped to fake a longer shot and no extracted frame is treated as a
 moving picture. A still that cannot be prepared is reported on its row, never
 replaced by an unrelated clip.
 """
 out=copy.deepcopy(rows);root=Path(root)
 for row in out:
  matches=matching_assets(dict(p,source_bindings={}) if row.get('strict_production') else p,row.get('source_file',''))
  status=str(row.get('visual_status','')).upper()
  if len(matches)==1 and matches[0]['kind']=='image' and row.get('requested_treatment')=='EDITORIAL FREEZE-FRAME':
   row.setdefault('import_blockers',[]).append('Editorial freeze-frame requires a video source, not a photograph.')
   continue
  if len(matches)==1 and matches[0]['kind']=='image':
   if row.get('strict_production') and status!='STILL':
    row.setdefault('import_blockers',[]).append('A video treatment cannot use a still image.')
    continue
   # An uploaded photograph covers the whole beat at native length.
   row['source_in_ms']=0;row['source_out_ms']=row['timeline_out_ms']-row['timeline_in_ms']
   row['visual_status']='PHOTOGRAPH'
   continue
  if status!='STILL':continue
  if len(matches)!=1 or matches[0]['kind']!='video':continue
  source=matches[0];path=root/source['path'];ms=row['source_in_ms'];key=hashlib.sha256((source['id']+str(ms)+('-covering-frame' if row.get('strict_production') else '')).encode()).hexdigest()[:16];aid='frame-'+key
  existing=next((a for a in p['assets'] if a['id']==aid),None)
  try:
   if not path.is_file():raise ValueError('The local source file is missing.')
   if ms/1000>=source['duration']:raise ValueError('Requested still is beyond the source duration.')
   dest=root/'assets'/(aid+'.jpg')
   if not dest.exists():
    cap=capture(path)
    try:
     fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
     if not math.isfinite(fps) or fps<=0:raise ValueError('Cannot determine the source frame rate.')
     index=(math.floor(ms/1000*fps+1e-9) if row.get('strict_production') else round(ms/1000*fps));cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,index);ok,frame=cap.read()
     if not ok:raise ValueError('Cannot decode the requested still frame.')
     if not cv2.imwrite(str(dest),frame,[cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY,94]):raise ValueError('Cannot save extracted frame.')
    finally:cap.release()
   name='frame_'+Path(source.get('original_name',source['name'])).stem+'_'+str(ms)+'.jpg'
   if not existing:
    existing={'id':aid,'name':name,'original_name':name,'path':dest.relative_to(root).as_posix(),'kind':'image','duration':0,'source':source.get('source',''),'people':[],'category':'general','verified':False,'rights':source.get('rights','unreviewed'),'notes':'Frame extracted automatically at the document-requested time; content/identity not visually verified. '+str(row.get('coverage_review','')),'derived_from':{'asset_id':source['id'],'source_file':source.get('original_name',source['name']),'requested_time_ms':ms,'source_id':row.get('source_id','')},'editorial_source_id':row.get('source_id','')};p['assets'].append(existing)
    if source.get('verified') and (p.get('media_approval_policy') or {}).get('auto_upload'):
     from approval import approve_asset
     approve_asset(p,existing,'derived_frame_from_approved_local_upload')
   row['source_file']=name;row['source_in_ms']=0;row['source_out_ms']=row['timeline_out_ms']-row['timeline_in_ms'];row['visual_status']='STILL_EXTRACTED';row['extracted_from_file']=source.get('original_name',source['name']);row['extracted_frame_ms']=ms
  except Exception as e:row.setdefault('import_blockers',[]).append('Still extraction failed: '+str(e))
 return out

def samples(root,asset,cut):
 with _SAMPLE_LOCK:return _samples(root,asset,cut)

def _samples(root,asset,cut):
 """One contact strip of IN / middle / final frame. Never labels its content automatically.

 A photograph has no middle or last frame: it is shown once, labelled as a still, so the
 strip never implies movement that the source does not have.
 """
 from PIL import Image,ImageOps,ImageDraw
 root=Path(root);path=root/asset['path']
 if asset.get('kind')=='image':
  key=hashlib.sha256((str(path)+str(path.stat().st_mtime_ns)+'still').encode()).hexdigest()[:24]
  dest=root/'.cache/planned_samples'/(key+'.jpg');dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
  if not dest.exists():
   canvas=Image.new('RGB',(960,210),'#14212b');draw=ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
   im=ImageOps.contain(ImageOps.exif_transpose(Image.open(path)).convert('RGB'),(632,178))
   canvas.paste(im,((960-im.width)//2,5+(178-im.height)//2))
   draw.text((8,190),'photograph | whole beat | no movement added',fill='white')
   canvas.save(dest,quality=85)
  return dest
 inside=cut['source_in_ms'];outside=cut['source_out_ms']
 if not path.is_file():raise ValueError('Source file not found.')
 key=hashlib.sha256((str(path)+str(path.stat().st_mtime_ns)+str(inside)+str(outside)).encode()).hexdigest()[:24]
 dest=root/'.cache/planned_samples'/(key+'.jpg');dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
 if not dest.exists():
  canvas=Image.new('RGB',(960,210),'#14212b');draw=ImageDraw.Draw(canvas);cap=capture(path)
  try:
   fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS) or 30
   points=[inside,(inside+outside)//2,max(inside,outside-round(1000/fps))]
   for i,ms in enumerate(points):
    cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_MSEC,ms);ok,frame=cap.read()
    if not ok:raise ValueError('Could not decode a planned-cut sample at '+str(ms)+' ms.')
    im=Image.fromarray(cv2.cvtColor(frame,cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB));im=ImageOps.contain(im,(316,178));canvas.paste(im,(i*320+(320-im.width)//2,5+(178-im.height)//2));draw.text((i*320+8,190),str(ms/1000)+'s | '+['IN','MIDDLE','LAST'][i],fill='white')
  finally:cap.release()
  canvas.save(dest,quality=85)
 return dest

==================== END FILE: conform.py ====================


==================== FILE: cue_script.py ====================
"""Strict reader for one-picture-per-cue scripts; declarations are not verification."""
import json
import re
from edl import parse, timecode_ms
from section_script import norm

CLOCK = r'\d{1,2}:\d{2}:\d{2}[.,]\d{3}'

def is_cue_script(text):
    return bool(re.search(r'^\*\*Picture placement\*\*', text, re.M))

def parse_cue_script(text, blocks):
    from document_plan import tables, _clock_span
    text=text.replace('\r\n','\n')
    if not blocks: raise ValueError('Import the original SRT before reading this script.')
    if re.search(r'https?://(?:www\.)?(?:youtube\.com|youtu\.be)/',text,re.I): raise ValueError('YouTube URLs are not permitted in the production script.')
    headings=list(re.finditer(r'^##\s+Srt(\d+)\s*$',text,re.M|re.I))
    if [int(h[1]) for h in headings]!=[int(b['number']) for b in blocks]: raise ValueError('Script cue sections must match the uploaded SRT in order.')
    register={}
    for table in tables(text):
        if table and {'Asset','Full source','Source IN → exclusive OUT','Type','Identification / context'}<=set(table[0]):
            for r in table:
                key=r['Asset'].upper()
                if key in register:raise ValueError('Duplicate clip in source register: '+key)
                register[key]=r
    result=[]
    for i,h in enumerate(headings):
        n=int(h[1]);cue=blocks[i];sid=h[0].strip()[3:];content=text[h.end():headings[i+1].start() if i+1<len(headings) else len(text)]
        content=re.split(r'^##\s+(?!Srt\d+)',content,maxsplit=1,flags=re.M|re.I)[0]
        original=re.search(r'Original SRT cue\s*\*\*(\d+)\*\*\s*·\s*Original time\s*\*\*('+CLOCK+r')\s*-->\s*('+CLOCK+r')\*\*',content,re.I)
        placement=re.search(r'\*\*Picture placement\*\*\s*('+CLOCK+r')\s*→\s*('+CLOCK+r')',content,re.I)
        if len(re.findall(r'\*\*(?:VIDEO|STILL)\s*·', content, re.I))!=1:raise ValueError(sid+': exactly one shot instruction is required per SRT cue.')
        shot=re.search(r'\*\*(VIDEO|STILL)\s*·\s*(?:(C\d+)\s*·\s*)?(S\d+\.(?:mp4|mov))\*\*\s*·\s*Source IN\s*('+CLOCK+r')\s*·\s*OUT\s*('+CLOCK+r')\s+exclusive\s*·\s*[\d.]+\s*s\s*·\s*1\.0×\s*·\s*loop OFF',content,re.I)
        narration=re.search(r'\*\*Exact narration\*\*\s*\n\s*\n(.*?)\n\s*\n',content,re.S|re.I)
        if not original or not placement or not shot or not narration:raise ValueError(sid+': cue timing, narration or video instruction is missing or unsupported.')
        ms=lambda x:timecode_ms(x,sid)
        tin,tout=ms(placement[1]),ms(placement[2]);sin,sout=ms(shot[4]),ms(shot[5])
        if int(original[1])!=n or (ms(original[2]),ms(original[3]))!=(cue['start_ms'],cue['end_ms']) or (tin,tout)!=(cue['start_ms'],cue['end_ms']):raise ValueError(sid+': SRT and picture clocks differ.')
        if sout-sin!=tout-tin:raise ValueError(sid+': source and picture durations differ.')
        typ,clip,file=shot[1].upper(),(shot[2] or '').upper(),shot[3];entry=register.get(clip) if clip else None
        if clip and (not entry or entry['Full source'].casefold()!=file.casefold() or entry['Type'].upper()!=typ or _clock_span(entry['Source IN → exclusive OUT'])!=(sin,sout)):raise ValueError(sid+': source interval differs from selected asset register.')
        def field(name):
            m=re.search(r'^\*\*'+re.escape(name)+r'\.\*\*\s*(.*)$',content,re.M|re.I)
            if not m:raise ValueError(sid+': '+name+' is missing.')
            return m[1].strip()
        visible,caption,direction=field('Picture'),field('Editorial caption'),field('Direction')
        authored=' '.join(narration[1].split());mismatch=norm(authored)!=norm(cue.get('text',''))
        warnings=['Editorial caption and treatment require implementation and review before rendering.']
        if mismatch:warnings.append('Script narration differs from the uploaded SRT; neither is changed.')
        if typ=='STILL':warnings.append('Static broadcast interval requires still-policy and rights review; omitted from moving-only review.')
        result.append(dict(beat_id=sid,shot_id=sid,clip_id=clip,srt_blocks=[n],timeline_in_ms=tin,timeline_out_ms=tout,source_file=file,source_in_ms=sin,source_out_ms=sout,visual_status=typ,requested_treatment='VIDEO' if typ=='VIDEO' else 'STILL HOLD',visible_content=visible,picture_treatment=typ,required_caption=caption,match_reason=direction,limit_on_implication='Source content, identity, rights and full editorial instructions require review.',script_narration=authored,srt_narration=cue.get('text',''),narration_mismatch=mismatch,register_window=entry['Source IN → exclusive OUT'] if entry else '',register_visible=entry['Identification / context'] if entry else '',register_restrictions=entry['Type'] if entry else '',coverage_review='Declared in script; not independently verified.',import_blockers=warnings,strict_production=True,transition_out='cut'))
    return parse(json.dumps(result).encode(),'.json')

==================== END FILE: cue_script.py ====================


==================== FILE: cut_audit.py ====================
"""Compare saved cuts with document timecodes, without claiming visual correspondence."""
from document_plan import rows_for
from production import matching_assets

def audit(p):
 rows,mode=rows_for(p);by_id={r['beat_id']:r for r in rows};assets={a['id']:a for a in p['assets']};out=[]
 for i,s in enumerate(p['scenes'],1):
  r=by_id.get(s.get('plan_row_id'));a=assets.get(s.get('asset_id'));derived=(a or {}).get('derived_from') or {};duration=(s['frame_out']-s['frame_in'])/30*1000
  inside=derived.get('requested_time_ms') if derived else s.get('in_s',0)*1000
  outside=inside if derived else inside+duration
  record={'scene':i,'srt_blocks':'/'.join(map(str,s['srt_blocks'])),'plan_row':s.get('plan_row_id',''),'parent_beat':r.get('parent_beat_id','') if r else '', 'expected_file':r.get('source_file','') if r else '', 'selected_file':derived.get('source_file') or (a or {}).get('original_name',''), 'expected_in_ms':r.get('source_in_ms') if r else None,'saved_in_ms':inside,'in_delta_ms':round(inside-r['source_in_ms'],6) if r and r.get('source_in_ms') is not None else None,'expected_out_ms':r.get('source_out_ms') if r else None,'saved_picture_out_ms':outside,'render_source_out_ms':outside+(s.get('transition_handle_frames',4)/30*1000 if a and a['kind']=='video' and s['transition']=='dissolve' else 0),'expected_timeline_in_ms':r.get('timeline_in_ms') if r else None,'expected_timeline_out_ms':r.get('timeline_out_ms') if r else None,'saved_timeline_in_ms':s['frame_in']/30*1000,'saved_timeline_out_ms':s['frame_out']/30*1000,'transition_extra_frames':s.get('transition_handle_frames',0) if s['transition']=='dissolve' else 0,'visual_match':'NOT_CHECKED','status':'MATCHES_TIMECODES'}
  if not r:record['status']='NO_CONFORMED_PLAN_LINK'
  elif not a:record['status']='NO_ASSIGNED_MEDIA'
  elif (derived.get('asset_id') or a['id']) not in [a['id'] for a in matching_assets(p,r['source_file'])]:record['status']='DIFFERENT_SOURCE'
  elif str(r.get('visual_status','')).upper()=='STILL' and a['kind']!='image':record['status']='STILL_NOT_EXTRACTED'
  elif abs(record['in_delta_ms'])>1.001:record['status']='IN_POINT_MISMATCH'
  elif s['frame_in']!=round(r['timeline_in_ms']*.03) or s['frame_out']!=round(r['timeline_out_ms']*.03):record['status']='TIMELINE_MISMATCH'
  elif not derived and a['kind']=='video' and abs(outside-r['source_out_ms'])>1000/30+1:record['status']='OUT_POINT_MISMATCH'
  out.append(record)
 return {'project_id':p['id'],'project_title':p['title'],'mode':mode,'planned_rows':len(rows),'saved_scenes':len(out),'timing_matches':sum(r['status']=='MATCHES_TIMECODES' for r in out),'visual_verification':False,'note':'A timecode match proves saved interval conformity, not that the picture depicts the intended person/event. CSV source OUT excludes separately listed outgoing dissolve handles. Still OUT is not a moving interval.','rows':out}

==================== END FILE: cut_audit.py ====================

==================== FILE: detailed_script.py ====================
"""Strict parser for explicitly structured SrtNN-letter production shots.

No source identity, visual content or official provenance is inferred. Unsupported
instructions are retained as blockers rather than silently omitted.
"""
import json
import re
from edl import parse, timecode_ms

SHOT = re.compile(r'^srt0*(\d+)(?:-([a-z]+))?$', re.I)
CLIP = re.compile(r'^C\d+$', re.I)
SOURCE = re.compile(r'^S\d+\.(?:mp4|mov|jpg|jpeg|png)$', re.I)
TREATMENTS = {'VIDEO': 'VIDEO', 'CONTEXT VIDEO': 'CTX', 'ARCHIVAL REUSE': 'ARCHIVAL',
              'STILL HOLD': 'STILL', 'EDITORIAL FREEZE-FRAME': 'STILL'}


def key(text):
    return re.sub(r'[^a-z0-9]+', '_', str(text).strip().lower()).strip('_')


def value(row, *names):
    return next((str(row[n]).strip() for n in names if n in row and str(row[n]).strip()), '')


def clock(value_, label):
    if not value_: raise ValueError(label+' is required.')
    return timecode_ms(value_, label)


def span(row, whole, start, end, label, end_optional=False):
    text=value(row,*whole)
    if text:
        parts=re.split(r'\s*(?:→|-->|->|–|—)\s*',text)
        if len(parts)==1 and end_optional:return clock(parts[0],label+' IN'),None
        if len(parts)!=2:raise ValueError(label+' must contain exactly one IN → OUT range.')
        return clock(parts[0],label+' IN'),clock(parts[1],label+' OUT')
    a=value(row,*start);b=value(row,*end)
    return clock(a,label+' IN'),clock(b,label+' OUT') if b else (None if end_optional else clock(b,label+' OUT'))


def parse_detailed(text, blocks):
    """Return strict rows, [] for a legacy script, or fail closed on unsupported shot structure."""
    from document_plan import tables
    if re.search(r'^##\s+Srt\d+\s*$',text,re.M|re.I) and re.search(r'^###\s+Srt\d+(?:-[A-Za-z]+)?\s*$',text,re.M|re.I):
        from section_script import parse_section_script
        return parse_section_script(text,blocks)
    found=[]
    for table in tables(text):
        if not table:continue
        header={key(k) for k in table[0]}
        if header & {'shot','shot_id','shot_reference','shot_ref'}:
            found.extend({key(k):v for k,v in row.items()} for row in table)
    if not found:
        if re.search(r'\bSrt\s*0*\d+\s*-\s*[A-Za-z]+\b',text,re.I):
            raise ValueError('Detailed SrtNN-A shots were found, but no supported shot table was recognized. Provide explicit shot, source, timeline, source time and treatment columns; no fallback was applied.')
        return []
    prose=[line.strip() for line in text.splitlines() if line.strip() and not line.strip().startswith(('|','#')) and not re.fullmatch(r'[-*_ ]+',line.strip())]
    if prose:raise ValueError('The detailed script contains text outside its shot tables. Its instructions cannot be safely interpreted automatically; review and adapt the parser before assembly.')
    for table in tables(text):
        if table and not ({key(k) for k in table[0]} & {'shot','shot_id','shot_reference','shot_ref'}):
            raise ValueError('The detailed script contains an additional unsupported table; its instructions cannot be silently ignored.')
    if re.search(r'https?://(?:www\.)?(?:youtube\.com|youtu\.be)/', text,re.I):
        raise ValueError('Remove YouTube URLs from the production script; use uploaded filenames and source times.')
    if not blocks:raise ValueError('Import the authored SRT before reading detailed shot times.')
    normalized=[];last_block=0;last_suffix=0;seen=set();last_t=0
    for i,row in enumerate(found,1):
        label=f'Shot row {i}'
        known={'shot','shot_id','shot_reference','shot_ref','clip','clip_id','clip_reference','clip_ref','source_file','source','source_video','filename','file','source_filename','narration_timeline_in_out','timeline_in_out','narration_in_out','timeline','narration_timeline_in','timeline_in','narration_in','narration_timeline_out','timeline_out','narration_out','treatment','shot_treatment','source_in_out','source_time','source_range','source_in','source_time_in','source_frame','frame_time','source_out','source_time_out','visual_description','visual','visible_content','description','restrictions','restriction','limitations','limit_on_implication','contextual_caption','caption','required_caption','source_audio','news_audio','audio_instruction','notes','editorial_notes'}
        unknown=[k for k,v in row.items() if k not in known and str(v).strip() not in ('','-','N/A','n/a')]
        if unknown:raise ValueError(label+': unsupported column(s) '+', '.join(unknown)+'; instructions cannot be silently ignored.')
        sid=value(row,'shot','shot_id','shot_reference','shot_ref');match=SHOT.fullmatch(sid)
        if not match:raise ValueError(label+': use an SRT cue ID such as Srt01.')
        block=int(match[1]);suffix=0
        for letter in (match[2] or "A").upper():suffix=suffix*26+ord(letter)-ord('A')+1
        if block<last_block or (block==last_block and suffix<=last_suffix):raise ValueError(label+': shots must be listed in SRT and letter order.')
        if sid.casefold() in seen:raise ValueError(label+': duplicate shot ID '+sid+'.')
        seen.add(sid.casefold());last_block,last_suffix=block,suffix
        clip=value(row,'clip','clip_id','clip_reference','clip_ref')
        src=value(row,'source_file','source','source_video','filename','file','source_filename')
        if clip and not CLIP.fullmatch(clip):raise ValueError(label+': the optional reference is not recognized.')
        if not SOURCE.fullmatch(src):raise ValueError(label+': provide an exact source filename such as S01.mp4.')
        tin,tout=span(row,('narration_timeline_in_out','timeline_in_out','narration_in_out','timeline'),('narration_timeline_in','timeline_in','narration_in'),('narration_timeline_out','timeline_out','narration_out'),'Narration timeline')
        if tin!=last_t or tout<=tin:raise ValueError(label+': timeline must be ordered, contiguous, non-overlapping and start at zero.')
        last_t=tout
        treatment=value(row,'treatment','shot_treatment').upper().replace('–','-').replace('—','-')
        if treatment not in TREATMENTS:raise ValueError(label+': unsupported or missing treatment '+repr(treatment)+'.')
        still=TREATMENTS[treatment]=='STILL'
        sin,sout=span(row,('source_in_out','source_time','source_range'),('source_in','source_time_in','source_frame','frame_time'),('source_out','source_time_out'), 'Source time',end_optional=still)
        if sout is not None and sout<=sin:raise ValueError(label+': source OUT must be later than IN (OUT is exclusive).')
        if not still and sout-sin != tout-tin:raise ValueError(label+': source and narration durations differ; no speed change or hold was assumed.')
        touching=[b['number'] for b in blocks if b['start_ms']<tout and b['end_ms']>tin]
        if block not in touching:raise ValueError(label+': '+sid+' does not overlap its named SRT block.')
        visual=value(row,'visual_description','visual','visible_content','description')
        limitations=value(row,'restrictions','restriction','limitations','limit_on_implication')
        context=value(row,'contextual_caption','caption','required_caption')
        audio=value(row,'source_audio','news_audio','audio_instruction')
        notes=value(row,'notes','editorial_notes')
        blockers=[]
        if not visual: blockers.append('Visual description missing; inspect and specify it.')
        if re.search(r'\b(?:caption|on.screen text|lower.third|subtitle)\b',visual+' '+notes+' '+limitations,re.I):blockers.append('A caption or on-screen text instruction needs manual implementation and review.')
        if re.search(r'\b(?:use|include|keep|play|unmute|retain)\b.{0,30}\b(?:source|news|original)\s+(?:audio|sound|voice|voiceover)\b',notes+' '+limitations,re.I):blockers.append('Source audio instruction needs manual implementation; source audio is muted by default.')
        if re.search(r'\b(?:dissolve|crossfade|fade to black|loop|replay|reverse)\b',notes+' '+limitations,re.I):blockers.append('A transition or playback instruction needs manual implementation and review.')
        if context and context.casefold() not in ('none','no','n/a','-'):blockers.append('Required contextual caption needs manual implementation and review; automatic caption placement is not supported.')
        if audio and not re.fullmatch(r'(?:mute|muted|none|no|n/a|-)',audio,re.I):blockers.append('Source audio instruction needs manual implementation; source audio is muted by default.')
        if re.search(r'\b(?:slow motion|speed up|sped up|time.?lapse|\d+(?:\.\d+)?\s*[x×])\b',notes+' '+limitations,re.I):blockers.append('A speed-change instruction needs manual implementation; normal speed was not substituted.')
        normalized.append({'beat_id':sid,'shot_id':sid,'clip_id':clip.upper(),'srt_blocks':touching,'timeline_in_ms':tin,'timeline_out_ms':tout,
                           'source_file':src,'source_in_ms':sin,'source_out_ms':sout if sout is not None else sin+1,
                           'original_source_out_ms':sout,'visual_status':TREATMENTS[treatment], 'requested_treatment':treatment,
                           'visible_content':visual,'picture_treatment':treatment,'limit_on_implication':limitations,
                           'required_caption':context,'source_audio_instruction':audio,'match_reason':notes,
                           'coverage_review':'Requires human inspection; filename is not visual or source-provenance verification.',
                           'import_blockers':blockers,'strict_production':True,'transition_out':'cut'})
    if last_t < max(int(b['end_ms']) for b in blocks):raise ValueError('The shot table ends before the final narration block.')
    return parse(json.dumps(normalized).encode(),'.json')

==================== END FILE: detailed_script.py ====================


==================== FILE: docs/PRODUCTION_AUDIT.md ====================
# Production audit — what it actually takes to build a master

Verified by running the app, not by reading it. Every claim below was reproduced against a
live server on port 8003 with real files. Nothing here is a guess.

**Test case used:** `Maliyah_Bass_Production_Guide_and_SRT_Map.md` — 120 blocks,
630.256 s, 30 research leads, zero verified source timecodes, zero acquired footage.

---

## 1. The one-line summary

The engine is production-grade. **The paperwork pipeline is where the time goes.** Two of
the three inputs the app asks for are not the ones that decide whether a cut is correct,
and the two inputs that do decide it (real footage, and a source IN/OUT per beat) are the
only ones nothing in the app helps you produce.

| Layer | State | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Render engine (FFmpeg, frame-exact, no loop/freeze) | **Solid** | 120 scenes → 10:30.27 master, 18907 frames, decoded clean |
| Cut conformance (`picture_flow.py`) | **Solid** | 120/120 beats placed inside one decoded shot |
| Narration-breath cut easing | **Solid** | 0 cuts over speech; unchanged audio/SRT |
| SRT-in-Markdown recovery | **Solid** | 120 blocks recovered verbatim, 630.256 s |
| Block-map parsing | **Good but brittle** | needs `.` not `,` in CSV clocks |
| Building a block map from footage | **Missing** | no shot index, no candidate search, no export |
| Photo / still support | **Advertised, unusable** | build hard-fails on any image |
| Mapping approval | **Missing** | 120 scenes = 120 PATCH requests |
| Mapping coverage feedback | **Missing** | failures surface one CSV row at a time |

---

## 2. A real end-to-end run (proves the pipeline works)

Uploaded: SRT (extracted from the guide), the guide as production script, a 120-row block
map, a 630.256 s narration, and nine 140 s source clips.

```
build    : 120 scenes · mode srt_block_map · 0 validation errors     (11 s)
flow     : cuts 119 · over_speech 0 · 120/120 inside one decoded shot
render   : review master 10:30.27 · 1280x720 · 30 fps · 34.7 MB     (2 min 52 s)
approve  : 120 PATCH requests                                     (10.5 s)
final    : final master 10:30.27 · 1280x720 · 30 fps · 36.1 MB
frame QC : sampled 6 real timecodes — pictures change where the map says
```

That run is reproducible and fast. Everything in section 4 is a gap in *preparing* those
inputs, not in rendering them.

---

## 3. What you must supply, and what each part buys you

| Input | Format | Required? | What it controls | Failure mode if missing/wrong |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Narration | mp3/wav/m4a/aac/flac/ogg | **Yes** | Timeline length, breath positions for cut easing | Duration must be within 0.25 s of the SRT end or export is blocked |
| SRT | `.srt`, or a Markdown block table inside the script | **Yes** | Every beat boundary and its wording | `parse_srt` needs strict `00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:00,000` and a blank line between blocks |
| Production script | txt/md/json/docx | **Yes** (auto-assembly checks for it) | Context, names, treatments; can carry the SRT table | A script *without* a beat table places nothing — see gap 1 |
| Block map | CSV | Only if you declare one | **The actual edit**: which file, which source IN/OUT, per block | See gaps 2–6 |
| Footage | mp4/mov | **Yes** | The picture | One block that overruns its source aborts the whole build |
| Photos | jpg/png/webp | Optional idea | Insert/cover gaps | Cannot be used at all today — gap 3 |

### The judgement that only you can make

The engine will happily play a shot that shows the wrong thing. It verifies that a beat
sits inside **one continuous decoded shot**; it cannot verify *which* shot. The former
takes seconds; the latter is the whole editorial job. Plan for that asymmetry: the app
should be doing the first, you should only be doing the second.

---

## 4. Verified gaps (each reproduced live)

### Gap 1 — A script with no beat table builds nothing and hard-fails
Uploading the guide alone gives 120 recovered SRT blocks and **0 beat rows**
(`rows_for` → `mode=''`). Then `build` falls back to keyword assignment, places 0 scenes,
and `validate_assembly` raises *"no native MP4 interval"*, so the storyboard is **never
saved**. Same result with 9 clips uploaded. With no map, no scenes.
*Why it matters:* the guide's own section 5 ("narration-led visual beat plan") is prose
plus a table with the wrong headers (`SRT | Documentary IN → OUT | …`), so the parser
cannot see it. A document that reads like a plan does not act like one.

### Gap 2 — Nothing helps you create the block map
`base_rows_for` only recognises a table with exactly `Timeline in → out` and
`Src IN → OUT`. There is no shot index, no candidate search, no coverage matrix, no
partial-map save, and no map export back out (`GET srt-block-map` → 405; only
`bundle.json` carries it). You write 120–500 rows by hand, from frame inspection.

### Gap 3 — Photos could not be used: stills were blocked in the only supported mode — **fixed**
A map pointing at `shot1.jpg` builds fine, then: *"Scene 1: clips-only timeline contains a
still image."* Every new project is `picture_policy='clips_only'`, the policy can only be
changed by editing `project.json`, and the still path also dead-ends in
`validate_assembly` (needs a video). `conform.stills()` — the code that would extract
frames — is only reachable in a policy you cannot select. **News-video cases are photo
heavy; this alone can stall an edit.**

### Gap 4 — A CSV block map rejected the most natural timecode format — **fixed**
Writing `00:00:05,400` in a CSV splits the cell (`Row 1: too many CSV cells`). You must
write `00:00:05.400`. The SRT accepts `,` and the map does not; the error message does not
say this. This cost a full failed iteration in the test run.

### Gap 5 — A block map must cover every block, or nothing applies
The map is validated as one contiguous unit: block numbers must be exactly 1..N in order,
the timeline must start at 0 and run unbroken to the SRT end. A map for "the 40 blocks I
have footage for" is rejected wholesale. There is no partial mapping and no per-block gap
row (`gap_note` exists in the schema but block maps are rejected before it is consulted).

### Gap 6 — No map-in-progress, so there is no incremental workflow
Because of gaps 4–5, the realistic loop is: inspect all footage → write a complete
120-row map → upload → discover one bad row → fix → repeat. Each round trip costs the
whole map.

### Gap 7 — No coverage planning against the footage you actually hold
Feasibility check on the guide (its own sampled windows vs the timeline it assigns each
lead):

```
V19 needs 146.3s of timeline, was sampled for ~36s
V22 needs 137.9s, sampled ~78s      V24 needs 109.7s, sampled ~18s
V18 needs  81.8s, sampled ~36s      V2 needs   42.5s, sampled ~24s
... 12 of 21 leads are arithmetically impossible without reuse
```

Separately, the 120 beats need **120 distinct continuous shots; 76 must be ≥5 s and 32
must be ≥6 s** (mean beat 5.25 s, max 7.0 s). A news package cut at 2–3 s per shot cannot
supply that without repeated footage — which then trips the 6-slot repeat policy *fast*.
This arithmetic is knowable in seconds before you cut a single clip, and nothing in the
app tells you.

### Gap 8 — Approval is one request per scene
Final export requires `s['approved']` on every scene; the only bulk endpoint is
`assets/approve-all`, which approves **files**, not scenes. 120 scenes = 120 PATCH calls.
*Fix suggestion (10 lines):* `POST /api/projects/<pid>/scenes/approve-all` with an
`attested` flag, alongside the existing per-scene PATCH.

### Gap 9 — Auto-assembly silently does nothing — **fixed**
`maybe_queue` needed `srt` + `voiceover` + `production_script` **and** a ready plan. Without
a map it returned silently: status stayed `idle`, 0 scenes, no message. Upload order made it
worse — the SRT arrives last, after the footage, so nothing was queued at that point either.

**Fixed.** `automatic.automatic_readiness(p)` is now the single answer to "why is nothing
happening", and it is published to the project (`automatic_status.json`) and returned by
every project read as `automatic_readiness`. It reports `status` — `blocked`, `needs_plan`,
`manual`, `paused`, `ready` or `built` — plus `missing` (the inputs still absent), `plan`
(which plan the app found) and `auto_enabled`. The studio renders it as a warning notice
with the reason in plain words and the next action as buttons: download the block-map
template, upload a map, upload a script, or rebuild deliberately. A missing plan, an
unreadable map and a map naming a source file that is not uploaded each publish `blocked`
with the offending name. Auto-assembly can no longer fail without saying so.

### Gap 10 — Rebuild discards human review — **fixed**
`POST /storyboard {replace:true}` started from a clean `scenes` array: approvals, in-point
overrides and notes were lost. There was no "rebuild only blocks 40–60". For a 120-beat
edit that is a lot of re-review per iteration.

**Fixed, in two parts.**
*Approval carry-over.* A rebuild now compares each new beat with the saved one on the
fields that decide what the picture actually is (`srt_blocks`, `frame_in`/`frame_out`,
`asset_id`, `in_s`, transition and handle frames, narration, person, category, context-only,
focus). An exact match inherits the approval and its record, stamped
`carried_from_rebuild`; anything that moved stays unapproved so it is looked at again. It
never approves a beat a human did not already approve. Measured: a full 120-beat rebuild
carried **119 of 120** approvals and asked for one review — the single beat whose in-point
had been changed.
*Block-range rebuild.* `POST /storyboard {replace:true, blocks:[40,…,60]}` rebuilds only
those blocks. Beats touching those blocks are replaced in place with a frame offset, every
other beat keeps its identity, approvals and notes, and `rebuild_report` records
`scope`, `beats`, `approvals_carried` and `needs_review`. The rebuild is refused with a
plain reason if the range would change the length of the surrounding timeline, rather than
saving a storyboard with a gap. Measured on the 120-beat project: one block rebuilt in **0.19 s**, 119 of
120 beats byte-identical afterwards, approvals intact. At 30-minute scale (240 beats, 15
sources) blocks 1, 57, 160 and 240 each rebuilt in **~0.4 s**, 239 of 240 approvals carried
every time, the timeline stayed contiguous and exactly 1800.00 s, and the resulting master
measured **54000 frames / 30:00.00**. A range that genuinely changes its own length (a map
row shortened by 1 s) is still refused with the plain reason. The editor offers it per beat
as "Rebuild this block".

Re-basing a sub-second range onto frame 0 rounds each end independently and can leave a
1-frame residue (a 268-frame range measuring 269 alone). That residue is absorbed at the
last rebuilt beat and reported as `rounding_frames`; anything larger is not, so the
continuity guard still refuses it.

One genuine bug surfaced while testing this and is fixed with it: `picture_flow.snap_cuts`
re-labelled each scene's `srt_blocks` from whatever happened to overlap it after cuts moved
into narration pauses. A beat could therefore end up carrying the *next* block's number and
narration in its saved record. The pass now reports overlaps (`block_conflicts`) instead of
rewriting the storyboard.

### Gap 11 — No frame-QC surface
There is a `/scenes/<id>/thumbnail` and a planned-cut sample strip (`conform.samples`), but
nothing in the UI that shows the chosen frame for every beat in one pass, and no
"in-point moved since review" marker. In the last real production, wrong casting was only
caught by contact sheets built with external ffmpeg — three separate passes.

### Gap 12 — Narration flow needs a real voice track
`narration_pauses` decodes silences at −32 dB. In the audit run (a synthetic tone) it found
**0 pauses**, so 0 of 119 cuts were eased into a breath — the one feature that makes cuts
feel professional simply reported nothing useful.

### Gap 13 — Small edges
- No "test the narration against the SRT" step — the user first learns of a mismatch at build time (`±0.25 s` rule).
- Dissolves are documented and validated, then forced back to cuts by `transition_policy='clean_cuts_only'`.
- No resolution/target setting in the UI (manifest hard-codes 1280×720; portrait 720×1280).
- `#render-button` is disabled as soon as any validation error exists, so you cannot start a review render to *look* at the problem.

---

## 5. The workflow that works today, in order

1. **Narration** — upload first. Its duration is the edit length.
2. **SRT** — upload, or let the script's block table seed it (an explicit `.srt` wins).
3. **Production script** — upload. If it carries the block table, its wording is recovered.
4. **Footage** — upload all of it in one batch (numbered names, `v1.mp4`…).
5. **Inspect**: `reviews/Full_source_analysis.json` after a build lists decoded shot changes
   per file. This is the only shot map available; use it to find continuous windows.
6. **Block map** — write with `.` decimals, one row per block, complete 1..N, contiguous,
   source OUT = IN + beat duration exactly.
7. **Build** — then read the flow summary, `cut-audit` and the frame strips.
8. **Approve** every scene (one request each today), then **final export**.

## 6. What "perfect" actually requires (the checklist that recurs for every case)

- **120 distinct continuous shots.** Not 120 clips — 120 *shots that each hold for the
  whole beat*. 76 of them ≥5 s if the SRT is cut at ~5 s per line.
- **Source OUT = source IN + beat duration, to the frame.** Any overrun aborts the build.
- **No repeated or overlapping intervals** within 6 slots, per source.
- **A verified identity for anyone named in the narration.** Otherwise the check demands
  per-scene context disclosure, and final export needs rights notes.
- **Presenters/reporters excluded** from every chosen window — something only continuous
  inspection catches, since sample frames cannot prove a window is presenter-free.
- **Absent footage must be named as a gap**, not covered with an unrelated shot. The
  engine cannot tell the difference; a viewer can.

---

## 6A. Scale and generality check (30-minute programme)

Run against a deliberately different production from the one the audit was written
from: 30:00 narration, 240 SRT blocks of uneven length (1.2 s–12 s), 240 mapped beats
where the first 24 beats each span two blocks, 15 source clips, and a mixed
photograph/still policy.

| Question | Result |
|---|---|
| Does a 30-minute SRT build? | 240 scenes, 0 errors, 37 s |
| Do multi-block beats work? | Yes — narration merged per beat, 239 cuts, none over speech |
| Do photographs work at this size? | 3 photo beats and stills held correctly |
| Does it render? | 30:00.00, 54,000 frames, 1280×720, 23.4 MB |
| Is a re-render cheap? | All 240 segments reused; one-beat edit re-encoded one segment (12 s) |

Three generalisation bugs were found and fixed by this run:

1. Contiguous beats from one file were flagged as "repeats footage" (frame rounding left
   a sub-frame overlap). The check now compares real source intervals.
2. The feasibility report called a photograph "not decoded" and declared a photo plan
   impossible. Photographs are now unbounded picture sources in that arithmetic.
3. The build spent 97% of its time seeking by timestamp in the decoded-frame check
   (46 ms per seek). It now seeks by frame number and reuses one decoder per source.

## 7. What to implement (ranked by time saved per case)

| # | Change | Why |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | ~~**Still/photo mode**~~ — **done**: `picture_policy` `clips_only`/`mixed` switchable, uploaded photographs and `STILL` map rows (one frame pulled from a clip at its stated time), two layouts, still-aware decoded-frame check | Unblocked photo-heavy cases |
| 2 | ~~**Shot index → candidate list**~~ — **done**: `GET /shot-index` lists every decoded shot with exact clocks; `GET /candidates?length_s=` returns only shots that hold that exact length; the inspector shows the fits for the selected beat | Turned "watch 15 videos" into a list |
| 3 | ~~**Feasibility report before cutting**~~ — **done**: `GET /feasibility` compares seconds needed per source against the file length and decoded shot lengths; the same arithmetic now blocks export readiness when a plan demands more picture than a file holds | Killed dead-end plans (gap 7) |
| 4 | ~~**Block-map builder + export**~~ — **done**: `GET /block-map-template` writes one row per SRT block with clocks and block references filled and picture columns blank by design, importable as named gaps; test proved template → fill from the shot index → re-upload → build 120 scenes, 0 errors | Removed hand-writing and round trips (gaps 2, 5, 6) |
| 5 | ~~**Tolerant CSV clocks**~~ — **done**: `00:00:05,400` and `00:00:05.400` both parse unquoted, six media-column spellings accepted, `source_out_excl` still understood | Removed a whole class of silent failure (gap 4) |
| 6 | ~~**Bulk scene approval**~~ — **done**: `POST /scenes/approve` uses the same per-scene rules as export readiness, approves only clean beats, reports the rest with reasons, and offers an all-or-nothing mode. 120 clicks → 1 | Done (gap 8) |
| 7 | ~~**Incremental rebuild**~~ — **done**: `POST /storyboard {blocks:[…]}` rebuilds one range in place with a frame offset, carries the approval of every beat whose picture did not change (119 of 120 on a full rebuild), stamps `carried_from_rebuild`, refuses a range that would move the surrounding timeline, and reports `rebuild_report`; the editor has a per-beat "Rebuild this block" | Iteration went from re-reviewing a whole edit to 0.2 s and one beat (gap 10) |
| 8 | ~~**Frame-QC sheet**~~ — **done**: `GET /frame-qc?sheet=N` draws 20 beats per sheet, each stamping source file and in-point, with `moved ±Ns` and an amber bar when the in-point no longer matches the plan; undecodable beats are drawn blank and listed | Caught wrong casting before a 3-minute render (gap 11) |
| 9 | ~~**Loud auto-assembly**~~ — **done**: `automatic_readiness` reports `blocked`/`needs_plan`/`manual`/`paused`/`ready`/`built` with the missing inputs and the plan it found; published to `automatic_status.json` and shown as a warning notice with template / upload-map / upload-script / rebuild buttons | Killed the silent-idle trap (gap 9) |
| 10 | **Template generator** — emit a ready-to-fill block map from any SRT (120 rows, clocks filled) | Makes the app reusable across cases |

---

## 9. Bottom line

Uploading is three documents plus footage. **Building the master is one input: the block
map.** Everything the engine needs to be professional (shot conformance, breath cuts,
no loop/freeze, frame-exact length) is already in place and proven at 120 beats. What is
missing is everything that helps you *produce* that map from real footage — and one
blocker (photos) that stops photo-heavy cases outright.

If we implement items 1–5 above, the same guide becomes a repeatable template: paste a new
SRT, drop in the footage, get a candidate map, fill the blanks, render.

==================== END FILE: docs/PRODUCTION_AUDIT.md ====================


==================== FILE: docs/PRODUCTION_SCRIPT_READING_RULES.md ====================
# Production script reading requirements

The production script, SRT and uploaded files remain unchanged. A source filename and its explicit time range specify what to play; a separate clip reference is not required. These rules describe the intended review workflow, not a claim that video content or publication rights are verified.

## Source-based instructions

- Use exactly one shot per SRT cue, in original SRT order. Each shot covers its cue’s complete timeline IN/OUT and uses the cue ID (for example, Srt001), not A/B/C subdivisions. Preserve narration wording, names and timestamps. If an older script splits a cue or spans cues, report it for review rather than silently merging or splitting its instructions.
- Identify each full source by its exact uploaded filename, such as `S01.mp4`. Numbering may have gaps and does not establish playback order. Do not rename sources or silently substitute a different upload.
- For each moving shot specify its timeline IN/OUT and its source IN/OUT. Source OUT is exclusive. Use the exact source interval at 1.0×, with looping off; keep continuous footage advancing across subtitle boundaries.
- Separate clip labels are optional in supported scripts and are not displayed on the storyboard. Older scripts that contain them remain readable; their original text and references are preserved. Where an older script provides a linked register, conflicting instructions are reported rather than silently overridden.
- The editor can read supported labelled plain-text cue instructions, cue-per-section, section-per-SRT shot and detailed shot-table formats. For labelled plain-text cues, a `PROPOSED:` source remains provisional and requires human inspection. A source-only version of those formats can omit separate clip references. Unsupported or ambiguous formats and instructions require review; the editor must not guess source times. The current structured plan parser also requires a contiguous timeline starting at zero, so SRTs with gaps cannot yet be conformed to this rule automatically.

## Safety and editorial review

- Match footage to the narrative, identity, chronology and context. A filename match or decoded frame does not verify what a video shows, its provenance, or rights to publish it.
- Never use loops, screenshots, extracted video frames, freeze-frames, false motion, unlisted footage or unrelated repetition to fill gaps. Static photograph or document intervals, if explicitly scheduled under the approved workflow, count toward the still budget and need separate eligibility and rights checks.
- The review can play explicitly labelled native static intervals directly from their original videos without extracting or freezing frames. Their complete duration counts toward the still budget and their eligibility, identity and rights remain unverified. Extracted stills, freeze instructions and unresolved source ranges remain unassigned. Playback pauses at missing or invalid shots; it does not guess a replacement. The review is not a finished edit or export.
- Scripted captions appear as review text, not rendered captions. Caption layout, visual verification, media conformity and rights decisions remain outstanding. No final render is promised until required checks pass.

## Explicit uploaded photographs in labelled scripts

For a cue that calls for a real uploaded photo, use its exact original filename and `Treatment: PHOTO` (also accepted: `PHOTOGRAPH`, `STILL PHOTO`, `STILL HOLD`). Set both `Source IN: N/A` and `Source OUT: N/A`: a photograph has no video clock. The narration timeline must still match the one SRT cue exactly. For example:

```text
Shot: Srt001
Narration timeline: 00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:04,000
Exact narration: Original words
Full source: victim photo.jpg
Source IN: N/A
Source OUT: N/A
Treatment: PHOTO
Visual direction: Victim photograph; verify identity
Continuity/caption: Review source, rights and intended use
```

The photo must be a unique, original uploaded JPG, PNG or WebP, not an extracted video frame. It is held static for the cue in the review timeline. Missing or duplicate filenames remain unresolved. Actual identity, relevance, still eligibility and rights require human review; an uploaded filename is not proof. The review reports scheduled still time including native static intervals against the 10% maximum, and unresolved material cannot be assumed to be genuine motion. Photo use requires the mixed-picture policy for export, but a review draft is never a final render. This syntax does not change existing scripts or automatically replace video instructions with photos.

==================== END FILE: docs/PRODUCTION_SCRIPT_READING_RULES.md ====================


==================== FILE: document_plan.py ====================
"""Read explicit Markdown source/beat tables; never equate a plan with visual proof."""
import json,re,hashlib,copy
from production import document,asset_matches,reserve_id,matching_assets

def clean(s):return re.sub(r'[*`]', '',str(s)).strip()
def tables(text):
 lines=text.splitlines();i=0
 while i<len(lines):
  if not lines[i].lstrip().startswith('|'):i+=1;continue
  rows=[]
  while i<len(lines) and lines[i].lstrip().startswith('|'):
   line=lines[i].strip()
   if not re.fullmatch(r'[|\s:–-]+',line):rows.append([clean(c) for c in re.split(r'(?<!\\)\|',line.strip('|'))])
   i+=1
  if len(rows)>1:yield [dict(zip(rows[0],row)) for row in rows[1:] if len(row)==len(rows[0])]

def _clock_ms(text):
 """HH:MM:SS.mmm, HH:MM:SS,mmm or MM:SS.mmm -> milliseconds, else None."""
 m=re.fullmatch(r'(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?(?:[.,](\d{1,3}))?',str(text).strip())
 if not m:return None
 a,b,c,frac=m.groups()
 hours,minutes,seconds=(0,int(a),int(b)) if c is None else (int(a),int(b),int(c))
 if minutes>59 or seconds>59:return None
 return (hours*3600+minutes*60+seconds)*1000+int(((frac or '0')+'000')[:3])

def _clock_span(cell):
