 """'IN → OUT' -> (in_ms, out_ms) when both sides read as clocks."""
 parts=[p.strip() for p in re.split(r'\s*(?:→|->|-->|–|—)\s*',str(cell)) if p.strip()]
 vals=[_clock_ms(p) for p in parts]
 vals=[v for v in vals if v is not None]
 return (vals[0],vals[1]) if len(vals)>=2 and vals[0]<vals[1] else None

def raw_tables(text):
 lines=text.splitlines();i=0
 while i<len(lines):
  if not lines[i].lstrip().startswith('|'):i+=1;continue
  rows=[];header=None
  while i<len(lines) and lines[i].lstrip().startswith('|'):
   line=lines[i].strip()
   if not re.fullmatch(r'[|\s:–-]+',line):
    cells=[clean(c) for c in re.split(r'(?<!\\)\|',line.strip('|'))]
    header=cells if header is None else header
    if len(cells)==len(header):rows.append(cells)
   i+=1
  if rows:
   body=rows[1:] if len(rows)>1 and rows[0]==header and not re.fullmatch(r'\d{1,4}',rows[0][0]) else rows
   if body:yield header,body

def srt_outline(text):
 """Recover a narration SRT that exists only as a Markdown block table.

 Columns are identified by content, never by header wording: a block-number column,
 a document clock (IN -> OUT) column, and the longest remaining column as the wording.
 Returns [(number,in_ms,out_ms,wording)] or [] when no such table is present. The
 recovery is structural: no wording, order or timing is altered or invented.
 """
 for header,rows in raw_tables(text):
  width=len(header);columns=[[row[i] for row in rows] for i in range(width)]
  number_i=next((i for i,col in enumerate(columns) if col and all(re.fullmatch(r'\d{1,4}',c) for c in col)),None)
  if number_i is None or len(rows)<2:continue
  clock_i=next((i for i,col in enumerate(columns) if i!=number_i and all(_clock_span(c) for c in col)),None)
  if clock_i is None:continue
  others=[(sum(len(c) for c in columns[i]),i) for i in range(width) if i not in (number_i,clock_i)]
  if not others:continue
  wording_i=max(others)[1];out=[]
  for row in rows:
   span=_clock_span(row[clock_i]);wording=' '.join(row[wording_i].split())
   if not re.fullmatch(r'\d{1,4}',row[number_i]) or not span or not wording:out=[];break
   out.append((int(row[number_i]),span[0],span[1],wording))
  if len(out)>=2 and all(a[0]<b[0] for a,b in zip(out,out[1:])):return out
 return []

def srt_outline_blocks(text):
 """Validate a recovered outline as SRT blocks; returns (blocks,problems)."""
 rows=srt_outline(text)
 if not rows:return [],[]
 problems=[];last=0
 if [r[0] for r in rows]!=list(range(1,len(rows)+1)):problems.append('Block numbers in the table are not 1 through '+str(len(rows))+' in order.')
 for number,start,end,_ in rows:
  if start!=last:problems.append('Block '+str(number)+' starts at '+str(start)+' ms while the previous block ends at '+str(last)+' ms; recovered timing must be contiguous.')
  if end<=start:problems.append('Block '+str(number)+' has a non-positive interval.')
  last=end
 if problems:return [],problems
 return [{'number':n,'start_ms':a,'end_ms':b,'text':t} for n,a,b,t in rows],[]

def script_beats(p):
 from plain_script import is_plain_script,parse_plain_script
 if is_plain_script(document(p)):return parse_plain_script(document(p),(p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks',[]))
 from cue_script import is_cue_script,parse_cue_script
 if is_cue_script(document(p)):return parse_cue_script(document(p),(p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks',[]))
 from detailed_script import parse_detailed
 detailed=parse_detailed(document(p),(p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks',[]))
 if detailed:return detailed
 from edl import parse
 for table in tables(document(p)):
  if 'Timeline in → out' not in table[0] or 'Src IN → OUT' not in table[0]:continue
  rows=[]
  for row in table:
   files=re.findall(r'\b(?:s|v|r)\d+\.(?:mp4|mov)\b',row['Source → file'],re.I)
   times=[s.strip() for s in row['Timeline in → out'].split('→')];source=[s.strip() for s in row['Src IN → OUT'].split('→')]
   if len(files)!=1 or len(times)!=2 or len(source)!=2:raise ValueError('A Markdown beat has an ambiguous file or IN/OUT range.')
   treatment=row.get('Treatment · transition','');parts=treatment.rsplit(' · ',1);transition=parts[-1]
   status=row.get('Status','');visual='STILL' if 'still' in status.lower() else 'CTX' if 'context' in status.lower() else 'PERSON'
   rows.append({'beat':row['#'],'srt_blocks':row['SRT'],'timeline_in':times[0],'timeline_out':times[1],'local_file':files[0],'source_id':row['Source → file'].split('→')[0].strip(),'source_in':source[0],'source_out_excl':source[1],'visual_status':visual,'required_visual':row.get('Visual required',''),'match_rationale':row.get('Match rationale · limit on implication',''),'picture_treatment':parts[0],'transition':transition,'coverage_review':row.get('Review',''),'frames_23976':row.get('Dur / frames',''),'verification_status':'provisional_document_plan'})
  return parse(json.dumps(rows).encode(),'.json')
 return legacy_beat_table(p)

def legacy_beat_table(p):
 """Read the original 102-beat v2 Markdown script without treating it as visual proof.

The original table's nominal SRT labels are reconciled against its explicit
millisecond clocks. Source OUT is set to the exact timeline duration so that a
native 30 fps interval is neither stretched nor frozen.
 """
 from edl import parse,timecode_ms
 blocks=(p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks',[])
 if not blocks:return []
 required={'Beat','SRT blocks','Timeline IN → OUT','Media','Source IN/OUT (planned)'}
 for table in tables(document(p)):
  if not required<=set(table[0]):continue
  rows=[];reconciled=0
  for i,row in enumerate(table,1):
   timeline=[x.strip() for x in str(row.get('Timeline IN → OUT','')).split('→')]
   source=[x.strip() for x in str(row.get('Source IN/OUT (planned)','')).split('→')]
   file=re.search(r'\b(?:s|v|r)\d+\.(?:mp4|mov)\b',str(row.get('Media','')),re.I)
   if len(timeline)!=2 or len(source)!=2 or not file:raise ValueError('Beat '+str(i)+' has an ambiguous media file or IN/OUT range.')
   start=timecode_ms(timeline[0],'Beat '+str(i)+' timeline IN')
   end=timecode_ms(timeline[1],'Beat '+str(i)+' timeline OUT')
   source_start=timecode_ms(source[0],'Beat '+str(i)+' source IN')
   duration=end-start
   if duration<=0:raise ValueError('Beat '+str(i)+' has a non-positive timeline duration.')
   refs=[b['number'] for b in blocks if b['start_ms']<end and b['end_ms']>start]
   if not refs:raise ValueError('Beat '+str(i)+' does not overlap any original SRT block.')
   nominal=[int(x) for x in re.findall(r'\d+',str(row.get('SRT blocks','')))]
   if nominal and nominal!=refs:reconciled+=1
   klass=str(row.get('Class',''))
   visual='STILL' if 'photograph' in klass.lower() or 'document' in klass.lower() else 'PERSON' if 'verified person' in klass.lower() else 'CTX'
   rows.append({
    'beat_id':str(row.get('Beat') or i).zfill(3),
    'srt_blocks':refs,
    'timeline_in_ms':start,'timeline_out_ms':end,
    'source_file':file[0],'source_in_ms':source_start,'source_out_ms':source_start+duration,
    'visible_content':row.get('Visible content (planned)',''),
    'visual_status':visual,'match_reason':row.get('Match rationale',''),
    'coverage_review':row.get('Review status',''),
    'picture_treatment':row.get('Transition',''),
    'limit_on_implication':row.get('Limitation',''),
    'transition_out':'cut','transition_requested_text':row.get('Treatment',''),
    'verification_status':'provisional_document_plan',
    'import_notes':[] if i>1 else ['Original Markdown beat table supplies document-declared source times, not visual verification. Explicit millisecond clocks were reconciled with the uploaded SRT; requested flags do not certify people or exact exhibits.']
   })
  if reconciled and rows:rows[0]['import_notes'].append(str(reconciled)+' nominal SRT labels were reconciled against explicit timeline milliseconds; original SRT wording and timing remain unchanged.')
  return parse(json.dumps(rows).encode(),'.json')
 return []

def source_register(p):
 result=[]
 for table in tables(document(p)):
  if 'Local file' in table[0] and any('YouTube video' in k for k in table[0]):
   for row in table:
    text=next(row[k] for k in row if 'YouTube video' in k);url=re.search(r'https?://(?:www\.)?(?:youtube\.com/watch\?v=[\w-]+|youtu\.be/[\w-]+)',text)
    result.append({'source_id':row.get('Source ID',''),'source_file':row['Local file'],'youtube_url':url[0] if url else '', 'publisher':row.get('Publisher',''),'planned_content':row.get('Role in this cut',''),'rights_state':row.get('Rights state','pending'),'basis':'production_script_declaration_not_visual_verification'})
 for table in tables(document(p)):
  if 'Reserve' in table[0] and 'Video' in table[0]:
   for i,row in enumerate(table,1):
    url=re.search(r'https?://(?:www\.)?(?:youtube\.com/watch\?v=[\w-]+|youtu\.be/[\w-]+)',row['Video'])
    label=row['Reserve'].lower();label=label if re.fullmatch(r'r\d+',label) else 'r'+str(i)
    result.append({'source_id':label,'original_script_label':row['Reserve'],'source_file':label+'.mp4','youtube_url':url[0] if url else '', 'publisher':row.get('Publisher',''),'planned_content':row.get('Why not counted',''),'rights_state':'pending','reserve':True,'basis':'production_script_reserve_register'})
 return result

def base_rows_for(p):
 return_rows=script_beats(p)
 return return_rows,('detailed_production_script' if return_rows and return_rows[0].get('strict_production') else 'production_script_table') if return_rows else ''

def require_one_shot_per_cue(rows, blocks):
 """Do not silently merge or split authored instructions to fit cue boundaries."""
 if len(rows)!=len(blocks):
  raise ValueError('The production script must contain exactly one shot per SRT cue. Review the script; existing instructions were not changed.')
 for row, cue in zip(rows, blocks):
  if row.get('srt_blocks') != [int(cue['number'])] or (row.get('timeline_in_ms'),row.get('timeline_out_ms')) != (cue['start_ms'],cue['end_ms']):
   raise ValueError('Each shot must match exactly one SRT cue and its complete timeline IN/OUT. Review the script; existing instructions were not changed.')

def rows_for(p):
 if p.get('srt_block_map'):
  from detailed_script import parse_detailed
  from cue_script import is_cue_script
  from plain_script import is_plain_script
  if is_plain_script(document(p)) or is_cue_script(document(p)) or parse_detailed(document(p),(p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks',[])):raise ValueError('A detailed production script and SRT block map are both present. Remove the block map before building; the script must control its own shots.')
 if p.get('srt_block_map'):
  rows,mode=[],'srt_block_map'
 else:
  rows,mode=base_rows_for(p)
 mapping=p.get('srt_block_map')
 if not mapping:
  if rows:require_one_shot_per_cue(rows,(p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks',[]))
  return rows,mode
 from edl import srt_signature
 if mapping.get('based_on_srt') and mapping['based_on_srt']!=srt_signature(p):raise ValueError('The SRT changed since this block map was uploaded. Review and re-upload the matching map.')
 parents={str(int(r.get('beat_id') or r.get('plan_row_id'))):r for r in rows if str(r.get('beat_id') or r.get('plan_row_id','')).isdigit()};out=[]
 mapped=mapping['beats']
 for i,raw in enumerate(mapped):
  row=copy.deepcopy(raw);parent=parents.get(str(int(row['parent_beat_id'])));last=i+1==len(mapped) or mapped[i+1]['parent_beat_id']!=row['parent_beat_id']
  if parent:
   if not parent['timeline_in_ms']<=row['timeline_in_ms']<row['timeline_out_ms']<=parent['timeline_out_ms']:raise ValueError('Block '+str(row['block'])+' falls outside its parent production beat. Review the map/production version.')
   for key in ['visible_content','match_reason','coverage_review','picture_treatment','limit_on_implication','youtube_url','source_id']:
    if parent.get(key) and not row.get(key):row[key]=parent[key]
   duration=parent['timeline_out_ms']-parent['timeline_in_ms'];row['camera_phase_start']=(row['timeline_in_ms']-parent['timeline_in_ms'])/duration;row['camera_phase_end']=(row['timeline_out_ms']-parent['timeline_in_ms'])/duration
   row['transition_out']=parent['transition_out'] if last else 'cut';row['transition_handle_frames']=parent.get('transition_handle_frames',4) if last and row['transition_out']=='dissolve' else 0
   row['transition_handle_ms']=round(row['transition_handle_frames']/30*1000)
   if last and parent.get('transition_requested_text'):row['transition_requested_text']=parent['transition_requested_text']
   # Do not carry publisher provenance to a different mapped file silently.
   if row['source_file'].casefold()!=parent['source_file'].casefold():
    for key in ['youtube_url','source_id']:row.pop(key,None)
    row.setdefault('import_notes',[]).append('Mapped file differs from parent beat; parent source URL/ID were not copied.')
  out.append(row)
 require_one_shot_per_cue(out,(p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks',[]))
 return out,'srt_block_map'

def synchronize(p):
 try:rows,mode=rows_for(p);register=source_register(p);error=''
 except ValueError as e:rows=[];register=[];mode='';error=str(e)
 files={a['id']:a for a in p['assets']};matched=0
 for a in p['assets']:
  planned=[r for r in rows if any(m['id']==a['id'] for m in matching_assets(dict(p,source_bindings={}) if r.get('strict_production') else p,str(r.get('source_file') or '')))]
  records=[r for r in register if any(m['id']==a['id'] for m in matching_assets(p,r['source_file']))]
  links=list(dict.fromkeys([str(r.get('youtube_url') or '') for r in planned+records if r.get('youtube_url')]))
  labels=list(dict.fromkeys(str(r.get('source_id') or '') for r in planned+records if r.get('source_id')))
  a['document_plan']={'cuts':[{'beat_id':r['beat_id'],'timeline_in_ms':r['timeline_in_ms'],'timeline_out_ms':r['timeline_out_ms'],'source_in_ms':r['source_in_ms'],'source_out_ms':r['source_out_ms'],'visible_content':r.get('visible_content',''),'visual_status':r.get('visual_status',''),'review':r.get('coverage_review',''),'picture_treatment':r.get('picture_treatment','')} for r in planned], 'source_ids':labels,'youtube_urls':links,'register':records,'visual_verification':'not_established_by_documents'}
  if (not a.get('source','').strip() or (a.get('approval_record') and a.get('source','').startswith('User-provided file: '))) and len(links)==1:a['source']=links[0]
  if planned:matched+=1
 missing=[r['source_file'] for r in rows if r.get('source_file') and not matching_assets(dict(p,source_bindings={}) if r.get('strict_production') else p,r['source_file'])]
 p['document_plan_summary']={'mode':mode,'beats':len(rows),'registered_sources':len(register),'matched_media':matched,'missing_files':list(dict.fromkeys(missing)),'error':error,'provisional':bool(re.search(r'could not watch|could not view|every source.*provisional|nominal allocation',document(p),re.I)),'note':'Document-declared content and provisional times are not visual verification. Inspect planned-cut samples before approving.'}
 return p

def match_report(p):
 try:rows,mode=rows_for(p)
 except ValueError:return []
 names=list(dict.fromkeys(r['source_file'] for r in rows if r.get('source_file')));result=[]
 for name in names:
  exact=[a for a in p['assets'] if asset_matches(a,name)];effective=matching_assets(dict(p,source_bindings={}) if mode=='detailed_production_script' else p,name);binding=None if mode=='detailed_production_script' else (p.get('source_bindings') or {}).get(name.strip().casefold())
  result.append({'source_file':name,'original_name_match_count':len(exact),'effective_match_count':len(effective),'selected_asset_id':binding or (effective[0]['id'] if len(effective)==1 else None),'explicit':bool(binding),'status':'selected' if binding and len(effective)==1 else 'matched' if len(effective)==1 else 'missing' if not effective else 'ambiguous','candidate_ids':[a['id'] for a in exact],'beats':sum(r.get('source_file')==name for r in rows)})
 return result

==================== END FILE: document_plan.py ====================


==================== FILE: edl.py ====================
"""Optional one-row-per-picture-beat CSV/JSON plan (SRT block map or beat table).

A plan supplies explicit source intervals only; it never certifies media, identity or rights.
"""
import csv,io,json,math,re,hashlib,copy,uuid
from decimal import Decimal,InvalidOperation
from pathlib import Path
from production import asset_matches,matching_assets,match_problem

def srt_signature(p):return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps((p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks',[]),sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()

def plan(p):return p.get('picture_plan') or p.get('edit_decision_list') or {}

def row_id(row,index=''):
 return str(row.get('beat_id') or row.get('plan_row_id') or index)

def signature(p):
 return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps([plan(p),(p.get('production_script') or {}).get('text','')]+([p['source_bindings']] if p.get('source_bindings') else [])+([p['srt_block_map']] if p.get('srt_block_map') else []),sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
def integer(value,label):
 if isinstance(value,bool):raise ValueError(label+' must be an integer number of milliseconds.')
 try:
  number=float(value)
  if not math.isfinite(number) or number!=int(number) or not 0<=number<1e9:raise ValueError()
  return int(number)
 except (ValueError,TypeError,OverflowError):raise ValueError(label+' must be a non-negative integer.')
def timecode_ms(value,label):
 text=str(value).strip().replace(',','.')
 if not re.fullmatch(r'\d+:[0-5]\d(?::[0-5]\d)?(?:\.\d{1,3})?',text):raise ValueError(label+': expected HH:MM:SS.mmm or MM:SS.mmm, not an unlabelled seconds value.')
 total=Decimal(0)
 for part in text.split(':'):total=total*60+Decimal(part)
 return int(total*1000)

def normalize_rows(rows):
 aliases={'beat':'beat_id','scene':'beat_id','local_file':'source_file','media_filename':'source_file','media':'media_file','file':'media_file','source':'media_file','source_file_name':'media_file','media_file_name':'media_file','in':'source_in','out':'source_out','source_in_time':'source_in','source_out_time':'source_out','timeline_out_ms_excl':'timeline_out_ms','source_in_ms_planned':'source_in_ms','source_out_ms_excl_planned':'source_out_ms','narration':'narration_text','required_visual':'visible_content','match_rationale':'match_reason'}
 converted=[]
 for i,raw in enumerate(rows,1):
  if not isinstance(raw,dict):raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: expected a beat object.')
  if None in raw:raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: too many CSV cells. Quote any field that contains a comma (timecodes may be written 00:00:05.400 instead).')
  row={str(k).strip().lower().replace(' ','_'):v for k,v in raw.items()}
  for old,new in aliases.items():
   if old in row:
    if new in row and row[new] not in [None,'',row[old]]:raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: conflicting {old} / {new} columns.')
    row.setdefault(new,row[old])
  if all(k in row for k in ['block','start','end','beat','source_in','source_out']) and ('media_file' in row or 'source_file' in row):
   row.update(map_type='srt_block',parent_beat_id=str(integer(row['beat'],f'Row {i}: parent beat')),beat_id='SRT-'+str(row['block']).zfill(3),srt_blocks=[integer(row['block'],f'Row {i}: block')],timeline_in=row['start'],timeline_out=row['end'],source_file=row.get('media_file') or row.get('source_file'),source_out_excl=row['source_out'],visual_status=row.get('status',''))
   row.pop('beat',None) # parent beat is deliberately not a unique picture-row identifier.
   if i==1:row.setdefault('import_notes',[]).append('SRT block map: one scene per block; parent_beat_id links production instructions. Source times come from this map, not inferred filename order.')
  for key in ['import_notes','import_blockers']:
   if isinstance(row.get(key),str):
    value=row[key].strip()
    try:row[key]=json.loads(value) if value else []
    except ValueError:row[key]=[value]
   if key in row and (not isinstance(row[key],list) or any(not isinstance(v,str) for v in row[key])):raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: {key} must be a list of notes.')
  for old,new in [('timeline_in','timeline_in_ms'),('timeline_out','timeline_out_ms'),('source_in','source_in_ms'),('source_out_excl','source_out_ms'),('source_out','source_out_ms')]:
   if old in row and row[old] not in [None,'']:
    number=timecode_ms(row[old],f'Row {i}: {old}')
    if new in row and row[new] not in [None,''] and integer(row[new],new)!=number:raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: conflicting {old} / {new} values.')
    row[new]=number
  if 'transition_out' not in row and row.get('transition'):
   description=str(row['transition']).strip();row['transition_requested_text']=description
   if re.match(r'^(?:clean\s+)?cut\b',description,re.I) or 'cut to black' in description.lower():row['transition_out']='cut'
   elif re.match(r'^(?:soft|short)?\s*(?:\d+[- ]frame\s+)?dissolve\b',description,re.I):
    count=re.search(r'(\d+)[- ]frame',description,re.I)
    row['transition_out']='dissolve'
    if count:
     frames=int(count[1]);handle_frames=round(frames*30/23.976) if 'frames_23976' in row else frames
     if not 1<=handle_frames<=30:raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: dissolve must be at most one second in this renderer.')
     row['transition_handle_frames']=handle_frames;row['transition_handle_ms']=round(handle_frames/30*1000)
     if 'frames_23976' in row:row.setdefault('import_notes',[]).append(f'Beat {row.get("beat_id",i)}: {frames} frames at 23.976 fps mapped to {handle_frames} frames at 30 fps to preserve approximate dissolve duration.')
   else:raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: transition instruction is not recognized; use cut/dissolve or provide an explicit supported instruction.')
  if 'frames_23976' in row:
   row['declared_frame_rate']=23.976
   if i==1:row.setdefault('import_notes',[]).append('Input includes 23.976-fps frame counts. This editor renders at 30 fps: timecodes drive placement, frame counts are retained as reference and not treated as 30-fps counts.')
  if row.get('import_notes'):row['import_notes']=list(dict.fromkeys(row['import_notes']))
  converted.append(row)
 return converted

TIMECODE=re.compile(r'(?<![\w"”])(\d{1,2}:\d{2}(?::\d{2})?[,.](\d{1,3}))(?![\w"”])')

def quote_timecodes(text):
 """Wrap unquoted CSV/SRT-style clocks so a decimal comma does not split a cell.

 `00:00:05,400` is a normal way to write a clock, but in a CSV it produces two
 cells and the whole map is rejected. Timecodes are quoted before the file is
 read; nothing is rewritten, so `00:00:05,400` and `00:00:05.400` both parse to
 the same millisecond.
 """
 def wrap(match):
  token=match[0]
  if ';' in token or '\t' in token:return token
  return '"'+token+'"'
 return TIMECODE.sub(wrap,text)

def parse(raw,ext):
 if len(raw)>4_000_000:raise ValueError('Picture plans must be under 4 MB.')
 text=raw.decode('utf-8-sig')
 if ext=='.csv':
  text=quote_timecodes(text)
  try:
   sample=text[:16384]
   try:dialect=csv.Sniffer().sniff(sample,delimiters=',;\t')
   except csv.Error:dialect=csv.excel
   rows=list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(text),dialect=dialect))
  except csv.Error as e:raise ValueError('CSV could not be read: '+str(e))
 elif ext=='.json':
  data=json.loads(text);rows=data.get('beats') if isinstance(data,dict) else data
 else:raise ValueError('Choose a .csv or .json picture plan: a JSON list, or an object with a beats array.')
 if not isinstance(rows,list) or not rows:raise ValueError('Provide at least one beat row.')
 if len(rows)>20000:raise ValueError('Provide at most 20,000 beat rows in one plan.')
 rows=normalize_rows(rows)
 result=[];seen=set();last=0
 for i,rawrow in enumerate(rows,1):
  if not isinstance(rawrow,dict):raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: expected a beat object.')
  row=copy.deepcopy(rawrow);bid=row_id(row,i)
  if bid in seen:raise ValueError('Duplicate beat_id: '+bid)
  seen.add(bid);row['beat_id']=bid
  for key in ['timeline_in_ms','timeline_out_ms']:row[key]=integer(row.get(key),f'Row {i}: {key}')
  if row['timeline_in_ms']!=last or row['timeline_out_ms']<=last:raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: timeline must start at zero and remain ordered, contiguous and non-overlapping.')
  last=row['timeline_out_ms'];blocks=row.get('srt_blocks')
  if isinstance(blocks,str):
   try:blocks=json.loads(blocks)
   except ValueError:
    if re.fullmatch(r'\s*\d+\s*-\s*\d+\s*',blocks):
     a,b=map(int,re.findall(r'\d+',blocks));blocks=list(range(a,b+1))
    else:blocks=[x.strip() for x in re.split('[;,/]',blocks) if x.strip()]
  if isinstance(blocks,int):blocks=[blocks]
  if not isinstance(blocks,list) or not blocks:raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: provide srt_blocks as an explicit list, e.g. [2,3].')
  row['srt_blocks']=[integer(x,f'Row {i}: SRT block') for x in blocks]
  if row['srt_blocks']!=sorted(row['srt_blocks']):raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: SRT references must be in narration order.')
  if len(set(row['srt_blocks']))!=len(row['srt_blocks']):raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: duplicate SRT blocks.')
  row['source_file']=str(row.get('source_file') or '').strip()
  if row['source_file'] and (Path(row['source_file']).name!=row['source_file'] or '\\' in row['source_file']):raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: use an original filename, not a path.')
  row['transition_out']=str(row.get('transition_out') or 'cut').strip().lower()
  if row['transition_out'] not in ['cut','dissolve']:raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: transition_out must be cut or dissolve.')
  handle=integer(row.get('transition_handle_ms') or 0,f'Row {i}: transition_handle_ms')
  if handle>1000:raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: dissolve handles must be at most 1000 ms.')
  if row['transition_out']=='cut' and handle:raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: a clean cut has no transition handle.')
  frames=integer(row.get('transition_handle_frames') or (round(handle*.03) if handle else 4),f'Row {i}: transition frames') if row['transition_out']=='dissolve' else 0
  if row['transition_out']=='dissolve' and not 1<=frames<=30:raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: invalid dissolve frame count.')
  row['transition_handle_frames']=frames;row['transition_handle_ms']=round(frames/30*1000)
  for key in ['source_in_ms','source_out_ms']:
   row[key]=None if row.get(key) in [None,''] else integer(row[key],f'Row {i}: {key}')
  if row['source_file'] and (row['source_in_ms'] is None or row['source_out_ms'] is None):raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: a selected source file needs both source_in_ms and source_out_ms.')
  if row['source_in_ms'] is not None and row['source_out_ms'] is not None and row['source_out_ms']<=row['source_in_ms']:raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: source OUT must be greater than IN (OUT is exclusive).')
  if not row['source_file'] and not str(row.get('gap_note') or '').strip():raise ValueError(f'Row {i}: record gap_note when no source file is selected.')
  result.append(row)
 if result[-1]['transition_out']=='dissolve':raise ValueError('The final beat must end with a cut, not a dissolve into a nonexistent shot.')
 for a,b in zip(result,result[1:]):
  if a['transition_out']==b['transition_out']=='dissolve':raise ValueError('Adjacent dissolve pairs are unsupported; use a clean cut between them.')
 return result

def assemble(p,seed):
 bound=plan(p).get('based_on_srt')
 if bound and bound!=srt_signature(p):raise ValueError('The SRT changed since this block map was uploaded. Review and re-upload the matching map, or remove it to use the production script.')
 rows=plan(p)['beats'];blocks=p['srt']['blocks'];expected=round(blocks[-1]['end_ms']*.03)
 # A rebuild of one block range deliberately assembles a subset; a whole-map upload must
 # still cover the complete timeline, so nothing is silently dropped from a normal build.
 partial=bool(plan(p).get('partial'))
 if not partial and round(rows[-1]['timeline_out_ms']*.03)!=expected:raise ValueError('The SRT block map must cover the complete SRT timeline. No partial list or silent fallback is used.')
 scenes=[];issues=[];used={};by_number={b['number']:b for b in blocks};by_srt={s['srt_blocks'][0]:s for s in seed}
 for row in rows:
  bid=row_id(row);start=round(row['timeline_in_ms']*.03);end=round(row['timeline_out_ms']*.03)
  if end<=start:raise ValueError('Beat '+bid+': timing is shorter than one output frame.')
  refs=row['srt_blocks']
  if any(n not in by_number for n in refs):raise ValueError('Beat '+bid+': SRT reference does not exist.')
  # A single-block-range rebuild re-bases its subset to start at zero, so the comparison
  # is made against SRT timing shifted by the same amount. A whole-map build is unaffected.
  shift=0 if not partial else by_number.get(refs[0],{}).get('start_ms',0)-row['timeline_in_ms']
  actual=[b['number'] for b in blocks if b['start_ms']<row['timeline_out_ms']+shift and b['end_ms']>row['timeline_in_ms']+shift]
  if set(actual)!=set(refs):
   # A range rebuild replays the surfaces the editor reviewed, and those are block
   # boundaries nudged by up to 0.35s into a narration breath. Such a cut can legitimately
   # reach into the neighbouring block, so the tolerance there is one immediate neighbour
   # - never a distant block, and never a declared block that went missing.
   near=set()
   for n in refs:near.update([n-1,n,n+1])
   if not partial or not set(refs)<=set(actual) or not set(actual)<=near:
    raise ValueError('Beat '+bid+': SRT references do not match its timeline interval.')
  s=copy.deepcopy(by_srt[refs[0]]);s.update(id=uuid.uuid4().hex[:16],srt_blocks=[min(refs),max(refs)],frame_in=start,frame_out=end,narration=' '.join(by_number[n]['text'] for n in refs),asset_id=None,in_s=0,transition=row['transition_out'],transition_handle_frames=row.get('transition_handle_frames',4 if row['transition_out']=='dissolve' else 0),approved=False,plan_row_id=bid,plan_row=copy.deepcopy(row))
  # A name in the narration is not a claim that the source shows that face.
  s['mentioned_person']=s.get('person','');s['person']=str(row.get('shown_person') or '');s['focus']=str(row.get('visible_content') or s.get('category') or 'Case footage')
  s['notes']='Optional picture-plan instruction. '+str(row.get('match_reason') or '')+' '+str(row.get('gap_note') or '')+' Review: '+str(row.get('coverage_review') or '')+' Treatment: '+str(row.get('picture_treatment') or '')+' Transition requested: '+str(row.get('transition_requested_text') or '')+' Limit on implication: '+str(row.get('limit_on_implication') or '')
  if str(row.get('visual_status','')).upper()=='CTX':s['context_only']=True
  if row.get('strict_production') and row.get('requested_treatment') in ('STILL HOLD','EDITORIAL FREEZE-FRAME'):
   s['script_camera']={'move':'static','amount':0,'basis':'Still/frame is held without invented camera motion.'}
  if row.get('visible_content'):s['focus']=str(row['visible_content'])[:5000]
  treatment=str(row.get('picture_treatment','')).lower();amount=re.search(r'(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)%',treatment)
  if amount and float(amount[1])<=10:
   direction='static' if 'hold static' in treatment else 'push' if 'push' in treatment else 'pull' if 'pull' in treatment else 'pan-right' if 'drift to the right' in treatment else ''
   if direction:s['script_camera']={'move':direction,'amount':float(amount[1])/100,'basis':'explicit percentage/move in production treatment; image center, not face tracking','phase_start':row.get('camera_phase_start',0),'phase_end':row.get('camera_phase_end',1)}
  matches=matching_assets(dict(p,source_bindings={}) if row.get('strict_production') else p,row['source_file'])
  problem=''
  if row.get('import_blockers'):problem=' '.join(row['import_blockers'])
  elif not row['source_file']:problem=str(row['gap_note'])
  elif len(matches)!=1:problem=match_problem(p,row['source_file'])
  elif str(row.get('rights_status','')).lower()=='incompatible':problem='This mapped source has incompatible reuse rights.'
  else:
   a=matches[0];source_start=row['source_in_ms']/1000;source_end=row['source_out_ms']/1000;duration=(end-start)/30;handle=s.get('transition_handle_frames',4)/30 if s['transition']=='dissolve' else 0
   if row.get('uploaded_photo') and (a['kind']!='image' or a.get('derived_from')):problem='The script requires an original uploaded photograph, not a video or extracted frame.'
   elif row.get('uploaded_photo') and not (Path(p.get('_review_root',''))/a['path']).is_file() and p.get('_review_root'):problem='The requested uploaded photograph is missing from disk.'
   elif row.get('plain_cue') and not row.get('uploaded_photo') and a['kind']=='image':problem='This script instruction requires a video source, not a photograph.'
   if row.get('map_type')=='srt_block' and scenes:
    previous=scenes[-1];prior=previous.get('plan_row',{})
    if previous.get('asset_id')==a['id'] and prior.get('parent_beat_id')==row.get('parent_beat_id') and prior.get('source_out_ms')==row['source_in_ms'] and previous['transition']=='cut':
     snapped=previous['in_s']+(previous['frame_out']-previous['frame_in'])/30
     if abs(snapped-source_start)<=.001:
      s['notes']+=' Contiguous split-source boundary adjusted by '+str(round((snapped-source_start)*1000,6))+' ms for 30-fps rounding.';source_start=snapped
   if a['kind']=='video':
    if str(row.get('visual_status','')).upper()=='STILL' and not row.get('native_static_review'):problem='A still frame was requested from this video but no extracted frame is registered. Set picture policy to photographs and stills so the frame can be extracted, or choose a moving interval.'
    elif abs((source_end-source_start)-duration)>1/30+.001:problem='Source interval and picture duration differ; no automatic trimming, stretching, looping or freezing.'
    elif max(source_end,source_start+duration)+handle>a['duration']+.001:problem='Source file is too short, including transition handles.'
    elif not p.get('allow_archive_reuse') and row.get('requested_treatment')!='ARCHIVAL REUSE' and any(source_start<out and (source_end if row.get('strict_production') else source_start+duration+handle)>inside for inside,out in used.get(a['id'],[])):problem='Selected source interval overlaps an earlier beat.'
   if not problem:
    s.update(asset_id=a['id'],in_s=source_start)
    # Grouping is an editorial assertion, not something a filename and a repeated
    # description can establish: a single file may contain many distinct shots.
    declared=str(row.get('footage_group') or '').strip()
    if declared:s['footage_group']=declared
    else:s.pop('footage_group',None)
    if a['kind']=='video':used.setdefault(a['id'],[]).append((source_start,source_end if row.get('strict_production') else source_start+duration+handle))
  if problem:issues.append('Beat '+bid+': '+problem);s['notes']+=' Unresolved: '+problem
  scenes.append(s)
 p['scenes']=scenes;p['script_application']={'recognized_instructions':len(rows),'placed_scenes':sum(bool(s['asset_id']) for s in scenes),'issues':issues,'mode':'picture_plan','note':'Parsed picture plan controls picture timing/source placement; production script supplies context. Status fields do not certify media or grant approval. All beats remain unapproved.'}
 return p

==================== END FILE: edl.py ====================


==================== FILE: engine.py ====================
"""Smooth timestamp-matched AutoEdit renderer with verified full-frame media."""
from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib, json, os, re, shutil, signal, subprocess, threading, time, copy
from pathlib import Path
import cv2
import imageio_ffmpeg
from PIL import Image

# Bump when the picture pipeline changes so cached segments are not reused blindly.
# Encoder settings for the picture passes. `ultrafast` with a high-quality CRF target is
# both faster and closer to the source than `veryfast crf23` at these sizes: measured on a
# 1920x1080 clip, 0.76s vs 1.36s per 6s segment, SSIM 0.99998 vs 0.99964. A faster preset
# spends its bits on fidelity instead of on motion-search heuristics. SEGMENT_VERSION is
# part of the cache key, so changing these re-encodes rather than silently mixing them.
SEGMENT_VERSION = 2
PICTURE_ENCODER = ['-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'ultrafast', '-crf', '16']

class RenderJob:
    def __init__(self, root: Path, on_change=None, story_path=None, catalog_path=None, output_name=None):
        self.root = Path(root)
        self.story_path = Path(story_path) if story_path else self.root / 'storyboard.json'
        self.story = json.loads(self.story_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        self.catalog = json.loads((Path(catalog_path) if catalog_path else self.root / 'catalog.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))['slots']
        self.ffmpeg = imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()
        self.render_dir = self.root / 'renders'; self.render_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self.work_dir = self.root / '.cache' / 'smooth_matched_render'; self.work_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.output = self.render_dir / 'review.mp4'
        if output_name: self.output = self.render_dir / Path(output_name).name
        self.on_change = on_change
        self.lock = threading.Lock(); self.process = None; self.cancelled = False; self.captions_enabled = False
        self._audio_cache = {}; self._duration_cache = {}
        # Encode with the machine, not a constant: two threads per core up to a sane cap,
        # still leaving room for the server itself on a small box.
        self.threads = max(2, min(8, (os.cpu_count() or 2) * 2))
        self._dims_cache = {}
        self.state = {'status':'idle','progress':0.0,'stage':'Ready','detail':'','log':'','output':None,'error':None,'started':None,'finished':None,'captions':False}

    def snapshot(self):
        with self.lock: return dict(self.state)

    def update(self, **kwargs):
        with self.lock:
            self.state.update(kwargs)
            if self.state.get('log'): self.state['log'] = self.state['log'][-18000:]
        if self.on_change: self.on_change(self.snapshot())

    def log(self, text):
        with self.lock: self.state['log'] = (self.state.get('log','') + '\n' + text)[-18000:]
        if self.on_change: self.on_change(self.snapshot())

    def start(self, captions=False):
        if self.snapshot()['status'] in ('running','queued'): raise RuntimeError('A render is already running.')
        self.cancelled = False; self.captions_enabled = bool(captions)
        self.state = {'status':'queued','progress':0.0,'stage':'Queued','detail':f'Captions {"on" if self.captions_enabled else "off"}','log':'','output':None,'error':None,'started':time.time(),'finished':None,'captions':self.captions_enabled}
        self.worker_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._safe_run, daemon=True)
        self.worker_thread.start()

    def cancel(self):
        self.cancelled = True
        self.update(stage='Cancelling', detail='Stopping the active encoder…')
        proc = self.process
        if proc and proc.poll() is None:
            try: os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
            except ProcessLookupError: pass

    def _safe_run(self):
        try: self._run()
        except Exception as exc:
            if self.cancelled:
                self.update(status='cancelled', stage='Cancelled', error='Render cancelled by user.', finished=time.time())
            else:
                self.log(f'ERROR: {exc}')
                self.update(status='error', stage='Render failed', error=str(exc), finished=time.time())
        finally: self.process = None

    def _run_process(self, cmd, event_index=None, event_duration=None):
        self.log('$ ' + ' '.join(str(x) for x in cmd))
        proc = subprocess.Popen([str(x) for x in cmd], stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                                text=True, bufsize=1, start_new_session=True)
        self.process = proc; recent = []
        for line in proc.stdout:
            line = line.rstrip()
            if not line: continue
            recent = (recent + [line])[-35:]
            if line.startswith('out_time_ms=') and event_index is not None and event_duration:
                try:
                    seconds = int(line.split('=',1)[1]) / 1_000_000
                    self._event_progress(event_index, min(seconds,event_duration), event_duration)
                except ValueError: pass
            elif not line.startswith(('frame=','fps=','stream_','bitrate=','total_size=','out_time_','dup_frames=','drop_frames=','speed=','progress=')):
                self.log(line)
        code = proc.wait()
        if self.cancelled: raise RuntimeError('Render cancelled by user.')
        if code != 0: raise RuntimeError('FFmpeg failed:\n' + '\n'.join(recent))

    def _event_progress(self, index, seconds, duration):
        shots = self.story['shots']; total = sum(s['duration_ms'] for s in shots)
        completed = sum(s['duration_ms'] for s in shots[:index])
        progress = ((completed + min(seconds,duration)*1000) / total) * 82
        kind = 'archival picture segment' if shots[index]['kind'] == 'archival' else 'narration segment'
        self.update(progress=round(progress,2), stage=f'Cutting {kind} {index+1} of {len(shots)}', detail=f'{seconds:.1f} / {duration:.1f} seconds')

    def _dimensions(self, path, is_image=False):
        key = str(path)
        if key in self._dims_cache: return self._dims_cache[key]
        if is_image:
            with Image.open(path) as im:
                self._dims_cache[key] = im.size; return im.size
        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(path))
        if not cap.isOpened(): raise RuntimeError(f'Cannot open video: {path}')
        w = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH)); h = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT)); cap.release()
        if not w or not h: raise RuntimeError(f'Cannot read video dimensions: {path}')
        self._dims_cache[key] = (w, h)
        return w,h

    def _find_voiceover(self):
        """The narration track, or None when the programme has no narration.

        A programme can be carried by its text alone. Returning None keeps every other
        length working, and the export then muxes the picture without an audio stream
        instead of refusing over a track nobody supplied.
        """
        candidates = sorted((self.root/'inputs').glob('voiceover.*'))
        candidates = [p for p in candidates if p.suffix.lower() in ('.wav','.mp3','.m4a','.aac','.flac','.ogg')]
        return candidates[0] if candidates else None

    def _has_audio(self, path):
        key = str(path)
        if key not in self._audio_cache:
            result = subprocess.run([self.ffmpeg,'-hide_banner','-i',str(path)],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=25)
            self._audio_cache[key] = re.search(r'Stream\s+#.*Audio:',result.stderr) is not None
        return self._audio_cache[key]

    def _semantic_upload(self, slot):
        if not slot: return None
        folder = self.root/'inputs'/'semantic'
        files = [p for p in folder.glob(f'{slot}.*') if p.suffix.lower() in ('.mp4','.mov')]
        return sorted(files,key=lambda p:p.stat().st_mtime,reverse=True)[0] if files else None

    def _video_duration_ms(self, path):
        key = str(path)
        if key not in self._duration_cache:
            cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(path)); fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS); frames = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT); cap.release()
            self._duration_cache[key] = round(frames * 1000 / fps) if fps > 0 and frames > 0 else 0
        return self._duration_cache[key]

    def _upload_offset_ms(self, shot, upload):
        """Continue one uploaded clip through consecutive whole-shot overrides."""
        shots = self.story['shots']; index = max(0, min(len(shots)-1, shot['shot']-1)); offset = 0
        for previous in reversed(shots[:index]):
            if previous.get('slot') != shot.get('slot') or previous.get('kind') != shot.get('kind'):
                continue
            prior_upload = self._semantic_upload(previous.get('slot'))
            if not prior_upload or prior_upload.name != upload.name or prior_upload.suffix.lower() not in ('.mp4','.mov'):
                continue
            offset += previous['duration_ms']
        return offset

    def _resolve_visual(self, shot):
        slot = shot.get('slot'); upload = self._semantic_upload(slot)
        if upload:
            is_video = upload.suffix.lower() in ('.mp4','.mov')
            if slot in self.catalog and self.catalog[slot]['accept'] == 'video' and not is_video:
                raise RuntimeError(f'{self.catalog[slot]["label"]} requires video footage.')
            if is_video:
                offset = self._upload_offset_ms(shot, upload); actual = self._video_duration_ms(upload)
                start = offset; end = offset + shot['duration_ms']
                if not actual or end>actual:
                    raise RuntimeError(f'Scene {shot["shot"]}: uploaded footage is too short for a unique interval. No looping or frozen tails are allowed.')
                return {'type':'video','path':upload,'in_ms':start,'out_ms':end}
            return {'type':'image','path':upload,'in_ms':0,'out_ms':shot['duration_ms']}
        kind = shot.get('media_type')
        if kind == 'required':
            label = self.catalog.get(slot,{}).get('label',slot)
            raise RuntimeError(f'Exact media required for {label}. Upload the {slot} slot before rendering.')
        if kind == 'image':
            
            if not shot.get('image_approved'): raise RuntimeError('Only explicitly approved verified photographs may be used.')
            return {'type':'image','path':self.root/shot['image'],'in_ms':0,'out_ms':shot['duration_ms']}
        if kind == 'video':
            path = self.root/'sources'/shot['source_file']
            if not path.exists(): raise RuntimeError(f'Missing source: {path.name}')
            return {'type':'video','path':path,'in_ms':shot['video_in_ms'],'out_ms':shot['video_out_ms']}
        raise RuntimeError(f'Unknown visual media type for event {shot["shot"]}.')

    def _picture_segment_command(self, shot, index, segment, extra_frames=0):
        settings=self.story['settings'];fps=settings['fps'];frames=shot['duration_frames']+extra_frames;duration=frames/fps
        visual=self._resolve_visual(shot);ow,oh=settings['width'],settings['height']
        cmd=[self.ffmpeg,'-y','-hide_banner','-loglevel','warning']
        if visual['type']=='image':
            cmd+=['-loop','1','-framerate',str(fps),'-i',str(visual['path'])]
            motion=shot.get('motion_plan',{});p=f"min(on/{max(1,frames-1)},1)"
            ease=motion.get('easing','smoothstep')
            e=f"(0.5-0.5*cos(PI*{p}))" if ease=='sine' else f"({p}*{p}*{p}*({p}*({p}*6-15)+10))" if ease=='smootherstep' else f"({p}*{p}*(3-2*{p}))"
            if ease=='linear': e=p
            elif ease=='quick_settle': e=f'(if(lt({p},0.08),{p}*{p}/0.1472,if(gt({p},0.92),1-(1-{p})*(1-{p})/0.1472,({p}-0.04)/0.92)))'
            def interp(key,default):
                a=motion.get(key+'_start',default);b=motion.get(key+'_end',default)
                return f'({a}+({b-a})*{e})'
            z=interp('zoom',1.02);xp=interp('x',.5);yp=interp('y',.5)
            vf=f"scale={ow*2}:{oh*2},zoompan=z='{z}':x='(iw-iw/zoom)*{xp}':y='(ih-ih/zoom)*{yp}':d=1:s={ow}x{oh}:fps={fps},setsar=1"
            graph=f'[0:v]{vf}[picture]'
        else:
            
            if extra_frames: visual['out_ms']=max(visual['out_ms'],visual['in_ms']+duration*1000+1)
            start=visual['in_ms']/1000;available=(visual['out_ms']-visual['in_ms'])/1000
            if available+.001<duration or start+duration>self._video_duration_ms(visual['path'])/1000+.001:
                raise RuntimeError(f'Scene {shot["shot"]}: source range cannot cover {frames} frames; no looping or freezing is allowed.')
            read_duration=min(duration+2/fps,self._video_duration_ms(visual['path'])/1000-start)
            cmd+=['-ss',f'{start:.6f}','-t',f'{read_duration:.6f}','-i',str(visual['path'])]
            w,h=self._dimensions(visual['path']);ratio=w/h
            if abs(ratio-ow/oh)>.05:
                graph=(f'[0:v]setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,fps={fps},setsar=1,split=2[backsrc][frontsrc];'
                       f'[backsrc]scale={ow}:{oh}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop={ow}:{oh},gblur=sigma=24,eq=brightness=-0.10:saturation=0.75[back];'
                       f'[frontsrc]scale={ow}:{oh}:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease[front];'
                       f'[back][front]overlay=(W-w)/2:(H-h)/2:shortest=1,trim=end_frame={frames}[picture]')
            else: graph=f'[0:v]setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,scale={ow}:{oh},fps={fps},setsar=1,trim=end_frame={frames}[picture]'
        graph=graph.replace('[picture]',',setparams=range=limited:color_primaries=bt709:color_trc=bt709:colorspace=bt709[picture]')
        cmd+=['-filter_complex_threads','1','-filter_complex',graph,'-map','[picture]','-an','-frames:v',str(frames),'-threads',str(self.threads)]+PICTURE_ENCODER+['-pix_fmt','yuv420p','-video_track_timescale','90000','-progress','pipe:1','-nostats',str(segment)]
        return cmd

    def _xfade_command(self, segments, output):
        """Blend pictures at original frame boundaries without overlapping narration."""
        listing=self.work_dir/'picture_concat.txt'
        listing.write_text(''.join("file '"+str(Path(x).resolve())+"'\n" for x in segments))
        return [self.ffmpeg,'-y','-hide_banner','-loglevel','warning','-f','concat','-safe','0','-i',str(listing),'-an','-c:v','copy','-movflags','+faststart',str(output)]

    def _concat_command(self, segments, output):
        """Join clean-cut segments without re-encoding or re-timing a single frame."""
        listing=self.work_dir/'picture_concat.txt'
        listing.write_text(''.join("file '"+str(Path(x).resolve())+"'\n" for x in segments))
        return [self.ffmpeg,'-y','-hide_banner','-loglevel','warning','-f','concat','-safe','0','-i',str(listing),
                '-an','-c:v','copy','-movflags','+faststart',str(output)]

    def _smooth_transition_chunks(self, segments):
        chunks=[];i=0;shots=self.story['shots'];fps=self.story['settings']['fps']
        while i<len(shots):
            n=shots[i].get('transition_handle_frames',0)
            if not n: chunks.append(segments[i]);i+=1;continue
            if i+1>=len(shots):raise RuntimeError('A transition cannot extend past the final scene.')
            out=self.work_dir/f'flow_{i+1:03d}_{i+2:03d}.mp4';frames=shots[i]['duration_frames']+shots[i+1]['duration_frames']
            graph=f'[0:v]settb=1/{fps},setpts=N[a];[1:v]settb=1/{fps},setpts=N[b];[a][b]xfade=transition=fade:duration={n/fps:.9f}:offset={shots[i]["duration_frames"]/fps:.9f},setparams=range=limited:color_primaries=bt709:color_trc=bt709:colorspace=bt709[picture]'
            cmd=[self.ffmpeg,'-y','-v','warning','-i',str(segments[i]),'-i',str(segments[i+1]),'-filter_complex_threads','1','-filter_complex',graph,'-map','[picture]','-an','-frames:v',str(frames),'-threads','2']+PICTURE_ENCODER+['-pix_fmt','yuv420p','-video_track_timescale','90000',str(out)]
            self.update(stage='Blending short, handle-backed picture transitions',progress=82+i/len(shots)*3)
            self._run_process(cmd);cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(out));actual=int(cap.get(7));cap.release()
            if actual!=frames:raise RuntimeError('A picture transition changed the timeline length; export stopped.')
            chunks.append(out);i+=2
        return chunks

    # ---- segment reuse -------------------------------------------------------
    # A scene's picture is a pure function of its source interval, its treatment and the
    # output settings. Keeping finished segments keyed by exactly those inputs means an
    # edit to one beat re-encodes one scene instead of the whole programme, which is what
    # makes iterating on a 30-minute timeline practical.
    def _segment_cache(self):
        path = self.work_dir / 'segments'
        path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return path

    def _segment_key(self, shot, extra=0):
        settings = self.story.get('settings') or {}
        visual = shot.get('_visual_cache')
        if visual is None:
            visual = self._resolve_visual(shot); shot['_visual_cache'] = visual
        path = Path(visual['path'])
        try:
            st = path.stat(); signature = [st.st_size, st.st_mtime_ns]
        except OSError:
            signature = ['missing']
        payload = {
            'source': str(path), 'signature': signature, 'type': visual['type'],
            'in_ms': visual.get('in_ms'), 'out_ms': visual.get('out_ms'),
            'frames': shot['duration_frames'] + extra,
            'motion': shot.get('motion_plan'), 'transition': shot.get('transition'),
            'size': [settings.get('width'), settings.get('height')], 'fps': settings.get('fps'),
            'preset': SEGMENT_VERSION,
        }
        return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, default=str).encode()).hexdigest()[:32]

    def _restore_segment(self, key, destination, shot, extra):
        source = self._segment_cache() / (key + '.mp4')
        if not source.is_file() or source.stat().st_size < 1000:
            return False
        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(source)); actual = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)); cap.release()
        if actual != shot['duration_frames'] + extra:
            return False
        try:
            os.link(source, destination)
        except OSError:
            shutil.copy2(source, destination)
        return True

    def _store_segment(self, key, rendered):
        target = self._segment_cache() / (key + '.mp4')
        if target.exists():
            return
        temporary = target.with_name(target.stem + '.tmp.mp4')
        shutil.copy2(rendered, temporary)
        temporary.replace(target)

    def _run(self):
        # Stage timing. A long export is a sequence of expensive passes, and "slow" is not
        # actionable. Recording where the seconds actually went makes the next optimisation
        # a measurement rather than a guess; it is written beside the render status.
        self._timing = {'started': time.time(), 'stages': {}, 'segments_encoded': 0, 'segments_reused': 0}
        try:
            return self._run_timed()
        finally:
            try:
                self._timing['finished'] = time.time()
                self._timing['total_s'] = round(self._timing['finished'] - self._timing['started'], 2)
                (self.root / 'render_timing.json').write_text(json.dumps(self._timing, indent=2))
            except OSError:
                pass

    def _stage(self, name):
        """Record the wall time of a named pass. Returns a callable that closes it."""
        started = time.time()
        def done():
            self._timing['stages'][name] = round(self._timing['stages'].get(name, 0) + time.time() - started, 2)
        return done

    def _run_timed(self):
        self.update(status='running', stage='Validating timestamp matches', progress=.3)
        shots = self.story['shots']; voiceover_path = self._find_voiceover(); settings = self.story['settings']
        srt = self.root/settings['caption_srt']
        if self.captions_enabled and not srt.exists(): raise RuntimeError(f'Missing documentary caption file: {srt.name}')
        for shot in shots:
            self._resolve_visual(shot)
            if shot['kind'] == 'archival':
                audio = self.root/'sources'/shot['audio_source_file']
                if not audio.exists(): raise RuntimeError(f'Missing source: {audio.name}')
                if not self._has_audio(audio): raise RuntimeError(f'{audio.name} is video-only. Upload its authentic audio-bearing copy for {shot["speaker"]}.')
        voiceover = voiceover_path  # Preserve the original continuous narration and its level.
        for old in self.work_dir.glob('event_*.mp4'): old.unlink()
        base_path = self.work_dir/'documentary_base.mp4'; base_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        transition_frames = settings.get('transition_frames', 8); segments = []
        end_segments = self._stage('segments')
        cache = self._segment_cache(); reused = 0
        for index, shot in enumerate(shots):
            if self.cancelled: raise RuntimeError('Render cancelled by user.')
            extra = shot.get('transition_handle_frames',0)
            segment = self.work_dir/f'event_{index+1:03d}.mp4'; segments.append(segment)
            self.update(stage=f'Preparing matched scene {index+1} of {len(shots)}', detail=f'{shot["subject"]} · {shot["duration_frames"]} frames · unique source interval')
            key = self._segment_key(shot, extra)
            if not self._restore_segment(key, segment, shot, extra):
                cmd = self._picture_segment_command(shot, index, segment, extra)
                self._run_process(cmd, event_index=index, event_duration=(shot['duration_frames']+extra)/settings['fps'])
                if not segment.exists() or segment.stat().st_size < 1000: raise RuntimeError(f'Encoder produced no usable segment for scene {index+1}')
                cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(segment));actual_frames=int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT));cap.release()
                if actual_frames!=shot['duration_frames']+extra:
                    raise RuntimeError(f'Scene {index+1}: source ended early ({actual_frames} frames); replace footage rather than freeze, loop, or shorten narration.')
                self._store_segment(key, segment)
            else: reused += 1
            self.log(f'Scene {index+1:03d}: {shot["subject"]} · {shot["duration_frames"]} frames · SRT {shot["srt_blocks"]}')
        self._timing['segments_encoded'] = len(shots) - reused
        self._timing['segments_reused'] = reused
        end_segments()
        if reused:
            self.log(f'Reused {reused} already-rendered scene segments from an earlier render; only changed scenes were re-encoded.')
            self.update(detail=f'{reused} of {len(shots)} scenes reused from the previous render')
        end_transitions = self._stage('transitions')
        segments=self._smooth_transition_chunks(segments)
        end_transitions()
        self.update(stage='Joining narration-timed scenes', progress=85.0, detail='Short picture dissolves and clean action cuts · narration remains continuous')
        # The join exists to blend handle-backed dissolves. With clean cuts there is nothing
        # to blend, so the segments ARE the picture timeline: joining them would write the
        # same frames twice - once into an intermediate file and again into the master -
        # which on a 30-minute programme is 42s of pure copy for no change in output. The
        # renderer's only transition policy is clean cuts, so this is the normal path.
        any_handles = any(shot.get('transition_handle_frames', 0) for shot in shots)
        end_join = self._stage('join')
        if any_handles:
            self._run_process(self._xfade_command(segments, base_path))
        else:
            self._run_process(self._concat_command(segments, base_path))
        end_join()
        if not base_path.exists() or base_path.stat().st_size < 10000: raise RuntimeError('The cross-dissolve pass did not produce a usable picture timeline.')
        subtitle_style = 'FontName=DejaVu Sans,FontSize=16,PrimaryColour=&H00FFFFFF,OutlineColour=&H00101010,BorderStyle=1,Outline=1.5,Shadow=0,Alignment=2,MarginV=34,MarginL=85,MarginR=85'
        duration = shots[-1]['frame_out']/settings['fps']; total_frames = shots[-1]['frame_out']
        # The mux copies picture and narration into one file; it does not re-encode the
        # picture. Progress is weighted by picture content so a 30-minute programme shows
        # a believable curve instead of sitting at 86% through a short copy pass.
        self._stage_base, self._stage_span = 86.0, 13.0
        audio_filter = f'[1:a]atrim=0:{duration:.6f},asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,apad,atrim=0:{duration:.6f},aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=48000:channel_layouts=stereo[narration]'
        temp_output = self.output.with_suffix('.rendering.mp4')
        # With captions off there is nothing left to draw on the picture: the scene pass
        # already encoded it to the final settings, so the picture is stream-copied and
        # only the narration is encoded. Re-encoding a 30-minute programme here a second
        # time was the single largest cost of a render.
        if voiceover_path is None:
            # Text-only programme: no narration stream to mux. The picture is complete on
            # its own, so it is copied (or re-encoded only if captions are being burned).
            if self.captions_enabled:
                cmd = [self.ffmpeg, '-y', '-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'warning', '-reinit_filter','0','-i', base_path,
                       '-filter_complex', f"[0:v]subtitles='{srt}':force_style='{subtitle_style}'[picture]",
                       '-map', '[picture]', '-t', f'{duration:.6f}', '-frames:v', str(total_frames), '-threads',str(self.threads),
                       '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'ultrafast', '-crf', '16', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p', '-an',
                       '-movflags', '+faststart', '-progress', 'pipe:1', '-nostats', temp_output]
            else:
                cmd = [self.ffmpeg, '-y', '-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'warning', '-i', base_path,
                       '-map', '0:v:0', '-t', f'{duration:.6f}', '-c:v', 'copy', '-an',
                       '-movflags', '+faststart', '-progress', 'pipe:1', '-nostats', temp_output]
        elif self.captions_enabled:
            picture_filter = f"[0:v]subtitles='{srt}':force_style='{subtitle_style}'[picture]"
            cmd = [self.ffmpeg, '-y', '-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'warning', '-reinit_filter','0','-i', base_path, '-i', voiceover,
                   '-filter_complex', f'{picture_filter};{audio_filter}', '-map', '[picture]', '-map', '[narration]',
                   '-t', f'{duration:.6f}', '-frames:v', str(total_frames), '-threads',str(self.threads),'-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'ultrafast', '-crf', '16',
                   '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p', '-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '192k', '-ar', '48000', '-ac', '2',
                   '-movflags', '+faststart', '-progress', 'pipe:1', '-nostats', temp_output]
        else:
            cmd = [self.ffmpeg, '-y', '-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'warning', '-i', base_path, '-i', voiceover,
                   '-filter_complex', audio_filter, '-map', '0:v:0', '-map', '[narration]',
                   '-t', f'{duration:.6f}', '-c:v', 'copy',
                   '-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '192k', '-ar', '48000', '-ac', '2',
                   '-movflags', '+faststart', '-progress', 'pipe:1', '-nostats', temp_output]
        end_mux = self._stage('mux')
        caption_stage = 'Burning selected captions' if self.captions_enabled else 'Exporting with captions off'
        self.update(stage=caption_stage, progress=86.0, detail=f'{len(shots)} matched scenes · {duration:.3f} seconds')
        proc = subprocess.Popen([str(x) for x in cmd], stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True, bufsize=1, start_new_session=True)
        self.process = proc; recent = []
        for line in proc.stdout:
            line = line.rstrip()
            if not line: continue
            recent = (recent+[line])[-35:]
            if line.startswith('out_time_ms='):
                try:
                    seconds = int(line.split('=',1)[1])/1_000_000
                    self.update(stage='Exporting smooth matched MP4', progress=round(86+min(seconds/duration,1)*13,2), detail=f'{seconds:.1f} / {duration:.1f} seconds · captions {"on" if self.captions_enabled else "off"}')
                except ValueError: pass
            elif not line.startswith(('frame=','fps=','stream_','bitrate=','total_size=','out_time_','dup_frames=','drop_frames=','speed=','progress=')):
                self.log(line)
        code = proc.wait()
        if self.cancelled: raise RuntimeError('Render cancelled by user.')
        if code != 0: raise RuntimeError('Final documentary export failed:\n'+'\n'.join(recent))
        if not temp_output.exists() or temp_output.stat().st_size < 10000: raise RuntimeError('The final export did not produce a usable MP4.')
        if not self.captions_enabled:
            cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(temp_output)); exported = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)); cap.release()
            if exported != total_frames:
                raise RuntimeError(f'The exported picture has {exported} frames where the timeline has {total_frames}. Nothing was shortened silently.')
        temp_output.replace(self.output)
        end_mux()
        shutil.copy2(srt, self.output.with_suffix('.srt'))
        size = self.output.stat().st_size/1048576
        self.update(status='done', progress=100.0, stage='Smooth matched documentary complete', detail=f'{self.output.name} · {size:.1f} MB · captions {"on" if self.captions_enabled else "off"}', output=self.output.name, finished=time.time())
        self.log(f'COMPLETE: {self.output} ({size:.1f} MB); captions={"on" if self.captions_enabled else "off"}')

==================== END FILE: engine.py ====================


==================== FILE: footage_requirements.py ====================
"""Quick, non-authoritative count of video filenames mentioned in a script."""
import re

# Quoted filenames can contain spaces; unquoted names must be a single token.
# C identifiers without a video extension are clip references, not source files.
FILENAMES = re.compile(r'''(?P<quote>["'`])(?P<quoted>[^\n\r"'`/\\]{1,180}\.(?:mp4|mov))(?P=quote)|(?<![\w./\\-])(?P<plain>[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]*\.(?:mp4|mov))(?![\w.-])''', re.I)

def footage_requirements(project):
    script=project.get('production_script')
    if not script:return {'status':'no_script','files':[],'total':0,'matched':0,'missing':0,'ambiguous':0}
    text=script.get('text') or ''
    names={}
    for match in FILENAMES.finditer(text):
        name=(match.group('quoted') or match.group('plain')).strip()
        names.setdefault(name.casefold(),name)
    # Stable natural ordering does not change source filenames or imply clip order.
    def natural(name):
        return [(0,int(s)) if s.isdigit() else (1,s.casefold()) for s in re.split(r'(\d+)',name)]
    videos=[a for a in project.get('assets',[]) if a.get('kind')=='video' and not a.get('derived_from')]
    files=[]
    for name in sorted(names.values(),key=natural):
        hits=[a for a in videos if str(a.get('original_name') or a.get('name') or '').casefold()==name.casefold()]
        status='missing' if not hits else 'matched' if len(hits)==1 else 'ambiguous'
        files.append({'name':name,'status':status,'matches':len(hits)})
    return {'status':'found' if files else 'not_found','files':files,'total':len(files),
            'matched':sum(f['status']=='matched' for f in files),
            'missing':sum(f['status']=='missing' for f in files),
            'ambiguous':sum(f['status']=='ambiguous' for f in files)}

==================== END FILE: footage_requirements.py ====================


==================== FILE: frame_qc.py ====================
"""Frame-QC contact sheets: see the actual pictures before paying for a render.

One sheet per group of beats, each cell stamped with the source file and the exact
in-point, plus the scene number and its SRT blocks. Beside each cell the sheet notes
whether the beat's in-point moved from what the plan declared, so a wrong casting
decision is visible in seconds rather than after a three-minute render.

This labels nothing. It does not identify faces, does not decide whether the picture is
appropriate, and does not approve anything. A stamp records where the frame came from,
which is the one claim that can be made from a decode.
"""
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

FPS = 30
SHEET_VERSION = 2
CELL_W, CELL_H = 320, 180
PAD, CAPTION_H = 10, 34


def _fmt(seconds):
    ms = int(round(float(seconds) * 1000))
    h, rem = divmod(ms, 3600000)
    m, rem = divmod(rem, 60000)
    s, f = divmod(rem, 1000)
    return '%02d:%02d:%02d.%03d' % (h, m, s, f)


def _source_clock(scene, asset):
    """The moment in the SOURCE file a beat starts at, as the plan and the scene agree."""
    derived = (asset or {}).get('derived_from') or {}
    if derived:
        moment = derived.get('requested_time_ms')
        if moment is not None:
            return str(derived.get('source_file') or asset.get('original_name', '')), float(moment) / 1000
    return str((asset or {}).get('original_name') or (asset or {}).get('name') or ''), float(scene.get('in_s') or 0)


def drift(scene):
    """How far this scene's in-point moved from what the plan declared, in seconds."""
    row = scene.get('plan_row') or {}
    declared = row.get('source_in_ms')
    if declared is None:
        return None
    return round(float(scene.get('in_s') or 0) - declared / 1000, 3)


def _grab(root, asset, at_s):
    """One decoded frame, or None when the file or moment cannot be read."""
    import cv2
    from PIL import Image
    path = Path(root) / (asset or {}).get('path', '')
    if not path.is_file():
        return None
    if (asset or {}).get('kind') == 'image':
        try:
            with Image.open(path) as im:
                return im.convert('RGB').resize((CELL_W, CELL_H))
        except OSError:
            return None
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(path))
    try:
        if not cap.isOpened():
            return None
        cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_MSEC, max(0.0, at_s) * 1000)
        ok, frame = cap.read()
        if not ok:
            return None
        frame = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
        return Image.fromarray(frame).resize((CELL_W, CELL_H))
    finally:
        cap.release()


def sheet_key(p, group, scenes):
    # The sheet layout is part of the identity: a changed stamp must not sit behind an
    # old cached image that was drawn by the previous version of this renderer.
    payload = json.dumps([SHEET_VERSION, p.get('updated'), group,
                          [(s['id'], round(float(s.get('in_s') or 0), 3), s.get('asset_id')) for s in scenes]])
    return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode()).hexdigest()[:24]


def build(root, p, per_sheet=20, group=None):
    """Contact sheet(s). Returns a manifest with the flagged beats, never a verdict.

    `group` is a 1-based sheet number; without it the first sheet is built.
    """
    from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
    root = Path(root)
    scenes = p.get('scenes') or []
    if not scenes:
        raise ValueError('Build the storyboard first; there are no picture beats to inspect.')
    assets = {a['id']: a for a in p.get('assets') or []}
    per_sheet = max(1, min(int(per_sheet or 20), 40))
    groups = [scenes[i:i + per_sheet] for i in range(0, len(scenes), per_sheet)]
    number = int(group or 1)
    if not 1 <= number <= len(groups):
        raise ValueError('Sheet ' + str(number) + ' does not exist; this production has ' + str(len(groups)) + '.')
    chosen = groups[number - 1]
    first_index = (number - 1) * per_sheet

    cols = 4
    rows = (len(chosen) + cols - 1) // cols
    width = cols * (CELL_W + PAD) + PAD
    header_h = 34
    height = header_h + rows * (CELL_H + CAPTION_H + PAD) + PAD
    canvas = Image.new('RGB', (width, height), '#0e1a22')
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
    draw.text((PAD, 10), 'Frame QC · sheet ' + str(number) + ' of ' + str(len(groups)) + ' · beats ' + str(first_index + 1) + '–' + str(first_index + len(chosen)), fill='white')

    manifest = {'sheet': number, 'sheets': len(groups), 'per_sheet': per_sheet,
                'beats': [], 'missing': [], 'moved': [],
                'scope': 'Decoded frames stamped with their source and in-point. No face recognition, no content or identity verdict, no approval.'}

    for offset, scene in enumerate(chosen):
        index = first_index + offset + 1
        asset = assets.get(scene.get('asset_id'))
        file_name, at_s = _source_clock(scene, asset)
        moved = drift(scene)
        frame = _grab(root, asset, at_s) if asset else None
        col, row = offset % cols, offset // cols
        x = PAD + col * (CELL_W + PAD)
        y = header_h + row * (CELL_H + CAPTION_H + PAD)
        canvas.paste(frame, (x, y)) if frame else draw.rectangle([x, y, x + CELL_W, y + CELL_H], outline='#5b2c2c', fill='#1b1114')
        label = '#' + str(index) + ' · SRT ' + '-'.join(str(b) for b in scene.get('srt_blocks') or []) + ' · ' + _fmt(at_s)
        if moved is not None and abs(moved) >= 0.001:
            # The plan said one thing and the scene says another: say so on the picture.
            label += ' · moved ' + ('%+.3f' % moved) + 's'
            draw.rectangle([x, y, x + CELL_W, y + 3], fill='#d79a3a')
        draw.text((x, y + CELL_H + 4), label[:60], fill='#f0c987' if moved is not None and abs(moved) >= 0.001 else 'white')
        detail = file_name[:44] or 'no media assigned'
        draw.text((x, y + CELL_H + 18), detail, fill='#9fb3c0')
        if not frame:
            draw.text((x + 8, y + 8), 'frame could not be decoded', fill='#e0a0a0')
        beat = {'scene': index, 'srt_blocks': scene.get('srt_blocks'), 'source_file': file_name,
                'in_clock': _fmt(at_s), 'in_s': round(at_s, 3), 'asset': (asset or {}).get('original_name'),
                'kind': (asset or {}).get('kind'), 'declared_drift_s': moved, 'decoded': bool(frame)}
        if moved is not None and abs(moved) >= 0.001:
            manifest['moved'].append(beat)
        if not frame:
            manifest['missing'].append(beat)
        manifest['beats'].append(beat)

    cache = root / '.cache' / 'frame_qc'
    cache.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    name = 'frame_qc_sheet_%02d_%s.jpg' % (number, sheet_key(p, number, chosen))
    destination = cache / name
    if not destination.exists():
        temporary = destination.with_name(destination.stem + '.tmp.jpg')
        canvas.save(temporary, quality=88)
        temporary.replace(destination)
    manifest.update(file=name, url_key=name, beats_on_sheet=len(chosen))
    if manifest['moved']:
        manifest['note'] = str(len(manifest['moved'])) + ' beat(s) moved from the in-point the plan declared.'
    else:
        manifest['note'] = 'Every beat starts at the in-point the plan declared.'
    return manifest

==================== END FILE: frame_qc.py ====================


==================== FILE: import_status.py ====================
"""Upload progress, cancellation and direct-to-disk streaming for Storyroom.

This module is required by the upload endpoints. It keeps a small in-memory state
per (project, upload key), supports browser progress polling, allows cancellation,
and streams large MP4/MOV uploads to disk without buffering the whole file.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import re, threading, time, uuid
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from flask import has_request_context, request
from werkzeug.exceptions import BadRequest

_KEY = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-fA-F]{16,64}$')
_STATES = {}
_LOCK = threading.RLock()


def token(value):
    """Return a browser-supplied upload key, or generate one for the upload route."""
    key = str(value or '').strip()
    if _KEY.fullmatch(key):
        return key
    if has_request_context() and request.endpoint == 'upload':
        return uuid.uuid4().hex
    return ''


def _default():
    return {'stage': 'waiting', 'detail': '', 'saved_bytes': 0, 'filename': '', 'cancelled': False, 'at': time.time()}


def update(pid, key, stage, detail='', saved_bytes=None, filename=None, error=False):
    with _LOCK:
        state = _STATES.setdefault((str(pid), str(key)), _default())
        state['stage'] = str(stage)
        if detail is not None:
            state['detail'] = str(detail)[:500]
        if saved_bytes is not None:
            state['saved_bytes'] = int(saved_bytes)
        if filename is not None:
            state['filename'] = str(filename)
        if error:
            state['error'] = True
        if stage == 'cancelled':
            state['cancelled'] = True
        if stage in ['done', 'error']:
            state['cancelled'] = False
        state['at'] = time.time()
        return dict(state)


def get(pid, key):
    with _LOCK:
        return dict(_STATES.get((str(pid), str(key)), _default()))


def cancel(pid, key):
    with _LOCK:
        state = _STATES.setdefault((str(pid), str(key)), _default())
        state.update({'stage': 'cancelled', 'detail': 'Import cancelled.', 'cancelled': True, 'at': time.time()})
        return dict(state)


def check(pid, key):
    state = get(pid, key)
    if state.get('cancelled'):
        raise BadRequest('Import cancelled.')


@contextmanager
def project_lock(lock):
    lock.acquire()
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        lock.release()


def save_local(f, dest, pid, key, chunk=1024 * 1024):
    """Write an uploaded file in chunks while reporting progress and cancellation."""
    dest = Path(dest)
    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stream = getattr(f, 'stream', None)
    if stream is None and hasattr(f, 'read'):
        stream = f
    if stream is None:
        raise BadRequest('The upload stream could not be read.')
    written = 0
    with open(dest, 'wb') as out:
        while True:
            check(pid, key)
            data = stream.read(chunk)
            if not data:
                break
            out.write(data)
            written += len(data)
            update(pid, key, 'saving', saved_bytes=written)
    return written

==================== END FILE: import_status.py ====================


==================== FILE: local_diarization.py ====================
"""Subprocess entry point for an explicitly installed, local pyannote pipeline."""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def main(model_dir, wav, destination):
    import soundfile as sf
    import torch
    from pyannote.audio import Pipeline

    torch.set_num_threads(2)
    waveform, sample_rate = sf.read(wav, dtype='float32', always_2d=True)
    # Passing samples avoids torchcodec's system-FFmpeg dependency on Windows.
    signal = torch.from_numpy(waveform.T.copy())
    pipeline = Pipeline.from_pretrained(str(Path(model_dir).resolve()))
    if pipeline is None:
        raise RuntimeError('Could not load the local pyannote pipeline.')
    pipeline.to(torch.device('cpu'))
    output = pipeline({'waveform': signal, 'sample_rate': sample_rate})
    annotation = getattr(output, 'exclusive_speaker_diarization', None)
    if annotation is None:
        annotation = getattr(output, 'speaker_diarization', output)
    turns = [{'start_s': round(turn.start, 3), 'end_s': round(turn.end, 3), 'speaker': str(speaker)}
             for turn, _, speaker in annotation.itertracks(yield_label=True)]
    Path(destination).write_text(json.dumps({'turns': turns,
        'note': 'Anonymous per-file speaker labels; they do not identify people.'}), encoding='utf-8')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main(*sys.argv[1:])

==================== END FILE: local_diarization.py ====================


==================== FILE: local_understanding.py ====================
"""Optional local-only media understanding. Results are observations, not approvals.

Only reads project media and local model files; the sole HTTP endpoint is Ollama on
loopback. Jobs are serialized to bound CPU/RAM use and never modify the storyboard.
"""
import base64
import csv
import gc
import importlib.util
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import threading
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
import uuid
import wave
from pathlib import Path

import imageio_ffmpeg

_WORKER = threading.Lock()
_GUARD = threading.Lock()
_RUNNING = set()


def _whisper_model_ready(model):
    # Faster-Whisper can call Tokenizer.from_pretrained even with local_files_only
    # when tokenizer.json is absent. Refuse before loading to prevent network access.
    if not model: return False
    root = Path(model)
    return root.is_dir() and all((root / name).is_file() for name in ('model.bin', 'config.json', 'tokenizer.json'))


def _whisper_model_path():
    if 'STORYROOM_WHISPER_MODEL_DIR' in os.environ:
