        return os.environ['STORYROOM_WHISPER_MODEL_DIR'].strip()
    return str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / '.cache' / 'storyroom-models' / 'whisper-small')


def _yamnet_model_path():
    if 'STORYROOM_YAMNET_MODEL' in os.environ:
        return os.environ['STORYROOM_YAMNET_MODEL'].strip()
    return str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / '.cache' / 'storyroom-models' / 'yamnet.onnx')


def capabilities():
    model = _whisper_model_path()
    local_model = _whisper_model_ready(model)
    return {'ffmpeg': bool(imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()),
            'faster_whisper': bool(importlib.util.find_spec('faster_whisper')),
            'whisper_model_ready': local_model,
            'scenedetect': bool(importlib.util.find_spec('scenedetect')),
            'librosa': bool(importlib.util.find_spec('librosa')),
            'sound_classifier': bool(importlib.util.find_spec('onnxruntime') and _yamnet_model_path() and Path(_yamnet_model_path()).is_file()),
            'ollama_url': 'http://127.0.0.1:11434',
            'vision_model': os.environ.get('STORYROOM_OLLAMA_MODEL', 'qwen3-vl:2b'),
            'speaker_diarization': bool(importlib.util.find_spec('pyannote') and os.environ.get('STORYROOM_PYANNOTE_MODEL_DIR') and Path(os.environ['STORYROOM_PYANNOTE_MODEL_DIR']).is_dir()),
            'note': 'CPU int8 transcription requires a local Faster-Whisper model directory; visual analysis requires a locally running Ollama with the selected vision model. No models are downloaded by Storyroom.'}


def _write(path, data):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = path.with_name(path.name + '.' + uuid.uuid4().hex + '.tmp')
    try:
        tmp.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
        tmp.replace(path)
    finally:
        tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)


def _signature(path):
    st = path.stat()
    return {'size': st.st_size, 'mtime_ns': st.st_mtime_ns}


def result_path(root, target):
    return Path(root) / 'reviews' / 'understanding' / (target + '.json')


def read_result(root, target, path):
    p = result_path(root, target)
    if not p.exists(): return {'status': 'not_started'}
    try:
        result = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        if result.get('source_signature') != _signature(path):
            return {'status': 'stale', 'detail': 'Source changed; run the analysis again.'}
        if result.get('audio_timing_version') != 2:
            result['timing_warning'] = 'Audio stages were generated before the delayed-audio alignment fix. Reanalyse if this source has a delayed audio track.'
        with _GUARD: active = (str(root), target) in _RUNNING
        if result.get('status') in ('queued', 'running') and not active:
            result['status'] = 'interrupted'
        return result
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return {'status': 'error', 'detail': 'Analysis report could not be read.'}


def enqueue(root, target, path, kind, duration):
    root, path = Path(root), Path(path)
    key = (str(root), target)
    with _GUARD:
        if key in _RUNNING: return False
        _RUNNING.add(key)
    try:
        data = {'status': 'queued', 'source_signature': _signature(path), 'source': path.name,
                'kind': kind, 'duration_s': duration, 'stages': {}, 'started_at': time.time(), 'audio_timing_version': 2}
        _write(result_path(root, target), data)
        threading.Thread(target=_run, args=(root, target, path, data), daemon=True).start()
        return True
    except Exception:
        with _GUARD: _RUNNING.discard(key)
        raise


def enqueue_sound_events(root, target, path):
    """Reanalyse just sound events, preserving other completed observations."""
    root, path = Path(root), Path(path)
    key = (str(root), target)
    with _GUARD:
        if key in _RUNNING: raise ValueError('An analysis is already running for this source.')
        # Read directly under the guard: read_result also takes _GUARD.
        report = result_path(root, target)
        if not report.is_file(): raise ValueError('Run a full analysis first.')
        try: data = json.loads(report.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        except (OSError, ValueError): raise ValueError('Analysis report could not be read.')
        if data.get('status') not in ('complete', 'partial') or data.get('source_signature') != _signature(path):
            raise ValueError('A current completed or partial report is required.')
        model = _yamnet_model_path()
        if not model or not Path(model).is_file(): raise ValueError('No local YAMNet model is configured.')
        _RUNNING.add(key)
    try:
        data['status'] = 'queued'
        data['stages']['sound_events'] = {'status': 'queued'}
        _write(report, data)
        def run():
            try:
                with _WORKER:
                    data['status'] = 'running'; _write(report, data)
                    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='storyroom-sound-') as tmp:
                        wav = Path(tmp) / 'audio.wav'
                        def classify():
                            _decode_audio(path, wav)
                            return _sound_events(wav)
                        _stage(data, report, 'sound_events', classify)
                    data['status'] = 'complete' if all(x['status'] in ('complete', 'not_requested') for x in data['stages'].values()) else 'partial'
                    data['finished_at'] = time.time()
                    if _signature(path) != data['source_signature']:
                        data['status'] = 'stale'; data['detail'] = 'Source changed during analysis; run again.'
                    _write(report, data)
            except Exception as e:
                data['status'] = 'partial'; data['stages']['sound_events'] = {'status': 'unavailable', 'detail': str(e)[:600]}
                try: _write(report, data)
                except OSError: pass
            finally:
                with _GUARD: _RUNNING.discard(key)
        threading.Thread(target=run, daemon=True).start()
    except Exception:
        with _GUARD: _RUNNING.discard(key)
        raise


def enqueue_visual(root, target, path):
    """Reanalyse visual frames without retranscribing or replacing other stages."""
    root, path = Path(root), Path(path)
    key = (str(root), target)
    with _GUARD:
        if key in _RUNNING: raise ValueError('An analysis is already running for this source.')
        report = result_path(root, target)
        if not report.is_file(): raise ValueError('Run a full analysis first.')
        try: data = json.loads(report.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        except (OSError, ValueError): raise ValueError('Analysis report could not be read.')
        if data.get('status') not in ('complete', 'partial') or data.get('source_signature') != _signature(path):
            raise ValueError('A current completed or partial report is required.')
        cuts = (data.get('stages') or {}).get('scene_changes') or {}
        if cuts.get('status') != 'complete' or not cuts.get('result'):
            raise ValueError('A completed scene-change analysis is required.')
        previous = (data['stages'].get('visual') or {}).get('result')
        _RUNNING.add(key)
    try:
        data['status'] = 'queued'; data['stages']['visual'] = {'status': 'queued'}
        if previous: data['stages']['visual']['result'] = previous
        _write(report, data)
        def run():
            try:
                with _WORKER:
                    data['status'] = 'running'; _write(report, data)
                    def checkpoint(result):
                        data['stages']['visual'] = {'status': 'running', 'result': result}; _write(report, data)
                    _stage(data, report, 'visual', lambda: _vision(path, cuts['result'], checkpoint))
                    # _stage clears the pending value. Preserve old observations if
                    # the new attempt failed before producing its first frame.
                    if data['stages']['visual']['status'] == 'unavailable' and previous:
                        data['stages']['visual']['status'] = 'partial'
                        data['stages']['visual']['result'] = previous
                    data['status'] = 'complete' if all(x['status'] in ('complete', 'not_requested') for x in data['stages'].values()) else 'partial'
                    data['finished_at'] = time.time()
                    if _signature(path) != data['source_signature']:
                        data['status'] = 'stale'; data['detail'] = 'Source changed during analysis; run again.'
                    _write(report, data)
            except Exception as e:
                data['status'] = 'partial'; data['stages']['visual'] = {'status': 'unavailable', 'detail': str(e)[:600]}
                if previous:
                    data['stages']['visual'].update(status='partial', result=previous)
                try: _write(report, data)
                except OSError: pass
            finally:
                with _GUARD: _RUNNING.discard(key)
        threading.Thread(target=run, daemon=True).start()
    except Exception:
        with _GUARD: _RUNNING.discard(key)
        raise


def _stage(data, report, name, fn):
    data['stages'][name] = {'status': 'running'}; _write(report, data)
    try:
        data['stages'][name] = {'status': 'complete', 'result': fn()}
    except Exception as e:
        partial = data['stages'][name].get('result')
        data['stages'][name] = {'status': 'partial' if partial else 'unavailable', 'detail': str(e)[:600]}
        if partial: data['stages'][name]['result'] = partial
    _write(report, data)


def _decode_audio(source, wav):
    # Preserve audio's start relative to the media timeline. Otherwise FFmpeg's
    # WAV muxer starts a delayed stream at zero and every observation shifts early.
    # start_at_zero anchors to the container start, not the audio stream start;
    # aresample inserts silence or trims negative samples against that origin.
    cmd = [imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe(), '-nostdin', '-v', 'error', '-y',
           '-copyts', '-start_at_zero', '-i', str(source), '-map', '0:a:0',
           '-af', 'aresample=async=1:first_pts=0', '-ac', '1', '-ar', '16000',
           '-c:a', 'pcm_s16le', str(wav)]
    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, timeout=7200)
    if proc.returncode: raise RuntimeError('No decodable audio stream: ' + proc.stderr.decode(errors='replace')[-300:])


def _transcribe(wav, on_progress=None):
    model_path = _whisper_model_path()
    if not _whisper_model_ready(model_path):
        raise RuntimeError('Set STORYROOM_WHISPER_MODEL_DIR to a complete local Faster-Whisper model directory containing model.bin, config.json and tokenizer.json. No download is attempted.')
    from faster_whisper import WhisperModel
    model = WhisperModel(model_path, device='cpu', compute_type='int8', cpu_threads=max(1, min(2, os.cpu_count() or 1)), local_files_only=True)
    try:
        segments, info = model.transcribe(str(wav), beam_size=3, vad_filter=True, word_timestamps=True)
        items = []
        def snapshot():
            return {'language': info.language, 'language_probability': info.language_probability, 'segments': list(items)}
        checkpoint_at = time.monotonic()
        for s in segments:
            items.append({'start_s': round(s.start, 3), 'end_s': round(s.end, 3), 'text': s.text.strip(),
                          'words': [{'start_s': round(w.start, 3) if w.start is not None else None, 'end_s': round(w.end, 3) if w.end is not None else None, 'text': w.word} for w in (s.words or [])]})
            if on_progress and (len(items) == 1 or time.monotonic() - checkpoint_at >= 5):
                on_progress(snapshot()); checkpoint_at = time.monotonic()
        return snapshot()
    finally:
        del model; gc.collect()


def _diarize(wav):
    """Load an optional local-only community-1 pipeline in an isolated subprocess."""
    model = os.environ.get('STORYROOM_PYANNOTE_MODEL_DIR', '').strip()
    if not model or not Path(model).is_dir():
        raise RuntimeError('Set STORYROOM_PYANNOTE_MODEL_DIR to a downloaded local pyannote community-1 pipeline.')
    env = dict(os.environ, HF_HUB_OFFLINE='1', TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE='1',
               PYANNOTE_METRICS_ENABLED='0', HF_HUB_DISABLE_TELEMETRY='1')
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='storyroom-diarization-') as tmp:
        result = Path(tmp) / 'speakers.json'
        proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(Path(__file__).with_name('local_diarization.py')),
                               str(Path(model).resolve()), str(wav), str(result)],
                              env=env, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, timeout=7200)
        if proc.returncode:
            raise RuntimeError('Local pyannote failed: ' + proc.stderr.decode(errors='replace')[-500:])
        return json.loads(result.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))


def _align_speakers(transcript, speakers):
    """Annotate words/segments by strongest overlap; labels are anonymous per file."""
    turns = speakers.get('turns', [])
    def label(item):
        start, end = item.get('start_s'), item.get('end_s')
        if start is None or end is None: return None
        overlap = [(max(0, min(end, t['end_s']) - max(start, t['start_s'])), t['speaker']) for t in turns]
        best = max(overlap, default=(0, None))
        return best[1] if best[0] > 0 else None
    for segment in transcript.get('segments', []):
        segment['speaker'] = label(segment)
        for word in segment.get('words', []): word['speaker'] = label(word)


def _audio_features(wav):
    import numpy as np
    import librosa
    chunks = []; silence = []; quiet_start = None; quiet_end = None
    with wave.open(str(wav), 'rb') as f:
        sr = f.getframerate(); samples = sr * 10; position = 0
        while True:
            raw = f.readframes(samples)
            if not raw: break
            y = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype='<i2').astype(np.float32) / 32768
            if not len(y): break
            rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(y * y)))
            # Signal statistics only: they are not a classifier for music or sound events.
            centroid = float(np.mean(librosa.feature.spectral_centroid(y=y, sr=sr))) if len(y) >= 2048 else 0.0
            chunks.append({'start_s': round(position / sr, 3), 'end_s': round((position + len(y)) / sr, 3),
                           'rms': round(rms, 5), 'spectral_centroid_hz': round(centroid, 1), 'low_energy': rms < 0.01})
            # Absolute threshold, 100 ms windows; merge across chunk boundaries.
            frame = sr // 10
            for offset in range(0, len(y), frame):
                fragment = y[offset:offset + frame]
                low = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(fragment * fragment))) < 0.01
                start = (position + offset) / sr
                end = (position + offset + len(fragment)) / sr
                if low:
                    if quiet_start is None: quiet_start = start
                    quiet_end = end
                elif quiet_start is not None:
                    if quiet_end - quiet_start >= 0.5: silence.append({'start_s': round(quiet_start, 3), 'end_s': round(quiet_end, 3)})
                    quiet_start = quiet_end = None
            position += len(y)
    if quiet_start is not None and quiet_end - quiet_start >= 0.5:
        silence.append({'start_s': round(quiet_start, 3), 'end_s': round(quiet_end, 3)})
    return {'windows': chunks, 'low_energy_intervals': silence,
            'note': 'Approximate low-energy intervals use an absolute RMS threshold; they are not guaranteed silence or a music/sound-event classification.'}


def _sound_events(wav):
    """CPU YAMNet waveform ONNX: independent 3-second source-time windows."""
    import numpy as np
    import onnxruntime as ort
    model = _yamnet_model_path()
    if not model or not Path(model).is_file():
        raise RuntimeError('Set STORYROOM_YAMNET_MODEL to a compatible local YAMNet waveform ONNX model.')
    with (Path(__file__).parent / 'data' / 'yamnet_class_map.csv').open(encoding='utf-8', newline='') as f:
        labels = [row['display_name'] for row in csv.DictReader(f)]
    opts = ort.SessionOptions(); opts.intra_op_num_threads = 2; opts.inter_op_num_threads = 1
    session = ort.InferenceSession(model, sess_options=opts, providers=['CPUExecutionProvider'])
    inputs, outputs = session.get_inputs(), session.get_outputs()
    shape = inputs[0].shape if len(inputs) == 1 else []
    names = [o.name for o in outputs]
    fixed = shape == [1, 48000] and 'scores' in names
    dynamic = len(shape) == 1 and not isinstance(shape[0], int) and 'output_0' in names
    if len(inputs) != 1 or inputs[0].type != 'tensor(float)' or not (fixed or dynamic):
        raise RuntimeError('Incompatible YAMNet model: expected a supported float32 waveform input and score output.')
    output_name = 'scores' if fixed else 'output_0'
    windows = []
    with wave.open(str(wav), 'rb') as f:
        if f.getframerate()!=16000 or f.getnchannels()!=1 or f.getsampwidth()!=2:
            raise ValueError('Expected mono 16 kHz PCM audio.')
        position = 0
        while True:
            raw=f.readframes(48000)
            if not raw: break
            y=np.frombuffer(raw, dtype='<i2').astype(np.float32)/32768.0
            length=len(y)
            top=[]
            # A very short zero-padded tail can be confidently misclassified as silence.
            if length >= 16000:
                if fixed: y=np.pad(y, (0, 48000-length))[None, :]
                scores=np.asarray(session.run([output_name], {inputs[0].name: y})[0])
                if scores.ndim != 2 or scores.shape[1] != len(labels) or not np.isfinite(scores).all():
                    raise RuntimeError('YAMNet returned incompatible or invalid class scores.')
                # Score frames cover approximately 0.96 s, starting every 0.48 s.
                # Exclude frames extending into padding rather than mislabelling it.
                valid=max(1, min(scores.shape[0], 1 + int((length/16000-0.96)/0.48)))
                mean=scores[:valid].mean(axis=0)
                top=[{'label': labels[int(i)], 'score': round(float(mean[i]), 4)}
                     for i in np.argsort(mean)[::-1][:5] if mean[i] >= 0.1]
            windows.append({'start_s': round(position/16000,3), 'end_s': round((position+length)/16000,3), 'predictions': top, 'too_short': length < 16000})
            position += length
    return {'windows': windows, 'note': 'YAMNet estimates for independent 3-second windows, not verified sound events. Low-scoring classes are omitted; speech and music can overlap. Tails under one second are not classified.'}


def _scenes(path, duration):
    from scenedetect import detect, ContentDetector
    spans = detect(str(path), ContentDetector(), show_progress=False)
    if not spans: return [{'start_s': 0.0, 'end_s': round(duration, 3)}]
    return [{'start_s': round(a.get_seconds(), 3), 'end_s': round(b.get_seconds(), 3)} for a, b in spans]


def _sample_frames(scenes, max_frames, interval_s=10):
    """Spread the budget over the entire source, including within long scenes."""
    candidates = []
    for scene in scenes:
        length = max(0, scene['end_s'] - scene['start_s'])
        count = max(1, int((length + interval_s - 1e-9) // interval_s))
        # Bound planning memory even for multi-hour files.
        count = min(count, max_frames * 10)
        for i in range(count):
            candidates.append((scene['start_s'] + length * (i + .5) / count, scene))
    if len(candidates) > max_frames:
        candidates = [candidates[int((i + .5) * len(candidates) / max_frames)] for i in range(max_frames)]
    return candidates


def _require_offline_ollama(opener, model):
    """Fail closed before sending image data to a loopback Ollama server.

    Loopback alone is insufficient: Ollama can forward cloud models remotely.
    Require the server's cloud-disabled status and reject remote model metadata.
    """
    if not model or model.lower().endswith((':cloud', '-cloud')):
        raise RuntimeError('Cloud Ollama models are not permitted for local analysis.')
    try:
        with opener.open('http://127.0.0.1:11434/api/status', timeout=10) as response:
            status = json.load(response)
        if not isinstance(status, dict) or not isinstance(status.get('cloud'), dict) or status['cloud'].get('disabled') is not True:
            raise RuntimeError('Ollama cloud must be disabled before analysing frames. Start Ollama with OLLAMA_NO_CLOUD=1.')
        request = urllib.request.Request('http://127.0.0.1:11434/api/show',
            data=json.dumps({'model': model}).encode(), headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, method='POST')
        with opener.open(request, timeout=10) as response: details = json.load(response)
    except (OSError, urllib.error.URLError, ValueError) as e:
        raise RuntimeError('Cannot verify Ollama local-only status and model. Use a recent Ollama with OLLAMA_NO_CLOUD=1: ' + str(e)) from e
    if not isinstance(details, dict) or details.get('remote_host') or details.get('remote_model'):
        raise RuntimeError('Ollama model is remote or its local status could not be verified.')
    capabilities = details.get('capabilities')
    if capabilities is not None and (not isinstance(capabilities, list) or 'vision' not in capabilities):
        raise RuntimeError('The selected local Ollama model does not report vision support.')


def _vision(path, scenes, on_progress=None):
    # No arbitrary URL or redirects: image data stays on this computer.
    model = os.environ.get('STORYROOM_OLLAMA_MODEL', 'qwen3-vl:2b')
    max_frames = max(1, min(300, int(os.environ.get('STORYROOM_VISION_MAX_FRAMES', '60'))))
    sampled = _sample_frames(scenes, max_frames)
    observations = []
    attempted_chat = False
    def snapshot():
        return {'observations': list(observations), 'sampled_frames': len(observations),
                'planned_frames': len(sampled), 'total_scenes': len(scenes),
                'note': 'Sampled still frames do not describe every moment or establish identities.'}
    class NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):
        def redirect_request(self, *args, **kwargs): return None
    opener = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}), NoRedirect())
    try:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='storyroom-frames-') as tmp:
            for at, scene in sampled:
                _require_offline_ollama(opener, model)
                image = Path(tmp) / 'frame.jpg'
                proc = subprocess.run([imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe(), '-nostdin', '-v', 'error', '-y', '-ss', str(at), '-i', str(path), '-frames:v', '1', '-vf', 'scale=640:640:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease:force_divisible_by=2', str(image)], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, timeout=120)
                if proc.returncode or not image.is_file(): raise RuntimeError('Could not extract frame at %.3fs' % at)
                payload = {'model': model, 'stream': False, 'keep_alive': '2m', 'options': {'num_ctx': 2048, 'num_thread': 2, 'num_predict': 256}, 'messages': [{'role': 'user',
                    'content': 'Describe only what is visibly supported: activity, objects, people, setting, and readable text. Say when uncertain; do not infer real identities.',
                    'images': [base64.b64encode(image.read_bytes()).decode('ascii')]}]}
                req = urllib.request.Request('http://127.0.0.1:11434/api/chat', data=json.dumps(payload).encode(), headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, method='POST')
                try:
                    attempted_chat = True
                    with opener.open(req, timeout=300) as response: result = json.load(response)
                except (OSError, urllib.error.URLError) as e:
                    raise RuntimeError('Local Ollama unavailable or model missing: ' + str(e)) from e
                text = (result.get('message') or {}).get('content', '').strip()
                if not text: raise RuntimeError('Ollama returned no visual description.')
                observations.append({'frame_time_s': round(at, 3), 'scene_start_s': scene['start_s'], 'scene_end_s': scene['end_s'], 'description': text})
                if on_progress: on_progress(snapshot())
        return snapshot()
    finally:
        if attempted_chat:
            # Keep the model warm between frames, then release it to conserve RAM.
            request = urllib.request.Request('http://127.0.0.1:11434/api/generate',
                data=json.dumps({'model': model, 'keep_alive': 0}).encode(), headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, method='POST')
            try:
                with opener.open(request, timeout=10): pass
            except (OSError, urllib.error.URLError): pass


def _run(root, target, source, data):
    key = (str(root), target); report = result_path(root, target)
    try:
        with _WORKER:
            data['status'] = 'running'; _write(report, data)
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='storyroom-audio-') as tmp:
                wav = Path(tmp) / 'audio.wav'
                try:
                    _decode_audio(source, wav)
                except Exception as e:
                    for stage in ('transcript', 'audio_features'):
                        data['stages'][stage] = {'status': 'unavailable', 'detail': str(e)[:600]}
                    _write(report, data)
                else:
                    def checkpoint_transcript(result):
                        data['stages']['transcript'] = {'status': 'running', 'result': result}
                        _write(report, data)
                    _stage(data, report, 'transcript', lambda: _transcribe(wav, checkpoint_transcript))
                    _stage(data, report, 'audio_features', lambda: _audio_features(wav))
                    if _yamnet_model_path() and Path(_yamnet_model_path()).is_file():
                        _stage(data, report, 'sound_events', lambda: _sound_events(wav))
                    else:
                        data['stages']['sound_events'] = {'status': 'not_requested', 'detail': 'Optional local sound-event model is not configured.'}
                    if os.environ.get('STORYROOM_PYANNOTE_MODEL_DIR'):
                        _stage(data, report, 'speakers', lambda: _diarize(wav))
                        if data['stages']['speakers']['status'] == 'complete' and data['stages']['transcript']['status'] == 'complete':
                            _align_speakers(data['stages']['transcript']['result'], data['stages']['speakers']['result'])
                    else:
                        data['stages']['speakers'] = {'status': 'not_requested', 'detail': 'Optional local pyannote speaker diarization is not configured.'}
            if data['kind'] == 'video':
                _stage(data, report, 'scene_changes', lambda: _scenes(source, data['duration_s']))
                cuts = data['stages']['scene_changes']
                if cuts['status'] == 'complete':
                    def checkpoint_visual(result):
                        data['stages']['visual'] = {'status': 'running', 'result': result}
                        _write(report, data)
                    _stage(data, report, 'visual', lambda: _vision(source, cuts['result'], checkpoint_visual))
                else:
                    data['stages']['visual'] = {'status':'unavailable','detail':'Scene detection failed; no visual frames analysed.'}
            if 'sound_events' not in data['stages']:
                data['stages']['sound_events'] = {'status': 'unavailable', 'detail': 'No decoded audio for sound classification.'}
            if 'speakers' not in data['stages']:
                data['stages']['speakers'] = {'status': 'unavailable', 'detail': 'No decoded audio for diarization.'}
            data['status'] = 'complete' if all(x['status'] in ('complete', 'not_requested') for x in data['stages'].values()) else 'partial'
            data['finished_at'] = time.time()
            if _signature(source) != data['source_signature']:
                data['status'] = 'stale'; data['detail'] = 'Source changed during analysis; run again.'
            _write(report, data)
    except Exception as e:
        data['status'] = 'error'; data['detail'] = str(e)[:600]
        try: _write(report, data)
        except OSError: pass
    finally:
        with _GUARD: _RUNNING.discard(key)


def scene_context(report, scene):
    """Project source-time observations onto a saved scene without asserting verification."""
    source_in = float(scene.get('in_s') or 0)
    timeline_in = scene['frame_in'] / 30
    duration = (scene['frame_out'] - scene['frame_in']) / 30
    source_out = source_in + duration
    result = {'status': report.get('status'), 'source_in_s': round(source_in, 3),
              'source_out_s': round(source_out, 3), 'timeline_in_s': round(timeline_in, 3),
              'transcript': [], 'sound_events': [], 'visual': [], 'speakers': [],
              'scene_changes': [], 'audio_features': [], 'low_energy_intervals': [], 'stage_status': {},
              'note': 'Source observations are estimates; timeline times are mapped from the saved in-point and not editorial verification.',
              'timing_warning': report.get('timing_warning')}
    if report.get('status') not in ('complete', 'partial'): return result
    stages = report.get('stages') or {}
    def interval(rows):
        found = []
        for item in rows:
            a, b = float(item['start_s']), float(item['end_s'])
            if a >= source_out or b <= source_in: continue
            row = dict(item)
            row['timeline_start_s'] = round(timeline_in + max(a, source_in) - source_in, 3)
            row['timeline_end_s'] = round(timeline_in + min(b, source_out) - source_in, 3)
            found.append(row)
        return found
    def data(name):
        stage = stages.get(name) or {}
        return stage.get('result', {}) if stage.get('status') in ('complete', 'partial') else {}
    result['stage_status'] = {name: stage.get('status', 'unknown') for name, stage in stages.items()}
    result['transcript'] = interval(data('transcript').get('segments', []))
    for segment in result['transcript']:
        # Keep original words unchanged; map only words with known overlapping times.
        segment['timeline_words'] = interval([word for word in segment.get('words', [])
                                              if word.get('start_s') is not None and word.get('end_s') is not None])
    result['sound_events'] = interval(data('sound_events').get('windows', []))
    result['speakers'] = interval(data('speakers').get('turns', []))
    result['scene_changes'] = interval(data('scene_changes') or [])
    result['audio_features'] = interval(data('audio_features').get('windows', []))
    result['low_energy_intervals'] = interval(data('audio_features').get('low_energy_intervals', []))
    for item in data('visual').get('observations', []):
        at = float(item['frame_time_s'])
        if source_in <= at < source_out:
            result['visual'].append(dict(item, timeline_time_s=round(timeline_in + at - source_in, 3)))
    return result

==================== END FILE: local_understanding.py ====================


==================== FILE: picture_flow.py ====================
"""Cut conformance and narration flow.

Two separate questions, never conflated:

1. Picture flow - does every picture beat sit inside ONE decoded shot of its
   source, and does it leave and return to a source without a visible jump cut?
2. Narration flow - does every picture change land where the narrator is not
   speaking, or at least where the SRT already breaks the line?

Frames are decoded to answer "is this interval playable and continuous". No
function here claims to know WHO or WHAT a frame shows; semantic identity stays
a human review question. Nothing is trimmed, stretched, looped or frozen: a beat
that cannot be made to fit inside one shot is reported, not faked.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import bisect, json, math, os, subprocess

FPS = 30
SHOT = 1 / FPS
# A detected shot change this close to a requested IN is treated as already aligned.
ALIGN_TOLERANCE = 0.12
# How far a declared moment may travel to stay inside one decoded shot.
CONFORM_WINDOW = 8.0
# How far a declared moment may travel backwards to continue a running take.
CONTINUITY_WINDOW = 2.0
# How far a cut may move to land in a breath while the narration keeps playing.
SNAP_WINDOW = 0.35
MIN_SNAP_SCENE = 1.2
# Prefer a free moment over one a later beat declared for itself.
COLLISION_PENALTY = 0.6


def _analysis(root):
    path = Path(root) / 'reviews/Full_source_analysis.json'
    if not path.is_file():
        return {}
    try:
        data = json.loads(path.read_text())
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return {}
    return {s.get('file'): s for s in data.get('sources', []) if s.get('complete_decode_success')}


def shot_index(root):
    """Decoded shot boundaries per original filename, plus black intervals."""
    index = {}
    for name, source in _analysis(root).items():
        duration = float(source.get('duration_s') or 0)
        cuts = sorted({float(x) for x in source.get('scene_change_times_s') or []})
        # Ignore detector noise at the very head/tail of the file.
        cuts = [c for c in cuts if 0.25 < c < duration - 0.25]
        index[name] = {
            'duration': duration,
            'shots': [(a, b) for a, b in zip([0.0] + cuts, cuts + [duration]) if b - a > 0.2],
            'black': [(float(x['start']), float(x['end'])) for x in source.get('black_intervals') or []],
            'decoded_cuts': len(cuts),
        }
    return index


def _shots_for(index, filename):
    record = index.get(filename)
    if record:
        return record['shots']
    return None


def _black_overlap(index, filename, start, end):
    for a, b in (index.get(filename) or {}).get('black', []):
        if start < b - 0.02 and end > a + 0.02:
            return True
    return False


def crossing_cuts(shots, start, end):
    """Detected shot changes strictly inside a requested interval."""
    return [c for _, c in zip(shots, [s for s, _ in shots[1:]]) if start + 0.02 < c < end - 0.02]


def best_interval(shots, declared, duration):
    """Nearest playable interval of `duration` seconds inside one decoded shot."""
    for a, b in shots:
        if b - a < duration + 0.02:
            continue
        candidate = min(max(declared, a), b - duration)
        yield abs(candidate - declared), candidate, (a, b)


def _cooldown_window(p):
    policy = p.get('repetition_policy') or {}
    return int(policy.get('cooldown_slots') or 0)


def _recent(placed, filename, window):
    """Intervals of this source placed inside the project's repetition window."""
    taken = []
    for entry in placed[-window:] if window else []:
        if entry['asset'] == filename:
            taken.append((entry['start_s'], entry['end_s']))
    return taken


def _taken(taken, start, end):
    return any(start < stop - .05 and end > inside + .05 for inside, stop in taken)


def _shot_holding(shots, start, end):
    for a, b in shots:
        if a - SHOT <= start and end <= b + SHOT:
            return (a, b)
    return None


def conform_picture(p, root, window=CONFORM_WINDOW, continuity=CONTINUITY_WINDOW, apply=True):
    """Bend requested source IN points onto continuous decoded shots.

    Preference order, only as far as needed:
      1. the declared moment already sits inside one decoded shot - untouched;
      2. the declared moment returns to the shot the previous beat was running
         from - joined to that take instead of jump-cutting;
      3. the nearest decoded shot that can hold the whole beat;
      4. nothing fits - reported, never trimmed, stretched, looped or frozen.
    """
    index = shot_index(root)
    assets = {a['id']: a for a in p.get('assets', [])}
    scenes = p.get('scenes') or []
    report = {'scope': 'decoded shot continuity; not identity or content verification',
              'sources_indexed': len(index), 'beats': [], 'summary': {}}
    previous = None
    placed, window_slots = [], _cooldown_window(p)
    window = window_slots or window
    declared_by_asset = {}
    for number, beat in enumerate(scenes):
        beat_asset = (assets.get(beat.get('asset_id')) or {})
        beat_name = beat_asset.get('original_name') or beat_asset.get('name') or ''
        declared_by_asset.setdefault(beat_name, []).append(
            (number, float(beat.get('in_s') or 0),
             float(beat.get('in_s') or 0) + (beat['frame_out'] - beat['frame_in']) / FPS))
    counts = {'already_inside_one_shot': 0, 'continuous_with_previous': 0, 'continued_running_take': 0,
              'slid_inside_a_shot': 0, 'slid_far_inside_a_shot': 0,
              'jump_cut_within_shot': 0, 'repeats_recent_footage': 0, 'no_single_shot_fits': 0, 'not_decoded': 0}
    for i, scene in enumerate(scenes, 1):
        asset = assets.get(scene.get('asset_id')) or {}
        filename = asset.get('original_name') or asset.get('name') or ''
        duration = (scene['frame_out'] - scene['frame_in']) / FPS
        declared = float(scene.get('in_s') or 0)
        record = {'scene': i, 'asset': filename, 'srt_blocks': scene.get('srt_blocks'),
                  'duration_s': round(duration, 3), 'declared_in_s': round(declared, 3),
                  'conformed_in_s': round(declared, 3), 'shift_s': 0.0,
                  'shot': None, 'status': '', 'note': '', 'review': False}
        shots = _shots_for(index, filename)
        if not shots:
            record['status'] = 'not_decoded'
            counts['not_decoded'] += 1
            report['beats'].append(record)
            previous = None
            continue
        taken = _recent(placed, filename, window)
        previous_take = previous if previous and previous['asset'] == filename else None
        carry_on = None
        if previous_take and previous_take['exit_s'] + duration <= previous_take['shot'][1] + SHOT:
            carry_on = previous_take['exit_s']
        if carry_on is not None and _taken(taken, carry_on, carry_on + duration):
            carry_on = None
        # 1. the declared moment is already playable and whole inside one shot
        holding = None
        if not _black_overlap(index, filename, declared, declared + duration):
            holding = _shot_holding(shots, declared, declared + duration)
        if holding and _taken(taken, declared, declared + duration):
            # An earlier beat now runs through this exact moment; relocate instead of repeating it.
            holding = None
            declared_repeat = True
        else:
            declared_repeat = False
        if holding:
            shot = holding
            same_take = bool(previous_take) and previous_take['shot'] == shot
            if same_take and abs(previous_take['exit_s'] - declared) <= SHOT + .001:
                start = declared
                record['status'] = 'continuous_with_previous'
                record['note'] = 'Runs on from the previous beat with no jump.'
            elif same_take and carry_on is not None:
                start = carry_on
                record['status'] = 'continued_running_take'
                record['note'] = ('Stayed in the same take as the previous beat and continued it from %.2fs instead of '
                                  'jumping inside the shot. The declared moment was %.2fs earlier in the take.'
                                  % (start, max(0.0, start - declared)))
            elif same_take:
                start = declared
                record['status'] = 'jump_cut_within_shot'
                record['note'] = 'Re-enters the same shot at a moment the previous beat already ran past; treat as a deliberate jump cut or choose another take.'
                record['review'] = True
            else:
                start = declared
                record['status'] = 'already_inside_one_shot'
        elif carry_on is not None and not _taken(taken, carry_on, carry_on + duration):  # 2. keep the running take alive
            start, shot, record['status'] = carry_on, previous_take['shot'], 'continued_running_take'
            record['note'] = 'Continued the previous beat\'s take at %0.2fs because the declared moment crosses a shot change.' % start
        else:  # 3. the nearest decoded shot that can hold the beat whole
            def steals_another_beat(start_at, stop_at):
                return any(n != i - 1 and start_at < other_end - .05 and stop_at > other_start + .05
                           for n, other_start, other_end in declared_by_asset.get(filename, []))
            options = []
            for distance, start_at, shot_range in best_interval(shots, declared, duration):
                if _taken(taken, start_at, start_at + duration):
                    continue
                collision = steals_another_beat(start_at, start_at + duration)
                options.append((distance + (COLLISION_PENALTY if collision else 0.0), start_at, shot_range, collision))
            reused = False
            if not options:  # every usable interval already ran recently; report the nearest one
                options = list(best_interval(shots, declared, duration))
                reused = bool(options)
            if not options:
                record['status'] = 'no_single_shot_fits'
                record['note'] = ('No decoded shot of %s is long enough for this %.2fs beat. '
                                  'Shorten the beat in the SRT block map or use another take; nothing was trimmed or frozen.'
                                  % (filename, duration))
                record['review'] = True
                counts['no_single_shot_fits'] += 1
                report['beats'].append(record)
                previous = None
                continue
            score, start, shot = min(options, key=lambda o: (o[0], o[1]))[:3]
            collision = len(options[0]) > 3 and min(options, key=lambda o: (o[0], o[1]))[3]
            distance = score - (COLLISION_PENALTY if collision else 0.0)
            if reused:
                record['status'] = 'repeats_recent_footage'
                record['note'] = ('No unused moment of %s can hold this %.2fs beat. The nearest usable interval was already '
                                  'used within the last %d slots; treat it as a deliberate callback, shorten the beat or add another take.'
                                  % (filename, duration, window_slots or window))
                record['review'] = True
            elif distance > window:
                record['status'] = 'slid_far_inside_a_shot'
                record['note'] = ('Moved %.2fs from the declared moment: every playable moment closer to it is already used '
                                  'by another beat or crosses a shot change. The nearest free shot is %0.2f-%0.2f. Check this beat still shows the intended moment.'
                                  % (distance, shot[0], shot[1]))
                record['review'] = True
            else:
                record['status'] = 'slid_inside_a_shot'
                record['note'] = ('Moved %.2fs so the beat sits inside one decoded shot (%0.2f-%0.2f) instead of crossing a shot change.%s'
                                  % (distance, shot[0], shot[1],
                                     ' The nearer moment was already used by another beat.' if collision else ''))
        counts[record['status']] += 1
        if record['status'] in ('repeats_recent_footage',):
            placed.append({'asset': filename, 'start_s': scene['in_s'], 'end_s': scene['in_s'] + duration,
                           'repeat': True})
        scene['in_s'] = round(max(0.0, start), 3)
        scene['picture_flow'] = {'status': record['status'], 'declared_in_s': record['declared_in_s'],
                                 'conformed_in_s': scene['in_s'], 'shift_s': round(scene['in_s'] - declared, 3),
                                 'shot': [round(shot[0], 3), round(shot[1], 3)], 'note': record['note'],
                                 'review': record['review']}
        record['conformed_in_s'] = scene['in_s']
        record['shift_s'] = round(scene['in_s'] - declared, 3)
        record['shot'] = [round(shot[0], 3), round(shot[1], 3)]
        report['beats'].append(record)
        placed.append({'asset': filename, 'start_s': scene['in_s'], 'end_s': scene['in_s'] + duration})
        previous = {'asset': filename, 'shot': shot, 'exit_s': scene['in_s'] + duration}
    # Suggest where a beat that could not be placed cleanly could go instead.
    used_by_asset = {}
    for entry in placed:
        used_by_asset.setdefault(entry['asset'], []).append((entry['start_s'], entry['end_s']))
    for record in report['beats']:
        if not record['review']:
            continue
        duration = record['duration_s']
        suggestions = []
        for name in sorted(index):
            if name == record['asset']:
                continue
            shots = _shots_for(index, name) or []
            taken = _recent([{'asset': k, 'start_s': a, 'end_s': b} for k, v in used_by_asset.items() for a, b in v],
                            name, window)
            found = None
            for a, b in shots:
                if b - a < duration + 0.02:
                    continue
                for start_at in (a, b - duration):
                    if not _taken(taken, start_at, start_at + duration):
                        found = (round(start_at, 2), round(a, 2), round(b, 2))
                        break
                if found:
                    break
            if found:
                suggestions.append({'asset': name, 'start_s': found[0], 'shot': [found[1], found[2]]})
            if len(suggestions) >= 3:
                break
        record['suggestions'] = suggestions
    report['summary'] = {'beats': len(scenes), 'counts': counts,
                         'untouched': counts['already_inside_one_shot'] + counts['continuous_with_previous'],
                         'moved': sum(counts[k] for k in ('continued_running_take', 'slid_inside_a_shot', 'slid_far_inside_a_shot')),
                         'needs_review': sum(1 for b in report['beats'] if b['review'])}
    return report


def narration_pauses(root, min_pause=0.15, noise='-32dB', refresh=False):
    """Decoded silences in the uploaded narration, cached beside the project."""
    root = Path(root)
    source = next((f for f in sorted((root / 'inputs').glob('voiceover.*'))), None)
    if not source:
        return {'source': '', 'pauses': [], 'decoded': False, 'reason': 'No narration audio is uploaded.'}
    key = {'size': source.stat().st_size, 'mtime': source.stat().st_mtime_ns,
           'min_pause': min_pause, 'noise': noise}
    cache = root / 'reviews/Narration_pauses.json'
    if cache.is_file() and not refresh:
        try:
            old = json.loads(cache.read_text())
            if old.get('key') == key:
                return old
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            pass
    try:
        import imageio_ffmpeg
        ffmpeg = imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()
        result = subprocess.run([ffmpeg, '-hide_banner', '-nostats', '-i', str(source),
                                 '-af', 'silencedetect=noise=%s:d=%s' % (noise, min_pause),
                                 '-f', 'null', '-'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900)
        text = result.stderr
    except Exception as error:  # ffmpeg missing or the audio will not decode
        return {'source': source.name, 'pauses': [], 'decoded': False, 'reason': str(error)[:300]}
    pauses, start = [], None
    for line in text.splitlines():
        if 'silence_start:' in line:
            try:
                start = float(line.split('silence_start:')[1].split()[0])
            except (IndexError, ValueError):
                start = None
        elif 'silence_end:' in line and start is not None:
            try:
                end = float(line.split('silence_end:')[1].split()[0])
            except (IndexError, ValueError):
                end = None
            if end is not None and end - start >= min_pause:
                pauses.append((round(start, 3), round(end, 3)))
            start = None
    record = {'key': key, 'source': source.name, 'min_pause': min_pause, 'noise': noise,
              'pauses': pauses, 'decoded': True,
              'note': 'Silences are measured from the uploaded narration, not assumed from SRT punctuation.'}
    try:
        cache.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        cache.write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
    except OSError:
        pass
    return record


def _blocks(p):
    return (p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks') or []


def classify_cut(seconds, pauses, blocks):
    """Where a picture change lands relative to the spoken narration."""
    for start, end in pauses:
        if start + 0.02 <= seconds <= end - 0.02:
            return 'in_a_breath', min(seconds - start, end - seconds)
    for block in blocks:
        if abs(seconds - block['start_ms'] / 1000) <= 0.02:
            return 'on_a_block_boundary', 0.0
    for block in blocks:
        if block['start_ms'] / 1000 < seconds < block['end_ms'] / 1000:
            return 'over_speech', 0.0
    return 'between_blocks', 0.0


def _nearest_pause(seconds, pauses, window):
    best = None
    for start, end in pauses:
        if end - start < 0.1:
            continue
        target = min(max(seconds, start + 0.06), end - 0.06)
        distance = abs(target - seconds)
        if distance <= window and (best is None or distance < best[0]):
            best = (distance, target, (start, end))
    return best


def snap_cuts(p, root, window=SNAP_WINDOW, min_scene=MIN_SNAP_SCENE, apply=True):
    """Move picture changes into the narrator's breaths without moving the narration.

    Scene lengths change by at most `window`; total duration, SRT wording and the
    narration audio are untouched. A block that ends up straddling a cut is owned
    by exactly one scene, so no line is spoken twice or dropped.
    """
    scenes = p.get('scenes') or []
    blocks = _blocks(p)
    pauses = narration_pauses(root)
    report = {'scope': 'cut placement against decoded narration silences',
              'decoded': bool(pauses.get('decoded')), 'source': pauses.get('source', ''),
              'min_pause': pauses.get('min_pause'), 'pauses': len(pauses.get('pauses') or []),
              'before': {}, 'after': {}, 'cuts': []}
    if not scenes or not blocks:
        report['reason'] = 'A storyboard and an SRT are both required.'
        return report
    pauses = pauses.get('pauses') or []
    if not pauses:
        report['reason'] = pauses.get('reason') if isinstance(pauses, dict) else 'No narration silences were decoded.'
    fps = FPS
    boundaries = [s['frame_in'] for s in scenes[1:]]
    report['before'] = _cut_counts([b / fps for b in boundaries], pauses, blocks)
    moved = 0
    for index, frame in enumerate(boundaries):
        seconds = frame / fps
        quality, _ = classify_cut(seconds, pauses, blocks)
        entry = {'cut': index + 1, 'before_s': round(seconds, 3), 'after_s': round(seconds, 3),
                 'quality_before': quality, 'quality_after': quality, 'moved': False, 'block': None}
        if quality != 'in_a_breath' and pauses:
            candidate = _nearest_pause(seconds, pauses, window)
            if candidate:
                _, target, pause = candidate
                new_frame = round(target * fps)
                left = new_frame - scenes[index]['frame_in']
                right = scenes[index + 1]['frame_out'] - new_frame
                if left >= min_scene * fps and right >= min_scene * fps:
                    scenes[index]['frame_out'] = new_frame
                    scenes[index + 1]['frame_in'] = new_frame
                    seconds = new_frame / fps
                    moved += 1
                    entry.update(after_s=round(seconds, 3), moved=True,
                                 quality_after=classify_cut(seconds, pauses, blocks)[0],
                                 pause=[round(pause[0], 3), round(pause[1], 3)])
        scene = scenes[index]
        scene['cut_sync'] = {'cut_at_s': round(seconds, 3), 'quality': entry['quality_after'],
                             'moved_from_s': entry['before_s'] if entry['moved'] else None}
        report['cuts'].append(entry)
    # Check that a straddling line still belongs to exactly one scene, WITHOUT rewriting
    # the storyboard. A cut may move by up to `window`, so a scene's own SRT reference can
    # legitimately no longer cover every block that overlaps it; rewriting the reference
    # here silently replaced the plan's own record of which line a beat carries, which is
    # how a beat ended up labelled with the next block's number. Report it instead.
    conflicts = []
    for index, scene in enumerate(scenes, 1):
        start, end = scene['frame_in'] / fps, scene['frame_out'] / fps
        touching = [b['number'] for b in blocks if b['start_ms'] / 1000 < end - 1e-6 and b['end_ms'] / 1000 > start + 1e-6]
        declared = set(scene.get('srt_blocks') or [])
        extra = [n for n in touching if n not in declared]
        if extra:
            conflicts.append({'scene': index, 'declared': sorted(declared), 'also_touches': extra})
    report['block_conflicts'] = conflicts
    if conflicts:
        report.setdefault('notes', []).append(
            'A cut moved into a breath, so %d scene(s) now overlap a neighbouring SRT block. '
            'The storyboard keeps the block references the plan gave it; nothing was re-labelled.' % len(conflicts))
    report['after'] = _cut_counts([s['frame_in'] / fps for s in scenes[1:]], pauses, blocks)
    report['summary'] = {'cuts': len(boundaries), 'moved': moved,
                         'in_a_breath_before': report['before'].get('in_a_breath', 0),
                         'in_a_breath_after': report['after'].get('in_a_breath', 0),
                         'over_speech_before': report['before'].get('over_speech', 0),
                         'over_speech_after': report['after'].get('over_speech', 0)}
    return report


def _cut_counts(cut_times, pauses, blocks):
    counts = {}
    for seconds in cut_times:
        quality, _ = classify_cut(seconds, pauses, blocks)
        counts[quality] = counts.get(quality, 0) + 1
    return counts


def apply_flow(p, root, snap=True):
    """Run the full pass for one project and return a JSON-ready report."""
    snap_report = snap_cuts(p, root) if snap else {'summary': {}, 'cuts': [], 'reason': 'Snapping not requested.'}
    conform = conform_picture(p, root)
    return {'project_id': p.get('id'), 'project_title': p.get('title'),
            'picture': conform, 'narration': snap_report,
            'summary': {'beats': len(p.get('scenes') or []),
                        'shot_conform': conform.get('summary', {}),
                        'cut_flow': snap_report.get('summary', {})},
            'note': ('Requested source moments were bent onto continuous decoded shots and picture changes were '
                     'moved into decoded narration pauses. Narration, SRT wording and total duration are unchanged. '
                     'Neither step establishes who or what a frame shows.')}


def outstanding_notes(report):
    """Plain-language notes for the pre-render check. Never an export blocker."""
    picture = (report or {}).get('picture') or {}
    narration = (report or {}).get('narration') or {}
    notes = []
    psum, nsum = picture.get('summary') or {}, narration.get('summary') or {}
    if psum:
        notes.append('Picture flow: %(untouched)s of %(beats)s beats already sat inside one decoded shot; '
                     '%(moved)s were conformed to the nearest playable moment inside a single shot.' % psum)
        review = [b for b in picture.get('beats', []) if b.get('review')]
        if review:
            scenes = ', '.join(str(b['scene']) for b in review[:10]) + ('…' if len(review) > 10 else '')
            notes.append('Picture flow: %d beats need a human look (scene %s) — the declared moment moved far '
                         'or re-entered a shot already used. Timing was not trimmed, looped or frozen.' % (len(review), scenes))
    if nsum:
        notes.append('Narration flow: %(in_a_breath_before)s of %(cuts)s cuts already landed in a decoded narration pause; '
                     'after snapping %(in_a_breath_after)s do. Scene lengths moved by at most 0.35s; the narration audio, '
                     'SRT wording and total duration are unchanged.' % nsum)
    elif narration.get('reason'):
        notes.append('Narration flow: ' + str(narration['reason']))
    conflicts = narration.get('block_conflicts') or []
    if conflicts:
        scenes = ', '.join(str(c['scene']) for c in conflicts[:10]) + ('…' if len(conflicts) > 10 else '')
        notes.append('Narration flow: %d cut(s) moved into a breath, so scene %s now overlap a neighbouring SRT '
                     'block. The beat keeps the block reference the plan gave it; nothing was re-labelled.' % (len(conflicts), scenes))
    return notes


def csv_rows(report):
    """One row per picture beat for the cut-flow audit."""
    picture = {b['scene']: b for b in (report.get('picture') or {}).get('beats', [])}
    cuts = {c['cut']: c for c in (report.get('narration') or {}).get('cuts', [])}
    rows = [['scene', 'asset', 'srt_blocks', 'duration_s', 'declared_in_s', 'conformed_in_s', 'shift_s',
             'shot_start_s', 'shot_end_s', 'picture_status', 'cut_to_next_after_s', 'cut_quality', 'note']]
    for number in sorted(picture):
        beat, cut = picture[number], cuts.get(number, {})
        shot = beat.get('shot') or ['', '']
        rows.append([number, beat.get('asset', ''), '/'.join(map(str, beat.get('srt_blocks') or [])),
                     beat.get('duration_s', ''), beat.get('declared_in_s', ''), beat.get('conformed_in_s', ''),
                     beat.get('shift_s', ''), shot[0], shot[1], beat.get('status', ''),
                     cut.get('after_s', ''), cut.get('quality_after', ''), beat.get('note', '')])
    return rows

==================== END FILE: picture_flow.py ====================


==================== FILE: plain_script.py ====================
"""Strict reader for one-shot-per-cue, labelled plain-text production scripts."""
import json
import re
from edl import parse, timecode_ms
from section_script import norm

HEAD = re.compile(r'^Shot:\s*(Srt\d+)\s*$', re.M | re.I)
FIELDS = {'Narration timeline', 'Exact narration', 'Full source', 'Source IN', 'Source OUT', 'Treatment', 'Visual direction', 'Continuity/caption'}

def is_plain_script(text):
    return bool(re.search(r'^Shot:\s*Srt\d+', text, re.M | re.I))

def parse_plain_script(text, blocks):
    if not blocks: raise ValueError('Import the original SRT before reading the script.')
    # The authored summary and source audit are preserved in the document and remain
    # unverified review instructions. Only the explicitly headed shot section is parsed.
    if re.search(r'^##\s+Storyboard Production Script\s*$',text,re.M|re.I):
        sections=list(re.finditer(r'^##\s+Storyboard Production Script\s*$',text,re.M|re.I))
        if len(sections)!=1:raise ValueError('The script has multiple storyboard sections.')
        section=sections[0];end=re.search(r'^##\s+',text[section.end():],re.M)
        body=text[section.end():section.end()+end.start()] if end else text[section.end():]
        body=re.sub(r'\n\s*---\s*$', '', body).strip()
        if HEAD.search(text[:section.start()]) or (end and HEAD.search(text[section.end()+end.start():])):
            raise ValueError('Shot instructions occur outside the storyboard section.')
        text=body
    heads=list(HEAD.finditer(text))
    if not heads or text[:heads[0].start()].strip(): raise ValueError('Unsupported text before the first shot.')
    if [int(re.search(r'\d+',h[1])[0]) for h in heads] != [int(b['number']) for b in blocks]:
        raise ValueError('The script must have exactly one shot for each SRT cue, in order.')
    rows=[]
    for i,h in enumerate(heads):
        sid=h[1]; cue=blocks[i]; content=text[h.end():heads[i+1].start() if i+1<len(heads) else len(text)]
        fields={}
        for line in content.splitlines():
            if not line.strip(): continue
            k,sep,v=line.partition(':')
            if not sep or k not in FIELDS or k in fields: raise ValueError(sid+': duplicate or unsupported instruction: '+line[:120])
            fields[k]=v.strip()
        if set(fields)!=FIELDS or any(not v for v in fields.values()):raise ValueError(sid+': missing required labelled instruction.')
        clock=lambda v:timecode_ms(v.strip(),sid)
        parts=re.split(r'\s*(?:-->|→|->)\s*',fields['Narration timeline'])
        if len(parts)!=2:raise ValueError(sid+': narration timeline must have IN and OUT.')
        tin,tout=map(clock,parts)
        if (tin,tout)!=(cue['start_ms'],cue['end_ms']):raise ValueError(sid+': narration timeline does not match the SRT cue.')
        source=fields['Full source'];proposed=bool(re.match(r'^PROPOSED:\s*',source,re.I));source=re.sub(r'^PROPOSED:\s*','',source,flags=re.I)
        if not re.fullmatch(r'[^/\\\r\n]+\.(?:mp4|mov|jpg|jpeg|png|webp)',source,re.I):raise ValueError(sid+': an exact full video or photograph filename is required.')
        treatment=fields['Treatment'];photo=bool(re.fullmatch(r'(?:PHOTO|PHOTOGRAPH|STILL PHOTO|STILL HOLD)',treatment,re.I))
        if photo:
            if not re.search(r'\.(?:jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$',source,re.I):raise ValueError(sid+': a photo treatment requires an uploaded image filename, not a video frame.')
            if any(v.strip().upper() not in ('N/A','NA','NONE','—','-') for v in (fields['Source IN'],fields['Source OUT'])):raise ValueError(sid+': photos have no source IN/OUT; use N/A for both.')
            sin=0;sout=tout-tin;typ='STILL';requested='STILL HOLD'
        else:
            if not re.search(r'\.(?:mp4|mov)$',source,re.I):raise ValueError(sid+': video and native static treatments require a video filename.')
            sin=clock(fields['Source IN']);sout=clock(fields['Source OUT'])
            if sout<=sin or sout-sin!=tout-tin:raise ValueError(sid+': source range must match the cue duration at 1.0×.')
            if re.fullmatch(r'VIDEO\s*,\s*1\.0[×x]\s*,\s*loop\s+OFF',treatment,re.I):typ='VIDEO';requested='VIDEO'
            elif re.fullmatch(r'STILL\s*(?:\(native static interval\)|[—–-]\s*native static interval in source video)',treatment,re.I):typ='STILL';requested='STILL HOLD'
            else:raise ValueError(sid+': unsupported treatment; no playback changes were assumed.')
        mismatch=norm(fields['Exact narration'])!=norm(cue.get('text',''))
        warnings=['Visual content, identity, source version and rights are not verified.','Caption and continuity instructions require review before rendering.']
        if proposed:warnings.append('Source is marked PROPOSED in the script; verify before approval.')
        if mismatch:warnings.append('Script narration differs from the uploaded SRT; neither was changed.')
        if typ=='STILL':warnings.append(('Uploaded photograph held static' if photo else 'Native static interval played from the source')+' for review only; eligibility, still budget, identity and rights remain unverified.')
        rows.append(dict(beat_id=sid,shot_id=sid,clip_id='',srt_blocks=[cue['number']],timeline_in_ms=tin,timeline_out_ms=tout,source_file=source,source_in_ms=sin,source_out_ms=sout,visual_status=typ,requested_treatment=requested,visible_content=fields['Visual direction'],picture_treatment=treatment,required_caption=fields['Continuity/caption'],match_reason=fields['Continuity/caption'],limit_on_implication='Declared visual is not independently verified.',script_narration=fields['Exact narration'],srt_narration=cue.get('text',''),narration_mismatch=mismatch,register_window='',register_visible='',register_restrictions='',coverage_review='Requires visual inspection and rights review.',import_blockers=warnings,strict_production=True,transition_out='cut',source_proposed=proposed,plain_cue=True,uploaded_photo=photo,native_static_interval=(typ=='STILL' and not photo)))
    return parse(json.dumps(rows).encode(),'.json')

==================== END FILE: plain_script.py ====================


==================== FILE: preview_render.py ====================
"""Low-resolution, temporary playback proxy; never an export or an editorial change."""
import json, shutil, subprocess, threading, time, uuid
from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import imageio_ffmpeg

JOBS={}
GUARD=threading.Lock()


def status(root, pid, updated):
    with GUARD:
        job=JOBS.get(pid)
        if job and job['updated']==updated:
            result=dict(job)
        else:
            path=Path(root)/'.cache/continuous_preview/status.json'
            try:result=json.loads(path.read_text())
            except (OSError,ValueError):return {'status':'idle'}
    if result.get('updated')!=updated:return {'status':'stale'}
    if result.get('status')=='done' and not (Path(root)/'.cache/continuous_preview/preview.mp4').is_file():return {'status':'idle'}
    return result


def start(root, project, on_complete):
    root=Path(root);pid=project['id'];updated=project['updated']
    with GUARD:
        existing=JOBS.get(pid)
        if existing and existing['status'] in ('queued','running'):
            raise ValueError('A smooth preview is already being prepared. Please wait.')
        job={'status':'queued','progress':0,'updated':updated,'detail':'Preparing a temporary preview.'};JOBS[pid]=job
    threading.Thread(target=_run,args=(root,project,job,on_complete),daemon=True,name='preview-'+pid).start()
    return dict(job)


def _run(root,p,job,on_complete):
    ff=imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe();base=root/'.cache/continuous_preview';base.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    work=base/('work-'+uuid.uuid4().hex);work.mkdir();segments=[]
    def update(**kw):
        with GUARD:job.update(kw)
    try:
        assets={a['id']:a for a in p['assets']};scenes=p['scenes'];last=0
        if not scenes:raise ValueError('Build a storyboard before preparing a preview.')
        for i,s in enumerate(scenes):
            a=assets.get(s.get('asset_id'));frames=s['frame_out']-s['frame_in']
            if not a or frames<=0 or s['frame_in']!=last:raise ValueError(f'Scene {i+1} has missing media or a timeline gap.')
            if s.get('transition')!='cut':raise ValueError('This temporary preview currently supports clean cuts only.')
            path=(root/a['path']).resolve()
            if not path.is_relative_to(root.resolve()) or not path.is_file():raise ValueError(f'Scene {i+1} media is missing.')
            if a['kind']=='video' and (s['in_s']<0 or s['in_s']+frames/30>a['duration']+.001):raise ValueError(f'Scene {i+1} footage is too short.')
            if a['kind'] not in ('video','image'):raise ValueError(f'Scene {i+1} has unsupported media.')
            last=s['frame_out']
        # Fixed light-weight geometry preserves project aspect without changing export settings.
        aspects={'landscape':(16,9),'portrait':(9,16),'square':(1,1),'vertical_4_5':(4,5),'cinema':(239,100)}
        aw,ah=aspects.get(p.get('format'),(16,9));w=max(2,int(640*aw/max(aw,ah))//2*2);h=max(2,int(640*ah/max(aw,ah))//2*2)
        update(status='running')
        for i,s in enumerate(scenes):
            a=assets[s['asset_id']];path=root/a['path'];frames=s['frame_out']-s['frame_in'];out=work/f'{i:05}.mp4'
            update(progress=round(90*i/len(scenes),1),detail=f'Preparing scene {i+1} of {len(scenes)}')
            cmd=[ff,'-nostdin','-y','-v','error','-threads','2']
            if a['kind']=='image':cmd+=['-loop','1','-framerate','30','-i',str(path)]
            else:cmd+=['-ss',str(s['in_s']),'-i',str(path)]
            vf=f'scale={w}:{h}:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,pad={w}:{h}:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2,fps=30,setsar=1,format=yuv420p'
            cmd+=['-vf',vf,'-frames:v',str(frames),'-an','-c:v','libx264','-preset','ultrafast','-crf','27','-threads','2','-video_track_timescale','90000',str(out)]
            result=subprocess.run(cmd,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,stderr=subprocess.PIPE,timeout=max(120,frames/30*20))
            if result.returncode:raise ValueError(f'Scene {i+1} could not be decoded: '+result.stderr.decode(errors='replace')[-400:])
            cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(out));count=int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT));cap.release()
            if count!=frames:raise ValueError(f'Scene {i+1} produced {count} of {frames} frames; preview stopped rather than freezing or shortening it.')
            segments.append(out)
        listing=work/'files.txt';listing.write_text(''.join("file '"+str(x.resolve()).replace("'", "'\\''")+"'\n" for x in segments))
        update(progress=92,detail='Joining clips and continuous narration')
        cmd=[ff,'-nostdin','-y','-v','error','-f','concat','-safe','0','-i',str(listing)]
        voice=p.get('voiceover')
        if voice:
            audio=(root/voice['path']).resolve()
            if not audio.is_relative_to(root.resolve()) or not audio.is_file():raise ValueError('Narration file is missing.')
            cmd+=['-i',str(audio),'-map','0:v:0','-map','1:a:0','-c:a','aac','-b:a','96k']
        else:cmd+=['-map','0:v:0','-an']
        temp=work/'preview.mp4';cmd+=['-t',str(last/30),'-c:v','copy','-movflags','+faststart',str(temp)]
        result=subprocess.run(cmd,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,stderr=subprocess.PIPE,timeout=max(120,last/30*4))
        if result.returncode:raise ValueError('Could not join preview: '+result.stderr.decode(errors='replace')[-400:])
        cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(temp));count=int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT));cap.release()
        if count!=last:raise ValueError(f'Preview has {count} frames instead of {last}.')
        # The caller checks the version and publishes under the project lock.
        if not on_complete(p['updated'],temp,base):update(status='stale',detail='The project changed; prepare a new preview.')
        else:update(status='done',progress=100,detail='Temporary smooth preview ready.')
    except Exception as e:update(status='error',detail=str(e)[:600])
    finally:shutil.rmtree(work,ignore_errors=True)

==================== END FILE: preview_render.py ====================


==================== FILE: production.py ====================
"""Document-led planning for any programme. Deterministic parsing, never factual/visual verification."""
import json as _json
from pathlib import Path as _Path
_PROFILE_FILE=_Path(__file__).with_name('blueprints')/'style_profiles.json'
try:PROFILES=_json.loads(_PROFILE_FILE.read_text())
except (OSError,ValueError):PROFILES={}
import collections,json,re,zipfile,xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from pathlib import Path

def reserve_id(asset):
 name=Path(str((asset.get('derived_from') or {}).get('source_file') or asset.get('original_name') or asset.get('name',''))).stem.lower()
 return name if re.fullmatch(r'r\d+',name) else ''
def asset_used(p,a):
 selected={s.get('asset_id') for s in p['scenes']}
 return a['id'] in selected or any(c['id'] in selected and (c.get('derived_from') or {}).get('asset_id')==a['id'] for c in p['assets'])

def asset_matches(asset,name):
 original=str(asset.get('original_name') or asset.get('name',''))
 return original.casefold()==name.casefold() or bool(not asset.get('derived_from') and reserve_id(asset) and reserve_id(asset)==name.casefold())

def matching_assets(p,name):
 key=str(name).strip().casefold();binding=(p.get('source_bindings') or {}).get(key)
 if binding:return [a for a in p['assets'] if a['id']==binding]
 return [a for a in p['assets'] if asset_matches(a,str(name))]

def match_problem(p,name):
 matches=matching_assets(p,name)
 if len(matches)==1:return ''
 if (p.get('source_bindings') or {}).get(str(name).strip().casefold()):return 'Selected upload is no longer present for '+str(name)+'. Select another copy in Import media.'
 if not matches:return 'No uploaded file matches '+str(name)+'. If it is uploaded under another filename, link that upload in Import media.'
 return str(len(matches))+' uploaded files match '+str(name)+'. Choose one using Use this upload for script matching in Import media.'

def read_document(file,ext):
 raw=file.read(4_000_001)
 if len(raw)>4_000_000:raise ValueError('Production scripts must be under 4 MB.')
 if ext=='.docx':
  import io
  with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(raw)) as z:
   info=z.getinfo('word/document.xml')
   if info.file_size>8_000_000:raise ValueError('The Word document is too large.')
   root=ET.fromstring(z.read(info))
   text='\n'.join(''.join(p.itertext()) for p in root.iter('{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main}p'))
 else:text=raw.decode('utf-8-sig')
 if not text.strip():raise ValueError('The production script is empty.')
 return text

def document(p):return (p.get('production_script') or {}).get('text','')
def structured(p):
 try:
  d=json.loads(document(p));return d if isinstance(d,dict) else {}
 except ValueError:return {}

def infer(p):
 production=document(p);d=structured(p);narration='\n'.join(b['text'] for b in (p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks',[]));text=production+'\n'+narration
 ignore=set('the a an this that when after before then police officer detective detectives district attorney judge court monday tuesday wednesday thursday friday saturday sunday january february march april may june july august september october november december video scene srt source original production script case homicide murder chapter part he she his her they their in on at but now and was found missing footage convicted sentenced guilty not'.split())
 names=[]
 for m in re.finditer(r"\b[A-Z][a-z]+(?:[-'][A-Za-z]+)?(?:[ \t]+[A-Z][a-z]+(?:[-'][A-Za-z]+)?){1,2}\b",text):
  name=' '.join(m[0].split())
  if any(w.casefold() in ignore for w in name.split()):continue
  names.append(name)
 counts=collections.Counter(names)
 explicit=d.get('case_name') or d.get('case')
 if isinstance(explicit,dict):explicit=explicit.get('name')
 label=re.search(r'(?im)^\s*(?:case(?: name)?|victim)\s*:\s*([^\n|]+)',production)
 anchor=str(explicit or (label[1].strip() if label else ''))[:160]
 if not anchor and counts:
  def score(name):
   contexts=[text[max(0,m.start()-70):m.end()+90].lower() for m in re.finditer(re.escape(name),text)]
   victim=sum(bool(re.search(r'victim|murdered|killed|missing|body of|death of|homicide of',c)) for c in contexts)
   return victim*3+min(counts[name],8)
  ranked=sorted(counts,key=lambda n:(-score(n),-counts[n],text.index(n)))
  anchor=ranked[0]
  if len(ranked)>1 and score(ranked[0])==score(ranked[1]) and counts[ranked[0]]==counts[ranked[1]]:
   # Same-case actors can be searched together; do not assert which one is the victim.
   ambiguity='Several actors have equal prominence; selected the first named actor as a search anchor, not a victim identification.'
  else:ambiguity='Name roles are document-derived proposals, not independently verified identities.'
 else:ambiguity='Case identifier comes from the uploaded production script.'
 people=d.get('people',[])
 if isinstance(people,str):people=[x.strip() for x in people.split(',') if x.strip()]
 if isinstance(people,list):people=[x.get('name','') if isinstance(x,dict) else str(x) for x in people]
 else:people=[]
 people=list(dict.fromkeys(people+list(counts)))[:40]
 if anchor and anchor not in people:people.insert(0,anchor)
  # Profile is inferred from whichever registered profile best matches the narration's
 # wording. Nothing here names a genre of programme: a script about a market, a product,
 # a process or a person resolves to the profile whose hints actually fire, and to the
 # neutral default when none do.
 profile='documentary';best=0
 blocks=(p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks',[])
 for name,meta in (PROFILES or {}).items():
  hits=sum(len(re.findall(h.get('pattern',''),narration,re.I)) for h in meta.get('hints') or [])
  if hits>best and hits>max(2,len(blocks)*.25):profile,best=name,hits
 return {'anchor':anchor,'people':people,'aliases':d.get('case_aliases',[]),'locations':d.get('locations',[]),'confirmed':True,'profile':profile,'basis':ambiguity,'origin':'uploaded_production_script_and_srt','text_characters':len(text)}

def seconds(value):
 if isinstance(value,(int,float)):return float(value)
 parts=str(value).strip().replace(',','.').split(':');total=0
 for part in parts:total=total*60+float(part)
 return total

def instructions(p):
 d=structured(p);rows=d.get('scenes',[]);issues=[];result=[]
 if not isinstance(rows,list):issues.append('The script scenes field must be a list.');rows=[]
 if not rows:
  # Recognize explicit references only; never infer a scene from a source number.
  for line in document(p).splitlines():
   asset=re.search(r'\b((?:s|v)\d+\.(?:mp4|mov)|r\d+(?:\.(?:mp4|mov))?)\b',line,re.I);block=re.search(r'\b(?:SRT|Scene)\s*#?\s*(\d+)(?:\s*[-–]\s*(\d+))?',line,re.I)
   if not asset or not block:continue
   start=re.search(r'\b(?:in(?:_s)?|from|start)\s*[:=]?\s*(\d+(?::\d+){0,2}(?:[.,]\d+)?)',line,re.I)
   end=re.search(r'\b(?:out(?:_s)?|to|end)\s*[:=]?\s*(\d+(?::\d+){0,2}(?:[.,]\d+)?)',line,re.I)
   rows.append({'srt_blocks':[int(block[1]),int(block[2] or block[1])],'asset':asset[1],'in_s':seconds(start[1]) if start else None,'out_s':seconds(end[1]) if end else None,'transition':'dissolve' if re.search(r'\bdissolve\b',line,re.I) else 'cut','note':line})
 if document(p).strip() and not rows:issues.append('No explicit SRT/file/source-time edit instructions were recognized. No footage placement was guessed; check the uploaded script format.')
 for row in rows:
  if not isinstance(row,dict):issues.append('Ignored a non-object scene instruction.');continue
  blocks=row.get('srt_blocks',row.get('srt',row.get('scene')))
  if isinstance(blocks,int):blocks=[blocks,blocks]
  if isinstance(blocks,str):
   nums=re.findall(r'\d+',blocks);blocks=[int(nums[0]),int(nums[-1])] if nums else None
  if not isinstance(blocks,list) or len(blocks)!=2:issues.append('Scene instruction has no explicit SRT block range.');continue
  try:
   start=None if row.get('in_s') is None else seconds(row['in_s']);end=None if row.get('out_s') is None else seconds(row['out_s'])
   if start is not None and (not 0<=start<1e8):raise ValueError()
   if end is not None and (not 0<end<1e8):raise ValueError()
   result.append({**row,'srt_blocks':[int(x) for x in blocks],'asset':Path(str(row.get('asset') or row.get('file') or '')).name,'in_s':start,'out_s':end})
  except (ValueError,TypeError):issues.append('Ignored an instruction with invalid source timing or SRT numbers.')
 return result,issues

def apply(p):
 rows,issues=instructions(p);used=collections.defaultdict(list);assets=p['assets'];scenes=p['scenes'];applied=0
 for row in rows:
  selected=[s for s in scenes if row['srt_blocks'][0]<=s['srt_blocks'][0] and s['srt_blocks'][1]<=row['srt_blocks'][1]]
  matches=matching_assets(p,row['asset'])
  if not selected:issues.append(f"{row['asset']}: no matching SRT scenes.");continue
  if len(matches)!=1:issues.append(match_problem(p,row['asset']));continue
  a=matches[0]
  if a['kind']=='video' and row['in_s'] is None:issues.append(f"{row['asset']}: script does not specify a source in-point; no interval was guessed.");continue
  cursor=row['in_s'] or 0;proposals=[];problem=''
  for s in selected:
   transition='dissolve' if row.get('transition')=='dissolve' and s is selected[-1] and s is not scenes[-1] else 'cut'
