   dur=(s['frame_out']-s['frame_in']+(4 if transition=='dissolve' else 0))/30;stop=cursor+dur
   if a['kind']=='video':
    if stop>min(a['duration'],row['out_s'] if row['out_s'] is not None else a['duration'])+.001:problem='requested interval is too short; no loop/freeze/slowdown was added';break
    if any(cursor<end and stop>start for start,end in used[a['id']]):problem='source interval would repeat another scripted scene';break
   if s.get('script_instruction'):problem='two script instructions target the same scene';break
   proposals.append((s,cursor,stop,transition));cursor=stop
  if problem:issues.append(f"{row['asset']}: {problem}.");continue
  for s,start,stop,transition in proposals:
   s.update(asset_id=a['id'],in_s=start,transition=transition,approved=False,script_instruction=True,notes='Production-script edit instruction; actual file identity/context still require verification. '+str(row.get('note','')))
   for key in ['focus','emotion','person','purpose']:
    if row.get(key):s[key]=str(row[key])[:5000]
   move=row.get('motion')
   if move in ['auto','push','pull','pan-left','pan-right','rise','reveal']:s['motion']=move
   if a['kind']=='video':used[a['id']].append((start,stop))
   applied+=1
 # Never leave invalid dissolve pairs; prefer clean narration-led cuts.
 for i,s in enumerate(scenes):
  if s['transition']=='dissolve' and (i==len(scenes)-1 or scenes[i+1]['transition']=='dissolve'):s['transition']='cut'
 p['script_application']={'recognized_instructions':len(rows),'placed_scenes':applied,'issues':issues,'note':'Scripted placement is not visual/identity verification. Unrecognized prose is not silently treated as an edit decision.'}
 return p

==================== FILE: scene_exports.py ====================
"""Create individually named scene clips from a current, already rendered timeline."""
import re
import json
import shutil
import subprocess
import threading
import time
import uuid
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import imageio_ffmpeg

JOBS = {}
GUARD = threading.Lock()


def scene_name(index):
    return f'C{index:02d}.mp4'


def clip_filename(scene, index):
    row=scene.get('plan_row') or {}
    return row['clip_id'] + '.mp4' if row.get('strict_production') and row.get('clip_id') else scene_name(index)


def status(root, pid, updated):
    with GUARD:
        job = JOBS.get(pid)
        if job and job['updated'] == updated:
            result = dict(job)
        else:
            try:
                result = json.loads((Path(root) / 'scene_exports_status.json').read_text())
            except (OSError, ValueError):
                return {'status': 'idle'}
    if result.get('updated') != updated:
        return {'status': 'stale'}
    if result.get('status') == 'done' and not (Path(root) / 'renders' / 'scene_exports' / result['output']).is_file():
        return {'status': 'idle'}
    return result


def start(root, project, source, source_kind, publish):
    pid = project['id']
    with GUARD:
        current = JOBS.get(pid)
        if current and current['status'] in ('queued', 'running'):
            raise ValueError('Scene clips are already being prepared.')
        state = {'status': 'queued', 'progress': 0, 'updated': project['updated'], 'source_kind': source_kind, 'naming': 'C', 'detail': 'Preparing scene clips.'}
        JOBS[pid] = state
    thread = threading.Thread(target=_run, args=(Path(root), project, Path(source), state, publish), name='scene-export-' + pid, daemon=True)
    thread.start()
    return dict(state)


def _run(root, project, source, state, publish):
    work = root / '.cache' / ('scene-export-' + uuid.uuid4().hex)
    work.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    def update(**kwargs):
        with GUARD:
            state.update(kwargs)
    try:
        scenes = project['scenes']
        if not scenes or not source.is_file():
            raise ValueError('The selected timeline video is unavailable.')
        names=[clip_filename(scene,i) for i,scene in enumerate(scenes,1)]
        if any(not re.fullmatch(r'C\d+\.mp4',name) for name in names) or len(set(name.casefold() for name in names))!=len(names):
            raise ValueError('The script reuses a clip reference across shots. Separate exports cannot safely use the same C filename twice; use the full master until those clips are defined uniquely.')
        last = 0
        for i, scene in enumerate(scenes):
            if scene['frame_in'] != last or scene['frame_out'] <= last:
                raise ValueError(f'Scene {i+1} has an invalid timeline interval.')
            last = scene['frame_out']
        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(source))
        total = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT));fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS);cap.release()
        if total != last or abs(fps - 30) > .01:
            raise ValueError('The selected timeline video does not match the current storyboard frame count or frame rate.')
        output_name = 'scenes_' + time.strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S') + '_' + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8] + '.zip'
        archive = work / output_name
        ff = imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()
        update(status='running')
        with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, 'w', compression=zipfile.ZIP_STORED, allowZip64=True) as z:
            for i, scene in enumerate(scenes, 1):
                update(progress=round((i-1)*100/len(scenes), 1), detail=f'Preparing {names[i-1]} ({i} of {len(scenes)})')
                frames = scene['frame_out'] - scene['frame_in'];duration = frames / 30
                path = work / names[i-1]
                cmd = [ff, '-nostdin', '-y', '-v', 'error', '-ss', f"{scene['frame_in']/30:.9f}", '-i', str(source),
                       '-t', f'{duration:.9f}', '-map', '0:v:0', '-map', '0:a:0?', '-c:v', 'libx264',
                       '-preset', 'ultrafast', '-crf', '18', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p', '-threads', '2',
                       '-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '192k', '-movflags', '+faststart', str(path)]
                completed = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, timeout=max(120, duration*20))
                if completed.returncode:
                    raise ValueError(f'{names[i-1]} could not be created: ' + completed.stderr.decode(errors='replace')[-400:])
                cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(path));actual = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT));cap.release()
                if actual != frames:
                    raise ValueError(f'{names[i-1]} has {actual} frames instead of {frames}; no incomplete archive was saved.')
                z.write(path, names[i-1]);path.unlink()
        if publish(project['updated'], archive, output_name, state['source_kind']):
            update(status='done', progress=100, detail=f'{len(scenes)} scene clips ready.', output=output_name)
        else:
            update(status='stale', detail='The project changed while the scene clips were being prepared. Start again for the current edit.')
    except Exception as exc:
        update(status='error', detail=str(exc)[:600])
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)

==================== END FILE: scene_exports.py ====================


==================== FILE: section_script.py ====================
"""Read section-per-SRT production scripts without claiming visual verification."""
import json
import re
from edl import parse, timecode_ms

CLOCK = r'\d{1,2}:\d{2}:\d{2}[.,]\d{3}'
SHOT_FIELDS = {'Narration placement', 'Source', 'Extraction', 'Actually visible', 'Edit and continuity', 'Editorial caption', 'Audio', 'Meaning and limits'}


def clock(text):return timecode_ms(text, 'Production script clock')

def norm(text):return re.sub(r'[^a-z0-9]+', '', text.casefold())

def parse_section_script(text, blocks):
    from document_plan import tables
    if not blocks:raise ValueError('Import the original SRT before interpreting this script.')
    if re.search(r'https?://(?:www\.)?(?:youtube\.com|youtu\.be)/', text, re.I):raise ValueError('YouTube URLs are not permitted in the production script.')
    headings = list(re.finditer(r'^##\s+Srt(\d+)\s*$', text, re.M|re.I))
    if not headings:raise ValueError('No SRT sections found.')
    by_number={int(b['number']):b for b in blocks}
    if [int(m[1]) for m in headings]!=[int(b['number']) for b in blocks]:raise ValueError('Script SRT sections must match the uploaded SRT, once each and in order.')
    register={}
    for table in tables(text):
        if table and {'Clip','Source','Original window or extraction time','Visible content','Treatment and restrictions'}<=set(table[0]):
            for item in table:
                clip=item['Clip'].strip().upper()
                if clip in register:raise ValueError('Duplicate source-register clip '+clip+'.')
                register[clip]=item
    rows=[];last=0
    for i,section in enumerate(headings):
        number=int(section[1]);end=headings[i+1].start() if i+1<len(headings) else len(text)
        content=text[section.end():end]
        content=re.split(r'^##\s+(?!Srt\d+)',content,maxsplit=1,flags=re.M|re.I)[0]
        cue=by_number[number]
        timeline=re.search(r'\*\*Narration timeline\*\*\s*('+CLOCK+r')\s*[–—-]\s*('+CLOCK+r')',content)
        if not timeline or (clock(timeline[1]),clock(timeline[2]))!=(cue['start_ms'],cue['end_ms']):raise ValueError(f'Srt{number:02}: script and SRT cue clocks do not agree.')
        narration=re.search(r'\*\*Exact narration\*\*\s*\n\s*\n((?:>[^\n]*\n?)+)',content)
        if not narration:raise ValueError(f'Srt{number:02}: approved narration is missing.')
        authored=' '.join(re.sub(r'^>\s*','',line).strip() for line in narration[1].splitlines())
        mismatch=norm(authored)!=norm(cue.get('text',''))
        shot_headers=list(re.finditer(r'^###\s+(Srt\d+(?:-[A-Za-z]+)?)\s*$',content,re.M|re.I))
        if not shot_headers:raise ValueError(f'Srt{number:02}: no shots found.')
        expected=1
        for j,sh in enumerate(shot_headers):
            sid=sh[1];m=re.fullmatch(r'Srt0*(\d+)(?:-([A-Za-z]+))?',sid,re.I);suffix=0
            for letter in (m[2] or "A").upper():suffix=suffix*26+ord(letter)-64
            if int(m[1])!=number or suffix!=expected:raise ValueError(sid+': shots must be in block and letter order.')
            expected+=1
            shot=content[sh.end():(shot_headers[j+1].start() if j+1<len(shot_headers) else len(content))]
            fields={}
            for field in re.finditer(r'^- \*\*([^*]+)\*\*\s*(.*)$',shot,re.M):
                if field[1] in fields:raise ValueError(sid+': duplicate '+field[1]+'.')
                fields[field[1]]=field[2].strip()
            if set(fields)!=SHOT_FIELDS:raise ValueError(sid+': missing or unsupported shot instructions: '+', '.join(sorted(set(fields)^SHOT_FIELDS)))
            placement=re.search(r'('+CLOCK+r')\s*[–—-]\s*('+CLOCK+r')',fields['Narration placement'])
            if not placement:raise ValueError(sid+': narration placement is missing.')
            tin,tout=clock(placement[1]),clock(placement[2])
            if tin!=last or tout<=tin or tin<cue['start_ms'] or tout>cue['end_ms']:raise ValueError(sid+': timeline gap, overlap or out-of-block shot.')
            last=tout
            src=re.search(r'`(S\d+\.(?:mp4|mov))`\s*·\s*(?:\*\*Clip\*\*\s*(C\d+)\s*·\s*)?\*\*Treatment\*\*\s*(.*)',fields['Source'],re.I)
            if not src:raise ValueError(sid+': source or treatment is missing or unsupported.')
            file,clip=src[1],(src[2] or '').upper();entry=register.get(clip) if clip else None
            if clip and (not entry or entry['Source'].strip().casefold()!=file.casefold()):raise ValueError(sid+': clip reference does not match the source register.')
            treatment_text=src[3].strip().rstrip('.');treatment=re.sub(r'\s*·\s*intentional archival reuse\s*$','',treatment_text,flags=re.I).upper()
            if treatment=='NORMAL-SPEED VIDEO':treatment='VIDEO'
            if treatment not in ('VIDEO','STILL HOLD','EDITORIAL FREEZE-FRAME'):raise ValueError(sid+': unsupported treatment '+treatment_text)
            ext=fields['Extraction'];frame=re.search(r'Source frame\s+('+CLOCK+r')',ext,re.I);rng=re.search(r'Source IN\s+('+CLOCK+r')\s*·\s*source OUT\s+('+CLOCK+r')\s*\(exclusive\)',ext,re.I)
            if treatment=='VIDEO':
                if not rng:raise ValueError(sid+': explicit source IN and exclusive OUT are required.')
                source_in,source_out=clock(rng[1]),clock(rng[2])
                if source_out-source_in!=tout-tin:raise ValueError(sid+': source and timeline durations differ.')
            else:
                if not frame:raise ValueError(sid+': explicit source frame is required.')
                source_in=clock(frame[1]);source_out=source_in+1
            window=entry['Original window or extraction time'] if entry else '';times=re.findall(CLOCK,window)
            if entry and not times:raise ValueError(sid+': source register has no clock.')
            if entry and treatment=='VIDEO' and (len(times)!=2 or source_in<clock(times[0]) or source_out>clock(times[1])):raise ValueError(sid+': segment is outside the registered source window.')
            if entry and treatment=='STILL HOLD' and ('Frame' not in window or source_in!=clock(times[0])):raise ValueError(sid+': still frame differs from its registered extraction.')
            if entry and treatment=='EDITORIAL FREEZE-FRAME' and (len(times)!=2 or source_in!=clock(times[1])-1):raise ValueError(sid+': freeze must be the last displayed frame before registered exclusive OUT.')
            if not re.fullmatch(r'Mute source\. Continue narration\.',fields['Audio'],re.I):raise ValueError(sid+': unsupported audio instruction; no audio was substituted.')
            warnings=['Editorial caption and its layout must be implemented and reviewed before rendering.']
            if mismatch:warnings.append('Approved script narration differs from uploaded SRT wording; compare without changing either file.')
            if treatment=='EDITORIAL FREEZE-FRAME':warnings.append('Freeze must use the final displayed frame before the previous exclusive OUT; exact frame selection needs implementation and verification.')
            if re.search(r'\b(?:crop|inset|plain field|plain dark field|names under|text beside)',fields['Edit and continuity'],re.I):warnings.append('Requested crop, inset or text placement requires explicit implementation and review.')
            rows.append({'beat_id':sid,'shot_id':sid,'clip_id':clip,'srt_blocks':[number],'timeline_in_ms':tin,'timeline_out_ms':tout,'source_file':file,'source_in_ms':source_in,'source_out_ms':source_out,'visual_status':'VIDEO' if treatment=='VIDEO' else 'STILL','requested_treatment':treatment,'intentional_archival_reuse':'archival reuse' in treatment_text.lower(),'visible_content':fields['Actually visible'],'picture_treatment':treatment,'limit_on_implication':fields['Meaning and limits'],'required_caption':fields['Editorial caption'],'source_audio_instruction':fields['Audio'],'match_reason':fields['Edit and continuity'],'script_narration':authored,'srt_narration':cue.get('text',''),'narration_mismatch':mismatch,'register_window':window,'register_visible':entry['Visible content'] if entry else '','register_restrictions':entry['Treatment and restrictions'] if entry else '','coverage_review':'Declared in script; source content, identity, version and rights require review.','import_blockers':warnings,'strict_production':True,'transition_out':'cut'})
        if last!=cue['end_ms']:raise ValueError(f'Srt{number:02}: shots do not cover the complete cue.')
    return parse(json.dumps(rows).encode(),'.json')

==================== END FILE: section_script.py ====================


==================== FILE: shot_plan.py ====================
"""Planning aids that never place a picture by themselves.

Three questions this module answers, each strictly from decoded facts and the
user's own documents:

1. Shot index - which continuous shots exist in each uploaded file, with their
   exact IN/OUT. A shot is a decoded span between detected changes; nothing here
   says WHO or WHAT it shows.
2. Candidates - which shot can physically hold a given beat (same length to the
   frame, after any transition handle). Blanks stay blank: a beat with no exact
   shot is reported as a gap, never paired with something merely close.
3. Feasibility - per source, seconds of picture the plan demands against seconds
   the file actually has, so a dead-end plan is visible before a long render.

`prefill` writes a block-map CSV from the SRT with every row's clocks filled and
every picture column left empty for the editor to decide. The empty rows are the
point: they carry the right timing and block references and cannot be mistaken
for an approved choice, because a row with no source file is a named gap.
"""
import csv, io, json, math
from pathlib import Path

FPS = 30
def _clock(ms):
    h, rem = divmod(int(ms), 3600000)
    m, rem = divmod(rem, 60000)
    s, f = divmod(rem, 1000)
    return '%02d:%02d:%02d.%03d' % (h, m, s, f)


MIN_SHOT = 0.4  # shorter than this is detector noise, not a usable picture


def _analysis(root):
    path = Path(root) / 'reviews/Full_source_analysis.json'
    if not path.is_file():
        return {}
    try:
        data = json.loads(path.read_text())
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return {}
    return {s.get('file'): s for s in data.get('sources', []) if s.get('complete_decode_success')}


def _black_overlap(source, start, end):
    for interval in source.get('black_intervals') or []:
        if start < float(interval['end']) - 0.02 and end > float(interval['start']) + 0.02:
            return True
    return False


def shots(root, assets=None):
    """Every usable continuous shot per decoded file, in source order."""
    index = []
    for name, source in sorted(_analysis(root).items()):
        duration = float(source.get('duration_s') or 0)
        cuts = sorted({float(x) for x in source.get('scene_change_times_s') or []})
        cuts = [c for c in cuts if 0.25 < c < duration - 0.25]
        edges = [0.0] + cuts + [duration]
        entries = []
        for start, end in zip(edges, edges[1:]):
            if end - start < MIN_SHOT:
                continue
            entries.append({
                'file': name,
                'in_s': round(start, 3),
                'out_s': round(end, 3),
                'in_clock': _clock(round(start * 1000)),
                'out_clock': _clock(round(end * 1000)),
                'duration_s': round(end - start, 3),
                'frames': int(math.floor((end - start) * FPS + 1e-6)),
                'black': _black_overlap(source, start, end),
            })
        index.append({
            'file': name,
            'duration_s': duration,
            'shots': entries,
            'coverage_s': round(sum(e['duration_s'] for e in entries), 3),
        })
    if assets:
        videos = {a.get('original_name') for a in assets if a.get('kind') == 'video'}
        photos = {a.get('original_name') for a in assets if a.get('kind') == 'image' and not a.get('derived_from')}
        known = {entry['file'] for entry in index}
        for name in sorted(videos - known):
            index.append({'file': name, 'duration_s': 0, 'shots': [], 'coverage_s': 0, 'not_decoded': True})
        # A photograph is a usable picture source of unbounded length: it can hold a beat
        # of any duration. Calling it "not decoded" made a photo plan look impossible.
        for name in sorted(photos - known):
            index.append({'file': name, 'kind': 'image', 'duration_s': 0, 'shots': [], 'coverage_s': 0, 'unbounded': True})
    return index


def _plan_rows(p):
    """The rows the plan is actually built from, or [] when there is no plan."""
    m = p.get('srt_block_map') or {}
    return m.get('beats') or []


def _beat_lengths(p):
    """Timeline length of each planned beat, in seconds, from the plan itself."""
    return [(r.get('timeline_out_ms', 0) - r.get('timeline_in_ms', 0)) / 1000 for r in _plan_rows(p)]


def feasibility(p, root, blocks=None):
    """Seconds required against seconds available, per source. Never guesses.

    `blocks` narrows the arithmetic to one rebuilt range, so a block-range rebuild is
    judged on the beats it is actually placing rather than the whole programme. A plan
    whose rows stop covering the SRT is called out on its own: that is how a map built for
    a shorter or differently shaped narration announces that it needs extending, instead
    of quietly leaving the tail of the programme unplanned.
    """
    index = {entry['file']: entry for entry in shots(root, p.get('assets'))}
    rows = _plan_rows(p)
    srt_blocks = (p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks') or []
    uncovered = []
    if rows and srt_blocks:
        covered = {n for row in rows for n in row.get('srt_blocks') or []}
        uncovered = [b['number'] for b in srt_blocks if b['number'] not in covered]
    if blocks:
        wanted = set(blocks)
        rows = [r for r in rows if set(r.get('srt_blocks') or []) & wanted]
    demand, unfilled = {}, []
    for row in rows:
        name = row.get('source_file') or ''
        length = (row.get('timeline_out_ms', 0) - row.get('timeline_in_ms', 0)) / 1000
        handle = (row.get('transition_handle_frames') or 0) / FPS if row.get('transition_out') == 'dissolve' else 0
        if not name:
            unfilled.append({'beat_id': row.get('beat_id'), 'needed_s': round(length, 3), 'reason': row.get('gap_note') or 'no source selected'})
            continue
        record = demand.setdefault(name, {'file': name, 'required_s': 0.0, 'beats': 0, 'declared_s': 0.0, 'longest_beat_s': 0.0})
        record['required_s'] += length + handle
        record['beats'] += 1
        record['longest_beat_s'] = max(record['longest_beat_s'], length + handle)
        if row.get('source_in_ms') is not None and row.get('source_out_ms') is not None:
            record['declared_s'] += (row['source_out_ms'] - row['source_in_ms']) / 1000
    uploaded = {a.get('original_name'): a for a in p.get('assets') or [] if a.get('kind') == 'video'}
    sources = []
    for name, record in sorted(demand.items()):
        entry = index.get(name) or {}
        if entry.get('unbounded'):
            # Photographs are exempt from capacity arithmetic; holding one is legitimate.
            sources.append({**record, 'decoded': True, 'kind': 'image', 'required_s': round(record['required_s'], 3),
                            'declared_s': round(record['declared_s'], 3), 'longest_beat_s': round(record['longest_beat_s'], 3),
                            'available_s': 0.0, 'usable_s': 0.0, 'longest_shot_s': 0.0, 'slack_s': 0.0, 'problems': []})
            continue
        decoded = bool(entry.get('shots'))
        # Before a file is decoded, its own stored duration is still a real figure: it is
        # what the upload reported. Never borrow another file's numbers.
        available = float(entry.get('duration_s') or (uploaded.get(name) or {}).get('duration') or 0)
        usable = float(entry.get('coverage_s') or 0)
        longest_shot = max([e['duration_s'] for e in entry.get('shots') or []], default=0.0)
        problems = []
        if not entry:
            problems.append('No uploaded file matches this name, so nothing can be placed from it.')
        elif entry.get('not_decoded'):
            problems.append('The file is uploading but has not been decoded yet.')
        elif not decoded:
            problems.append('Only the reported file length is known so far (' + str(round(available, 3)) + 's). Run the build once to decode its shots and check the plan against them.')
        else:
            if record['required_s'] > available + 1e-6:
                problems.append('Needs ' + str(round(record['required_s'], 3)) + 's of picture but the file is only ' + str(round(available, 3)) + 's long.')
            if record['longest_beat_s'] > longest_shot + 1e-6:
                problems.append('The longest beat from this file is ' + str(round(record['longest_beat_s'], 3)) + 's but its longest single shot is ' + str(round(longest_shot, 3)) + 's; a beat cannot straddle a shot change.')
            if record['required_s'] > usable + 1e-6:
                problems.append('Needs ' + str(round(record['required_s'], 3)) + 's and the decoded shots total ' + str(round(usable, 3)) + 's, so the plan reuses footage or overlaps intervals.')
        sources.append({**record,
                        'decoded': decoded,
                        'required_s': round(record['required_s'], 3),
                        'declared_s': round(record['declared_s'], 3),
                        'longest_beat_s': round(record['longest_beat_s'], 3),
                        'available_s': round(available, 3),
                        'usable_s': round(usable, 3),
                        'longest_shot_s': round(longest_shot, 3),
                        'slack_s': round(available - record['required_s'], 3),
                        'problems': problems})
    return {
        'has_plan': bool(rows),
        'beat_count': len(rows),
        'scope_blocks': blocks,
        'unplanned_blocks': uncovered[:200],
        'unplanned_block_count': len(uncovered),
        'unfilled_beats': unfilled,
        'sources': sources,
        'verdict': 'no_plan' if not rows else ('blocked' if (unfilled or uncovered or any(s['problems'] for s in sources)) else 'feasible'),
        'scope': 'Arithmetic on decoded durations and the plan as written. Says nothing about what any shot shows; that stays a human review.',
    }


def _candidates(entry, length, handle, limit):
    fits = []
    for shot in entry.get('shots') or []:
        need = length + handle
        if shot['duration_s'] + 1e-6 < need:
            continue
        if shot['black']:
            continue
        fits.append({**shot, 'slack_s': round(shot['duration_s'] - need, 3)})
    fits.sort(key=lambda s: (s['slack_s'], s['in_s']))
    return fits[:limit]


def shots_of_length(root, p, length, handle=0.0, limit=8):
    """Every shot able to hold a picture of this length, across all decoded files."""
    found = []
    for entry in shots(root, p.get('assets')):
        found.extend(_candidates(entry, length, handle, limit))
    found.sort(key=lambda s: (s['slack_s'], s['file'], s['in_s']))
    return found[:limit]


def candidates(p, root, beat_id=None, limit=8):
    """Exact-length shots per beat. A beat with no fit is reported, not filled."""
    beats = []
    for row in _plan_rows(p):
        if beat_id and str(row.get('beat_id')) != str(beat_id):
            continue
        length = (row.get('timeline_out_ms', 0) - row.get('timeline_in_ms', 0)) / 1000
        handle = (row.get('transition_handle_frames') or 0) / FPS if row.get('transition_out') == 'dissolve' else 0
        name = row.get('source_file') or ''
        index = shots(root, p.get('assets')) or []
        entry = next((e for e in index if e['file'] == name), None)
        if entry and entry.get('unbounded'):
            options = [{'file': name, 'in_s': 0.0, 'out_s': round(length + handle, 3), 'duration_s': None,
                        'frames': None, 'black': False, 'slack_s': None}]
        else:
            options = _candidates(entry, length, handle, limit) if entry else []
        beats.append({
            'beat_id': row.get('beat_id'),
            'srt_blocks': row.get('srt_blocks'),
            'source_file': name,
            'needed_s': round(length + handle, 3),
            'candidates': options,
            'coverage': 'exact' if options else ('no_source' if not entry else 'no_matching_shot'),
        })
    return {'beats': beats, 'fps': FPS}


PREFILL_COLUMNS = ['block', 'start', 'end', 'beat', 'media_file', 'source_in', 'source_out', 'status', 'gap_note']


def prefill(p, parent_beat=1):
    """A block-map CSV with every clock filled and every picture column blank.

    One row per SRT block, so the map covers the narration exactly. `gap_note`
    explains the blank, which keeps the file importable as an honest list of
    decisions still to make rather than an approved plan.
    """
    blocks = (p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks') or []
    if not blocks:
        raise ValueError('Upload the authored SRT first; the map is built from its blocks.')
    rows = []
    for block in blocks:
        rows.append({
            'block': str(block['number']),
            'start': _clock(block['start_ms']),
            'end': _clock(block['end_ms']),
            'beat': str(parent_beat),
            'media_file': '',
            'source_in': '',
            'source_out': '',
            'status': '',
            'gap_note': 'no source chosen yet for SRT block ' + str(block['number']) + ' (write source_in/source_out as HH:MM:SS.mmm; a row with no source stays a named gap)',
        })
    return rows


def prefill_csv(p, parent_beat=1):
    buffer = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.DictWriter(buffer, fieldnames=PREFILL_COLUMNS, lineterminator='\n')
    writer.writeheader()
    for row in prefill(p, parent_beat):
        writer.writerow(row)
    return buffer.getvalue()


def guidance(p, root, parent_beat=1):
    """Prefill rows plus the shots that can serve each one."""
    rows = prefill(p, parent_beat)
    index = shots(root, p.get('assets'))
    seen = {}
    for block, row in zip((p.get('srt') or {}).get('blocks') or [], rows):
        length = (block['end_ms'] - block['start_ms']) / 1000
        for entry in index:
            fits = _candidates(entry, length, 0, 3)
            if fits:
                seen.setdefault(row['block'], []).append({
                    'file': entry['file'],
                    'options': [{'in_s': s['in_s'], 'out_s': s['out_s'], 'slack_s': s['slack_s']} for s in fits],
                })
        row['possible_sources'] = seen.get(row['block'], [])
    return {'rows': rows, 'columns': PREFILL_COLUMNS, 'note': 'Clocks and block references are filled. Picture columns are deliberately blank: a row with no source file is a named gap, not an approved choice.'}

==================== END FILE: shot_plan.py ====================


==================== FILE: static/editor.css ====================

let sourceReady=false,sourceSeekToken=0,masterSeekToken=0,editorBuffering=false;
let timelineZoom=12,editorPlaying=false,editorMuted=false,editorCC=false,editorMaster=false,masterUrl='',sourceScene='',clockBase=0,clockAt=0,editorContext='',activeSRT=-1;
const masterNode=$('#program-master'),imageNode=$('#program-image'),narrNode=$('#program-narration');
const standbyNode=document.createElement('video');standbyNode.id='program-source-next';standbyNode.muted=true;standbyNode.playsInline=true;standbyNode.preload='auto';standbyNode.disablePictureInPicture=true;standbyNode.setAttribute('disableremoteplayback','');imageNode.before(standbyNode);

sourceNode.loop=false;nextSourceNode.loop=false;
const scenes=()=>P?P.scenes:[],assets=()=>P?P.assets:[];
const totalTime=()=>scenes().at(-1)?.frame_out/30||0;
const fmt=t=>{t=Math.max(0,t||0);const f=Math.floor(t*30+.00001),pad=n=>String(Math.floor(n)).padStart(2,'0');return `${pad(f/108000)}:${pad(f/1800%60)}:${pad(f/30%60)}:${pad(f%30)}`};
const frameAt=t=>Math.floor(t*30+.001);
const editorTime=()=>editorMaster?(masterNode.currentTime||0):(P?.voiceover&&narrNode.getAttribute('src')?narrNode.currentTime:clockBase+(editorPlaying&&!editorBuffering?(performance.now()-clockAt)/1000:0));
const deletingProjects=new Set();
list=async function(){const d=await api('/api/projects');projects=d.projects;PROFILES=d.profiles||PROFILES;$('#project-list').innerHTML=projects.map(p=>`<div class="project-row ${P?.id===p.id?'active':''}"><button type="button" class="project-link" onclick="openProject('${p.id}')">${E(p.title)}<small>${p.scenes} scenes</small></button><button type="button" class="project-delete" aria-label="Delete ${E(p.title)}" onclick="deleteProduction('${p.id}')" ${deletingProjects.has(p.id)?'disabled':''}>Delete</button></div>`).join('')||'<p class="muted">No productions yet.</p>';return d};
async function deleteProduction(id){
 const project=projects.find(p=>p.id===id);if(!project||deletingProjects.has(id))return;
 if(uploading){toast('Please wait for the import to finish before deleting a production.');return}
 if(!confirm(`Permanently delete “${project.title}”?\n\nThis deletes the production and all its uploaded files, scripts, previews, and exports stored in this project. This cannot be undone.`))return;
 const active=P?.id===id;
 if(active&&!leave())return;
 deletingProjects.add(id);const button=document.querySelector(`.project-delete[onclick="deleteProduction('${id}')"]`);if(button)button.disabled=true;
 if(active){pauseEditor();resetEditor()}
 try{
  await api('/api/projects/'+id,'DELETE',{confirm:true});
  if(P?.id===id){P=null;sceneIndex=0;dirty=false;history.replaceState(null,'',location.pathname+location.search);renderProject()}
  await list();toast('Production and its project files deleted.');
 }catch(e){if(active&&P?.id===id)renderProject();toast(e.message)}
 finally{deletingProjects.delete(id);if(button&&button.isConnected)button.disabled=false}
}

showHome=openProjects;

function closeTools(){pauseManagementMedia();for(const id of ['#projects-dialog','#manage-dialog'])if($(id).open)$(id).close()}

function resetEditor(){clearPreparedSource();editorBuffering=false;sourceSeekToken++;masterSeekToken++;sourceReady=false;sourceScene='';clockBase=0;clockAt=performance.now();masterUrl='';editorMaster=false;masterNode.pause();masterNode.removeAttribute('src');masterNode.load();sourceNode.pause();sourceNode.removeAttribute('src');sourceNode.load();narrNode.pause();narrNode.removeAttribute('src');narrNode.load();$('#timeline-scroll').scrollLeft=0}

function renderEditor(){

The review timeline opens at the first shot with explicit video IN/OUT times.':P&&!ss.length&&assets().some(a=>a.kind==='video')?'Click an uploaded video above to play it. function missingVisualHTML(s){return s?.replacement_issue?`<strong>Replaced footage needs attention</strong><span>${E(s.replacement_issue)} No substitute was chosen.</span><button class="button secondary" onclick="goToNextVideo()">Go to next available video</button>`:s?.missing_video_out?`<strong>Still placement not shown in video review</strong><span>${E(s.plan_row?.shot_id||'This shot')} specifies a still or freeze. No screenshot, extracted frame or guessed video is being shown.</span><button class="button secondary" onclick="goToNextVideo()">Go to next available video</button>`:s?.plan_row?.strict_production?`<strong>Scripted video unavailable</strong><span>${E(s.plan_row.shot_id||'This shot')} requires ${E(s.plan_row.source_file||'a source file')} at ${E(mapClock(s.plan_row.source_in_ms))}–${E(mapClock(s.plan_row.source_out_ms))}. ${E((P?.script_application?.issues||[]).find(x=>x.startsWith('Beat '+s.plan_row.shot_id+':'))||'The file may be missing, too short, or overlap another shot.')} No substitute was chosen.</span><button class="button secondary" onclick="goToNextVideo()">Go to next available video</button>`:'<strong>No visual assigned to this scene</strong><span>Choose uploaded media in the shot inspector, or import case footage.</span><button class="button secondary" onclick="manage(\'assets\')">Import media</button>'}
function goToNextVideo(){const ss=scenes(),video=s=>assets().some(a=>a.id===s.asset_id&&a.kind==='video');let index=ss.findIndex((s,i)=>i>sceneIndex&&video(s));if(index<0)index=ss.findIndex(video);if(index>=0)selectEditorScene(index);else toast('No playable scripted video is available.') }
function goToFirstVideo(){const index=scenes().findIndex(s=>assets().some(a=>a.id===s.asset_id&&a.kind==='video'));if(index>=0)selectEditorScene(index);else toast('No explicit video IN/OUT ranges are available yet.') }
function renderProgram(){
 const target=P?.render?.status==='done'&&P.render.current?P.render.url:(P?.preview?.status==='done'?P.preview.url:'');
 if(editorPlaying&&masterUrl!==target)return;
 const t=editorTime();
 if(target){
  if(masterUrl!==target){narrNode.pause();sourceNode.pause();masterUrl=target;editorMaster=true;masterNode.src=target;masterNode.load();const token=++masterSeekToken;masterNode.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',()=>{if(token===masterSeekToken&&masterNode.getAttribute('src')===target)masterNode.currentTime=Math.min(t,masterNode.duration||t)},{once:true})}
  editorMaster=true;masterNode.classList.add('is-active');sourceNode.classList.remove('is-active');imageNode.hidden=true;
 }else{
  if(masterUrl){masterNode.pause();masterNode.removeAttribute('src');masterNode.load();masterUrl=''}editorMaster=false;masterNode.classList.remove('is-active');
  const url=P?.voiceover?.url||'';if(narrNode.getAttribute('src')!==url){narrNode.pause();if(url){narrNode.src=url;narrNode.load()}else{narrNode.removeAttribute('src');narrNode.load()}}
  showSource(sceneIndex,t);
 }
 const assigned=scenes().length&&assets().some(a=>a.id===scenes()[sceneIndex]?.asset_id);$('#monitor-empty').hidden=!!scenes().length&&(editorMaster||assigned);if(scenes().length&&!assigned&&!editorMaster)$('#monitor-empty').innerHTML=missingVisualHTML(scenes()[sceneIndex]);else if(!scenes().length){if(P?.script_reading?.status==='read'){const r=P.script_reading;$('#monitor-empty').innerHTML=`<strong>Script read · storyboard not built</strong><span>${r.shots} shots found. Captions, stills and other script instructions need attention before an accurate edit can be assembled.</span><button class="button secondary" onclick="createScriptDraft()">Show shots in review timeline</button><button class="button secondary" onclick="manage('brief');showScriptReading()">Review the shot plan</button>`}else if(P&&assets().some(a=>a.kind==='video')){const count=assets().filter(a=>a.kind==='video').length;$('#monitor-empty').innerHTML=`<strong>${count} source video${count===1?'':'s'} uploaded</strong><span>${P.srt?'Your storyboard has not been built yet.':'Your SRT has not been imported into this project, so there is no timeline yet.'} You can play the uploaded videos now.</span><button class="button secondary" onclick="openFirstSourceFile()">Play an uploaded video</button><button class="button secondary" onclick="manage('brief')">Open setup</button>`}else {const missing=(P?.source_match_report||[]).filter(x=>!['matched','selected'].includes(x.status));$('#monitor-empty').innerHTML=missing.length?`<strong>Storyboard waiting for footage links</strong><span>Script filenames need matching uploads: ${missing.map(x=>E(x.source_file)).join(', ')}. Your imported files are still here.</span><button class="button secondary" onclick="manage('assets')">Resolve script filenames</button>`:P?.srt?'<strong>Storyboard not built yet</strong><span>Your SRT is imported. Build the storyboard in Setup to place picture beats on V1.</span><button class="button secondary" onclick="manage(\'brief\')">Open production setup</button>':'<strong>Keep the editor. Start a new story.</strong><span>Import narration, SRT and case media from Setup.</span><button class="button secondary" onclick="newProduction()">＋ New production</button>';}}$('#big-play').hidden=!scenes().length||(!assigned&&!editorMaster)||editorPlaying;$('#preview-mode').textContent=P?.script_moving_only?'SCRIPT REVIEW · INCOMPLETE':P?.review_only_draft?'REVIEW DRAFT · NOT FINAL':editorMaster?(target===P?.preview?.url?'SMOOTH PREVIEW':'CONTINUOUS MASTER'):(['queued','running'].includes(P?.preview?.status)?`PREPARING SMOOTH PREVIEW ${Math.floor(P.preview.progress||0)}%`:'SOURCE PREVIEW');
}
function clearPreparedSource(){
 prepareToken++;preparedScene='';preparedReady=false;nextSourceNode.pause();
 nextSourceNode.onloadedmetadata=nextSourceNode.onseeked=nextSourceNode.onloadeddata=nextSourceNode.onerror=null;
 nextSourceNode.removeAttribute('src');nextSourceNode.load();nextSourceNode.classList.remove('is-active');
}
function prepareNextSource(i,t){
 if(editorMaster||!editorPlaying)return;
 const current=scenes()[i],next=scenes()[i+1],asset=assets().find(a=>a.id===next?.asset_id);
 if(!current||!next||!asset||asset.kind!=='video'||current.frame_out/30-t>8)return;
 if(preparedScene===next.id)return;
 clearPreparedSource();preparedScene=next.id;const token=prepareToken,node=nextSourceNode,target=Number(next.in_s);
 const valid=()=>token===prepareToken&&preparedScene===next.id&&node===nextSourceNode;
 const ready=()=>{if(valid()&&!node.seeking&&node.readyState>=2&&Math.abs(node.currentTime-target)<.15)preparedReady=true};
 const seek=()=>{if(!valid()||node.readyState<1)return;if(!Number.isFinite(target)||target<0||target>node.duration+.034)return;try{node.currentTime=target;ready()}catch{}};
 node.onloadedmetadata=seek;node.onseeked=ready;node.onloadeddata=ready;node.onerror=()=>{if(valid())preparedReady=false};
 node.src=asset.url;node.load();
}
function usePreparedSource(s,a,t){
 if(preparedScene!==s.id||!preparedReady||nextSourceNode.getAttribute('src')!==a.url)return false;
 const offset=Math.max(0,t-s.frame_in/30);if(offset>.18)return false;
 sourceSeekToken++;sourceNode.pause();sourceNode.classList.remove('is-active');
 sourceNode.onloadedmetadata=sourceNode.onseeked=sourceNode.onloadeddata=sourceNode.onerror=null;
 const wasBuffering=editorBuffering;const old=sourceNode;sourceNode=nextSourceNode;nextSourceNode=old;prepareToken++;preparedScene='';preparedReady=false;
 sourceNode.onloadedmetadata=sourceNode.onseeked=sourceNode.onloadeddata=sourceNode.onerror=null;
 sourceReady=true;editorBuffering=false;clockAt=performance.now();sourceNode.playbackRate=1;sourceNode.classList.add('is-active');imageNode.hidden=true;$('#monitor-empty').hidden=true;
 if(editorPlaying&&wasBuffering&&P?.voiceover){narrNode.muted=editorMuted;narrNode.play().catch(()=>pauseEditor())}
 if(editorPlaying)sourceNode.play().catch(()=>{if(sourceScene===s.id&&!editorMaster)seekSourceAtTimeline(s,a,editorTime())});
 return true;
}
function seekSourceAtTimeline(s,a,t){
 // Freeze the preview clock while decoding. Never hide the last picture while audio runs.
 const token=++sourceSeekToken;sourceReady=false;sourceNode.pause();sourceNode.playbackRate=1;
 if(!P?.script_moving_only&&sourceNode.readyState>=2&&sourceNode.classList.contains('is-active')){
  try{const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=sourceNode.videoWidth;c.height=sourceNode.videoHeight;c.getContext('2d').drawImage(sourceNode,0,0);imageNode.src=c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',.88);imageNode.style.transform='none';imageNode.hidden=false}catch{}
 }
 sourceNode.classList.remove('is-active');editorBuffering=true;clockBase=t;clockAt=performance.now();narrNode.pause();
 const valid=()=>token===sourceSeekToken && sourceScene===s.id && !editorMaster;
 const desired=()=>Number(s.in_s)+Math.max(0,t-s.frame_in/30);
 const failed=()=>{if(!valid())return;sourceReady=false;pauseEditor();$('#monitor-empty').hidden=false;$('#monitor-empty').innerHTML='<strong>Media preview unavailable</strong><span>Playback paused with the narration; no empty slot was played.</span>'};
 let readyDone=false;
 const seek=()=>{if(!valid()||sourceNode.readyState<1)return;const target=desired();if(!Number.isFinite(target)||target<0||target>sourceNode.duration+.034){failed();return}try{sourceNode.currentTime=target;if(!sourceNode.seeking&&sourceNode.readyState>=2)ready()}catch{failed()}};
 const ready=()=>{if(readyDone||!valid()||sourceNode.seeking||sourceNode.readyState<2)return;if(Math.abs(sourceNode.currentTime-desired())>.15){seek();return}readyDone=true;sourceReady=true;editorBuffering=false;clockAt=performance.now();sourceNode.classList.add('is-active');imageNode.hidden=true;$('#monitor-empty').hidden=true;if(editorPlaying){const jobs=[sourceNode.play()];if(P?.voiceover){narrNode.muted=editorMuted;jobs.push(narrNode.play())}Promise.all(jobs).catch(()=>{if(valid()&&editorPlaying)failed()})}};
 sourceNode.onloadedmetadata=seek;sourceNode.onseeked=ready;sourceNode.onloadeddata=ready;sourceNode.onerror=failed;
 if(sourceNode.getAttribute('src')!==a.url){sourceNode.src=a.url;sourceNode.load()}else if(sourceNode.readyState>=1)seek();
}

// If the active video actually starves, hold narration until decoding resumes.
for(const node of [sourceNode,nextSourceNode]){
 node.addEventListener('waiting',()=>{
  if(node!==sourceNode||editorMaster||!editorPlaying||!sourceReady||editorBuffering)return;
  const now=editorTime();editorBuffering=true;clockBase=now;clockAt=performance.now();narrNode.pause();
 });
 node.addEventListener('playing',()=>{
  if(node!==sourceNode||editorMaster||!editorPlaying||!sourceReady||!editorBuffering)return;
  editorBuffering=false;clockAt=performance.now();
  if(P?.voiceover){narrNode.muted=editorMuted;narrNode.play().catch(()=>pauseEditor())}
 });
}

function showSource(i,t){
 if(editorMaster)return;const s=scenes()[i],a=assets().find(a=>a.id===s?.asset_id);if(!s||!a||s.missing_video_out){sourceSeekToken++;sourceReady=false;sourceNode.pause();sourceNode.classList.remove('is-active');if(sourceNode.getAttribute('src')){sourceNode.removeAttribute('src');sourceNode.load()}imageNode.hidden=true;sourceScene='';$('#blur-backdrop').getContext('2d').clearRect(0,0,960,540);if(s){if(editorPlaying)pauseEditor();$('#monitor-empty').hidden=false;$('#monitor-empty').innerHTML=missingVisualHTML(s)}return}
 if(sourceScene!==s.id){
  $('#monitor-empty').hidden=true;sourceScene=s.id;
  if(a.kind==='image'){sourceNode.pause();sourceSeekToken++;sourceReady=false;editorBuffering=false;clockAt=performance.now();imageNode.style.transform='none';sourceNode.onloadedmetadata=sourceNode.onseeked=sourceNode.onloadeddata=sourceNode.onerror=null;imageNode.src=a.url;imageNode.hidden=false;sourceNode.classList.remove('is-active')}
  else if(editorPlaying&&sourceReady&&!sourceNode.seeking&&sourceNode.getAttribute('src')===a.url&&Math.abs(sourceNode.currentTime-(Number(s.in_s)+Math.max(0,t-s.frame_in/30)))<.12){ /* Continuous interval in the same source: keep decoding without a seek. */ }
  else if(!usePreparedSource(s,a,t)){seekSourceAtTimeline(s,a,t)}
 }
 if(a.kind==='image'){if(s.plan_row?.uploaded_photo)imageNode.style.transform='none';else applyImageMotion(s,Math.max(0,t-s.frame_in/30));}
 else if(editorPlaying&&sourceReady&&!sourceNode.seeking){
  // Small browser clock differences are normal. Correct them gradually rather than
  // repeatedly pausing narration and seeking the picture on every animation frame.
  const drift=sourceNode.currentTime-(Number(s.in_s)+Math.max(0,t-s.frame_in/30));
  sourceNode.playbackRate=s.plan_row?.strict_production?1:(Math.abs(drift)<.06?1:Math.max(.85,Math.min(1.15,1-drift*.12)));
 }
}

function applyImageMotion(s,elapsed){const m=s.motion_plan;if(!m)return;const p=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,elapsed/m.duration_s)),q=m.easing==='linear'?p:m.easing==='quick_settle'?(p<.08?p*p/.1472:p>.92?1-(1-p)*(1-p)/.1472:(p-.04)/.92):m.easing==='sine'?.5-.5*Math.cos(Math.PI*p):m.easing==='smootherstep'?p*p*p*(p*(p*6-15)+10):p*p*(3-2*p);const z=m.zoom_start+(m.zoom_end-m.zoom_start)*q,x=m.x_start+(m.x_end-m.x_start)*q,y=m.y_start+(m.y_end-m.y_start)*q;imageNode.style.transform=`translate(${(.5-x)*(z-1)*100}%,${(.5-y)*(z-1)*100}%) scale(${z})`}
let lastBackdropDraw=0;
function drawBackdrop(){const now=performance.now();if(now-lastBackdropDraw<100)return;lastBackdropDraw=now;if(editorMaster||(imageNode.hidden&&!sourceReady))return;const node=imageNode.hidden?sourceNode:imageNode,w=node.videoWidth||node.naturalWidth,h=node.videoHeight||node.naturalHeight;if(!w||!h)return;const c=$('#blur-backdrop'),scale=Math.max(c.width/w,c.height/h);try{c.getContext('2d').drawImage(node,(c.width-w*scale)/2,(c.height-h*scale)/2,w*scale,h*scale)}catch{}}
// Correct known UTF-8 decoding artifacts for display only; never rewrite uploaded text.
function captionDisplayText(text){return String(text||'').replaceAll('â€¢','•').replaceAll('â€™','’')}
function updatePosition(){const t=editorTime();const scriptLabel=$('#script-caption-review');const draftRow=P?.review_only_draft?scenes()[sceneIndex]?.plan_row:null;
 const sourceDetail=$('#monitor-source-detail'),currentScene=scenes()[sceneIndex],actualAsset=assets().find(a=>a.id===currentScene?.asset_id);sourceDetail.hidden=!draftRow;
 if(draftRow){const range=draftRow.source_in_ms!=null&&draftRow.source_out_ms!=null?`${mapClock(draftRow.source_in_ms)} → ${mapClock(draftRow.source_out_ms)} (exclusive)`:'';const actual=actualAsset?.kind==='video'?(sourceReady?`${actualAsset.original_name||actualAsset.name} · playing ${mapClock(sourceNode.currentTime*1000)}`:`${actualAsset.original_name||actualAsset.name} · loading`):`${draftRow.source_file||'Source'} · unavailable`;sourceDetail.textContent=`${draftRow.shot_id||''} · ${actual} · script ${range}`;}
scriptLabel.hidden=!draftRow;if(draftRow){const cap=captionDisplayText(draftRow.required_caption);scriptLabel.textContent='Script caption (review only; not rendered): '+cap;}const readout=$('#source-clock');if(readout){const current=scenes()[sceneIndex],asset=assets().find(a=>a.id===current?.asset_id);readout.textContent=asset?.kind==='video'?(sourceReady?'Actual player source: '+sourceNode.currentTime.toFixed(3)+'s':'Seeking to the saved source interval…'):'Still candidate; image identity not automatically verified.'}$('#timecode').textContent=fmt(t)+' / '+fmt(totalTime());$('#playhead').style.left=t*timelineZoom+'px';const caption=P?.srt?.blocks.find(b=>t>=b.start_ms/1000&&t<b.end_ms/1000)?.text||'';$('#editor-captions').textContent=caption;$('#editor-captions').classList.toggle('captions-off',!editorCC);const blocks=P?.srt?.blocks||[],index=blocks.findIndex(b=>t>=b.start_ms/1000&&t<b.end_ms/1000);if(index!==activeSRT){document.querySelector('.caption-block.active')?.classList.remove('active');document.querySelector(`.caption-block[data-caption-index="${index}"]`)?.classList.add('active');activeSRT=index}}
function renderTimeline(){
 activeSRT=-1;const ss=scenes(),width=Math.max(totalTime()*timelineZoom,$('#timeline-scroll').clientWidth-2,600);$('#timeline-content').style.width=width+'px';$('#track-legend').textContent=`${ss.length} full-frame scenes · ${P?.srt?.blocks.length||0} SRT blocks`;
 const step=timelineZoom<5?60:30;let ticks='';for(let t=0;t<=totalTime()+step;t+=step)ticks+=`<span class="ruler-tick" style="left:${t*timelineZoom}px">${Math.floor(t/60).toString().padStart(2,'0')}:${String(t%60).padStart(2,'0')}</span>`;$('#ruler-track').innerHTML=ticks;
 $('#video-track').innerHTML=ss.map((s,i)=>{const a=assets().find(a=>a.id===s.asset_id),thumb=P?.script_moving_only?'':(s.thumbnail||(a&&P?`/api/projects/${P.id}/scenes/${s.id}/thumbnail${a?.revision?'?v='+encodeURIComponent(a.revision):''}`:''));return `<div class="shot-clip ${i===sceneIndex?'selected':''} ${a?.kind==='image'?'image-clip':''}" data-scene="${i}" role="button" tabindex="0" title="${E(s.narration)}" style="left:${s.frame_in/30*timelineZoom}px;width:${Math.max(3,(s.frame_out-s.frame_in)/30*timelineZoom-1)}px" onclick="selectEditorScene(${i})" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter')selectEditorScene(${i})">${thumb?`<img class="clip-thumb" src="${thumb}" loading="lazy" alt="" onerror="this.hidden=true">`:''}<div class="clip-text"><strong>${E((s.plan_row?.shot_id||('SRT '+s.srt_blocks.join('–'))).replace(/-[A-Za-z]+$/, ''))} · ${E(a?.original_name||a?.name||s.plan_row?.source_file||'MEDIA NEEDED')}</strong><span>${((s.frame_out-s.frame_in)/30).toFixed(1)}s · ${E(s.purpose||s.focus)}</span></div>${s.transition==='dissolve'?'<i class="transition-mark"></i>':''}</div>`}).join('');
 const blocks=P?.srt?.blocks||[];
 $('#caption-track').innerHTML=blocks.map((b,i)=>`<div class="caption-block" data-caption-index="${i}" title="${E(b.text)}" style="left:${b.start_ms/1000*timelineZoom}px;width:${Math.max(2,(b.end_ms-b.start_ms)/1000*timelineZoom-1)}px" onclick="seekEditor(${b.start_ms/1000},true)">${E(b.text)}</div>`).join('')+'<span class="caption-note" style="left:8px">SRT TIMING REFERENCE · ORIGINAL NARRATION ORDER PRESERVED · NOT BURNED IN</span>';
}
function renderInspector(){const s=scenes()[sceneIndex];$('#shot-number').textContent=s?((s.plan_row?.shot_id||'SRT '+s.srt_blocks.join('–')).replace(/-[A-Za-z]+$/, '')):'—';$('#monitor-shot-label').textContent=s?((s.plan_row?.shot_id||'SRT '+s.srt_blocks.join('–')).replace(/-[A-Za-z]+$/, '')):'NO SCENES';
 if(!s){const missing=(P?.source_match_report||[]).filter(x=>!['matched','selected'].includes(x.status));$('#inspector-content').innerHTML=missing.length?`<div class="inspector-group"><label>Waiting for footage links</label><p>The script refers to ${missing.map(x=>E(x.source_file)).join(', ')}. Select the correct existing videos or upload the missing files.</p><button type="button" onclick="manage('assets')">Resolve filenames</button></div>`:P?.srt?'<div class="inspector-group"><label>No picture beats yet</label><p>The SRT is imported. Open Setup and build the storyboard.</p><button type="button" onclick="manage(\'brief\')">Open setup</button></div>':'<div class="inspector-group"><label>Ready for a new production</label><p>Upload your production script, SRT and narration under Setup.</p></div>';return}
 const a=assets().find(a=>a.id===s.asset_id),m=s.motion_plan||{};
 const flow=s.picture_flow,cut=s.cut_sync;
 const flowLabel={already_inside_one_shot:'inside one shot',continuous_with_previous:'runs on from previous beat',continued_running_take:'continued take',slid_inside_a_shot:'conformed inside a shot',slid_far_inside_a_shot:'moved far — check it',repeats_recent_footage:'repeats recent footage',jump_cut_within_shot:'deliberate jump cut',no_single_shot_fits:'no shot fits this beat',not_decoded:'source not decoded'};
 const cutLabel={in_a_breath:'cut in a breath',on_a_block_boundary:'cut on a line break',over_speech:'cut over a spoken word',between_blocks:'cut between blocks'};
 const meta=`<div class="mini-meta"><span>SRT ${s.srt_blocks.join('–')}</span><span>${((s.frame_out-s.frame_in)/30).toFixed(3)}s</span></div><div class="mini-meta"><span>Timeline</span><span>${(s.frame_in/30).toFixed(3)} → ${(s.frame_out/30).toFixed(3)}s</span></div>${flow?`<div class="mini-meta"><span>Picture flow</span><span title="${E(flow.note||'')}">${E(flowLabel[flow.status]||flow.status)}${flow.shot?` · shot ${flow.shot[0]}–${flow.shot[1]}s`:''}</span></div>`:''}${cut?`<div class="mini-meta"><span>Cut to next</span><span>${E(cutLabel[cut.quality]||cut.quality)}${cut.moved_from_s!=null?` · moved from ${cut.moved_from_s}s`:''}</span></div>`:''}`;
 $('#inspector-content').innerHTML=`<form id="scene-form" onsubmit="saveEditorScene(event)"><div class="inspector-group"><label>Narration / picture beat</label><p class="narration-excerpt">${E(s.narration)}</p></div>${s.plan_row?.native_static_interval?'<div class="notice warn"><strong>Native static interval · review only</strong><p>Playing the original video, without a freeze or extracted frame. Count its full duration toward the still allowance; eligibility, identity and rights must be checked.</p></div>':''}${meta}${sourceTimingHTML(s,a)}<label>Emotion<input name="emotion" value="${E(s.emotion)}"></label><label>Primary focus<input name="focus" value="${E(s.focus)}"></label><label>Named person<input name="person" value="${E(s.person)}"></label><label>Selected visual<select name="asset_id">${options([['','Choose uploaded media'],...P.assets.filter(a=>!P.script_moving_only||a.kind==='video'||(s.plan_row?.uploaded_photo&&a.kind==='image'&&!a.derived_from)).map(a=>[a.id,(a.reserve?'[reserve '+a.source_label+'] ':'')+(a.verified?'✓ ':'? ')+a.name])],s.asset_id||'')}</select></label><label>${s.plan_row?.uploaded_photo?'Photo source time (not applicable)':'Video in-point (seconds)'}<input name="in_s" type="number" step=".001" min="0" value="${s.in_s}">${flow&&flow.declared_in_s!=null&&Math.abs(flow.declared_in_s-flow.conformed_in_s)>=.001?`<span class="small">Declared ${flow.declared_in_s}s · conformed to ${flow.conformed_in_s}s so the beat stays inside one continuous shot. ${E(flow.note||'')}</span>`:''}</label><label>Exit transition<select name="transition">${options(P.transition_policy==='clean_cuts_only'?[['cut','Clean cut · transitions disabled']]:[['cut','Clean action / phrase cut'],['dissolve','Short dissolve · handles required']],s.transition)}</select></label><label class="check-label"><input type="checkbox" name="context_only" ${s.context_only?'checked':''}>Disclosed wide context, not the exact person/exhibit</label><label>Editorial / exception notes<textarea name="notes" rows="3">${E(s.notes)}</textarea></label><div class="edit-actions"><button type="button" onclick="rebuildHere()">Rebuild this block</button></div><label class="check-label"><input type="checkbox" name="approved" ${s.approved?'checked':''}>Editorially approved</label><button class="button primary" type="submit">Save scene</button></form><div id="scene-understanding"></div><div id="plan-tools"></div>`;
 if(a?.kind==='video')loadSceneUnderstanding(s.id);
 $('#plan-tools').innerHTML=planToolsHTML();candidateHTMLForScene(s).then(h=>{const n=$('#plan-tools');if(n)n.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',h)});
 if(s.automatic_match&&!P.manual_scene_edits){const form=$('#scene-form');for(const name of ['person','context_only','approved']){const field=form.querySelector('[name="'+name+'"]');if(field)field.closest('label').hidden=true}form.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin','<div class="inspector-group"><label>Automatic clips-only assembly</label><p>Source interval checked and linked to the script/SRT. No manual portrait selection or scene approval is required for this prepared timeline.</p></div>')}
}
function sourceTimingHTML(s,a){if(!a)return '';if(a.kind==='image'&&!a.derived_from)return `<div class="inspector-group"><label>Uploaded photograph · static hold</label><p>${E(a.original_name||a.name)}</p><p>Timeline duration: ${((s.frame_out-s.frame_in)/30).toFixed(3)}s. No video source IN/OUT. Counts toward still allowance; identity and rights require review.</p><p id="source-clock" class="small"></p></div>`;if(s.automatic_match)return `<div class="inspector-group"><label>Analyzed MP4 / SRT linkage</label><p>${E(a.original_name||a.name)} · SRT ${E(s.automatic_match.srt_block)}</p><p>Saved IN: ${Number(s.in_s).toFixed(3)}s → ${(Number(s.in_s)+(s.frame_out-s.frame_in)/30).toFixed(3)}s</p><p>Source-bank match: ${E(s.automatic_match.bank_id)}. Provisional CSV times were checked against actual footage.</p><p id="source-clock" class="small"></p></div>`;const derived=a.derived_from,planned=s.plan_row;const actual=derived?derived.requested_time_ms/1000:Number(s.in_s);return `<div class="inspector-group"><label>Source cut</label><p>${E(derived?.source_file||a.original_name||a.name)}</p><p>${derived?'Extracted frame':'Saved IN'}: ${actual.toFixed(3)}s${a.kind==='video'?' → '+(actual+(s.frame_out-s.frame_in)/30).toFixed(3)+'s':''}</p><p>${planned?'Script IN: '+((planned.extracted_frame_ms??planned.source_in_ms)/1000).toFixed(3)+'s'+(planned.source_out_ms!=null?' · OUT: '+(planned.source_out_ms/1000).toFixed(3)+'s (exclusive)':''):'Source timing selected in the editor.'}</p><p id="source-clock" class="small"></p></div>`}


async function loadSceneUnderstanding(sceneId){
 const id=P.id;
 try{
  const r=await api(`/api/projects/${id}/scenes/${sceneId}/understanding`);
  const node=$('#scene-understanding');if(!node||P?.id!==id||scenes()[sceneIndex]?.id!==sceneId)return;
  if(!['partial','complete'].includes(r.status)){
   node.innerHTML=`<div class="inspector-group"><label>Source understanding</label><p class="small">${E(r.status==='not_started'?'No local analysis yet. Run it from Production setup.':r.status)}</p></div>`;return;
  }
  const entry=(time,text)=>({time:Number(time),html:`<p class="small">${Number(time).toFixed(2)}s · ${text}</p>`});
  const rows=[...r.transcript.map(x=>entry(x.timeline_start_s,`Dialogue: ${E(x.text)}`)),
   ...r.visual.map(x=>entry(x.timeline_time_s,`Frame: ${E(x.description)}`)),
   ...r.sound_events.filter(x=>x.predictions.length).map(x=>entry(x.timeline_start_s,`Sounds: ${x.predictions.map(p=>E(p.label)).join(', ')}`)),
   ...(r.speakers||[]).map(x=>entry(x.timeline_start_s,`Speaker: ${E(x.speaker)}`)),
   ...(r.low_energy_intervals||[]).map(x=>entry(x.timeline_start_s,'Low-energy audio (estimate)')),
   ...(r.scene_changes||[]).filter(x=>x.start_s>=r.source_in_s).map(x=>entry(x.timeline_start_s,'Detected source scene start'))].sort((a,b)=>a.time-b.time);
  node.innerHTML=`<div class="inspector-group"><label>Source understanding · timeline times</label><p class="small">Estimated observations, not verified. Only sampled frames and overlapping source intervals are shown; dialogue text may extend outside this beat.</p>${r.stage_status?.visual==='unavailable'?'<p class="small">Visual analysis unavailable for this source.</p>':''}${r.timing_warning?`<p class="small">${E(r.timing_warning)}</p>`:''}${rows.slice(0,12).map(x=>x.html).join('')||'<p class="small">No observations overlap this beat.</p>'}${rows.length>12?'<p class="small">More observations are available in the full report.</p>':''}</div>`;
 }catch(e){/* A missing or changed video leaves the existing inspector usable. */}
}

// ---- Planning aids: shot index, feasibility, block-map template -----------------
// Everything here reads decoded facts. Nothing places a picture by itself.
let planCache = {id: null, index: null, feasibility: null};

async function loadPlanning(id){
  if(planCache.id === id) return planCache;
  planCache = {id, index: null, feasibility: null};
  try{ planCache.index = await api(`/api/projects/${id}/shot-index`); }catch(e){ planCache.index = {error: e.message}; }
  try{ planCache.feasibility = await api(`/api/projects/${id}/feasibility`); }catch(e){ planCache.feasibility = {error: e.message}; }
  return planCache;
}

function planningSummaryHTML(){
  const f = planCache.feasibility || {};
  if(f.error) return `<div class="notice">Feasibility could not be read: ${E(f.error)}</div>`;
  const unfilled = (f.unfilled_beats || []).length;
  const blocked = (f.sources || []).filter(s => s.problems.length);
  const verdict = f.verdict === 'feasible' ? 'The plan fits the footage as written.'
    : f.verdict === 'no_plan' ? 'No block map yet, so there is nothing to check.'
    : `${unfilled} beat${unfilled===1?'':'s'} still unfilled and ${blocked.length} source${blocked.length===1?'':'s'} with a problem.`;
  const rows = (f.sources || []).map(s => `<tr><td>${E(s.file)}</td><td>${s.beats}</td><td>${s.required_s}s</td><td>${s.available_s}s</td><td>${s.longest_beat_s}s</td><td>${s.longest_shot_s}s</td></tr>`).join('');
  return `<p>${E(verdict)}</p>${rows?`<table class="plan-table"><thead><tr><th>Source</th><th>Beats</th><th>Needed</th><th>File length</th><th>Longest beat</th><th>Longest shot</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table>`:''}
  ${blocked.map(s => `<div class="notice warn"><strong>${E(s.file)}</strong>${s.problems.map(x => `<p class="small">${E(x)}</p>`).join('')}</div>`).join('')}
  ${unfilled ? `<p class="small">${unfilled} name${unfilled===1?'s':'s'} no source yet. Those rows are gaps by design; fill them in the block map rather than letting a clip be chosen by guesswork.</p>` : ''}`;
}

function planToolsHTML(){
  return `<div class="inspector-group"><label>Planning</label>
  <div class="inline"><button type="button" onclick="openPlanDialog('index')">Shot index</button><button type="button" onclick="openPlanDialog('feasibility')">Feasibility</button><button type="button" onclick="downloadMapTemplate()">Download map template</button><button type="button" onclick="openFrameQC(1)">Frame QC sheet</button><button type="button" onclick="approveReadyScenes()">Approve all ready beats</button></div>
  <p class="small">The template fills every clock and SRT block reference from your SRT and leaves the picture columns blank on purpose. A blank row is a named gap, not a choice.</p></div>`;
}

async function openPlanDialog(view){
  if(!P) return;
  $('#plan-title').textContent = view === 'index' ? 'Shot index' : 'Feasibility';
  $('#plan-body').innerHTML = '<p class="small">Reading decoded sources…</p>';
  $('#plan-dialog').showModal();
  await loadPlanning(P.id);
  if(view === 'index'){
    const sources = (planCache.index?.sources || []);
    if(!sources.length){ $('#plan-body').innerHTML = '<p>Upload at least one video, then run the build so the sources are decoded.</p>'; return; }
    $('#plan-body').innerHTML = sources.map(s => `<div class="card"><h3>${E(s.file)} · ${s.duration_s.toFixed(3)}s · ${s.shots.length} shots${s.not_decoded?' · not decoded yet':''}</h3>
      ${s.not_decoded ? '<p class="small">Run the build once so this file is decoded.</p>' : `<table class="plan-table"><thead><tr><th>#</th><th>IN</th><th>OUT</th><th>Length</th><th>Map columns</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${s.shots.map((x,i)=>`<tr><td>${i+1}</td><td>${x.in_s}s</td><td>${x.out_s}s</td><td>${x.duration_s}s</td><td><code>${x.in_clock} → ${x.out_clock}</code></td><td>${x.black?'black':''}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>`}</div>`).join('')
      + `<p class="small">A shot is a decoded span between detected changes. This list says nothing about who or what appears in it.</p>`;
  } else {
    $('#plan-body').innerHTML = `<div class="card"><h3>Does this plan fit the footage?</h3>${planningSummaryHTML()}</div>`;
  }
}

async function downloadMapTemplate(){
  if(!P) return;
  try{
    const r = await fetch(`/api/projects/${P.id}/block-map-template`);
    if(!r.ok){ const d = await r.json().catch(()=>({})); throw Error(d.error || 'The template could not be built.'); }
    const blob = await r.blob(); const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = url; a.download = 'block-map-template.csv'; a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    toast('Template downloaded. Clocks are filled; picture columns are blank on purpose.');
  }catch(e){ toast(e.message); }
}

async function candidateHTMLForScene(s){
  if(!P) return '';
  const length = (s.frame_out - s.frame_in) / 30;
  const handle = s.transition === 'dissolve' ? (s.transition_handle_frames || 0) / 30 : 0;
  try{
    const d = await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/candidates?length_s=${length.toFixed(6)}&handle_s=${handle.toFixed(6)}&limit=6`);
    if(!d.candidates.length){
      const anyDecoded = (planCache.index?.sources || []).some(x => !x.not_decoded && x.shots.length);
      if(!anyDecoded && !planCache.index) await loadPlanning(P.id);
      const decoded = (planCache.index?.sources || []).some(x => !x.not_decoded && x.shots.length);
      return `<div class="inspector-group"><label>Shots that fit this beat</label><p class="small">${decoded
        ? `No decoded shot is exactly ${(length+handle).toFixed(3)}s long. Nothing is offered rather than something approximate; the build reports this as a gap.`
        : 'The sources have not been decoded yet. Run the build once, then this list is exact.'}</p></div>`;
    }
    return `<div class="inspector-group"><label>Shots that fit this beat · ${(length+handle).toFixed(3)}s</label>${d.candidates.map(c=>`<p class="small"><strong>${E(c.file)}</strong> · <code>${c.in_clock} → ${c.out_clock}</code> · ${c.duration_s}s · ${c.slack_s}s spare</p>`).join('')}<p class="small">Exact-length shots only. Choosing one is still your editorial decision.</p></div>`;
  }catch(e){ return `<div class="inspector-group"><label>Shots that fit this beat</label><p class="small">${E(e.message)}</p></div>`; }
}


// ---- Bulk approval and frame QC ----------------------------------------------
async function approveReadyScenes(){
  if(!P || !leave()) return;
  const unapproved = P.scenes.filter(s => !s.approved).length;
  if(!unapproved){ toast('Every picture beat is already approved.'); return; }
  if(!confirm(`Approve every picture beat that passes the same checks export readiness uses? ${unapproved} beat(s) are not approved. A beat with any blocking item is left unapproved and listed with its reason. This is your attestation that you reviewed the pictures, not an identity or rights clearance.`)) return;
  try{
    const d = await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/scenes/approve`, 'POST', {attested: true, scope: 'ready'});
    P = d; renderProject(); await list();
    const done = d.bulk_approval?.approved?.length || 0, skipped = d.bulk_approval?.skipped || [];
    if(skipped.length){
      const first = skipped[0];
      toast(`${done} beat(s) approved. ${skipped.length} left unapproved — Scene ${first.scene}: ${first.reasons[0]}`);
    } else toast(`${done} picture beat(s) approved. All others were already approved.`);
  }catch(e){ toast(e.message); }
}

let qcCache = {id: null, data: null};
async function openFrameQC(sheet){
  if(!P) return;
  $('#plan-title').textContent = 'Frame QC';
  $('#plan-body').innerHTML = '<p class="small">Decoding one frame per beat and stamping its source timecode…</p>';
  if(!$('#plan-dialog').open) $('#plan-dialog').showModal();
  try{
    const d = await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/frame-qc?format=json&sheet=${sheet || 1}`);
    qcCache = {id: P.id, data: d};
    const sheets = Math.max(1, d.sheets);
    $('#plan-body').innerHTML = `<div class="card"><h3>Sheet ${d.sheet} of ${sheets} · beats ${(d.sheet-1)*d.per_sheet+1}–${(d.sheet-1)*d.per_sheet+d.beats_on_sheet}</h3>
      <p>${E(d.note)}</p>
      <img style="width:100%;border-radius:6px" src="${d.url}" alt="Contact sheet of decoded frames stamped with source and in-point">
      <div class="inline">${Array.from({length: sheets}, (_, i) => `<button type="button" onclick="openFrameQC(${i+1})" ${i+1===d.sheet?'disabled':''}>Sheet ${i+1}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      ${d.missing.length ? `<div class="notice warn"><strong>${d.missing.length} beat(s) could not be decoded</strong>${d.missing.slice(0,6).map(b => `<p class="small">Scene ${b.scene} · SRT ${(b.srt_blocks||[]).join('-')} · ${E(b.source_file || 'no media assigned')} at ${b.in_clock}</p>`).join('')}</div>` : ''}
      ${d.moved.length ? `<details><summary>${d.moved.length} beat(s) start somewhere other than the plan declared</summary>${d.moved.map(b => `<p class="small">Scene ${b.scene} · SRT ${(b.srt_blocks||[]).join('-')} · ${E(b.source_file)} · planned in-point moved by ${b.declared_drift_s}s → now ${b.in_clock}</p>`).join('')}</details>` : ''}
      <p class="small">${E(d.scope)}</p></div>`;
  }catch(e){
    $('#plan-body').innerHTML = `<div class="notice">${E(e.message)}</div>`;
  }
}

// Rebuild only the block range around the current beat, keeping approvals elsewhere.
async function rebuildHere(){
  if(!P||!leave())return;
  const s=P.scenes[sceneIndex];if(!s)return;
  const blocks=(s.srt_blocks||[])[0];
  if(blocks==null){toast('This beat has no SRT block to rebuild by.');return}
  if(!confirm(`Rebuild the plan for SRT block ${blocks} only? Picture beats outside this block keep their media and their approvals. Use the full rebuild for anything that changes timing.`))return;
  try{
    const d=await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/storyboard`,'POST',{replace:true,blocks:[blocks]});
    P=d;renderProject();await list();
    const r=P.rebuild_report||{};
    toast(`Rebuilt block ${blocks}. ${r.approvals_carried||0} approval(s) carried over; ${r.needs_review||0} beat(s) need review.`);
  }catch(e){toast(e.message)}
}

Select a gap to see its required source and range.</div>`:''}${errors.slice(0,3).map(x=>`<div class="validation-item warning">△ ${E(x)}</div>`).join('')}${errors.length>3?`<div class="validation-item">${errors.length-3} more items under Review / export checks.</div>`:''}${!editorMaster&&ss.length?(P?.review_only_draft?'<div class="validation-item">Review-only timeline. Rendering remains blocked until outstanding checks are resolved.</div>':'<div class="validation-item">A continuous master may be rendered when export checks pass.</div>'):''}`:'<div class="validation-item">Create a new production, then import the voiceover, SRT and source MP4s.</div>'}

function seekEditor(t,select=true){if(!scenes().length)return;t=Math.max(0,Math.min(totalTime()-.001,t));if(select&&!leave())return;const was=editorPlaying;pauseEditor();clockBase=t;clockAt=performance.now();if(editorMaster){masterSeekToken++;try{masterNode.currentTime=t}catch{}}else if(P?.voiceover){try{narrNode.currentTime=t}catch{}}if(select){const i=scenes().findIndex(s=>frameAt(t)>=s.frame_in&&frameAt(t)<s.frame_out);if(i>=0)sceneIndex=i}sourceScene='';if(!editorMaster)showSource(sceneIndex,t);renderInspector();markSelected();updatePosition();if(was)playEditor()}

Check the selected media or try again.')}}
function pauseEditor(){if(editorPlaying&&!editorMaster&&!P?.voiceover)clockBase=editorTime();editorPlaying=false;masterNode.pause();sourceNode.pause();narrNode.pause();$('#big-play').hidden=!scenes().length;clockAt=performance.now()}

function toggleEditorCaptions(){editorCC=!editorCC;$('#caption-toggle').textContent=editorCC?'CC On':'CC Off';updatePosition()}
function setEditorZoom(v){timelineZoom=Number(v);renderTimeline();updatePosition()}

function tickEditor(){if(editorPlaying){const t=editorTime();if(t>=totalTime()){pauseEditor()}else{const i=scenes().findIndex(s=>frameAt(t)>=s.frame_in&&frameAt(t)<s.frame_out);if(i>=0&&i!==sceneIndex){sceneIndex=i;if(!editorMaster)showSource(i,t);renderInspector();markSelected()}if(!editorMaster){showSource(sceneIndex,t);prepareNextSource(sceneIndex,t);drawBackdrop()}}}else if(!editorMaster)drawBackdrop();updatePosition();requestAnimationFrame(tickEditor)}
