// Manual block-map authoring. Only the visible fields are changed; other imported
// production instructions stay on the row. A draft remains in memory until Save.
let mapDraft=null;
async function openBlockMapEditor(){
 if(!P||!leave())return;
 const id=P.id;
 try{
  const data=await api(`/api/projects/${id}/block-map-editor`);
  if(!P||P.id!==id)return;
  mapDraft={id,signature:data.srt_signature,rows:data.rows,index:0,changed:false};
  renderMapEditor();$('#map-editor-dialog').showModal();
 }catch(e){toast(e.message)}
}
function mapValue(row,key){
 if(Object.hasOwn(row,'_edit_'+key))return row['_edit_'+key];
 if(key==='beat')return row.parent_beat_id??row.beat??'1';
 if(key==='media_file')return row.source_file??row.media_file??'';
 if(key==='source_in')return row.source_in??(row.source_in_ms==null?'':mapClock(row.source_in_ms));
 if(key==='source_out')return row.source_out??(row.source_out_ms==null?'':mapClock(row.source_out_ms));
 if(key==='status')return row.status??row.visual_status??'';
 return row[key]??'';
}
function mapClock(ms){const n=Math.round(Number(ms)),h=Math.floor(n/3600000),m=Math.floor(n/60000)%60,s=Math.floor(n/1000)%60;return `${String(h).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(m).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(s).padStart(2,'0')}.${String(n%1000).padStart(3,'0')}`}
function renderMapEditor(){
 const d=mapDraft,r=d.rows[d.index],block=P.srt?.blocks.find(b=>Number(b.number)===Number(r.block??r.srt_blocks?.[0]));
 const field=(label,key,placeholder='')=>`<label>${label}<input data-map-field="${key}" value="${E(mapValue(r,key))}" placeholder="${E(placeholder)}"></label>`;
 $('#map-editor-body').innerHTML=`<p class="small">Choose footage and exact source times for each SRT block. Nothing is selected automatically. Leave a source blank and explain the gap if footage is not ready. Save the map, then rebuild the storyboard; saving resets existing approvals.</p>
 <div class="inline"><button type="button" onclick="mapStep(-1)" ${d.index===0?'disabled':''}>← Previous</button><label>Block <select onchange="mapGo(this.value)">${d.rows.map((row,i)=>`<option value="${i}" ${i===d.index?'selected':''}>${E(row.block??row.srt_blocks?.[0]??i+1)}${mapValue(row,'media_file')?'':' · gap'}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><button type="button" onclick="mapStep(1)" ${d.index===d.rows.length-1?'disabled':''}>Next →</button></div>
 <div class="notice"><strong>${E(block?.start_ms!=null?mapClock(block.start_ms):r.start)} → ${E(block?.end_ms!=null?mapClock(block.end_ms):r.end)}</strong><p>${E(block?.text||'Narration not found for this block; review the map against the SRT.')}</p></div>
 <form id="map-row-form" onsubmit="event.preventDefault();saveBlockMap()"><div class="two">${field('Parent beat number','beat','1')}<label>Source filename<input data-map-field="media_file" list="map-source-files" value="${E(mapValue(r,'media_file'))}" placeholder="Select or type original filename"></label>${field('Source IN','source_in','00:00:00.000')}${field('Source OUT (exclusive)','source_out','00:00:04.000')}${field('Status (optional)','status','VIDEO, STILL or CTX')}${field('Footage group (optional)','footage_group','Same label for the same shot')}</div>${field('Picture description (optional)','visible_content','What the picture should show')}<label>Gap note (required if no source)<textarea data-map-field="gap_note" rows="2">${E(mapValue(r,'gap_note'))}</textarea></label><datalist id="map-source-files">${P.assets.map(a=>`<option value="${E(a.original_name||a.name)}"></option>`).join('')}</datalist><p class="small">Use HH:MM:SS.mmm for source times. OUT must follow IN; a moving interval must fit the block duration. Existing extra map instructions are preserved.</p><div id="map-save-error" class="notice warn" role="alert" hidden></div><div class="dialog-actions"><button type="button" onclick="closeBlockMapEditor()">Cancel</button><button class="new" type="submit">Save block map</button></div></form>`;
 $('#map-row-form').addEventListener('input',e=>{
  const key=e.target.dataset.mapField;if(!key)return;
  r['_edit_'+key]=e.target.value;d.changed=true;dirty=true;
 });
 // Show in-memory changes when returning to a row.
 $('#map-row-form').querySelectorAll('[data-map-field]').forEach(el=>{const k=el.dataset.mapField;if(Object.hasOwn(r,'_edit_'+k))el.value=r['_edit_'+k]});
}
function mapGo(i){mapDraft.index=Number(i);renderMapEditor()}
function mapStep(delta){mapGo(mapDraft.index+delta)}
function closeBlockMapEditor(){if(mapDraft?.changed&&!confirm('Discard unsaved block-map edits?'))return;mapDraft=null;dirty=false;$('#map-editor-dialog').close()}
$('#map-editor-dialog').addEventListener('cancel',e=>{e.preventDefault();closeBlockMapEditor()});
function mapSaveRows(rows){
 return rows.map(original=>{
  const r={...original},value=k=>r['_edit_'+k]??mapValue(r,k);
  const file=value('media_file').trim(),inside=value('source_in').trim(),out=value('source_out').trim(),beat=value('beat').trim(),status=value('status').trim();
  const description=value('visible_content'),group=value('footage_group'),gap=value('gap_note');
  // Normalized imports contain aliases and derived millisecond values. Remove them
  // before re-parsing edited clocks, otherwise the old values conflict with the edits.
  for(const key of Object.keys(r))if(key.startsWith('_edit_'))delete r[key];
  for(const key of ['beat_id','scene','local_file','media_filename','media','file','source','source_file_name','media_file_name','in','out','source_in_time','source_out_time','source_in_ms','source_out_ms','source_out_excl','source_in_ms_planned','source_out_ms_excl_planned'])delete r[key];
  r.beat=beat;r.media_file=file;r.source_file=file;r.source_in=inside;r.source_out=out;
  r.status=status;r.visual_status=status;r.visible_content=description;if('required_visual' in r)r.required_visual=description;r.footage_group=group;r.gap_note=gap;
  return r;
 });
}
async function saveBlockMap(){
 const d=mapDraft;if(!d||!P||P.id!==d.id)return;
 if(P.scenes.length&&!confirm('Saving the map resets storyboard approvals. Rebuild to apply the changes. Continue?'))return;
 const form=$('#map-row-form'),button=form.querySelector('button[type=submit]'),error=$('#map-save-error');button.disabled=true;error.hidden=true;
 try{
  const data=new FormData();data.append('kind','srt_block_map');data.append('expected_srt_signature',d.signature);
  data.append('file',new Blob([JSON.stringify(mapSaveRows(d.rows))],{type:'application/json'}),'authored-block-map.json');
  const result=await api(`/api/projects/${d.id}/upload`,'POST',data);
  if(P?.id===d.id)P=result;
  mapDraft=null;dirty=false;$('#map-editor-dialog').close();renderProject();toast('Block map saved. Rebuild the storyboard to apply it.');
 }catch(e){error.textContent=e.message;error.hidden=false;button.disabled=false}
}

let understandingTarget='',understandingTimer=null;
function understandingSources(){return [...(P?.voiceover?[['narration','Narration · '+P.voiceover.name]]:[]),...(P?.assets||[]).filter(a=>a.kind==='video').map(a=>[a.id,'Video · '+a.name])];}
async function openUnderstanding(){
 if(!P||!leave())return;
 understandingTarget=understandingSources()[0]?.[0]||'';
 $('#understanding-dialog').showModal();await refreshUnderstanding();
}
function closeUnderstanding(){clearTimeout(understandingTimer);$('#understanding-dialog').close()}
$('#understanding-dialog').addEventListener('close',()=>clearTimeout(understandingTimer));
async function refreshUnderstanding(){
 clearTimeout(understandingTimer);if(!P||!understandingTarget)return;
 const id=P.id,target=understandingTarget,body=$('#understanding-body');
 try{
  const [cap,result]=await Promise.all([api('/api/local-understanding/capabilities'),api(`/api/projects/${id}/understanding/${target}`)]);
  if(!$('#understanding-dialog').open||!P||id!==P.id||target!==understandingTarget)return;
  const stages=result.stages||{};
  const stage=(key,label,render)=>{const x=stages[key];if(!x)return '';return `<section class="card"><h3>${E(label)} · ${E(x.status)}</h3>${x.detail?`<p class="small">${E(x.detail)}</p>`:''}${x.result&&['complete','partial','running'].includes(x.status)?render(x.result):''}</section>`};
  body.innerHTML=`<p class="small">Local processing only. AI descriptions and timestamps are estimates, not verification. Analysis does not edit your captions or storyboard unless you explicitly apply the narration transcript.</p>
   <label>Media <select id="understanding-source" onchange="understandingTarget=this.value;refreshUnderstanding()">${understandingSources().map(([k,v])=>`<option value="${E(k)}" ${k===target?'selected':''}>${E(v)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>
   <p class="small">Whisper: ${cap.whisper_model_ready&&cap.faster_whisper?'local model available':'local model or package missing'} · PySceneDetect: ${cap.scenedetect?'installed':'missing'} · Librosa: ${cap.librosa?'installed':'missing'} · vision: ${E(cap.vision_model)} on local Ollama (availability checked when run) · sound events: ${cap.sound_classifier?'local model configured':'optional model not configured'} · diarization: ${cap.speaker_diarization?'local pipeline configured':'optional pipeline not configured'}.</p>
   <p>Status: <strong>${E(result.status)}</strong> ${result.detail?E(result.detail):''}</p>${result.timing_warning?`<p class="small">${E(result.timing_warning)}</p>`:''}<button type="button" onclick="runUnderstanding()" ${['queued','running'].includes(result.status)?'disabled':''}>${result.status==='not_started'?'Analyse locally':'Run analysis again'}</button>
   ${result.status!=='not_started'?`<a class="button" href="/api/projects/${E(id)}/understanding/${E(target)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open JSON report</a>`:''}
   ${cap.sound_classifier&&['complete','partial'].includes(result.status)?'<button type="button" onclick="runSoundEvents()">Analyse sound events only</button>':''}
   ${target!=='narration'&&['complete','partial'].includes(result.status)&&stages.scene_changes?.status==='complete'?'<button type="button" onclick="runVisualOnly()">Analyse video frames only</button>':''}
   ${stage('transcript','Transcript',r=>`<p class="small">Language: ${E(r.language)} · Review transcription before use; downloading does not change the project SRT.</p>${r.segments?.length?`<a class="button" href="/api/projects/${E(id)}/understanding/${E(target)}/transcript.srt" download>Download ${stages.transcript.status==='complete'?'':'partial '}SRT</a>`:''}${target==='narration'&&stages.transcript.status==='complete'&&r.segments?.length?'<button type="button" onclick="applyNarrationTranscript()">Use as project SRT</button>':''}${(r.segments||[]).map(x=>`<p><strong>${Number(x.start_s).toFixed(2)}–${Number(x.end_s).toFixed(2)}s</strong> ${x.speaker?`<span>${E(x.speaker)} · </span>`:''}${E(x.text)}</p>`).join('')||'<p>No speech detected.</p>'}`)}
   ${stage('scene_changes','Scene changes',r=>`<p>${r.length} detected spans</p><p class="small">${r.map(x=>`${Number(x.start_s).toFixed(2)}–${Number(x.end_s).toFixed(2)}s`).join(' · ')}</p>`)}
   ${stage('visual','Visual observations',r=>`<p class="small">${E(r.note)} ${r.sampled_frames} frames across ${r.total_scenes} detected scenes.</p>${r.observations.map(x=>`<p><strong>Frame ${Number(x.frame_time_s).toFixed(2)}s</strong> ${E(x.description)}</p>`).join('')}`)}
   ${stage('audio_features','Audio signal',r=>`<p class="small">${E(r.note)}</p>${(r.low_energy_intervals||[]).length?`<p>Low-energy intervals: ${r.low_energy_intervals.map(x=>`${Number(x.start_s).toFixed(1)}–${Number(x.end_s).toFixed(1)}s`).join(' · ')}</p>`:''}${r.windows.map(x=>`<p>${Number(x.start_s).toFixed(1)}–${Number(x.end_s).toFixed(1)}s · RMS ${x.rms} · centroid ${x.spectral_centroid_hz} Hz${x.low_energy?' · low energy':''}</p>`).join('')}`)}
   ${stage('sound_events','Sound events & music',r=>`<p class="small">${E(r.note)}</p>${r.windows.map(x=>`<p><strong>${Number(x.start_s).toFixed(2)}–${Number(x.end_s).toFixed(2)}s</strong> ${x.predictions.map(p=>`${E(p.label)} (${Number(p.score).toFixed(2)})`).join(' · ')||(x.too_short?'Too short to classify':'No strong prediction')}</p>`).join('')}`)}
   ${stage('speakers','Speakers',r=>`<p class="small">${E(r.note)}</p>${r.turns.map(x=>`<p>${Number(x.start_s).toFixed(2)}–${Number(x.end_s).toFixed(2)}s · ${E(x.speaker)}</p>`).join('')}`)}`;
  if(['queued','running'].includes(result.status))understandingTimer=setTimeout(refreshUnderstanding,3000);
 }catch(e){body.textContent='Could not load local analysis: '+e.message}
}
async function runUnderstanding(){
 const id=P.id,target=understandingTarget;
 try{await api(`/api/projects/${id}/understanding/${target}`,'POST');await refreshUnderstanding()}catch(e){toast(e.message)}
}

async function applyNarrationTranscript(){
 if(!P||understandingTarget!=='narration'||uploading)return;
 const id=P.id, replacing=!!(P.srt||P.scenes.length);
 const warning=replacing
  ? 'This machine transcript is unverified. Applying it replaces the existing SRT and clears the storyboard and its approvals. A ready automatic build may start. Continue?'
  : 'This machine transcript is unverified. Use it as the project SRT? A ready automatic build may start. Review the text and timing before export.';
 if(!confirm(warning))return;
 uploading=true;
 try{
  const updated=await api(`/api/projects/${id}/understanding/narration/apply-srt`,'POST',{confirm_generated:true,confirm_replace:replacing});
  if(P?.id!==id)return;
  P=updated;clearTimeout(understandingTimer);$('#understanding-dialog').close();renderProject();await list();
  toast('Machine transcript applied as SRT. Review text and timing before export.');
 }catch(e){toast(e.message)}finally{uploading=false}
}

async function runSoundEvents(){
 if(!P||!understandingTarget)return;
 const id=P.id,target=understandingTarget;
 try{await api(`/api/projects/${id}/understanding/${target}/sound-events`,'POST');await refreshUnderstanding()}catch(e){toast(e.message)}
}

async function runVisualOnly(){
 if(!P||!understandingTarget||understandingTarget==='narration')return;
 const id=P.id,target=understandingTarget;
 try{await api(`/api/projects/${id}/understanding/${target}/visual`,'POST');await refreshUnderstanding()}catch(e){toast(e.message)}
}

const $=s=>document.querySelector(s),E=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));let projects=[],P=null,tab='brief',sceneIndex=0,dirty=false,uploading=false,PROFILES={};

async function api(url,method='GET',data){const opts={method};if(data instanceof FormData)opts.body=data;else if(data!==undefined){opts.headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'};opts.body=JSON.stringify(data)}const r=await fetch(url,opts);let d;try{d=await r.json()}catch{throw Error('The server response could not be read. Please retry.')}if(!r.ok)throw Error(d.error||d.errors?.slice(0,3).join(' ')||'The request could not finish.');return d}
function toast(s){$('#toast').textContent=s;$('#toast').classList.add('show');clearTimeout(window.toastTimer);window.toastTimer=setTimeout(()=>$('#toast').classList.remove('show'),5000)}
window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',e=>{e.preventDefault();toast(e.reason?.message||'The operation could not finish.')});
function leave(){if(uploading){toast('Please wait for the import to finish.');return false}if(dirty&&!confirm('You have unsaved changes. Leave without saving?'))return false;dirty=false;return true}

function runtime(frames){const total=Math.round((frames||0)/30);const m=Math.floor(total/60),sec=total%60;return m+':'+String(sec).padStart(2,'0')}

function scriptReadingHTML(){
 const r=P?.script_reading;if(!r)return '';
 if(r.status==='error')return `<div class="notice warn"><strong>Production script needs attention</strong><p>${E(r.detail)}</p></div>`;
 if(r.status!=='read')return '';
 const missing=r.missing_files.length?`Missing or ambiguous files: ${r.missing_files.map(E).join(', ')}.`:'All referenced source filenames are uploaded.';
 const policy=P.picture_policy==='clips_only'&&r.still_shots+r.freeze_shots?'This project is set to moving footage only, but the script requests stills and freeze-frames.':'';
 return `<div class="notice warn"><strong>Script read: ${r.blocks} SRT blocks · ${r.shots} shots</strong><p>${E(missing)} ${r.video_shots} video shots, ${r.photo_shots||0} uploaded-photo shots, ${r.still_shots} total still holds and ${r.freeze_shots} freeze-frames.</p><p>${E(policy)} ${r.native_static_ms?`Script-declared native still intervals total ${(r.native_static_ms/1000).toFixed(3)} seconds (${(r.native_static_ms/r.duration_ms*100).toFixed(2)}% of the script timeline); other stills add to that budget. Eligibility, visual identity and rights remain unverified.`:''} ${r.photo_ms?`Named photos total ${(r.photo_ms/1000).toFixed(3)} seconds and count toward the 10% still limit, alongside native static intervals. Identity, eligibility and rights require review.`:''} ${r.caption_shots} shots have editorial captions; the current renderer cannot yet place them as instructed. ${r.narration_mismatch_blocks.length} SRT blocks have wording differences from the approved script. No files have been changed.</p><p>These requirements need attention before this script can produce an accurate storyboard or export. Filename matches and decoded frames do not verify content, source version or rights.</p>${!P.scenes.length?'<button type="button" onclick="createScriptDraft()">Show shots in review timeline</button> ':''}<button type="button" onclick="showScriptReading()">Read the full shot plan and differences</button><div id="script-reading-detail"></div></div>`;
}

 }catch(e){box.textContent=e.message}
}
function uploadFeedback(message,error=false){let node=$('#upload-feedback');if(!node&&$('#content')){node=document.createElement('div');node.id='upload-feedback';node.setAttribute('role','status');node.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');$('#content').prepend(node)}if(node){node.hidden=false;node.className='notice'+(error?' warn':'');node.innerHTML=`<span>${E(message)}</span>${window.activeImport?`<p>${window.activeImport.hasNext?'<button type="button" onclick="skipCurrentImport()">Next file (skip this one)</button> ':''}<button type="button" onclick="cancelCurrentImport()">Cancel remaining import</button></p>`:window.pendingImportDecision?'<p><button type="button" onclick="continueImportBatch()">Next file</button> <button type="button" onclick="stopImportBatch()">Stop batch</button></p>':''}`}if(!window.activeImport||error)toast(message)}
function automaticStatusHTML(){
 if(!P)return '';
 if(P.script_moving_only)return `<div class="notice warn"><strong>Script review · incomplete</strong><p>${P.script_application?.placed_scenes||0} shots are available in the review timeline. Missing, too-short, overlapping or unsupported still/frame shots remain unassigned; declared native static intervals can play from the original video for review and count toward the still budget; see the shot details for the reason. Named uploaded photos are held static; no frames, loops or guessed video ranges are used. Playback pauses at unresolved shots; captions and rights require review and export is blocked.</p></div>`;
 if(P.review_only_draft)return `<div class="notice warn"><strong>Script review timeline · not a finished edit</strong><p>Shots and source frames are shown for inspection. Editorial caption layout, framing and exact freeze frames still need implementation and review; export is blocked. Your original script, names, narration and SRT are unchanged.</p></div>`;
 if(!P.scenes.length&&P.script_reading?.status==='read'&&P.script_reading.blocker_count)return '';
 const s=P.automatic_status||{},r=P.render||{},ready=P.automatic_readiness||{},working=['queued','working','studying','storyboard_ready','rendering'].includes(s.status);
 const unmatched=(P.source_match_report||[]).filter(x=>!['matched','selected'].includes(x.status));
 if(!P.scenes.length&&s.status==='blocked'&&unmatched.length)return `<div class="notice warn"><strong>Storyboard is blocked by unmatched script filenames.</strong><p>${unmatched.map(x=>E(x.source_file)).join(', ')} need the correct uploaded footage. Your SRT and uploads are already present.</p><p><button type="button" onclick="changeTab('assets')">Resolve script filenames</button></p></div>`;
 // Never sit silently idle: if assembly will not run, say why and what to do about it.
 if(ready.status&&['blocked','needs_plan','manual','paused'].includes(ready.status)&&!P.scenes.length){
  const action=ready.status==='needs_plan'?'<p><button type="button" onclick="downloadMapTemplate()">Download the block-map template</button> <button type="button" onclick="chooseFile(\'srt_block_map\')">Upload a block map</button> <button type="button" onclick="chooseFile(\'production_script\')">Upload a production script</button></p>'
   :ready.status==='blocked'?`<p class="small">Still needed: ${E((ready.missing||[]).join(', ')||'see the message above')}</p>`
   :ready.status==='manual'?'<p><button type="button" onclick="buildStoryboard()">Rebuild deliberately</button></p>'
   :'';
  return `<div class="notice warn"><strong>Automatic assembly is not running — ${E({blocked:'inputs are missing',needs_plan:'there is no plan yet',manual:'manual edits take precedence',paused:'it is switched off for this production'}[ready.status])}.</strong><p>${E(ready.detail||s.detail||'')}</p>${action}</div>`;
 }
 if(P.scenes.length){
  const preview=P.preview||{},previewInfo=preview.status==='done'?'<p>Temporary smooth preview is ready. Pause playback to switch to it.</p>':['running','queued'].includes(preview.status)?`<p>Preparing smooth preview: ${Number(preview.progress||0).toFixed(0)}% · ${E(preview.detail||'')}</p>`:`<p><button type="button" onclick="prepareSmoothPreview()">Prepare smooth preview</button> ${preview.status==='error'?E(preview.detail):'Creates a temporary low-resolution playback copy without changing your edit.'}</p>`;
  const errors=P.validation?.errors||[];
  if(['running','queued'].includes(r.status))return `<div class="notice"><strong>${P.scenes.length} picture slots built · continuous master rendering.</strong><p>The Program monitor can use the rendered master when it is ready.</p></div>`;
  if(r.status==='done'&&r.current)return `<div class="notice"><strong>Continuous master ready.</strong><p>The Program monitor can play the rendered MP4.</p></div>`;
  if(errors.length)return `<div class="notice warn"><strong>Continuous master not rendered · ${errors.length} issue(s) need attention.</strong><p>The Program monitor is using source preview, which can pause while loading footage.</p>${errors.slice(0,6).map(x=>`<p class="small">${E(x)}</p>`).join('')}${previewInfo}</div>`;
  return `<div class="notice"><strong>${P.scenes.length} picture slots built · no current continuous master.</strong><p>${E(s.status==='error'?s.detail:'Use Review & export to start a render. Until then the Program monitor uses source preview.')}</p>${previewInfo}</div>`;
 }
 if(s.status==='error'||s.status==='interrupted')return `<div class="notice error"><strong>Automatic assembly stopped.</strong><p>${E(s.detail||'Review the uploaded files and build the storyboard again.')}</p></div>`;
 if(working)return `<div class="notice"><strong>Working automatically — please allow processing time.</strong><p>${E(s.detail||'Studying the complete source videos before assembly.')}</p><p class="small">The timeline will appear here when the background job finishes. You can leave this page open.</p></div>`;
 if(P.inputs_ready)return `<div class="notice"><strong>All required inputs are present.</strong><p>Use <em>Build storyboard →</em> below, or wait for automatic assembly. The system studies every uploaded MP4 before placing clean cuts.</p></div>`;
 return `<div class="notice"><strong>Import the production script, original narration, SRT and MP4 footage.</strong><p>After those inputs are present, the storyboard is assembled automatically. An SRT supplies timing and text, not pictures.</p></div>`;
}
function readableBytes(bytes){return bytes>=1024**3?(bytes/1024**3).toFixed(2)+' GB':bytes>=1024**2?(bytes/1024**2).toFixed(1)+' MB':(bytes/1024).toFixed(1)+' KB'}
function abortActiveImport(intent){const active=window.activeImport;if(!active||active.intent)return;active.intent=intent;uploadFeedback(intent==='skip'?'Skipping this file and stopping its transfer…':'Stopping the import…');fetch(`/api/projects/${active.id}/imports/${active.key}`,{method:'DELETE',signal:AbortSignal.timeout(4000)}).catch(()=>{}).finally(()=>active.xhr.abort())}
function cancelCurrentImport(){abortActiveImport('cancel')}
function skipCurrentImport(){abortActiveImport('skip')}
function continueImportBatch(){const resolve=window.pendingImportDecision;window.pendingImportDecision=null;if(resolve)resolve(true)}
function stopImportBatch(){const resolve=window.pendingImportDecision;window.pendingImportDecision=null;if(resolve)resolve(false)}
function uploadWithProgress(id,kind,file,index,count){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
 const streaming=kind==='asset'&&/\.(mp4|mov)$/i.test(file.name);if(streaming&&file.size>8*1024**3){reject(Error('This video exceeds the 8 GB per-file limit.'));return}
 const xhr=new XMLHttpRequest(),key=Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16)),b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join(''),started=performance.now();let timer,busyPoll=false,finished=false,transferDone=false;
 const prefix=`File ${index} of ${count} · ${file.name} · `,elapsed=()=>Math.round((performance.now()-started)/1000)+'s elapsed';
 window.activeImport={xhr,id,key,hasNext:kind==='asset'&&index<count,intent:null};
 const finish=(error,data)=>{if(finished)return;finished=true;clearInterval(timer);if(window.activeImport?.key===key)window.activeImport=null;error?reject(error):resolve(data)};
 xhr.open('POST',streaming?`/api/projects/${id}/media-stream?name=${encodeURIComponent(file.name)}`:`/api/projects/${id}/upload`);xhr.setRequestHeader('X-Upload-ID',key);if(streaming)xhr.setRequestHeader('Content-Type','application/octet-stream');xhr.timeout=(streaming&&file.size>100*1024**2?60:10)*60*1000;
 xhr.upload.onprogress=e=>{if(!finished)uploadFeedback(prefix+'Sending '+readableBytes(e.loaded)+' / '+readableBytes(e.total||file.size)+(e.lengthComputable?' ('+Math.floor(e.loaded/e.total*100)+'%)':'')+' · '+elapsed())};
 xhr.upload.onload=()=>{transferDone=true;if(!finished)uploadFeedback(prefix+'Sent '+readableBytes(file.size)+'. Waiting for the local server · '+elapsed())};
 xhr.onload=()=>{let data;try{data=JSON.parse(xhr.responseText)}catch{return finish(Error('The local server returned an unreadable response (HTTP '+xhr.status+'). Check its terminal window.'))}if(xhr.status>=200&&xhr.status<300)finish(null,data);else finish(Error(data.error||'Import failed (HTTP '+xhr.status+').'))};
 xhr.onerror=()=>finish(Error('Connection to the local editor was interrupted. Check that its server is still running. Check the library before retrying this file.'));
 xhr.onabort=()=>{const intent=window.activeImport?.key===key?window.activeImport.intent:null;const error=Error(intent==='skip'?'Skipped by request.':'Import stopped.');error.importAction=intent||'cancel';finish(error)};
 xhr.ontimeout=()=>{fetch(`/api/projects/${id}/imports/${key}`,{method:'DELETE'}).catch(()=>{});finish(Error('Import exceeded the '+(xhr.timeout/60000)+'-minute request limit. Check the local server terminal and media library before retrying.'))};
 const labels={waiting:'Waiting for the local server',waiting_for_project:'Waiting for another project operation',receiving:'Receiving / parsing uploaded data',saving:'Saving the file on disk',checking_metadata:'Checking audio/video metadata (15-second check limit)',checking_image:'Checking the image',reading_document:'Reading document instructions',indexing:'Updating the project library',done:'Import saved; receiving project response',error:'Server reported an import error'};
 timer=setInterval(async()=>{if(finished||busyPoll)return;busyPoll=true;try{const r=await fetch(`/api/projects/${id}/imports/${key}`,{signal:AbortSignal.timeout(4000)});if(r.ok){const state=await r.json();if(!finished&&(transferDone||!['waiting','waiting_for_project','receiving'].includes(state.stage)))uploadFeedback(prefix+(labels[state.stage]||state.stage)+(state.stage==='saving'&&state.saved_bytes?' · '+readableBytes(state.saved_bytes)+' written':'')+(state.detail?' — '+state.detail:'')+' · '+elapsed())}}catch{}finally{busyPoll=false}},1000);
 let data=file;if(!streaming){data=new FormData();data.append('file',file);data.append('kind',kind);data.append('replace','yes')}uploadFeedback(prefix+(streaming?'Streaming video directly · ':'Starting transfer · ')+readableBytes(file.size));xhr.send(data);
})}

async function importSelectedFiles(kind,selected){
 const files=Array.from(selected||[]);if(!files.length)return;if(!P){toast('Create or open a production first.');return}if(uploading){toast('Please wait for the current upload.');return}if(!leave())return;if(kind==='srt'&&P.scenes.length&&!confirm('Replacing SRT clears the current storyboard. Continue?'))return;
 const id=P.id;uploading=true;const completed=[],skipped=[],failures=[],replaced=[],warnings=[];let stopped=false;
 try{
  for(const [index,file] of files.entries()){
   try{
    const updated=await uploadWithProgress(id,kind,file,index+1,files.length);completed.push(file.name);
    if(updated.replaced_video){replaced.push(updated.replaced_video);warnings.push(...(updated.replacement_warnings||[]))}
    if(P?.id===id){if(updated.replaced_video){pauseEditor();clearPreparedSource();sourceScene='';sourceReady=false}P=updated;renderProject()}
   }catch(e){
    if(e.importAction==='cancel'){stopped=true;break}
    skipped.push(file.name);
    if(e.importAction==='skip')continue;
    failures.push(file.name+': '+(e.message||'Unknown error.'));
    if(kind==='asset'&&index+1<files.length){
     const proceed=await new Promise(resolve=>{window.pendingImportDecision=resolve;uploadFeedback('Could not import '+file.name+': '+(e.message||'Unknown error.')+' Choose Next file to continue with the remaining uploads. Check the library before retrying this file.',true)});
     if(proceed)continue;
    }
    stopped=true;break;
   }
  }
  if(P?.id===id){try{P=await api('/api/projects/'+id);renderProject()}catch{renderProject()}}
  await list();
  let message=completed.length?'Imported: '+completed.join(', ')+'.':'No files confirmed imported in this batch.';
  if(skipped.length)message+=' Skipped or failed: '+skipped.join(', ')+'. Check the library before retrying; an upload already being saved may still appear.';
  if(failures.length)message+=' Import errors: '+failures.join('; ')+'.';
  if(stopped)message+=' Remaining files were not sent.';
  if(replaced.length)message+=' Replaced existing footage: '+replaced.map(r=>r.name+(r.unassigned_scenes?' ('+r.unassigned_scenes+' shots need attention)':'')).join(', ')+'. Affected shots need review.';
  if(warnings.length)message+=' '+warnings.join(' ');
  uploadFeedback(message,!!(skipped.length||stopped||warnings.length));
 }catch(e){uploadFeedback('Could not finish the import batch: '+(e.message||'Unknown error.')+'. Check the library before retrying.',true)}finally{window.pendingImportDecision=null;uploading=false}
}

const POLICY_LABEL={clips_only:'Moving footage only',mixed:'Moving footage and photographs'};
const LAYOUT_LABEL={blurred_fit:'Fitted over a blurred copy of itself',full_bleed:'Cropped to fill the frame'};

Scene approvals will reset and you will need to rebuild so the new policy applies.':'Restrict this production to moving footage only? Scene approvals will reset and you will need to rebuild. Photographs already placed will be rejected.';if(!confirm(warn))return;try{P=await api('/api/projects/'+P.id,'PATCH',{picture_policy:value});renderProject();toast('Picture type changed to '+POLICY_LABEL[value]+'. Rebuild the storyboard to apply it.')}catch(e){toast(e.message)}}

async function setLayout(value){if(!P||!leave())return;try{P=await api('/api/projects/'+P.id,'PATCH',{image_layout:value});renderProject();toast('Photograph layout set to '+LAYOUT_LABEL[value].toLowerCase()+'. Render again to apply it.')}catch(e){toast(e.message)}}
async function chooseFile(kind){if(!P||!leave())return;const picker=$('#file-picker');picker.value='';picker.multiple=kind==='asset';picker.removeAttribute('accept');picker.onchange=()=>importSelectedFiles(kind,picker.files);picker.click()}

async function buildStoryboard(){
 if(window.buildingStoryboard||!leave()||!P)return;
 if(!$('#manage-dialog').open)manage('brief');
 if(P.scenes.length&&(!P.review_only_draft||P.manual_scene_edits)){updateBuildProgress(P.id,'Existing edits preserved','This timeline contains work that cannot be automatically replaced. Review it before rebuilding.',{done:true});return}
 if(P.scenes.length&&!confirm('Rebuild the existing review timeline from the currently uploaded script? The current review placements will be replaced. Uploaded script, SRT, narration and footage will be kept.'))return;
 const id=P.id,key=Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16)),b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
 window.buildingStoryboard=true;let timer;updateBuildProgress(id,'Building storyboard','Reading the production script and SRT…',{running:true});
 const buttons=[...document.querySelectorAll('button[onclick="buildStoryboard()"]')];buttons.forEach(b=>b.disabled=true);
 try{
  timer=setInterval(async()=>{try{const r=await fetch(`/api/projects/${id}/imports/${key}`);if(!r.ok)return;const state=await r.json();if(P?.id===id&&window.buildingStoryboard&&state.detail)updateBuildProgress(id,'Building storyboard',state.detail,{running:true})}catch{}},500);
  const response=await fetch(`/api/projects/${id}/script-draft`,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','X-Upload-ID':key},body:JSON.stringify({moving_only:true,replace_review:!!P.scenes.length})});
  let result;try{result=await response.json()}catch{throw Error('The server response could not be read. Check the project before retrying.')}
  if(!response.ok)throw Error(result.error||'The script could not be read.');
  if(P?.id!==id)return;P=result;sceneIndex=0;const placed=P.script_application?.placed_scenes||0,total=P.scenes.length,issues=(P.script_application?.issues||[]).length;
  updateBuildProgress(id,'Review timeline ready',`${placed} of ${total} cue shots assigned; ${issues} unresolved. Source content, photos, captions, still limits and rights still need review. No missing footage was substituted.`,{done:true});
  renderProject();await list();
 }catch(e){if(P?.id===id)updateBuildProgress(id,'Storyboard not built',e.message||'The build could not finish. Your uploads were kept.',{error:true})}
 finally{clearInterval(timer);window.buildingStoryboard=false;buttons.forEach(b=>{if(b.isConnected)b.disabled=false})}
}
const options=(items,v)=>items.map(([k,t])=>`<option value="${E(k)}" ${k===v?'selected':''}>${E(t)}</option>`).join('');
function media(a,controls=true){if(!a)return '<div class="media"><span class="muted">No visual assigned</span></div>';return `<div class="media">${a.kind==='image'?`<img src="${a.url}" alt="${E(a.name)}">`:`<video ${controls?'controls':''} preload="none" src="${a.url}${a.document_plan?.cuts?.length?'#t='+(a.document_plan.cuts[0].source_in_ms/1000):''}"></video>`}</div>`}
function assetsHTML(){return `<div class="section-title"><div><h2>Imported footage</h2><p class="small">Approve all listed sources together or review them individually. Your decision is not independent identity or copyright verification.</p></div><button class="new" onclick="chooseFile('asset')">＋ Import media</button></div>${footageRequirementsHTML()}${sourceMatchingHTML()}${P.assets.length?`${bulkReuseRightsHTML()}<div class="asset-grid">${P.assets.map(a=>`<div class="card asset-card">${media(a)}<h3>${E(a.name)}</h3><p class="small">${a.verified?'Media-use flag set; rights decision is separate':a.approval_record?.active===false?'Approval revoked':'Not auto-approved'}${a.reserve?' · '+E(a.source_label)+' · '+(assetIsUsed(a)?'Explicitly placed':'Held in reserve'):''}</p>${a.kind==='image'&&!a.derived_from?photoDetailsHTML(a):''}${reuseRightsHTML(a)}${sourceBindingHTML(a)}${plannedMediaHTML(a)}<button type="button" onclick="removeAsset('${E(a.id)}')">Remove from project</button></div>`).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="large-empty"><h2>Import your footage.</h2><p>Original filenames are retained for script matching. Record a reuse-rights decision for each file.</p><button type="button" onclick="chooseFile(&quot;asset&quot;)">Import media</button></div>'}`}
function photoDetailsHTML(a){
 return `<details class="reuse-rights"><summary>Photo identity and source</summary><p class="small">Identify the pictured person or document yourself. Changing the source resets the recorded reuse decision; this does not independently verify identity or rights.</p><form onsubmit="saveAsset(event,'${E(a.id)}')"><label>Person or people shown (comma separated)<input name="people" value="${E((a.people||[]).join(', '))}" placeholder="Exact names, if known"></label><label>Subject or relationship<input name="category" value="${E(a.category||'general')}" placeholder="Victim, suspect, document, relative, or other"></label><label>Photo source or provenance<input name="source" value="${E(a.source||'')}" placeholder="Where this photo came from"></label><label>Identity and context notes<textarea name="notes" rows="2">${E(a.notes||'')}</textarea></label><label class="check-label"><input type="checkbox" name="verified" ${a.verified?'checked':''}> I have checked the identity and context described above.</label><button type="submit">Save photo details</button></form></details>`;
}
function bulkReuseRightsHTML(){
 return `<section class="card reuse-rights bulk-reuse-rights"><h3>Approve reuse for all uploaded sources</h3><p class="small">The same decision and details will replace any previous rights decisions for all ${P.assets.length} files listed below. Only approve if the details apply to every file. New or replaced files need a new review. This is your attestation, not independent copyright clearance.</p><details><summary>Files included (${P.assets.length})</summary><ul>${P.assets.map(a=>`<li>${E(a.original_name||a.name)}</li>`).join('')}</ul></details><form onsubmit="saveAllReuseRights(event)">${P.assets.map(a=>`<input type="hidden" name="asset_id" value="${E(a.id)}">`).join('')}<label>Basis for approval<select name="basis" required>${options([['','Select a basis'],['permission','Permission'],['license','License'],['public_domain','Public domain'],['other','Other reviewed basis']],'')}</select></label><label>Evidence, intended use and restrictions for all files<textarea name="details" rows="4" maxlength="5000" required placeholder="Describe the rights holders, permission or licenses, intended use and limits for the listed files"></textarea></label><label class="check-label"><input type="checkbox" name="attested" required> I confirm I have reviewed the listed files and approve reuse for each under the details above.</label><button type="submit">Approve reuse for all ${P.assets.length} files</button></form></section>`;
}
async function saveAllReuseRights(e){
 e.preventDefault();const form=e.currentTarget,f=new FormData(form),button=form.querySelector('button[type=submit]'),ids=f.getAll('asset_id');
 if(!f.get('basis')||!String(f.get('details')||'').trim()||!f.has('attested')){toast('Choose a basis, provide supporting details and confirm the decision for every file.');return}
 button.disabled=true;try{P=await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/assets/reuse-rights`,'PUT',{asset_ids:ids,basis:f.get('basis'),details:f.get('details'),attested:f.has('attested')});dirty=false;renderProject();toast(`Reuse decision saved for ${ids.length} files. This does not independently verify rights or approve scenes.`)}catch(err){toast(err.message)}finally{if(button.isConnected)button.disabled=false}
}
function reuseRightsHTML(a){
 const r=a.reuse_review||{},decision=r.decision||'unreviewed',label={unreviewed:'Not yet reviewed',approved:'Approved by you',not_approved:'Not approved'};
 return `<details class="reuse-rights"><summary>Reuse rights · ${E(label[decision]||'Not yet reviewed')}</summary><p class="small">Record your own decision for this specific file and intended use. Replacing the file requires a new decision.</p><form onsubmit="saveReuseRights(event,'${E(a.id)}')"><label>Decision<select name="decision">${options([['unreviewed','Not yet reviewed'],['approved','I approve reuse for the stated use'],['not_approved','Do not approve reuse']],decision)}</select></label><label>Basis for approval<select name="basis">${options([['','Select a basis'],['permission','Permission'],['license','License'],['public_domain','Public domain'],['other','Other reviewed basis']],r.basis||'')}</select></label><label>Evidence, intended use and restrictions<textarea name="details" rows="3" maxlength="5000" placeholder="Record the rights holder, license or permission, intended use and limits">${E(r.details||'')}</textarea></label><label class="check-label"><input type="checkbox" name="attested"> I confirm this decision applies to this file and intended use.</label><button type="submit">Save reuse decision</button></form></details>`;
}
async function saveReuseRights(e,id){

 if(decision==='approved'&&(!f.get('basis')||!String(f.get('details')||'').trim()||!f.has('attested'))){toast('Choose a basis, describe the rights and intended use, and confirm your decision.');return}
 button.disabled=true;try{P=await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/assets/${id}/reuse-rights`,'PUT',{decision,basis:f.get('basis'),details:f.get('details'),attested:f.has('attested')});dirty=false;renderProject();toast('Reuse decision saved for this file. This does not verify rights or approve its scenes.')}catch(err){toast(err.message)}finally{if(button.isConnected)button.disabled=false}
}
async function saveAsset(e,id){e.preventDefault();const f=new FormData(e.target);const d=Object.fromEntries(f);d.people=d.people.split(',').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);d.verified=f.has('verified');P=await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/assets/${id}`,'PATCH',d);dirty=false;renderProject();toast('Asset review saved. Affected scene approvals have been reset.')}
async function saveScene(e){e.preventDefault();const f=new FormData(e.target),d=Object.fromEntries(f);d.asset_id=d.asset_id||null;d.in_s=Number(d.in_s);d.context_only=f.has('context_only');d.approved=f.has('approved');P=await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/scenes/${P.scenes[sceneIndex].id}`,'PATCH',d);dirty=false;renderProject();await list();toast('Scene saved. Export checks updated.')}
async function splitBeat(){if(!leave())return;const s=P.scenes[sceneIndex],dur=(s.frame_out-s.frame_in)/30;const v=prompt(`Split how many seconds after this scene begins? Assign the second visual and review both beats. Original audio and SRT are unchanged.')}

Editorial approval has been reset.')}
function deliveryHTML(){const v=P.validation,f=P.final_validation,r=P.render||{},running=['running','queued'].includes(r.status);return `<div class="two"><section class="card"><h3>Export readiness</h3><p class="small">Checks cover media assignment, user verification, named-person matching, unique source intervals, transition handles and narration timing.</p>${v.errors.length?`<div class="notice warn">${v.errors.length} review-export items need attention.</div><ul class="issues">${v.errors.slice(0,30).map(x=>`<li>${E(x)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>`:'<div class="notice">Technical and source checks passed for review export.</div>'}${v.notes.map(x=>`<p class="small">${E(x)}</p>`).join('')}<div class="actionbar"><button type="button" onclick="approveReadyScenes()">Approve all ready beats</button><button ${!v.can_export||running?'disabled':''} onclick="startExport(false)">Render review</button><button class="new" ${!f.can_export||running?'disabled':''} onclick="startExport(true)">Render final MP4</button></div><p class="small">${P.automatic_timeline&&!P.manual_scene_edits?'This prepared timeline has passed its source and timing checks. No per-slot assignments or approval clicks are needed.':'Final delivery additionally requires each scene’s approval and a documented reuse decision for every selected asset.'}</p>${f.errors.filter(x=>/approval|rights/.test(x)).slice(0,6).map(x=>`<p class="small">${E(x)}</p>`).join('')}</section><section class="card"><div class="eyebrow">CLEAN DELIVERY</div><h2>${E(r.stage||'Ready when you are')}</h2><p>No burned-in captions. Continuous original narration. Separate SRT and a saved review record.</p>${running?`<progress max="100" value="${r.progress||0}"></progress><p class="small">${Math.round(r.progress||0)}% · ${E(r.detail||'')}</p><button onclick="cancelExport()">Cancel export</button>`:''}${r.status==='error'?'<div class="notice error">Export could not finish. The saved production remains intact.</div><details><summary>Technical details</summary><pre style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-word;font-size:10px">'+E(r.error)+'</pre></details>':''}${r.url?`<video controls class="reference-video" src="${r.url}"></video><a class="download" href="${r.url}?download=1">↓ Clean MP4</a> <a class="download" href="${r.url.replace(/\.mp4$/,'.srt')}?download=1">↓ Separate SRT</a>`:''}<div class="notice">Review snapshots and media provenance remain in this production’s folder. Footage is never pulled in from another production automatically.</div></section></div>`}
async function startExport(final){P.render=(await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/render`,'POST',{final})).render;renderProject();toast((final?'Final':'Review')+' export started. You can leave this tab; keep the server running.')}
async function cancelExport(){if(!confirm('Stop this export?'))return;await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/cancel`,'POST',{});P=await api('/api/projects/'+P.id);renderProject()}
document.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.target.closest('#brief-form,#scene-form,.asset-card,.bulk-reuse-rights'))dirty=true});window.addEventListener('beforeunload',e=>{if(dirty||uploading){e.preventDefault();e.returnValue=''}});
// The familiar timeline editor owns navigation and startup (editor.js).

async function approveAllMedia(){if(!P||!leave())return;if(!confirm('Approve all '+P.assets.length+' files based on your declaration that you have reviewed their content and may reuse them? This is your attestation, not independent copyright or identity clearance. Scene approvals remain separate.'))return;try{P=await api('/api/projects/'+P.id+'/assets/approve-all','POST',{attested:true});renderProject();toast('All uploaded files marked approved by your attestation. async function setAutoMediaApproval(enabled){if(!P||!leave()){renderProject();return}if(enabled&&!confirm('Automatically approve future local media uploads in THIS project under your review/reuse declaration? This does not verify identities, grant copyright clearance or approve scenes.')){renderProject();return}try{P=await api('/api/projects/'+P.id+'/assets/approval-policy','PATCH',{auto_upload:enabled,attested:enabled});renderProject();toast(enabled?'Automatic approval enabled for future local media uploads in this project.':'Automatic approval disabled. Existing reviews were not changed.')}catch(e){renderProject();toast(e.message)}}

function plannedMediaHTML(a){const plan=a.document_plan;if(a.derived_from)return `<div class="notice">Extracted from ${E(a.derived_from.source_file)} at ${(a.derived_from.requested_time_ms/1000).toFixed(3)}s. Preview frame only; identity/content still need review.</div>`;if(!plan||(!plan.cuts.length&&!plan.register.length))return '';return `<div class="notice"><strong>${E(plan.source_ids.join(' · '))}</strong> · ${plan.cuts.length} planned cuts${plan.youtube_urls.map((url,i)=>`<p><a href="${E(url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">${E(plan.source_ids[i]||'Declared source')} — YouTube link</a></p>`).join('')}<br>${E(plan.register.map(r=>r.publisher+' — '+r.planned_content).join(' / '))}<p class="small">Declared by your documents; not visually verified by software.</p></div><details><summary>Inspect planned cuts (${plan.cuts.length})</summary>${plan.cuts.map(c=>`<div class="planned-cut"><b>Beat ${E(c.beat_id)} · ${(c.source_in_ms/1000).toFixed(3)}–${(c.source_out_ms/1000).toFixed(3)}s</b><p>${E(c.visible_content)}</p>${a.kind==='video'?`<button type="button" onclick="previewPlannedCut(this,${c.source_in_ms},${c.source_out_ms})">Play this source interval</button><img style="width:100%;margin-top:10px" loading="lazy" src="/api/projects/${P.id}/assets/${a.id}/planned-samples/${encodeURIComponent(c.beat_id)}" alt="IN, middle and final frame; visual match needs review">`:''}<p class="small">${E(c.review||'Picture match not independently verified.')}</p></div>`).join('')}</details>`}
function previewPlannedCut(button,inside,outside){const video=button.closest('.asset-card').querySelector('video');if(!video)return;document.querySelectorAll('#manage-dialog video').forEach(v=>{if(v!==video)v.pause()});video.currentTime=inside/1000;video.ontimeupdate=()=>{if(video.currentTime>=outside/1000){video.pause();video.ontimeupdate=null}};video.play().catch(e=>toast(e.message))}

function assetIsUsed(a){return (P?.scenes||[]).some(s=>s.asset_id===a.id||(P?.assets||[]).some(child=>child.id===s.asset_id&&child.derived_from?.asset_id===a.id))}

function flowHTML(){const f=P?.picture_flow,fp=f?.picture,fnn=f?.narration,s=P?.flow_summary?.picture?.counts||{},n=P?.flow_summary?.cut_flow||{},review=P?.flow_review||[];
if(!f)return '';
const counts=[['Untouched',s.already_inside_one_shot||0],['Slid inside a shot',s.slid_inside_a_shot||0],['Far slide · review',s.slid_far_inside_a_shot||0],['Continued a take',s.continued_running_take||0],['Jump cut',s.jump_cut_within_shot||0],['No single shot fits',s.no_single_shot_fits||0]].filter(x=>x[1]);
return `<div class="card"><h3>Narration & footage flow</h3><p>Every picture beat is checked against the decoded shots of its own source, and every cut against the decoded pauses in the uploaded narration. Requested source moments are bent onto continuous footage; the narration, SRT wording and total duration never move.</p>
<div class="inline">${counts.map(([label,value])=>`<span class="badge">${E(label)} · ${value}</span>`).join('')}</div>
${n.cuts?`<p><strong>${n.in_a_breath_after||0} of ${n.cuts} cuts</strong> now land in a breath (was ${n.in_a_breath_before||0}). ${n.moved||0} cuts moved by 0.35s or less to reach a breath; ${n.over_speech_after||0} cut on top of a spoken word.</p>`:''}
${fnn&&fnn.decoded===false?`<p class="small">${E(fnn.reason||'Narration silences could not be decoded.')}</p>`:''}
${review.length?`<details open><summary>${review.length} beat${review.length===1?'':'s'} need a human look</summary>${review.map(r=>`<p><strong>Scene ${r.scene} · ${E(r.asset)}</strong> — ${E(r.note||'')}<br><span class="small">Declared ${r.declared_in_s}s → ${r.conformed_in_s}s${r.shot?` · shot ${r.shot[0]}-${r.shot[1]}s`:''}${r.suggestions?.length?' · free footage of this length: '+r.suggestions.map(x=>E(x.asset)+' @'+x.start_s+'s').join(', '):''}</span></p>`).join('')}</details>`:'<p class="small">No beat needed a far slide. Every declared moment that could stay put did.</p>'}
<p class="small">A shot is a detected change of picture in your own upload. These checks say nothing about who or what a frame shows. <a class="download-link" href="/api/projects/${P.id}/flow-audit?download=1">↓ Flow audit CSV</a></p></div>${flowHTML()}</div>`}

function sourceMatchingHTML(){
 const rows=P.source_match_report||[];if(!rows.length)return '';
 const gaps=rows.filter(r=>!['matched','selected'].includes(r.status));
 const uploads=P.assets.filter(a=>a.kind==='video'&&!a.derived_from&&!a.reserve);
 return `<div class="card"><h3>Resolve script filenames</h3>${gaps.length?'<p>Choose the correct uploaded video for each unmatched script filename, or upload the missing file. Do not guess based on similar names.</p>':'<p>All planned filenames have one selected upload. Build the storyboard to apply them.</p>'}
 ${gaps.map(r=>`<label><strong>${E(r.source_file)}</strong> · ${r.beats} beats <select data-source-file="${E(r.source_file)}" onchange="linkMissingSource(this)"><option value="">Choose the correct upload…</option>${uploads.map(a=>`<option value="${E(a.id)}">${E(a.original_name||a.name)} · ${Number(a.duration||0).toFixed(1)}s</option>`).join('')}</select></label>`).join('')}
 ${!gaps.length?'<p><button type="button" onclick="buildStoryboard()">Build storyboard →</button></p>':''}
 <p class="small">The link preserves the original filename and does not verify video content or delete uploads. Once all names are resolved, build the storyboard.</p></div>`
}
async function linkMissingSource(select){
 const source=select.dataset.sourceFile,id=select.value;if(!id||!P)return;
 const asset=P.assets.find(a=>a.id===id);if(!asset)return;
 if(!confirm(`Use ${asset.original_name||asset.name} for ${source} in the production script?`)){select.value='';return}
 try{P=await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/source-bindings`,'POST',{source_file:source,asset_id:id});renderProject();toast('Source linked. Resolve remaining names, then build the storyboard.')}catch(e){select.value='';toast(e.message)}
}
function sourceBindingHTML(a){if(a.kind!=='video'||a.derived_from)return '';const linked=Object.entries(P.source_bindings||{}).filter(([name,id])=>id===a.id).map(([name])=>name);return `<div class="notice"><p class="small">Upload copy: ${E(a.id.slice(0,8))} · ${Number(a.duration||0).toFixed(3)}s${linked.length?' · Selected for '+E(linked.join(', ')):''}</p><button type="button" onclick="bindScriptSource('${E(a.id)}')">Use this upload for script matching</button></div>`}
async function bindScriptSource(id){if(!leave())return;const a=P.assets.find(a=>a.id===id);if(!a)return;const name=prompt('Which script filename should use this uploaded copy? Example: S01.mp4. Original upload: '+a.original_name,a.original_name||a.name);if(!name?.trim())return;try{P=await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/source-bindings`,'POST',{source_file:name.trim(),asset_id:id});renderProject();toast('Source linked. Rebuild the storyboard to apply it. Original uploads were not deleted.')}catch(e){toast(e.message)}}

async function removeSRTBlockMap(){if(!leave()||!confirm('Remove the block map? Rebuild afterwards to return to the production script.'))return;P=await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/srt-block-map`,'DELETE');renderProject();toast('Block map removed. Rebuild to apply the underlying picture plan.')}

async function removeAsset(id){
 if(!P||!leave())return;
 const asset=P.assets.find(a=>a.id===id);if(!asset)return;
 const derived=P.assets.filter(a=>a.derived_from?.asset_id===id),ids=new Set([id,...derived.map(a=>a.id)]);
 const affected=P.scenes.filter(s=>ids.has(s.asset_id)).length;
 const impact=(affected?` ${affected} storyboard scene(s) will become unassigned and need review.`:'')+(derived.length?` ${derived.length} extracted frame(s) will also be removed.`:'');
 if(!confirm(`Remove ${asset.original_name||asset.name} from this project? The project copy and its local analysis report will be deleted.${impact} Your original computer file and existing exports are not deleted.`))return;
 try{P=await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/assets/${id}`,'DELETE',{confirm:true,confirm_used:true});renderProject();await list();toast(P.removal_warnings?.length?P.removal_warnings.join(' '):'File removed from this project.')}catch(e){toast(e.message)}
}
async function removeInput(kind){
 if(!P||!leave())return;
 const label={voiceover:'narration',srt:'SRT',production_script:'production script'}[kind];if(!label)return;
 const impact=P.scenes.length?' Existing scene approvals will be reset; review or rebuild the storyboard.':'';
 const srtWarning=kind==='srt'&&P.scenes.length?' Export will be blocked until an SRT is imported and the storyboard is rebuilt.':'';
 if(!confirm(`Remove the ${label} from this project? The project copy${kind==='voiceover'?' and its local analysis report':''} will be deleted.${impact}${srtWarning} Your original computer file and existing exports are not deleted.`))return;
 try{P=await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/inputs/${kind}`,'DELETE',{confirm:true,confirm_used:true});renderProject();await list();toast(P.removal_warnings?.length?P.removal_warnings.join(' '):'File removed from this project.')}catch(e){toast(e.message)}
}

function sceneExportHTML(){
 const job=P.scene_exports||{},running=['queued','running'].includes(job.status),render=P.render||{},preview=P.preview||{};
 const masterReady=render.status==='done'&&render.current,previewReady=preview.status==='done';
 return `<div class="card"><h3>Save separate scene clips</h3><p class="small">One MP4 per timeline scene, named C01.mp4, C02.mp4, C03.mp4 and so on, in a ZIP. Each clip includes its corresponding portion of narration when present. Your source files are unchanged.</p>${running?`<progress max="100" value="${Number(job.progress||0)}"></progress><p>${E(job.detail||'Preparing scenes')}</p>`:`<div class="inline"><button type="button" ${!masterReady?'disabled':''} onclick="startSceneExports('render')">Create clips from rendered master</button><button type="button" ${!previewReady?'disabled':''} onclick="startSceneExports('preview')">Create clips from smooth preview</button></div>`}<p class="small">The preview option uses the temporary low-resolution preview and does not bypass final export checks. The master option requires a current rendered MP4.</p>${job.status==='error'?`<p class="small">Could not create clips: ${E(job.detail)}</p>`:''}${job.status==='done'&&job.url?`<a class="download" href="${E(job.url)}">↓ Download ${job.naming==='C'?'C-numbered':'previous S-numbered'} clips ZIP (${job.source_kind==='preview'?'low-resolution preview':'rendered master'})</a>`:''}</div>`;
}
async function startSceneExports(source){
 if(!P||!leave())return;
 if(source==='preview'&&!confirm('Create separate clips from the low-resolution smooth preview? These are for review, not a final approved export.'))return;
 try{await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}/scene-exports`,'POST',{source});P=await api(`/api/projects/${P.id}`);renderProject();toast('Preparing separate scene clips.')}catch(e){toast(e.message)}
}

==================== FILE: tests/test_block_map_editor.py ====================
import io
import json
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import app as server

SRT = b'1\n00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:04,000\nFirst line\n\n2\n00:00:04,000 --> 00:00:08,000\nSecond line\n'

class BlockMapEditorTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.original_projects = server.PROJECTS
        server.PROJECTS = Path(self.tmp.name)
        self.client = server.app.test_client()
        self.pid = self.client.post('/api/projects', json={'title': 'Map test'}).get_json()['id']
        self.upload('srt', SRT, 'narration.srt')

    def tearDown(self):
        server.PROJECTS = self.original_projects
        self.tmp.cleanup()

    def upload(self, kind, payload, filename, **extra):
        return self.client.post(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/upload', data={
            'kind': kind, 'file': (io.BytesIO(payload), filename), **extra,
        }, content_type='multipart/form-data')

    def editor(self):
        return self.client.get(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/block-map-editor')

    def test_prefill_save_reopen_and_metadata(self):
        response = self.editor()
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        data = response.get_json()
        self.assertEqual(len(data['rows']), 2)
        rows = data['rows']
        rows[0].update(media_file='source.mp4', source_in='00:00:01.000',
                       source_out='00:00:05.000', visible_content='A street',
                       footage_group='street', custom_instruction='preserved')
        result = self.upload('srt_block_map', json.dumps(rows).encode(), 'authored.json',
                             expected_srt_signature=data['srt_signature'])
        self.assertEqual(result.status_code, 200, result.get_json())
        saved = self.editor().get_json()['rows']
        self.assertEqual(saved[0]['source_in_ms'], 1000)
        self.assertEqual(saved[0]['footage_group'], 'street')
        self.assertEqual(saved[0]['custom_instruction'], 'preserved')
        self.assertEqual(saved[1]['source_file'], '')
        self.assertTrue(saved[1]['gap_note'])

    @unittest.skipUnless(shutil.which('node'), 'Node is needed to exercise browser serialization')
    def test_edit_imported_row_round_trip(self):
        data = self.editor().get_json()
        rows = data['rows']
        rows[0].update(media_file='old.mp4', source_in='00:00:01.000', source_out='00:00:05.000', required_visual='Old picture', custom_instruction='keep me')
        self.assertEqual(self.upload('srt_block_map', json.dumps(rows).encode(), 'map.json').status_code, 200)
        saved = self.editor().get_json()['rows']
        saved[0].update(_edit_media_file='new.mp4', _edit_source_in='00:00:09.000', _edit_source_out='00:00:13.000', _edit_beat='2', _edit_visible_content='New picture')
        js = (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'static/editor.js').read_text()
        helpers = js[js.index('function mapValue('):js.index('function renderMapEditor(')]
        helpers += js[js.index('function mapSaveRows('):js.index('async function saveBlockMap(')]
        code = helpers + "\nprocess.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(mapSaveRows(JSON.parse(process.argv[1]))));"
        payload = subprocess.check_output(['node', '-e', code, json.dumps(saved)])
        response = self.upload('srt_block_map', payload, 'authored.json', expected_srt_signature=data['srt_signature'])
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.get_json())
        row = self.editor().get_json()['rows'][0]
        self.assertEqual((row['source_file'], row['source_in_ms'], row['source_out_ms'], row['parent_beat_id']), ('new.mp4', 9000, 13000, '2'))
        self.assertEqual(row['custom_instruction'], 'keep me')
        self.assertEqual(row['visible_content'], 'New picture')

    def test_invalid_row_does_not_replace_map(self):
        data = self.editor().get_json()
        rows = data['rows']
        rows[0].update(media_file='source.mp4', source_in='00:00:05.000', source_out='00:00:01.000')
        result = self.upload('srt_block_map', json.dumps(rows).encode(), 'authored.json',
                             expected_srt_signature=data['srt_signature'])
        self.assertEqual(result.status_code, 400)
        self.assertIn('OUT', result.get_json()['error'])
        self.assertFalse(self.editor().get_json()['existing'])

    def test_changed_srt_rejects_draft_and_stale_map(self):
        data = self.editor().get_json()
        payload = json.dumps(data['rows']).encode()
        self.assertEqual(self.upload('srt_block_map', payload, 'authored.json', expected_srt_signature=data['srt_signature']).status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.upload('srt', SRT.replace(b'First line', b'New wording'), 'new.srt').status_code, 200)
        response = self.upload('srt_block_map', payload, 'authored.json', expected_srt_signature=data['srt_signature'])
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
        self.assertIn('SRT changed', response.get_json()['error'])
        self.assertEqual(self.editor().status_code, 400)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

==================== END FILE: tests/test_block_map_editor.py ====================


==================== FILE: tests/test_continuous_preview.py ====================
import tempfile,time,unittest,subprocess
import cv2, imageio_ffmpeg
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
from PIL import Image
import app as server
import preview_render

class ContinuousPreviewTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp=tempfile.TemporaryDirectory();self.patch=patch.object(server,'PROJECTS',Path(self.tmp.name));self.patch.start()
        self.c=server.app.test_client();self.pid=self.c.post('/api/projects',json={'title':'Preview test'}).get_json()['id'];self.root=Path(self.tmp.name)/self.pid
        Image.new('RGB',(200,120),'red').save(self.root/'assets'/'still.jpg')
        p=server.load(self.pid);p['assets']=[{'id':'a','name':'still.jpg','path':'assets/still.jpg','kind':'image','duration':0,'source':'','people':[],'category':'general','verified':False,'rights':'unreviewed','notes':''}]
        p['scenes']=[{'id':'b'*16,'frame_in':0,'frame_out':45,'asset_id':'a','approved':False,'emotion':'calm','purpose':'context','focus':'object','in_s':0,'transition':'cut','person':'','notes':'','context_only':False}];server.save(p)
    def tearDown(self):
        preview_render.JOBS.pop(self.pid,None);self.patch.stop();self.tmp.cleanup()
    def test_preview_is_temporary_and_does_not_bypass_export_validation(self):
        p=server.load(self.pid);self.assertFalse(server.check(p)['can_export'])
        self.assertEqual(self.c.post(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/continuous-preview',json={}).status_code,202)
        for _ in range(200):
            state=preview_render.status(self.root,self.pid,p['updated'])
            if state['status'] not in ('queued','running'):break
            time.sleep(.05)
        self.assertEqual(state['status'],'done',state)
        r=self.c.get(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}').get_json();self.assertEqual(r['preview']['status'],'done');self.assertFalse(r['validation']['can_export'])
        response=self.c.get(r['preview']['url']);self.assertEqual(response.status_code,200);response.close()
        p=server.load(self.pid);p['title']='Changed';server.save(p)
        self.assertEqual(self.c.get(r['preview']['url']).status_code,404)
        self.assertEqual(self.c.get(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}').get_json()['preview']['status'],'stale')
        self.assertEqual(len(server.load(self.pid)['scenes']),1)

    def test_video_and_narration_preview_keeps_frame_count(self):
        ff=imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe();source=self.root/'sources'/'motion.mp4';voice=self.root/'inputs'/'voiceover.wav'
        subprocess.run([ff,'-y','-v','error','-f','lavfi','-i','testsrc2=size=160x90:rate=30:duration=4','-c:v','libx264','-pix_fmt','yuv420p',str(source)],check=True)
        subprocess.run([ff,'-y','-v','error','-f','lavfi','-i','sine=frequency=440:duration=3',str(voice)],check=True)
        p=server.load(self.pid);p['assets']=[{'id':'v','name':'motion.mp4','path':'sources/motion.mp4','kind':'video','duration':4,'source':'','people':[],'category':'general','verified':False,'rights':'unreviewed','notes':''}];p['voiceover']={'name':'voiceover.wav','path':'inputs/voiceover.wav','duration':3}
        first=p['scenes'][0];first.update(frame_out=45,asset_id='v',in_s=0.5);second=dict(first,id='c'*16,frame_in=45,frame_out=90,in_s=2.0);p['scenes']=[first,second];server.save(p)
        self.assertEqual(self.c.post(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/continuous-preview',json={}).status_code,202)
        for _ in range(200):
            state=preview_render.status(self.root,self.pid,p['updated'])
            if state['status'] not in ('queued','running'):break
            time.sleep(.05)
        self.assertEqual(state['status'],'done',state)
        output=self.root/'.cache/continuous_preview/preview.mp4';cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(output));self.assertEqual(int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)),90);cap.release()
        info=subprocess.run([ff,'-hide_banner','-i',str(output)],capture_output=True,text=True).stderr;self.assertIn('Audio:',info)

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()
