==================== FILE: tests/test_cue_script.py ====================
import unittest
from cue_script import parse_cue_script

BLOCKS=[{'number':1,'start_ms':0,'end_ms':2000,'text':'First words'}, {'number':2,'start_ms':2000,'end_ms':4000,'text':'Second words'}]
SCRIPT='''# Example
## Selected asset register
| Asset | Full source | Source IN → exclusive OUT | Type | Identification / context |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| C001 | S01.mp4 | 00:00:01,000 → 00:00:03,000 | VIDEO | context |
| C002 | S02.mp4 | 00:00:05,000 → 00:00:07,000 | STILL | photograph |
## Srt001
Original SRT cue **1** · Original time **00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,000**

**Exact narration**

First words

**Picture placement** 00:00:00,000 → 00:00:02,000 · 2.000 s

**VIDEO · C001 · S01.mp4** · Source IN 00:00:01,000 · OUT 00:00:03,000 exclusive · 2.000 s · 1.0× · loop OFF

**Picture.** Context.

**Editorial caption.** Caption.

**Direction.** Enter on boundary.

## Srt002
Original SRT cue **2** · Original time **00:00:02,000 --> 00:00:04,000**

**Exact narration**

Second words

**Picture placement** 00:00:02,000 → 00:00:04,000 · 2.000 s

**STILL · C002 · S02.mp4** · Source IN 00:00:05,000 · OUT 00:00:07,000 exclusive · 2.000 s · 1.0× · loop OFF

**Picture.** Photograph.

**Editorial caption.** Caption.

**Direction.** Enter on boundary.
'''
class CueScriptTest(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_reads_exact_video_and_native_still_interval(self):
  rows=parse_cue_script(SCRIPT,BLOCKS)
  self.assertEqual([(r['shot_id'],r['source_file'],r['source_in_ms'],r['source_out_ms']) for r in rows],[('Srt001','S01.mp4',1000,3000),('Srt002','S02.mp4',5000,7000)])
  self.assertEqual(rows[1]['requested_treatment'],'STILL HOLD')
 def test_rejects_register_clock_or_order_changes(self):
  for text in [SCRIPT.replace('OUT 00:00:03,000 exclusive','OUT 00:00:03,100 exclusive'),SCRIPT.replace('C001 · S01.mp4','C001 · S03.mp4'),SCRIPT.replace('## Srt002','## Srt003')]:
   with self.assertRaises(ValueError):parse_cue_script(text,BLOCKS)

class SourceOnlyCueTest(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_source_filename_and_times_need_no_clip_label_or_register(self):
  text=SCRIPT[ SCRIPT.index('## Srt001'): ].replace('C001 · ','').replace('C002 · ','')
  rows=parse_cue_script(text,BLOCKS)
  self.assertEqual([r['source_file'] for r in rows],['S01.mp4','S02.mp4'])
  self.assertEqual([r['clip_id'] for r in rows],['',''])
  self.assertEqual((rows[0]['source_in_ms'],rows[0]['source_out_ms']),(1000,3000))
 def test_source_only_still_cannot_hide_a_bad_duration(self):
  text=SCRIPT[SCRIPT.index('## Srt001'):].replace('C001 · ','').replace('C002 · ','').replace('OUT 00:00:03,000 exclusive','OUT 00:00:03,100 exclusive')
  with self.assertRaises(ValueError):parse_cue_script(text,BLOCKS)

class OneCueInstructionTest(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_duplicate_video_instruction_rejected(self):
  bad=SCRIPT.replace('**VIDEO · C001 · S01.mp4**', '**VIDEO · C001 · S01.mp4**\n**VIDEO · C001 · S01.mp4**')
  with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'exactly one shot'):parse_cue_script(bad,BLOCKS)

==================== END FILE: tests/test_cue_script.py ====================


==================== FILE: tests/test_delete_project.py ====================
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import app as server
import automatic
import local_understanding
import preview_render
import scene_exports


class DeleteProjectTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.base = Path(self.temp.name)
        self.project_dir = self.base / 'projects'; self.project_dir.mkdir()
        self.patcher = patch.object(server, 'PROJECTS', self.project_dir); self.patcher.start()
        self.client = server.app.test_client()
        self.pid = self.client.post('/api/projects', json={'title': 'Delete me'}).get_json()['id']
        self.other = self.client.post('/api/projects', json={'title': 'Keep me'}).get_json()['id']
        self.root = self.project_dir / self.pid
        for name in ('sources/upload.mp4', 'assets/image.png', 'inputs/voiceover.wav', 'inputs/production_script.txt', 'renders/final.mp4', 'reviews/report.json', '.cache/preview/work.tmp'):
            path = self.root / name; path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True); path.write_bytes(b'project content')
        (self.project_dir / self.other / 'sources' / 'other.mp4').write_bytes(b'keep')
        self.external = self.base / 'outside'; self.external.mkdir(); (self.external / 'original.mp4').write_bytes(b'external original')
        (self.root / 'external-link').symlink_to(self.external, target_is_directory=True)
    def tearDown(self):
        server.JOBS.pop(self.pid, None);preview_render.JOBS.pop(self.pid, None);scene_exports.JOBS.pop(self.pid, None)
        with automatic._QUEUE_LOCK:automatic._PENDING.discard(self.pid)
        with local_understanding._GUARD:local_understanding._RUNNING.discard((str(self.root),'asset'))
        self.patcher.stop();self.temp.cleanup()
    def delete(self, data=None):return self.client.delete(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}', json=data)
    def test_confirm_then_delete_only_this_project_and_files(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.delete({}).status_code,400);self.assertTrue(self.root.exists())
        self.assertEqual(self.delete({'confirm':'true'}).status_code,400);self.assertTrue(self.root.exists())
        response=self.delete({'confirm':True});self.assertEqual(response.status_code,200);self.assertTrue(response.get_json()['deleted'])
        self.assertFalse(self.root.exists());self.assertTrue((self.external/'original.mp4').exists())
        self.assertTrue((self.project_dir/self.other/'sources/other.mp4').exists())
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}').status_code,404)
        self.assertEqual([p['id'] for p in self.client.get('/api/projects').get_json()['projects']],[self.other])
    def test_background_work_blocks_deletion(self):
        class Job:
            def snapshot(self):return {'status':'running'}
        server.JOBS[self.pid]=Job();self.assertEqual(self.delete({'confirm':True}).status_code,400);server.JOBS.pop(self.pid)
        with automatic._QUEUE_LOCK:automatic._PENDING.add(self.pid)
        self.assertEqual(self.delete({'confirm':True}).status_code,400)
        with automatic._QUEUE_LOCK:automatic._PENDING.discard(self.pid)
        for module in (preview_render,scene_exports):
            with module.GUARD:module.JOBS[self.pid]={'status':'running'}
            self.assertEqual(self.delete({'confirm':True}).status_code,400)
            with module.GUARD:module.JOBS.pop(self.pid)
        with local_understanding._GUARD:local_understanding._RUNNING.add((str(self.root),'asset'))
        self.assertEqual(self.delete({'confirm':True}).status_code,400)
        self.assertTrue(self.root.exists())
    def test_invalid_id_and_failure_are_not_reported_as_success(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete('/api/projects/not-an-id',json={'confirm':True}).status_code,404)
        with patch('app.shutil.rmtree',side_effect=OSError('locked')):
            result=self.delete({'confirm':True})
        self.assertEqual(result.status_code,400);self.assertIn('some project files may remain',result.get_json()['error']);self.assertTrue(self.root.exists())

if __name__ == '__main__':unittest.main()

==================== END FILE: tests/test_delete_project.py ====================


==================== FILE: tests/test_detailed_script.py ====================
import copy
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import imageio_ffmpeg
import app as server
from detailed_script import parse_detailed
from document_plan import rows_for
from edl import assemble

HEADER = '| Shot | Clip | Source file | Narration timeline IN → OUT | Source IN → OUT | Treatment | Visual description | Restrictions |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n'
BLOCKS = [{'number': 1, 'start_ms': 0, 'end_ms': 4000, 'text': 'One'}, {'number': 2, 'start_ms': 4000, 'end_ms': 8000, 'text': 'Two'}]
def table(rows):
    return HEADER + ''.join('| ' + ' | '.join(row) + ' |\n' for row in rows)
def row(shot, clip, source, timeline, interval, treatment='VIDEO', description='Street view', restrictions=''):
    return [shot, clip, source, timeline, interval, treatment, description, restrictions]
def valid_rows():
    return [row('Srt01-A','C12','S01.mp4','00:00.000 → 00:03.000','00:01.000 → 00:04.000'),
            row('Srt01-B','C02','S01.mp4','00:03.000 → 00:05.000','00:04.000 → 00:06.000', 'CONTEXT VIDEO'),
            row('Srt02-A','C10','S03.mp4','00:05.000 → 00:08.000','00:02.000 → 00:05.000')]

class DetailedScriptTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def parse(self,rows):return parse_detailed(table(rows),BLOCKS)
    def test_two_clocks_clip_order_and_cross_block(self):
        result=self.parse(valid_rows())
        self.assertEqual([r['clip_id'] for r in result],['C12','C02','C10'])
        self.assertEqual([r['source_file'] for r in result],['S01.mp4','S01.mp4','S03.mp4'])
        self.assertEqual(result[1]['srt_blocks'],[1,2])
        self.assertEqual((result[1]['timeline_in_ms'],result[1]['timeline_out_ms'],result[1]['source_in_ms'],result[1]['source_out_ms']),(3000,5000,4000,6000))
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'exactly one shot'):rows_for({'production_script':{'text':table(valid_rows())},'srt':{'blocks':BLOCKS}})
    def test_gap_overlap_bad_clocks_or_unrecognised_shape_fail_closed(self):
        for clock in ['00:03.100 → 00:05.000','00:02.900 → 00:05.000']:
            rows=valid_rows();rows[1][3]=clock
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):self.parse(rows)
        rows=valid_rows();rows[0][4]='00:01.000 → 00:03.000'
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):self.parse(rows)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):parse_detailed('Srt01-A C01 S01.mp4',BLOCKS)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):parse_detailed(table(valid_rows())+'\nhttps://youtube.com/watch?v=foo',BLOCKS)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):parse_detailed(table(valid_rows())+'\nUse this note to alter the edit.',BLOCKS)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):rows_for({'production_script':{'text':table(valid_rows())},'srt':{'blocks':BLOCKS},'srt_block_map':{'text':'something'}})
    def test_stills_and_unsupported_instructions(self):
        rows=valid_rows();rows[0][5]='EDITORIAL FREEZE-FRAME';rows[0][4]='00:45.000'
        r=self.parse(rows)[0];self.assertEqual(r['requested_treatment'],'EDITORIAL FREEZE-FRAME');self.assertEqual(r['source_in_ms'],45000)
        rows[0][5]='STILL HOLD';self.assertEqual(self.parse(rows)[0]['requested_treatment'],'STILL HOLD')
        for restriction in ['Add a caption', 'Use source audio', 'Slow motion']:
            rows=valid_rows();rows[0][7]=restriction
            self.assertTrue(self.parse(rows)[0]['import_blockers'],restriction)
        rows=valid_rows();rows[0][5]='FADE TO BLACK'
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):self.parse(rows)
    def test_missing_source_not_rebound_and_explicit_reuse(self):
        rows=valid_rows();assets=[{'id':'a','name':'S01.mp4','original_name':'S01.mp4','kind':'video','duration':10}]
        p={'assets':assets,'scenes':[],'allow_archive_reuse':False,'source_bindings':{'S03.mp4':'a'}}
        p['srt']={'blocks':BLOCKS};p['picture_plan']={'beats':self.parse(rows)};assemble(p,[{"srt_blocks":[1]},{"srt_blocks":[2]}]);self.assertIsNone(p['scenes'][2]['asset_id'])
        rows[2]=row('Srt02-A','C10','S01.mp4','00:05.000 → 00:08.000','00:01.000 → 00:04.000','ARCHIVAL REUSE')
        p['scenes']=[];p['srt']={'blocks':BLOCKS};p['picture_plan']={'beats':self.parse(rows)};assemble(p,[{"srt_blocks":[1]},{"srt_blocks":[2]}]);self.assertEqual(p['scenes'][2]['asset_id'],'a')
        rows[2][5]='VIDEO';p['scenes']=[];p['srt']={'blocks':BLOCKS};p['picture_plan']={'beats':self.parse(rows)};assemble(p,[{"srt_blocks":[1]},{"srt_blocks":[2]}]);self.assertIsNone(p['scenes'][2]['asset_id'])

def single_rows():
    return [row('Srt01','C12','S01.mp4','00:00.000 → 00:04.000','00:01.000 → 00:05.000'), row('Srt02','C10','S03.mp4','00:04.000 → 00:08.000','00:02.000 → 00:06.000')]

class RealBuildTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_build_uses_authored_cuts_and_rejects_unsupported(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, patch.object(server,'PROJECTS',Path(tmp)), patch('automatic.full_source_analysis',return_value={}):
            c=server.app.test_client();pid=c.post('/api/projects',json={'title':'Synthetic'}).get_json()['id'];root=Path(tmp)/pid
            p=server.load(pid);p['srt']={'blocks':copy.deepcopy(BLOCKS)};p['production_script']={'text':table(single_rows())};p['allow_archive_reuse']=True
            ff=imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()
            for name in ['S01.mp4','S03.mp4']:
                subprocess.run([ff,'-y','-v','error','-f','lavfi','-i','testsrc2=size=160x90:rate=30:duration=10','-c:v','libx264','-pix_fmt','yuv420p',str(root/name)],check=True)
                p['assets'].append({'id':name,'name':name,'original_name':name,'kind':'video','duration':10,'path':name,'verified':False,'source':'','context_only':False,'rights':'unreviewed','notes':''})
            server.save(p)
            with patch('automatic.native_rows',side_effect=AssertionError('native conform called')), patch('picture_flow.apply_flow',side_effect=AssertionError('flow called')):
                result=c.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/storyboard',json={})
            self.assertEqual(result.status_code,200,result.get_data(as_text=True))
            saved=server.load(pid);self.assertEqual([(s['frame_in'],s['frame_out'],s['in_s']) for s in saved['scenes']],[(0,120,1),(120,240,2)])
            self.assertFalse(saved['allow_archive_reuse']);self.assertEqual([s['plan_row']['clip_id'] for s in saved['scenes']],['C12','C10'])
            self.assertTrue(all(not s['approved'] for s in saved['scenes']))
            rows=single_rows();rows[0][7]='Required caption: Location';saved['production_script']['text']=table(rows);server.save(saved)
            failed=c.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/storyboard',json={'replace':True});self.assertEqual(failed.status_code,400);self.assertIn('caption',failed.get_data(as_text=True).lower())
            rows=single_rows();rows[0][5]='EDITORIAL FREEZE-FRAME';rows[0][4]='00:02.000'
            saved=server.load(pid);saved['production_script']['text']=table(rows);saved['picture_policy']='mixed';server.save(saved)
            frozen=c.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/storyboard',json={'replace':True});self.assertEqual(frozen.status_code,200,frozen.get_data(as_text=True))
            scene=server.load(pid)['scenes'][0];self.assertEqual(scene['plan_row']['extracted_frame_ms'],2000);self.assertEqual(scene['plan_row']['requested_treatment'],'EDITORIAL FREEZE-FRAME');self.assertEqual(scene['script_camera']['move'],'static')

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

class SourceOnlyDetailedTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_clip_column_can_be_omitted_without_changing_timing(self):
        text='| Shot | Source file | Narration timeline IN → OUT | Source IN → OUT | Treatment | Visual description |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Srt01-A | S01.mp4 | 00:00.000 → 00:04.000 | 00:01.000 → 00:05.000 | VIDEO | Street |\n| Srt02-A | S03.mp4 | 00:04.000 → 00:08.000 | 00:02.000 → 00:06.000 | VIDEO | Courthouse |\n'
        rows=parse_detailed(text,BLOCKS)
        self.assertEqual([r['clip_id'] for r in rows],['',''])
        self.assertEqual([(r['source_file'],r['source_in_ms'],r['source_out_ms']) for r in rows],[('S01.mp4',1000,5000),('S03.mp4',2000,6000)])

class OneCueTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_single_cue_id_builds_without_letter(self):
        p={'production_script':{'text':table(single_rows())},'srt':{'blocks':BLOCKS}}
        rows,mode=rows_for(p)
        self.assertEqual(mode,'detailed_production_script')
        self.assertEqual([r['shot_id'] for r in rows],['Srt01','Srt02'])
    def test_wrong_cue_timing_is_rejected_without_rewriting(self):
        rows=single_rows();rows[0][3]='00:00.000 → 00:03.000';rows[0][4]='00:01.000 → 00:04.000';rows[1][3]='00:03.000 → 00:08.000';rows[1][4]='00:02.000 → 00:07.000'
        p={'production_script':{'text':table(rows)},'srt':{'blocks':BLOCKS}}
        before=p['production_script']['text']
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'complete timeline'):rows_for(p)
        self.assertEqual(p['production_script']['text'],before)

==================== FILE: tests/test_footage_requirements.py ====================
import io
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
import app as server
from footage_requirements import footage_requirements

class FootageRequirementsTest(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_quick_scan_without_srt_deduplicates_names_not_clip_ids(self):
  p={'production_script':{'text':'C01 C02 S01.mp4 S02.mp4 s01.MP4 S10.mov `link 7.mp4`'},'assets':[
   {'name':'S01.mp4','original_name':'S01.mp4','kind':'video'},
   {'name':'S02.mp4','original_name':'S02.mp4','kind':'video'},
   {'name':'S02.mp4','original_name':'S02.mp4','kind':'video'},
   {'name':'S10.mov','original_name':'S10.mov','kind':'image'},
   {'name':'link 7.mp4','original_name':'link 7.mp4','kind':'video','derived_from':{'kind':'video'}}]}
  r=footage_requirements(p);self.assertEqual((r['total'],r['matched'],r['missing'],r['ambiguous']),(4,1,2,1));self.assertEqual([x['name'] for x in r['files']],['link 7.mp4','S01.mp4','S02.mp4','S10.mov'])
 def test_exact_name_and_unsupported_or_no_script(self):
  self.assertEqual(footage_requirements({})['status'],'no_script')
  self.assertEqual(footage_requirements({'production_script':{'text':'C01, C02; no filenames'}})['status'],'not_found')
  r=footage_requirements({'production_script':{'text':'S01.mp4 S1.mp4'},'assets':[{'kind':'video','original_name':'S1.mp4'}]})
  self.assertEqual((r['total'],r['matched'],r['missing']),(2,1,1))
 def test_upload_and_replace_script_without_srt_updates_public_requirements(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, patch.object(server,'PROJECTS',Path(tmp)):
   c=server.app.test_client();pid=c.post('/api/projects',json={'title':'Count test'}).get_json()['id']
   def upload(text):
    return c.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/upload',data={'kind':'production_script','file':(io.BytesIO(text.encode()),'plan.md')},content_type='multipart/form-data')
   result=upload('S01.mp4 S02.mp4 S01.mp4 C01');self.assertEqual(result.status_code,200)
   r=result.get_json()['footage_requirements'];self.assertEqual((r['total'],r['missing']),(2,2));self.assertIsNone(result.get_json()['srt'])
   result=upload('S03.mp4');self.assertEqual(result.get_json()['footage_requirements']['files'][0]['name'],'S03.mp4')
   result=c.delete(f'/api/projects/{pid}/inputs/production_script',json={'confirm':True})
   self.assertEqual(result.status_code,200);self.assertEqual(result.get_json()['footage_requirements']['status'],'no_script')

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

==================== FILE: tests/test_local_understanding.py ====================
import base64
import importlib.util
import io
import time
import json
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import imageio_ffmpeg
import local_understanding as lu

class LocalUnderstandingTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root = Path(self.tmp.name)
        self.ff = imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()

    def tearDown(self):
        self.tmp.cleanup()

    def make_video(self):
        path = self.root / 'video.mp4'
        subprocess.run([self.ff, '-v', 'error', '-y', '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', 'color=c=red:s=160x90:r=10:d=2',
                        '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', 'color=c=blue:s=160x90:r=10:d=2',
                        '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', 'sine=frequency=440:sample_rate=16000:duration=4',
                        '-filter_complex', '[0:v][1:v]concat=n=2:v=1:a=0[v]', '-map', '[v]', '-map', '2:a',
                        '-c:v', 'mpeg4', '-c:a', 'aac', '-shortest', str(path)], check=True)
        return path

    @unittest.skipUnless(importlib.util.find_spec('scenedetect') and importlib.util.find_spec('librosa'), 'install requirements-local-ai.txt')
    def test_real_video_audio_and_partial_results(self):
        path = self.make_video()
        data={'status':'queued','source_signature':lu._signature(path),'source':path.name,'kind':'video',
              'duration_s':4,'stages':{},'started_at':0}
        with patch.dict('os.environ', {'STORYROOM_WHISPER_MODEL_DIR':''}):
            with patch.object(lu, '_vision', side_effect=RuntimeError('No local model')):
                lu._run(self.root,'test',path,data)
        result=json.loads(lu.result_path(self.root,'test').read_text())
        self.assertEqual(result['status'],'partial')
        scenes=result['stages']['scene_changes']['result']
        self.assertEqual(len(scenes),2)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(scenes[1]['start_s'],2,places=1)
        self.assertGreater(result['stages']['audio_features']['result']['windows'][0]['rms'],0.05)
        self.assertEqual(result['stages']['transcript']['status'],'unavailable')
        self.assertEqual(result['stages']['speakers']['status'],'not_requested')

    @unittest.skipUnless(importlib.util.find_spec('librosa'), 'install requirements-local-ai.txt')
    def test_low_energy_intervals_cross_chunks(self):
        import wave
        import numpy as np
        wav=self.root/'silence.wav'
        y=np.full(12*16000, 8192, dtype='<i2')
        y[9*16000:11*16000]=0
        with wave.open(str(wav),'wb') as f:
            f.setnchannels(1); f.setsampwidth(2); f.setframerate(16000); f.writeframes(y.tobytes())
        result=lu._audio_features(wav)
        self.assertEqual(result['low_energy_intervals'],[{'start_s':9.0,'end_s':11.0}])

    def test_vision_samples_long_scene_and_spreads_budget(self):
        scenes=[{'start_s':0,'end_s':1000}]
        samples=lu._sample_frames(scenes,4)
        self.assertEqual(len(samples),4)
        self.assertGreater(samples[0][0],0)
        self.assertGreater(samples[-1][0],850)
        self.assertLess(samples[-1][0],1000)

    @unittest.skipUnless(importlib.util.find_spec('onnxruntime'), 'install requirements-local-ai.txt')
    def test_sound_event_windows_and_unclassified_short_tail(self):
        import wave
        import numpy as np
        import onnxruntime as ort
        from types import SimpleNamespace
        wav=self.root/'audio.wav'; model=self.root/'model.onnx';model.write_bytes(b'test')
        with wave.open(str(wav),'wb') as f:
            f.setnchannels(1);f.setsampwidth(2);f.setframerate(16000);f.writeframes(bytes(2 * (3*16000+8000)))
        class FakeSession:
            def __init__(self,*args,**kwargs):
                self.kwargs=kwargs
            def get_inputs(self):return [SimpleNamespace(name='input',shape=[1,48000],type='tensor(float)')]
            def get_outputs(self):return [SimpleNamespace(name='scores')]
            def run(self,outputs,feed):
                result=np.zeros((6,521),dtype=np.float32);result[:,0]=0.9;return [result]
        with patch.dict('os.environ',{'STORYROOM_YAMNET_MODEL':str(model)}), patch.object(ort,'InferenceSession',FakeSession):
            windows=lu._sound_events(wav)['windows']
        self.assertEqual((windows[0]['start_s'],windows[0]['end_s']),(0,3))
        self.assertEqual(windows[0]['predictions'][0]['label'],'Speech')
        self.assertTrue(windows[1]['too_short'])
        self.assertEqual(windows[1]['predictions'],[])

    @unittest.skipUnless(importlib.util.find_spec('onnxruntime'), 'install requirements-local-ai.txt')
    def test_dynamic_yamnet_waveform_and_output(self):
        import wave
        import numpy as np
        import onnxruntime as ort
        from types import SimpleNamespace
        wav=self.root/'dynamic.wav';model=self.root/'dynamic.onnx';model.write_bytes(b'x')
        with wave.open(str(wav),'wb') as f:
            f.setnchannels(1);f.setsampwidth(2);f.setframerate(16000);f.writeframes(bytes(2 * 32000))
        seen=[]
        class FakeSession:
            def __init__(self,*args,**kwargs): pass
            def get_inputs(self): return [SimpleNamespace(name='waveform',shape=['dynamic'],type='tensor(float)')]
            def get_outputs(self): return [SimpleNamespace(name='output_0')]
            def run(self,outputs,feed):
                seen.append((outputs,feed['waveform'].shape)); result=np.zeros((3,521),dtype=np.float32);result[:,132]=.8;return [result]
        with patch.dict('os.environ',{'STORYROOM_YAMNET_MODEL':str(model)}),patch.object(ort,'InferenceSession',FakeSession):
            result=lu._sound_events(wav)
        self.assertEqual(seen,[(['output_0'],(32000,))]);self.assertEqual(result['windows'][0]['predictions'][0]['label'],'Music')

    def test_sound_only_reanalysis_preserves_other_stages(self):
        source=self.root/'source.wav';source.write_bytes(b'audio');model=self.root/'model.onnx';model.write_bytes(b'model')
        report={'status':'partial','source_signature':lu._signature(source),'stages':{'transcript':{'status':'complete','result':{'segments':[{'text':'Keep me'}]}},'visual':{'status':'unavailable'}}}
        lu._write(lu.result_path(self.root,'narration'),report)
        with patch.dict('os.environ',{'STORYROOM_YAMNET_MODEL':str(model)}),patch.object(lu,'_decode_audio'),patch.object(lu,'_sound_events',return_value={'windows':[{'start_s':0,'end_s':1}]}):
            lu.enqueue_sound_events(self.root,'narration',source)
            deadline=time.monotonic()+5
            while time.monotonic()<deadline:
                result=lu.read_result(self.root,'narration',source)
                if result['status'] not in ('queued','running'):break
                time.sleep(.01)
        self.assertEqual(result['status'],'partial')
        self.assertEqual(result['stages']['transcript']['result']['segments'][0]['text'],'Keep me')
        self.assertEqual(result['stages']['sound_events']['status'],'complete')
        source.write_bytes(b'changed')
        with patch.dict('os.environ',{'STORYROOM_YAMNET_MODEL':str(model)}):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'current'):lu.enqueue_sound_events(self.root,'narration',source)

    def test_visual_only_reanalysis_preserves_results_and_old_frames_on_failure(self):
        source=self.root/'source.mp4';source.write_bytes(b'video')
        report={'status':'partial','source_signature':lu._signature(source),'stages':{
            'transcript':{'status':'complete','result':{'segments':[{'text':'Keep me'}]}},
            'scene_changes':{'status':'complete','result':[{'start_s':0,'end_s':3}]},
            'visual':{'status':'complete','result':{'observations':[{'description':'Old observation'}]}}}}
        lu._write(lu.result_path(self.root,'video'),report)
        with patch.object(lu,'_vision',side_effect=RuntimeError('No model')):
            lu.enqueue_visual(self.root,'video',source)
            deadline=time.monotonic()+5
            while time.monotonic()<deadline:
                result=lu.read_result(self.root,'video',source)
                if result['status'] not in ('queued','running'):break
                time.sleep(.01)
        self.assertEqual(result['status'],'partial')
        self.assertEqual(result['stages']['transcript']['result']['segments'][0]['text'],'Keep me')
        self.assertEqual(result['stages']['visual']['result']['observations'][0]['description'],'Old observation')
        self.assertEqual(result['stages']['visual']['status'],'partial')
        self.assertIn('No model',result['stages']['visual']['detail'])
        source.write_bytes(b'changed')
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'current'):lu.enqueue_visual(self.root,'video',source)

    def test_speakers_aligned_by_overlap_without_claiming_identity(self):
        transcript={'segments':[{'start_s':1,'end_s':3,'words':[{'start_s':1,'end_s':2}, {'start_s':2,'end_s':3}, {'start_s':4,'end_s':5}]}]}
        speakers={'turns':[{'start_s':0,'end_s':2,'speaker':'SPEAKER_00'},{'start_s':2,'end_s':3,'speaker':'SPEAKER_01'}]}
        lu._align_speakers(transcript,speakers)
        self.assertEqual([w['speaker'] for w in transcript['segments'][0]['words']],['SPEAKER_00','SPEAKER_01',None])

    @unittest.skipUnless(importlib.util.find_spec('faster_whisper'), 'install requirements-local-ai.txt')

        from types import SimpleNamespace
        model=self.root/'whisper';model.mkdir()

        seen={}
        class FakeModel:
            def __init__(self, path, **kwargs):seen.update(path=path,**kwargs)
            def transcribe(self, path, **kwargs):
                seen.update(kwargs)
                return iter([SimpleNamespace(start=1.25,end=2.1,text=' Hello',words=[SimpleNamespace(start=1.3,end=2.0,word=' Hello')])]),SimpleNamespace(language='en',language_probability=.9)
        with patch.dict('os.environ',{'STORYROOM_WHISPER_MODEL_DIR':str(model)}), patch.object(faster_whisper,'WhisperModel',FakeModel):

        self.assertEqual(seen['device'],'cpu');self.assertTrue(seen['local_files_only'])

    @unittest.skipUnless(importlib.util.find_spec('faster_whisper'), 'install requirements-local-ai.txt')
    def test_transcription_checkpoints_before_failure(self):
        import faster_whisper
        from types import SimpleNamespace
        model=self.root/'whisper';model.mkdir()
        for name in ('model.bin','config.json','tokenizer.json'):(model/name).write_bytes(b'test')
        def segments():
            yield SimpleNamespace(start=0,end=1,text=' A line.',words=[])
            raise RuntimeError('decode failed')
        class FakeModel:
            def __init__(self,*args,**kwargs):pass
            def transcribe(self,*args,**kwargs):return segments(),SimpleNamespace(language='en',language_probability=.9)
        data={'stages':{}};report=self.root/'partial.json'
        def checkpoint(result):data['stages']['transcript']={'status':'running','result':result};lu._write(report,data)
        with patch.dict('os.environ',{'STORYROOM_WHISPER_MODEL_DIR':str(model)}),patch.object(faster_whisper,'WhisperModel',FakeModel):
            lu._stage(data,report,'transcript',lambda:lu._transcribe(self.root/'audio.wav',checkpoint))
        stage=json.loads(report.read_text())['stages']['transcript']
        self.assertEqual(stage['status'],'partial')
        self.assertEqual(stage['result']['segments'][0]['text'],'A line.')

    def test_vision_checkpoints_frames_and_never_targets_remote_host(self):
        from urllib.error import URLError
        path=self.make_video();calls=[]
        class FakeOpener:
            def open(self, request, timeout):
                url=request if isinstance(request,str) else request.full_url
                payload=json.loads(request.data) if not isinstance(request,str) else None
                calls.append((url,payload))
                self_test.assertTrue(url.startswith('http://127.0.0.1:11434/'))
                if url.endswith('/api/status'):return io.BytesIO(b'{"cloud":{"disabled":true}}')
                if url.endswith('/api/show'):return io.BytesIO(b'{"capabilities":["vision"]}')
                if url.endswith('/api/generate'):return io.BytesIO(b'{}')
                self_test.assertEqual(payload['model'],'qwen3-vl:2b')
                self_test.assertTrue(base64.b64decode(payload['messages'][0]['images'][0]).startswith(b'\xff\xd8'))
                if sum(url.endswith('/api/chat') for url,_ in calls)==2:raise URLError('offline')
                return io.BytesIO(b'{"message":{"content":"A red frame"}}')
        self_test=self
        data={'stages':{}};report=self.root/'report.json';scenes=[{'start_s':0,'end_s':2},{'start_s':2,'end_s':4}]
        def checkpoint(result):
            data['stages']['visual']={'status':'running','result':result};lu._write(report,data)
        with patch.object(lu.urllib.request,'build_opener',return_value=FakeOpener()):
            lu._stage(data,report,'visual',lambda:lu._vision(path,scenes,checkpoint))
        saved=json.loads(report.read_text())['stages']['visual']
        self.assertEqual(saved['status'],'partial')
        self.assertEqual(saved['result']['sampled_frames'],1)
        self.assertEqual(saved['result']['planned_frames'],2)
        self.assertEqual(saved['result']['observations'][0]['frame_time_s'],1.0)
        self.assertTrue(calls[-1][0].endswith('/api/generate'))

    def test_vision_refuses_cloud_unverified_or_nonvisual_models_before_sending_frames(self):
        path=self.make_video();scenes=[{'start_s':0,'end_s':2}]
        for status, details in [
            ({'cloud':{'disabled':False}},{}),
            ({'cloud':{'disabled':True}},{'remote_host':'https://example.invalid'}),
            ({'cloud':{'disabled':True}},{'remote_model':'hosted'}),
            ({'cloud':{'disabled':True}},{'capabilities':['completion']}),
            ({},{}),
        ]:
            with self.subTest(status=status,details=details):
                calls=[]
                class FakeOpener:
                    def open(self,request,timeout):
                        url=request if isinstance(request,str) else request.full_url
                        calls.append(url)
                        if url.endswith('/api/status'):return io.BytesIO(json.dumps(status).encode())
                        if url.endswith('/api/show'):return io.BytesIO(json.dumps(details).encode())
                        raise AssertionError('image or unload must not be sent')
                with patch.object(lu.urllib.request,'build_opener',return_value=FakeOpener()):
                    with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):lu._vision(path,scenes)
                self.assertFalse(any(url.endswith('/api/chat') for url in calls))
        with patch.dict('os.environ',{'STORYROOM_OLLAMA_MODEL':'model:cloud'}),patch.object(lu.urllib.request,'build_opener') as opener:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError,'Cloud'):lu._vision(path,scenes)
            opener.return_value.open.assert_not_called()

    def test_vision_unloads_after_failed_first_request(self):
        from urllib.error import URLError
        path=self.make_video();calls=[]
        class FakeOpener:
            def open(self,request,timeout):
                url=request if isinstance(request,str) else request.full_url;calls.append(url)
                if url.endswith('/api/status'):return io.BytesIO(b'{"cloud":{"disabled":true}}')
                if url.endswith('/api/show'):return io.BytesIO(b'{"capabilities":["vision"]}')
                if url.endswith('/api/chat'):raise URLError('timeout')
                return io.BytesIO(b'{}')
        with patch.object(lu.urllib.request,'build_opener',return_value=FakeOpener()):
            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):lu._vision(path,[{'start_s':0,'end_s':2}])
        self.assertTrue(calls[-1].endswith('/api/generate'))

    def test_pyannote_runs_in_offline_subprocess(self):
        from types import SimpleNamespace
        model=self.root/'pipeline';model.mkdir()
        wav=self.root/'audio.wav';wav.write_bytes(b'dummy')
        def runner(args,**kwargs):
            Path(args[-1]).write_text(json.dumps({'turns':[]}))
            self.assertEqual(kwargs['env']['HF_HUB_OFFLINE'],'1')
            self.assertEqual(kwargs['env']['PYANNOTE_METRICS_ENABLED'],'0')
            return SimpleNamespace(returncode=0,stderr=b'')
        with patch.dict('os.environ', {'STORYROOM_PYANNOTE_MODEL_DIR':str(model)}), patch.object(lu.subprocess,'run',side_effect=runner):
            self.assertEqual(lu._diarize(wav),{'turns':[]})

    def test_delayed_video_audio_preserves_source_timeline(self):
        import wave
        import numpy as np
        video=self.root/'delayed.mp4';wav=self.root/'delayed.wav'
        subprocess.run([self.ff,'-v','error','-y','-f','lavfi','-i','color=c=black:s=160x90:r=10:d=5',
                        '-itsoffset','2','-f','lavfi','-i','sine=frequency=440:sample_rate=16000:duration=2',
                        '-map','0:v','-map','1:a','-c:v','mpeg4','-c:a','aac',str(video)],check=True)
        lu._decode_audio(video,wav)
        with wave.open(str(wav),'rb') as f:
            self.assertEqual(f.getframerate(),16000)
            audio=np.frombuffer(f.readframes(f.getnframes()),dtype='<i2').astype(np.float32)
        self.assertGreater(len(audio),int(3.9*16000))
        self.assertLess(np.sqrt(np.mean(audio[:int(1.8*16000)]**2)),10)
        self.assertGreater(np.sqrt(np.mean(audio[int(2.2*16000):int(3.8*16000)]**2)),1000)

    def test_ffmpeg_decodes_mp3(self):
        mp3=self.root/'narration.mp3'; wav=self.root/'decoded.wav'
        subprocess.run([self.ff,'-v','error','-y','-f','lavfi','-i','sine=frequency=440:duration=1',str(mp3)],check=True)
        lu._decode_audio(mp3,wav)
        self.assertGreater(wav.stat().st_size,16000)

    def test_source_observations_are_mapped_to_the_saved_scene(self):
        report={'status':'partial','stages':{
            'transcript':{'status':'complete','result':{'segments':[{'start_s':9.0,'end_s':11.0,'text':'Before and within'}, {'start_s':14.0,'end_s':15.0,'text':'outside'}]}},
            'visual':{'status':'complete','result':{'observations':[{'frame_time_s':10.5,'description':'visible'}, {'frame_time_s':14.0,'description':'outside'}]}},
            'sound_events':{'status':'complete','result':{'windows':[{'start_s':12.0,'end_s':15.0,'predictions':[{'label':'Music','score':.7}]}]}}}}
        scene={'in_s':10,'frame_in':300,'frame_out':420}
        context=lu.scene_context(report,scene)
        self.assertEqual((context['transcript'][0]['timeline_start_s'],context['transcript'][0]['timeline_end_s']),(10,11))
        self.assertEqual(context['visual'][0]['timeline_time_s'],10.5)
        self.assertEqual((context['sound_events'][0]['timeline_start_s'],context['sound_events'][0]['timeline_end_s']),(12,14))
        self.assertEqual(len(context['visual']),1)
        self.assertEqual(lu.scene_context({'status':'stale'},scene)['transcript'],[])

    def test_timeline_context_maps_each_stage_for_short_and_long_media(self):
        for duration in (60,600,1800,3600):
            with self.subTest(duration=duration):
                start=duration-10
                report={'status':'partial','stages':{
                    'transcript':{'status':'complete','result':{'segments':[{'start_s':start-1,'end_s':start+4,'text':'partial overlap',
                        'words':[{'start_s':start-1,'end_s':start,'text':'outside'},{'start_s':start+1,'end_s':start+2,'text':'inside'},{'start_s':None,'end_s':None,'text':'unknown'}]}]}},
                    'scene_changes':{'status':'complete','result':[{'start_s':start-2,'end_s':start+1},{'start_s':start+1,'end_s':duration}]},
                    'audio_features':{'status':'complete','result':{'windows':[{'start_s':start,'end_s':duration,'rms':.01}],'low_energy_intervals':[{'start_s':start+2,'end_s':start+8}]}},
                    'speakers':{'status':'complete','result':{'turns':[{'start_s':start-2,'end_s':start+5,'speaker':'SPEAKER_00'}]}},
                    'sound_events':{'status':'complete','result':{'windows':[{'start_s':start,'end_s':start+3,'predictions':[]}]}},
                    'visual':{'status':'partial','result':{'observations':[{'frame_time_s':start+1,'description':'visible'},{'frame_time_s':start+5,'description':'outside'}]}}}}
                scene={'in_s':start,'frame_in':900,'frame_out':1050}
                result=lu.scene_context(report,scene)
                self.assertEqual(result['transcript'][0]['timeline_start_s'],30)
                self.assertEqual(result['transcript'][0]['timeline_end_s'],34)
                self.assertEqual(len(result['transcript'][0]['words']),3)
                self.assertEqual([w['text'] for w in result['transcript'][0]['timeline_words']],['inside'])
                self.assertEqual(result['transcript'][0]['timeline_words'][0]['timeline_start_s'],31)
                self.assertEqual((result['speakers'][0]['timeline_start_s'],result['speakers'][0]['timeline_end_s']),(30,35))
                self.assertEqual([(x['timeline_start_s'],x['timeline_end_s']) for x in result['scene_changes']],[(30,31),(31,35)])
                self.assertEqual(result['low_energy_intervals'][0]['timeline_end_s'],35)
                self.assertEqual(len(result['visual']),1)
                self.assertEqual(result['stage_status']['visual'],'partial')
                self.assertNotIn('timeline_start_s',report['stages']['transcript']['result']['segments'][0]['words'][1])

    def test_pre_alignment_reports_warn_without_losing_observations(self):
        source=self.root/'audio.wav';source.write_bytes(b'audio')
        report={'status':'complete','source_signature':lu._signature(source),'stages':{'transcript':{'status':'complete','result':{'segments':[{'start_s':0,'end_s':1,'text':'Hello'}]}}}}
        lu._write(lu.result_path(self.root,'narration'),report)
        result=lu.read_result(self.root,'narration',source)
        self.assertIn('delayed-audio',result['timing_warning'])

        report['audio_timing_version']=2;lu._write(lu.result_path(self.root,'narration'),report)
        self.assertNotIn('timing_warning',lu.read_result(self.root,'narration',source))

    def test_stale_results_not_returned_for_changed_media(self):
        source=self.root/'audio.wav';source.write_bytes(b'first')
        lu._write(lu.result_path(self.root,'narration'), {'status':'complete','source_signature':lu._signature(source)})
        source.write_bytes(b'replaced longer media')
        self.assertEqual(lu.read_result(self.root,'narration',source)['status'],'stale')

    @unittest.skipUnless(importlib.util.find_spec('librosa'), 'install requirements-local-ai.txt')
    def test_uploaded_mp3_analysis_report_api(self):
        import app as server
        mp3=self.root/'narration.mp3'
        subprocess.run([self.ff,'-v','error','-y','-f','lavfi','-i','sine=frequency=440:duration=1',str(mp3)],check=True)
        projects=self.root/'projects'; projects.mkdir()
        with patch.object(server, 'PROJECTS', projects), patch.dict('os.environ', {'STORYROOM_UNDERSTANDING_AUTO':'0', 'STORYROOM_WHISPER_MODEL_DIR':''}):
            client=server.app.test_client()
            pid=client.post('/api/projects',json={'title':'Local report'}).get_json()['id']
            upload=client.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/upload',data={'kind':'voiceover','file':(io.BytesIO(mp3.read_bytes()),'narration.mp3')},content_type='multipart/form-data')
            self.assertEqual(upload.status_code,200,upload.get_json())
            url=f'/api/projects/{pid}/understanding/narration'
            self.assertEqual(client.get(url).get_json()['status'],'not_started')
            self.assertIn(client.post(url).get_json()['status'],('queued','running','partial'))
            for _ in range(100):
                result=client.get(url).get_json()
                if result['status'] not in ('queued','running'):break
                time.sleep(.1)
            self.assertEqual(result['status'],'partial',result)
            self.assertEqual(result['stages']['audio_features']['status'],'complete')
            project=client.get(f'/api/projects/{pid}').get_json()
            self.assertIsNone(project['srt'])
            self.assertEqual(project['scenes'],[])

    @unittest.skipUnless(importlib.util.find_spec('faster_whisper'), 'install requirements-local-ai.txt')
    def test_incomplete_model_never_calls_tokenizer_download(self):
        import faster_whisper
        model=self.root/'incomplete';model.mkdir();(model/'model.bin').write_bytes(b'test')
        with patch.dict('os.environ', {'STORYROOM_WHISPER_MODEL_DIR':str(model)}), patch.object(faster_whisper,'WhisperModel') as constructor:
            self.assertFalse(lu.capabilities()['whisper_model_ready'])
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError,'tokenizer.json'):lu._transcribe(self.root/'audio.wav')
            constructor.assert_not_called()

    def test_srt_download_keeps_source_timings_and_does_not_edit_project(self):
        import app as server
        mp3=self.root/'narration.mp3'
        subprocess.run([self.ff,'-v','error','-y','-f','lavfi','-i','sine=frequency=440:duration=3',str(mp3)],check=True)
        projects=self.root/'projects'; projects.mkdir()
        with patch.object(server, 'PROJECTS', projects), patch.dict('os.environ', {'STORYROOM_UNDERSTANDING_AUTO':'0'}):
            client=server.app.test_client();pid=client.post('/api/projects',json={'title':'SRT test'}).get_json()['id']
            client.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/upload',data={'kind':'voiceover','file':(io.BytesIO(mp3.read_bytes()),'narration.mp3')},content_type='multipart/form-data')
            root=projects/pid;source=root/'inputs'/'voiceover.mp3'
            report={'status':'partial','source_signature':lu._signature(source),'stages':{'transcript':{'status':'complete','result':{'segments':[{'start_s':.25,'end_s':1.5,'text':'Hello there.'},{'start_s':2,'end_s':2.9,'text':'Second line.'}]}}}}
            lu._write(lu.result_path(root,'narration'),report)
            r=client.get(f'/api/projects/{pid}/understanding/narration/transcript.srt')
            self.assertEqual(r.status_code,200)
            self.assertIn('00:00:00,250 --> 00:00:01,500',r.get_data(as_text=True))
            self.assertIn('transcript.srt',r.headers['Content-Disposition'])
            self.assertIsNone(client.get(f'/api/projects/{pid}').get_json()['srt'])
            report['stages']['transcript']['result']['segments'][1]['start_s']=1
            lu._write(lu.result_path(root,'narration'),report)
            self.assertEqual(client.get(f'/api/projects/{pid}/understanding/narration/transcript.srt').status_code,400)

    def test_apply_narration_requires_complete_and_explicit_replacement(self):
        import app as server
        mp3=self.root/'narration.mp3'
        subprocess.run([self.ff,'-v','error','-y','-f','lavfi','-i','sine=frequency=440:duration=3',str(mp3)],check=True)
        projects=self.root/'projects';projects.mkdir()
        with patch.object(server,'PROJECTS',projects), patch.dict('os.environ',{'STORYROOM_UNDERSTANDING_AUTO':'0'}), patch('automatic.maybe_queue'):
            client=server.app.test_client();pid=client.post('/api/projects',json={'title':'Apply test'}).get_json()['id']
            client.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/upload',data={'kind':'voiceover','file':(io.BytesIO(mp3.read_bytes()),'narration.mp3')},content_type='multipart/form-data')
            original=b'1\n00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,000\nOriginal caption.\n'
            client.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/upload',data={'kind':'srt','file':(io.BytesIO(original),'original.srt')},content_type='multipart/form-data')
            root=projects/pid;source=root/'inputs/voiceover.mp3';out=root/'inputs/captions.srt'
            report={'status':'partial','source_signature':lu._signature(source),'stages':{'transcript':{'status':'running','result':{'segments':[{'start_s':.25,'end_s':2.9,'text':'Machine text.'}]}}}}
            lu._write(lu.result_path(root,'narration'),report)
            url=f'/api/projects/{pid}/understanding/narration/apply-srt'
            self.assertEqual(client.post(url,json={}).status_code,400)
            self.assertEqual(client.post(url,json={'confirm_generated':True}).status_code,400)
            self.assertEqual(client.post(url,json={'confirm_generated':True,'confirm_replace':True}).status_code,400)
            self.assertEqual(out.read_bytes(),original)
            report['stages']['transcript']['status']='complete';lu._write(lu.result_path(root,'narration'),report)
            response=client.post(url,json={'confirm_generated':True,'confirm_replace':True})
            self.assertEqual(response.status_code,200)
            self.assertIn('00:00:00,250 --> 00:00:02,900',out.read_text())
            self.assertEqual(response.get_json()['srt']['blocks'][0]['text'],'Machine text.')
            source.write_bytes(source.read_bytes()+b'changed')
            self.assertEqual(client.post(url,json={'confirm_generated':True,'confirm_replace':True}).status_code,400)

    def test_no_remote_whisper_model_download(self):
        with patch.dict('os.environ', {'STORYROOM_WHISPER_MODEL_DIR':''}):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError,'model directory'):
                lu._transcribe(self.root/'audio.wav')

if __name__ == '__main__': unittest.main()

==================== FILE: tests/test_plain_script.py ====================
import copy
import unittest
from plain_script import parse_plain_script
from document_plan import rows_for

BLOCKS=[{'number':1,'start_ms':0,'end_ms':4000,'text':'Original words'}, {'number':2,'start_ms':4000,'end_ms':8000,'text':'Next words'}]
SCRIPT='''Shot: Srt001
Narration timeline: 00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:04,000
Exact narration: Original words
Full source: PROPOSED: First video.mp4
Source IN: 00:00:01,000
Source OUT: 00:00:05,000
Treatment: VIDEO, 1.0×, loop OFF
Visual direction: Street
Continuity/caption: Unverified

Shot: Srt002
Narration timeline: 00:00:04,000 --> 00:00:08,000
Exact narration: Next words
Full source: S02.mp4
Source IN: 00:00:05,000
Source OUT: 00:00:09,000
Treatment: STILL (native static interval)
Visual direction: Document
Continuity/caption: Check rights
'''
class PlainScriptTest(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_exact_cues_and_proposed_full_filename(self):
  rows,mode=rows_for({'production_script':{'text':SCRIPT},'srt':{'blocks':copy.deepcopy(BLOCKS)}})
  self.assertEqual(mode,'detailed_production_script');self.assertEqual([r['source_file'] for r in rows],['First video.mp4','S02.mp4']);self.assertEqual([r['shot_id'] for r in rows],['Srt001','Srt002']);self.assertTrue(rows[0]['source_proposed']);self.assertEqual(rows[1]['requested_treatment'],'STILL HOLD');self.assertEqual(rows[1]['source_out_ms'],9000)
 def test_reject_bad_instructions_without_guessing(self):
  for bad in [SCRIPT.replace('00:00:05,000\nTreatment','00:00:05,100\nTreatment',1),SCRIPT.replace('Shot: Srt002','Shot: Srt001'),SCRIPT.replace('loop OFF','loop ON'),SCRIPT.replace('Visual direction: Street','Visual direction: Street\nUnexpected instruction: crop'),SCRIPT.replace('Full source: S02.mp4','Full source: ../S02.mp4')]:
   with self.subTest(bad=bad):
    with self.assertRaises(ValueError):parse_plain_script(bad,BLOCKS)
 def test_srt_conflict_and_block_map_are_not_ignored(self):
  p={'production_script':{'text':SCRIPT},'srt':{'blocks':copy.deepcopy(BLOCKS)},'srt_block_map':{'text':'map'}}
  with self.assertRaises(ValueError):rows_for(p)
  p.pop('srt_block_map');p['srt']['blocks'][0]['end_ms']=3999
  with self.assertRaises(ValueError):rows_for(p)

class NativeStaticReviewTest(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_preview_uses_original_interval_and_upgrades_old_gap(self):
  import tempfile

   p=server.load(pid);p['srt']={'blocks':copy.deepcopy(BLOCKS)};p['production_script']={'text':SCRIPT};
   for name in ('First video.mp4','S02.mp4'):
    path=root/'sources'/name;path.write_bytes(b'placeholder');p['assets'].append({'id':name,'name':name,'original_name':name,'kind':'video','duration':20,'path':'sources/'+name,'verified':False,'source':'','people':[],'category':'general','rights':'unreviewed','notes':''})
   server.save(p)
   response=c.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/script-draft',json={'moving_only':True});self.assertEqual(response.status_code,200,response.get_data(as_text=True))
   saved=server.load(pid);native=saved['scenes'][1];self.assertEqual(native['asset_id'],'S02.mp4');self.assertEqual(native['in_s'],5);self.assertTrue(native['plan_row']['native_static_interval']);self.assertTrue(saved['review_only_draft']);self.assertFalse(server.check(saved)['can_export']);self.assertEqual(len(saved['assets']),2)
   native['asset_id']=None;native['in_s']=0;native['missing_video_out']=True;native['plan_row'].pop('native_static_interval');native['plan_row'].pop('native_static_review');saved['script_application']['issues'].append('Beat Srt002: omitted');server.save(saved)
   response=c.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/native-static-review');self.assertEqual(response.status_code,200,response.get_data(as_text=True));result=response.get_json();self.assertEqual(result['native_static_update']['placed'],['Srt002']);saved=server.load(pid);self.assertEqual(saved['scenes'][1]['asset_id'],'S02.mp4');self.assertFalse(saved['scenes'][1]['missing_video_out']);self.assertEqual(len(saved['assets']),2)
   self.assertEqual(saved['production_script']['text'],SCRIPT);self.assertEqual(saved['srt']['blocks'],BLOCKS)

class HeadedScriptTest(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_headed_script_and_native_format(self):
  text='# Project\n## Editor Assembly Instructions\nReview original sources.\n## Storyboard Production Script\n\n'+SCRIPT.replace('STILL (native static interval)','STILL — native static interval in source video')+'\n---\n\n## Final Coverage Summary\nClaims need review.\n'
  rows,mode=rows_for({'production_script':{'text':text},'srt':{'blocks':copy.deepcopy(BLOCKS)}})
  self.assertEqual(mode,'detailed_production_script');self.assertTrue(rows[1]['native_static_interval']);self.assertEqual(rows[1]['source_out_ms'],9000)
  with self.assertRaises(ValueError):parse_plain_script('Shot: Srt999\n'+text,BLOCKS)
 def test_review_rebuild_preserves_manual_edits(self):

==================== FILE: tests/test_preview_playback.py ====================
import subprocess
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

class PreviewPlaybackTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_prepared_cut_switches_without_pausing_narration(self):
        script=Path('static/editor.js').read_text()
        functions=script[script.index('function clearPreparedSource()'):script.index('function seekSourceAtTimeline(')]
        harness='''
const assert=require('assert');
function player(){return {currentTime:0,duration:100,readyState:2,seeking:false,src:'',classList:{add(){},remove(){}},pause(){this.pauses=(this.pauses||0)+1},play(){this.plays=(this.plays||0)+1;return Promise.resolve()},load(){},removeAttribute(){this.src=''},getAttribute(){return this.src}}}
let sourceNode=player(),nextSourceNode=player(),preparedScene='',preparedReady=false,prepareToken=0,sourceSeekToken=0,sourceReady=false,editorBuffering=false,editorMaster=false,editorPlaying=true,sourceScene='s2';
const imageNode={hidden:false},monitor={hidden:false};const $=()=>monitor;
const footage=[{id:'a',kind:'video',url:'/a'}];const shots=[{id:'s1',frame_out:300},{id:'s2',frame_in:300,in_s:12,asset_id:'a'}];
const scenes=()=>shots,assets=()=>footage,editorTime=()=>10;function seekSourceAtTimeline(){throw Error('unexpected fallback')}
''' + functions + '''
prepareNextSource(0,5);assert.equal(preparedScene,'s2');nextSourceNode.onloadedmetadata();
assert(preparedReady);const queued=nextSourceNode,old=sourceNode;
assert(usePreparedSource(shots[1],footage[0],10.05));assert.strictEqual(sourceNode,queued);assert.strictEqual(nextSourceNode,old);assert(sourceReady);assert.equal(sourceNode.plays,1);
assert(!usePreparedSource(shots[1],footage[0],10.05));
// A prepared cut may arrive while an earlier seek is buffering: narration must resume.
let P={voiceover:{}},editorMuted=false;let narrNode=player();function pauseEditor(){throw Error('unexpected pause')}
prepareNextSource(0,5);nextSourceNode.onloadedmetadata();editorBuffering=true;
assert(usePreparedSource(shots[1],footage[0],10.05));assert.equal(narrNode.plays,1);

'''
        subprocess.run(['node','-e',harness],check=True)

    def test_contiguous_same_video_is_not_restarted(self):
        script=Path('static/editor.js').read_text()
        function=script[script.index('function showSource('):script.index('function applyImageMotion(')]
        harness="""
const assert=require('assert');let sourceScene='previous',sourceReady=true,sourceSeekToken=0,editorPlaying=true,editorMaster=false;
let paused=0,seeks=0;const sourceNode={currentTime:13.42,seeking:false,readyState:2,pause(){paused++},getAttribute(){return '/clip'},classList:{remove(){} }};
const s={id:'next',asset_id:'a',frame_in:300,in_s:13.4},a={id:'a',kind:'video',url:'/clip'};
const scenes=()=>[s],assets=()=>[a],imageNode={hidden:false},$=()=>({hidden:false});
function usePreparedSource(){return false}function seekSourceAtTimeline(){seeks++}
"""+function+"""
showSource(0,10.02);assert.equal(paused,0);assert.equal(seeks,0);assert.equal(sourceScene,'next');sourceNode.currentTime=14;showSource(0,10.02);assert.equal(seeks,0);assert(sourceNode.playbackRate<1);
"""
        subprocess.run(['node','-e',harness],check=True)

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

==================== FILE: tests/test_remove_files.py ====================
import io
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import app as server
import local_understanding as lu


class RemoveFilesTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp=tempfile.TemporaryDirectory();self.patcher=patch.object(server,'PROJECTS',Path(self.tmp.name));self.patcher.start()
        self.client=server.app.test_client();self.pid=self.client.post('/api/projects',json={'title':'Removal test'}).get_json()['id']
        self.root=Path(self.tmp.name)/self.pid
    def tearDown(self):
        self.patcher.stop();self.tmp.cleanup()
    def asset(self):
        aid='a'*16;path=self.root/'sources'/f'{aid}.mp4';path.write_bytes(b'video')
        p=server.load(self.pid);p['assets'].append({'id':aid,'name':'test.mp4','original_name':'test.mp4','path':f'sources/{aid}.mp4','kind':'video','duration':10,'source':'','people':[],'category':'general','verified':False,'rights':'unreviewed','notes':''});server.save(p)
        return aid,path
    def test_stale_automatic_status_is_not_reported_as_working(self):
        server.write(self.root/'automatic_status.json',{'status':'studying','detail':'Working'})
        r=self.client.get(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}');self.assertEqual(r.get_json()['automatic_status']['status'],'interrupted')
        from automatic import _QUEUE_LOCK,_PENDING
        with _QUEUE_LOCK:_PENDING.add(self.pid)
        try:self.assertEqual(self.client.get(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}').get_json()['automatic_status']['status'],'studying')
        finally:
            with _QUEUE_LOCK:_PENDING.discard(self.pid)
    def test_remove_only_confirmed_asset_and_its_report(self):
        aid,path=self.asset();report=lu.result_path(self.root,aid);lu._write(report,{'test':True})
        other=self.root/'sources'/'unrelated.mp4';other.write_bytes(b'keep')
        url=f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/assets/{aid}'
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(url,json={}).status_code,400);self.assertTrue(path.exists())
        r=self.client.delete(url,json={'confirm':True});self.assertEqual(r.status_code,200)
        self.assertFalse(path.exists());self.assertFalse(report.exists());self.assertTrue(other.exists());self.assertEqual(r.get_json()['assets'],[])
    def test_used_asset_requires_confirmation_and_preserves_scene(self):
        aid,path=self.asset();p=server.load(self.pid);p['source_bindings']={'v7.mp4':aid};p['scenes']=[{'id':'b'*16,'frame_in':0,'frame_out':60,'asset_id':aid,'approved':True,'emotion':'calm','purpose':'context','focus':'object','in_s':0,'transition':'cut','person':'','notes':'','context_only':False}];server.save(p)
        url=f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/assets/{aid}'
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(url,json={'confirm':True}).status_code,400);self.assertTrue(path.exists())
        r=self.client.delete(url,json={'confirm':True,'confirm_used':True});self.assertEqual(r.status_code,200,r.get_json())
        p=server.load(self.pid);self.assertEqual(len(p['scenes']),1);self.assertIsNone(p['scenes'][0]['asset_id']);self.assertFalse(p['scenes'][0]['approved']);self.assertEqual(p['source_bindings'],{})
        self.assertFalse(path.exists());self.assertFalse(r.get_json()['validation']['can_export'])
    def test_removing_video_also_removes_derived_frames(self):
        aid,path=self.asset();p=server.load(self.pid);frame=self.root/'assets'/'frame.png';frame.write_bytes(b'frame')
        p['assets'].append({'id':'frame','name':'frame.png','path':'assets/frame.png','kind':'image','duration':0,'source':'','people':[],'category':'general','verified':False,'rights':'unreviewed','notes':'','derived_from':{'asset_id':aid}});server.save(p)
        url=f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/assets/{aid}'
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(url,json={'confirm':True}).status_code,400)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(url,json={'confirm':True,'confirm_used':True}).status_code,200)
        self.assertFalse(path.exists());self.assertFalse(frame.exists());self.assertEqual(server.load(self.pid)['assets'],[])
    def test_remove_script_and_narration(self):
        p=server.load(self.pid);p['production_script']={'name':'script.txt','text':'A script'};p['voiceover']={'name':'voice.mp3','path':'inputs/voiceover.mp3','duration':2};server.save(p)
        script=self.root/'inputs/production_script.txt';script.write_text('A script');voice=self.root/'inputs/voiceover.mp3';voice.write_bytes(b'audio')
        report=lu.result_path(self.root,'narration');lu._write(report,{'test':True})
        for kind in ('production_script','voiceover'):
            r=self.client.delete(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/inputs/{kind}',json={'confirm':True});self.assertEqual(r.status_code,200,r.get_json())
        self.assertFalse(script.exists());self.assertFalse(voice.exists());self.assertFalse(report.exists())

    def test_busy_analysis_and_auto_build_cannot_be_interrupted(self):
        from automatic import _PENDING,_QUEUE_LOCK
        aid,path=self.asset();key=(str(self.root),aid);url=f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/assets/{aid}'
        with lu._GUARD:lu._RUNNING.add(key)
        try:self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(url,json={'confirm':True}).status_code,400)
        finally:
            with lu._GUARD:lu._RUNNING.discard(key)
        with _QUEUE_LOCK:_PENDING.add(self.pid)
        try:self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(url,json={'confirm':True}).status_code,400)
        finally:
            with _QUEUE_LOCK:_PENDING.discard(self.pid)
        self.assertTrue(path.exists())
    def test_delete_srt_without_losing_existing_scenes(self):
        srt=b'1\n00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,000\nHello\n'
        self.client.post(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/upload',data={'kind':'srt','file':(io.BytesIO(srt),'captions.srt')},content_type='multipart/form-data')
        p=server.load(self.pid);p['scenes']=[{'id':'b'*16,'frame_in':0,'frame_out':60,'asset_id':None,'approved':True,'emotion':'calm','purpose':'context','focus':'object','in_s':0,'transition':'cut','person':'','notes':'','context_only':False}];server.save(p)
        url=f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/inputs/srt'
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(url,json={'confirm':True}).status_code,400)
        r=self.client.delete(url,json={'confirm':True,'confirm_used':True});self.assertEqual(r.status_code,200,r.get_json())
        p=r.get_json();self.assertIsNone(p['srt']);self.assertEqual(len(p['scenes']),1);self.assertFalse(p['scenes'][0]['approved']);self.assertFalse((self.root/'inputs/captions.srt').exists())
        self.assertTrue(any('SRT was removed' in x for x in p['validation']['errors']))
        self.client.post(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/upload',data={'kind':'srt','file':(io.BytesIO(srt),'captions.srt'),'replace':'yes'},content_type='multipart/form-data')
        self.assertNotIn('srt_removed',server.load(self.pid))

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

import io
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
import imageio_ffmpeg
import subprocess
import app as server

class ReplaceFootageTest(unittest.TestCase):
 @classmethod
 def setUpClass(cls):
  cls.tmpmedia=tempfile.TemporaryDirectory();cls.video={}
  for seconds in [2,5]:
   path=Path(cls.tmpmedia.name)/f'{seconds}.mp4'
   subprocess.run([imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe(),'-v','error','-y','-f','lavfi','-i',f'testsrc2=size=96x64:rate=30:duration={seconds}','-c:v','libx264','-pix_fmt','yuv420p',str(path)],check=True)
   cls.video[seconds]=path.read_bytes()
 @classmethod
 def tearDownClass(cls):cls.tmpmedia.cleanup()
 def setUp(self):
  self.tmp=tempfile.TemporaryDirectory();self.patch=patch.object(server,'PROJECTS',Path(self.tmp.name));self.patch.start();self.queue=patch('automatic.maybe_queue');self.queue.start()
  self.c=server.app.test_client();self.pid=self.c.post('/api/projects',json={'title':'Replacement'}).get_json()['id'];self.root=Path(self.tmp.name)/self.pid
 def tearDown(self):self.queue.stop();self.patch.stop();self.tmp.cleanup()
 def upload(self,name,data,stream=True):
  if stream:return self.c.post(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/media-stream?name={name}',data=data,content_type='application/octet-stream')
  return self.c.post(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/upload',data={'kind':'asset','file':(io.BytesIO(data),name)},content_type='multipart/form-data')
 def scene(self,aid,frames=90):
  return {'id':'scene1','srt_blocks':[1,1],'frame_in':0,'frame_out':frames,'narration':'words','purpose':'review','emotion':'neutral','focus':'test','category':'general','person':'','asset_id':aid,'in_s':0,'motion':'auto','transition':'cut','context_only':False,'notes':'','approved':True,'profile':'documentary','focus_position':'center','motion_strength':'subtle'}
 def test_replace_preserves_timeline_and_retires_old_source(self):
  first=self.upload('S14.mp4',self.video[5]).get_json();a=first['assets'][0];oldpath=self.root/a['path'];oldurl=a['url'];p=server.load(self.pid);p['scenes']=[self.scene(a['id'])];server.save(p)
  second=self.upload('S14.mp4',self.video[5],False);self.assertEqual(second.status_code,200,second.get_data(as_text=True));p=second.get_json();self.assertEqual(len(p['assets']),1);self.assertEqual(p['assets'][0]['id'],a['id']);self.assertEqual(p['assets'][0]['original_name'],'S14.mp4');self.assertNotEqual(oldurl,p['assets'][0]['url']);self.assertFalse(oldpath.exists());self.assertEqual((self.root/p['assets'][0]['path']).read_bytes(),self.video[5]);self.assertEqual(p['scenes'][0]['asset_id'],a['id']);self.assertFalse(p['scenes'][0]['approved']);self.assertTrue(any('replaced source' in e for e in p['validation']['errors']));self.assertEqual(p['replaced_video']['affected_scenes'],1)
  approved=self.c.patch(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/scenes/scene1',json={'approved':True});self.assertEqual(approved.status_code,200);self.assertNotIn('source_replacement_pending',approved.get_json()['scenes'][0])
 def test_invalid_upload_does_not_replace_and_short_source_unassigns(self):
  a=self.upload('S15.mp4',self.video[5]).get_json()['assets'][0];p=server.load(self.pid);p['scenes']=[self.scene(a['id'])];server.save(p)
  failed=self.upload('S15.mp4',b'invalid');self.assertEqual(failed.status_code,400);p=server.load(self.pid);self.assertEqual((self.root/p['assets'][0]['path']).read_bytes(),self.video[5]);self.assertEqual(p['scenes'][0]['asset_id'],a['id'])
  result=self.upload('S15.mp4',self.video[2]).get_json();self.assertEqual(result['replaced_video']['unassigned_scenes'],1);self.assertIsNone(result['scenes'][0]['asset_id']);self.assertIn('too short',result['scenes'][0]['replacement_issue'])
 def test_same_number_different_filename_and_existing_duplicates(self):
  first=self.upload('S14.mp4',self.video[5]).get_json()['assets'][0];self.upload('S014.mp4',self.video[5]);p=server.load(self.pid);dup=dict(first,id='duplicate',path='sources/duplicate.mp4');(self.root/dup['path']).write_bytes(self.video[5]);p['assets'].append(dup);p['scenes']=[self.scene('duplicate')];server.save(p)
  result=self.upload('s14.MP4',self.video[5]).get_json();self.assertEqual(len(result['assets']),2);self.assertEqual(result['replaced_video']['replaced_count'],2);self.assertEqual(result['scenes'][0]['asset_id'],first['id']);self.assertFalse((self.root/'sources/duplicate.mp4').exists());self.assertEqual({x['original_name'] for x in result['assets']},{'s14.MP4','S014.mp4'})
 def test_analysis_busy_does_not_delete_current_file(self):

  try:r=self.upload('S14.mp4',self.video[2]);self.assertEqual(r.status_code,400);self.assertTrue((self.root/asset['path']).exists())
  finally:
   with _GUARD:_RUNNING.discard(key)

import tempfile,unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
import app as server

class ReuseRightsTest(unittest.TestCase):
 def setUp(self):
  self.temp=tempfile.TemporaryDirectory();self.patch=patch.object(server,'PROJECTS',Path(self.temp.name));self.patch.start();self.c=server.app.test_client();self.pid=self.c.post('/api/projects',json={'title':'Rights test'}).get_json()['id'];self.root=Path(self.temp.name)/self.pid
  p=server.load(self.pid);(self.root/'sources/a.mp4').write_bytes(b'original');p['assets']=[{'id':'a','name':'a.mp4','original_name':'a.mp4','path':'sources/a.mp4','kind':'video','duration':20,'source':'https://example.com/source','people':[],'category':'general','verified':True,'rights':'reviewed_other','notes':'Automatic media approval'}];p['scenes']=[{'id':'s','asset_id':'a','frame_in':0,'frame_out':120,'in_s':0,'transition':'cut','focus':'scene','person':'','notes':'','approved':True,'srt_blocks':[1,1],'emotion':'neutral','purpose':'test','category':'general','profile':'documentary','motion':'auto'}];server.save(p)
 def tearDown(self):self.patch.stop();self.temp.cleanup()
 def put(self,data):return self.c.put(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/assets/a/reuse-rights',json=data)
 def test_approval_requires_attestation_basis_and_specific_details(self):
  for data in [{'decision':'approved'}, {'decision':'approved','basis':'license','details':'contract','attested':False}, {'decision':'approved','basis':'license','details':'','attested':True}]:
   self.assertEqual(self.put(data).status_code,400)
  result=self.put({'decision':'approved','basis':'license','details':'License from publisher for this video; expires in 2028.','attested':True});self.assertEqual(result.status_code,200,result.get_data(as_text=True));p=server.load(self.pid);record=p['assets'][0]['reuse_review'];self.assertEqual(record['decision'],'approved');self.assertEqual(record['basis'],'license');self.assertFalse(record['independent_verification']);self.assertFalse(p['scenes'][0]['approved']);self.assertEqual((self.root/'sources/a.mp4').read_bytes(),b'original')
 def test_unreviewed_auto_approval_is_not_rights_clearance(self):
  p=server.load(self.pid);a=p['assets'][0];errors,_=server.scene_review(p,0,p['scenes'][0],a,{},0,final=True);self.assertTrue(any('reuse-rights approval' in e for e in errors));self.assertEqual(self.put({'decision':'not_approved','details':'Pending rights'}).status_code,200);p=server.load(self.pid);errors,_=server.scene_review(p,0,p['scenes'][0],p['assets'][0],{},0,final=True);self.assertTrue(any('reuse-rights approval' in e for e in errors))
 def test_invalid_identifier_and_revocation(self):
  self.assertEqual(self.c.put(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/assets/no-such/reuse-rights',json={'decision':'unreviewed'}).status_code,404)
  self.put({'decision':'approved','basis':'permission','details':'Written permission from rights holder','attested':True});self.assertEqual(self.put({'decision':'unreviewed'}).status_code,200);self.assertEqual(server.load(self.pid)['assets'][0]['reuse_review']['decision'],'unreviewed')
 def test_bulk_approval_atomic_and_stale_list(self):
  p=server.load(self.pid);p['assets'].append({'id':'b','name':'b.mp4','original_name':'b.mp4','path':'sources/b.mp4','kind':'video','duration':20,'source':'second','people':[],'category':'general','verified':False,'rights':'unreviewed','notes':''});server.save(p)
  url=f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/assets/reuse-rights';data={'asset_ids':['a','b'],'basis':'other','details':'Permissions for both files for this production','attested':True}
  for invalid in [dict(data,attested=False),dict(data,details=' '),dict(data,asset_ids=['a']),dict(data,asset_ids=['a','a'])]:
   self.assertEqual(self.c.put(url,json=invalid).status_code,400);self.assertTrue(all('reuse_review' not in a for a in server.load(self.pid)['assets']))
  self.assertEqual(self.c.put(url,json=data).status_code,200);p=server.load(self.pid)
  self.assertTrue(all(a['reuse_review']['decision']=='approved' and a['reuse_review']['details']==data['details'] and not a['reuse_review']['independent_verification'] for a in p['assets']));self.assertFalse(p['scenes'][0]['approved']);self.assertEqual((self.root/'sources/a.mp4').read_bytes(),b'original')
  self.assertEqual(self.c.put(url,json=dict(data,details='Updated permissions')).status_code,200);self.assertEqual(server.load(self.pid)['assets'][0]['reuse_review_history'][0]['details'],data['details'])

==================== FILE: tests/test_scene_exports.py ====================
import json, subprocess, tempfile, time, unittest, zipfile
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
import cv2, imageio_ffmpeg
import numpy as np
import app as server
import scene_exports

class SceneExportsTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp=tempfile.TemporaryDirectory();self.patch=patch.object(server,'PROJECTS',Path(self.temp.name));self.patch.start()
        self.c=server.app.test_client();self.pid=self.c.post('/api/projects',json={'title':'Scene test'}).get_json()['id'];self.root=Path(self.temp.name)/self.pid
        p=server.load(self.pid);p['scenes']=[]
        for i in range(3):
            p['scenes'].append({'id':f'{i+1:016x}','frame_in':[0,23,61][i],'frame_out':[23,61,90][i],'asset_id':None,'approved':False,'emotion':'calm','purpose':'context','focus':'picture','in_s':0,'transition':'cut','person':'','notes':'','context_only':False})
        server.save(p);self.updated=p['updated'];preview=self.root/'.cache/continuous_preview';preview.mkdir(parents=True)
        ff=imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe();subprocess.run([ff,'-y','-v','error','-f','lavfi','-i','testsrc2=size=160x90:rate=30:duration=3','-f','lavfi','-i','sine=frequency=440:duration=3','-c:v','libx264','-pix_fmt','yuv420p','-c:a','aac','-shortest',str(preview/'preview.mp4')],check=True)
        (preview/'status.json').write_text(json.dumps({'status':'done','progress':100,'updated':self.updated}))
    def tearDown(self):
        scene_exports.JOBS.pop(self.pid,None);self.patch.stop();self.temp.cleanup()
    def test_preview_source_zip_has_named_frame_accurate_clips(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.c.post(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/scene-exports',json={'source':'render'}).status_code,400)
        self.assertEqual(self.c.post(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}/scene-exports',json={'source':'preview'}).status_code,202)
        for _ in range(200):
            state=scene_exports.status(self.root,self.pid,self.updated)
            if state['status'] not in ('running','queued'):break
            time.sleep(.05)
        self.assertEqual(state['status'],'done',state)
        out=self.c.get(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}').get_json();self.assertEqual(out['scene_exports']['source_kind'],'preview')
        archive=self.root/'renders/scene_exports'/state['output'];response=self.c.get(out['scene_exports']['url']);self.assertEqual(response.status_code,200);response.close()
        with zipfile.ZipFile(archive) as z:
            self.assertEqual(z.namelist(),['C01.mp4','C02.mp4','C03.mp4'])
            z.extractall(self.root/'clips')
        source=cv2.VideoCapture(str(self.root/'.cache/continuous_preview/preview.mp4'))
        for i in range(3):
            clip=self.root/'clips'/scene_exports.scene_name(i+1);cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(clip));self.assertEqual(int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)),[23,38,29][i])
            ok,frame=cap.read();cap.release();self.assertTrue(ok)
            source.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,[0,23,61][i]);ok,expected=source.read();self.assertTrue(ok)
            self.assertLess(float(np.mean(np.abs(frame.astype(float)-expected.astype(float)))),6)
            info=subprocess.run([imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe(),'-hide_banner','-i',str(clip)],capture_output=True,text=True).stderr;self.assertIn('Audio:',info)
        source.release()
        p=server.load(self.pid);p['title']='Change';server.save(p)
        self.assertEqual(self.c.get(f'/api/projects/{self.pid}').get_json()['scene_exports']['status'],'stale')
        self.assertTrue(archive.exists())
    def test_source_only_script_has_safe_legacy_export_name(self):
        self.assertEqual(scene_exports.clip_filename({'plan_row':{'strict_production':True,'clip_id':''}},2),'C02.mp4')

    def test_scene_number_scales_beyond_99(self):
        self.assertEqual([scene_exports.scene_name(i) for i in [1,9,10,99,100,1000]],['C01.mp4','C09.mp4','C10.mp4','C99.mp4','C100.mp4','C1000.mp4'])

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()
