==================== FILE: tests/test_script_draft.py ====================
import copy
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
import imageio_ffmpeg
import app as server
from test_section_script import SCRIPT as LEGACY_SCRIPT, BLOCKS

SCRIPT=(LEGACY_SCRIPT[:LEGACY_SCRIPT.index("### Srt01-B")] + LEGACY_SCRIPT[LEGACY_SCRIPT.index("## Final production checks"):]).replace("### Srt01-A", "### Srt01").replace("00:00:00.000–00:00:02.000 (2.000 s).", "00:00:00.000–00:00:04.000 (4.000 s).")

class ScriptDraftTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_draft_shows_sources_but_cannot_export_or_change_inputs(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, patch.object(server,'PROJECTS',Path(tmp)):
            client=server.app.test_client();pid=client.post('/api/projects',json={'title':'Synthetic'}).get_json()['id'];root=Path(tmp)/pid
            p=server.load(pid);p['srt']={'blocks':copy.deepcopy(BLOCKS),'name':'unchanged.srt'};p['production_script']={'name':'untouched.md','text':SCRIPT};p['picture_policy']='clips_only'
            (root/'inputs/captions.srt').write_text('Original names exactly')
            ff=imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()
            for name in ['S01.mp4','S02.mp4']:
                path=root/'sources'/name
                subprocess.run([ff,'-y','-v','error','-f','lavfi','-i','testsrc2=size=160x90:rate=30:duration=6','-c:v','libx264','-pix_fmt','yuv420p',str(path)],check=True)
                p['assets'].append({'id':name,'name':name,'original_name':name,'kind':'video','duration':6,'path':'sources/'+name,'verified':False,'source':'','people':[],'category':'general','rights':'unreviewed','notes':''})
            server.save(p)
            response=client.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/script-draft',json={});self.assertEqual(response.status_code,200,response.get_data(as_text=True))
            saved=server.load(pid);self.assertTrue(saved['review_only_draft']);self.assertEqual(len(saved['scenes']),1)
            self.assertIsNone(saved['scenes'][0]['asset_id']);self.assertFalse(any(a.get('derived_from') for a in saved['assets']));self.assertIn('no frame was extracted',saved['script_application']['issues'][0])
            self.assertEqual(saved['scenes'][0]['plan_row']['required_caption'],'“Context label”')
            self.assertEqual(saved['srt']['blocks'],BLOCKS);self.assertEqual(saved['production_script']['text'],SCRIPT);self.assertEqual(saved['picture_policy'],'clips_only')
            self.assertEqual((root/'inputs/captions.srt').read_text(),'Original names exactly')
            self.assertEqual(client.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/render',json={}).status_code,400)
            self.assertEqual(client.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/continuous-preview',json={}).status_code,400)
            self.assertEqual(client.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/scene-exports',json={'source':'preview'}).status_code,400)
            self.assertEqual(client.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/script-draft',json={}).status_code,400)
            names=[a['original_name'] for a in saved['assets'] if not a.get('derived_from')]
            moving=client.post(f'/api/projects/{pid}/moving-review',json={});self.assertEqual(moving.status_code,200,moving.get_data(as_text=True))
            changed=server.load(pid);self.assertTrue(changed['script_moving_only']);self.assertIsNone(changed['scenes'][0]['asset_id']);self.assertTrue(changed['scenes'][0]['missing_video_out'])
            self.assertEqual(changed['srt']['blocks'],BLOCKS);self.assertEqual(changed['production_script']['text'],SCRIPT)
            self.assertEqual(names,[a['original_name'] for a in changed['assets'] if not a.get('derived_from')]);self.assertFalse(server.check(changed)['can_export'])

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

==================== FILE: tests/test_section_script.py ====================
import unittest
from section_script import parse_section_script
from document_plan import rows_for

SCRIPT='''# Production

## Project instructions
Use the source footage.

## Source clip register
| Clip | Source | Original window or extraction time | Visible content | Treatment and restrictions |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| C02 | `S01.mp4` | Frame 00:00:01.000 | A sign | Keep original frame. |
| C09 | `S02.mp4` | 00:00:02.000–00:00:04.000 (OUT exclusive) | A street | Wide view. |

## Srt01

**Narration timeline** 00:00:00.000–00:00:04.000 · **Duration** 4.000 s

**Exact narration**

> Approved words

**Visual purpose** Context.

### Srt01-A

- **Narration placement** 00:00:00.000–00:00:02.000 (2.000 s).
- **Source** `S01.mp4` · **Clip** C02 · **Treatment** STILL HOLD.
- **Extraction** Source frame 00:00:01.000 · hold for 2.000 s in this block. Source IN/OUT do not apply to this hold.
- **Actually visible** A sign.
- **Edit and continuity** Start this shot. Keep the original frame.
- **Editorial caption** “Context label”
- **Audio** Mute source. Continue narration.
- **Meaning and limits** Not proof.

### Srt01-B

- **Narration placement** 00:00:02.000–00:00:04.000 (2.000 s).
- **Source** `S02.mp4` · **Clip** C09 · **Treatment** NORMAL-SPEED VIDEO.
- **Extraction** Source IN 00:00:02.000 · source OUT 00:00:04.000 (exclusive).
- **Actually visible** A street.
- **Edit and continuity** Start this shot.
- **Editorial caption** “Street context”
- **Audio** Mute source. Continue narration.
- **Meaning and limits** No evidence implied.

## Final production checks
Review everything.
'''
BLOCKS=[{'number':1,'start_ms':0,'end_ms':4000,'text':'Different words'}]

class SectionScriptTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_reads_shots_clocks_text_and_blocks_unsupported_work(self):
        rows= parse_section_script(SCRIPT,BLOCKS)
        self.assertEqual([r['clip_id'] for r in rows],['C02','C09'])
        self.assertEqual([r['requested_treatment'] for r in rows],['STILL HOLD','VIDEO'])
        self.assertEqual((rows[1]['source_in_ms'],rows[1]['source_out_ms']),(2000,4000))
        self.assertTrue(all(r['narration_mismatch'] and r['import_blockers'] for r in rows))
        p={'production_script':{'text':SCRIPT},'srt':{'blocks':BLOCKS}}
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'exactly one shot'):rows_for(p)
    def test_rejects_conflicting_register_clock_and_unknown_fields(self):
        for bad in [SCRIPT.replace('S02.mp4` · **Clip** C09','S03.mp4` · **Clip** C09'),
                    SCRIPT.replace('00:00:02.000–00:00:04.000 (2.000 s).','00:00:02.100–00:00:04.000 (2.000 s).'),
                    SCRIPT.replace('- **Audio** Mute source. Continue narration.','- **Unknown direction** Change the edit.\n- **Audio** Mute source. Continue narration.',1)]:
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):parse_section_script(bad,BLOCKS)
    def test_keeps_uploaded_srt_unchanged(self):
        original=BLOCKS[0]['text'];parse_section_script(SCRIPT,BLOCKS);self.assertEqual(BLOCKS[0]['text'],original)

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

class SourceOnlySectionTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_explicit_source_ranges_work_without_clip_reference_or_register(self):
        text=SCRIPT[:SCRIPT.index('## Source clip register')]+SCRIPT[SCRIPT.index('## Srt01'):]
        text=text.replace(' · **Clip** C02','').replace(' · **Clip** C09','')
        rows=parse_section_script(text,BLOCKS)
        self.assertEqual([r['source_file'] for r in rows],['S01.mp4','S02.mp4'])
        self.assertEqual([r['clip_id'] for r in rows],['',''])
        self.assertEqual((rows[1]['source_in_ms'],rows[1]['source_out_ms']),(2000,4000))

class OneCueSectionTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_section_with_unlettered_cue(self):
        text=SCRIPT[:SCRIPT.index('### Srt01-A')]+SCRIPT[SCRIPT.index('### Srt01-B'):]
        text=text.replace('### Srt01-B','### Srt01').replace('00:00:02.000–00:00:04.000 (2.000 s).','00:00:00.000–00:00:04.000 (4.000 s).').replace('source OUT 00:00:04.000','source OUT 00:00:06.000').replace('00:00:02.000–00:00:04.000 (OUT exclusive)','00:00:02.000–00:00:06.000 (OUT exclusive)')
        rows,mode=rows_for({'production_script':{'text':text},'srt':{'blocks':BLOCKS}})
        self.assertEqual(mode,'detailed_production_script');self.assertEqual(rows[0]['shot_id'],'Srt01')

==================== FILE: tests/test_shot_groups.py ====================
import json
import unittest
from automatic import repetition_errors
from edl import assemble, parse

class ShotGroupTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def make(self, grouped=False):
        rows=[]
        for i, (file, start) in enumerate([('a.mp4',0),('b.mp4',0),('a.mp4',20)], 1):
            row={'block':i,'start':f'00:00:{(i-1)*4:02}.000','end':f'00:00:{i*4:02}.000',
                 'beat':i,'media_file':file,'source_in':f'00:00:{start:02}.000',
                 'source_out':f'00:00:{start+4:02}.000','visible_content':'Same description'}
            if grouped and i in (1,3):row['footage_group']='shot A'
            rows.append(row)
        p={'srt':{'blocks':[{'number':i,'start_ms':(i-1)*4000,'end_ms':i*4000,'text':'Line'} for i in range(1,4)]},
           'picture_plan':{'beats':parse(json.dumps(rows).encode(),'.json')},
           'assets':[{'id':name,'name':name,'original_name':name,'kind':'video','duration':100} for name in ('a.mp4','b.mp4')],
           'repetition_policy':{'cooldown_slots':6},'allow_archive_reuse':True}
        assemble(p,[{'srt_blocks':[i,i]} for i in range(1,4)])
        return p

    def test_repeated_description_is_not_evidence_of_a_shot(self):
        p=self.make()
        self.assertFalse(any(s.get('footage_group') for s in p['scenes']))
        self.assertEqual(repetition_errors(p),[])

    def test_explicit_group_catches_non_overlapping_return(self):
        p=self.make(True)
        self.assertIn('same shot', repetition_errors(p)[0])

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

==================== FILE: tests/test_source_clip_names.py ====================
import unittest
from document_plan import script_beats
from production import instructions, matching_assets

class SourceClipNamesTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_s_sources_map_to_actual_names(self):
        table='| # | SRT | Timeline in → out | Source → file | Src IN → OUT | Treatment · transition |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | 1 | 00:00:00.000 → 00:00:05.000 | S01 → S01.mp4 | 00:00:10.000 → 00:00:15.000 | Native footage · cut |\n| 2 | 2 | 00:00:05.000 → 00:00:10.000 | S02 → S02.mp4 | 00:00:20.000 → 00:00:25.000 | Native footage · cut |\n'
        p={'production_script':{'text':table},'srt':{'blocks':[{'number':1,'start_ms':0,'end_ms':5000,'text':'First'}, {'number':2,'start_ms':5000,'end_ms':10000,'text':'Second'}]}}
        rows=script_beats(p)
        self.assertEqual([r['source_file'] for r in rows],['S01.mp4','S02.mp4'])
        p['assets']=[{'id':'a','name':'S01.mp4','original_name':'S01.mp4'},{'id':'b','name':'v1.mp4','original_name':'v1.mp4'}]
        self.assertEqual([x['id'] for x in matching_assets(p,'S01.mp4')],['a'])
        self.assertEqual([x['id'] for x in matching_assets(p,'v1.mp4')],['b'])
        self.assertEqual(matching_assets(p,'S1.mp4'),[])
        self.assertEqual(matching_assets({'assets':[p['assets'][1]]},'S01.mp4'),[])
    def test_legacy_table_and_prose_recognise_s_and_preserve_v(self):
        header='| Beat | SRT blocks | Timeline IN → OUT | Media | Source IN/OUT (planned) |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n'
        rows='| 1 | 1 | 00:00:00.000 → 00:00:05.000 | S01.mp4 | 00:00:10.000 → 00:00:15.000 |\n| 2 | 2 | 00:00:05.000 → 00:00:10.000 | v2.mp4 | 00:00:20.000 → 00:00:25.000 |\n'
        p={'production_script':{'text':header+rows},'srt':{'blocks':[{'number':1,'start_ms':0,'end_ms':5000},{'number':2,'start_ms':5000,'end_ms':10000}]}}
        self.assertEqual([r['source_file'] for r in script_beats(p)],['S01.mp4','v2.mp4'])
        p['production_script']['text']='SRT 1 S01.mp4 in: 2 out: 7\nSRT 2 v2.mp4 in: 10 out: 15'
        self.assertEqual([r['asset'] for r in instructions(p)[0]],['S01.mp4','v2.mp4'])

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

==================== FILE: tests/test_source_overlap_validation.py ====================
import unittest
import app

class SourceOverlapValidationTest(unittest.TestCase):
 def make(self, source_in, source_out, fi, fo):
  return {'in_s':source_in/1000,'frame_in':fi,'frame_out':fo,'transition':'cut','plan_row':{'strict_production':True,'source_file':'A.mp4','source_in_ms':source_in,'source_out_ms':source_out,'timeline_in_ms':round(fi*1000/30),'timeline_out_ms':round(fo*1000/30)}}
 def test_exact_ranges_touch_even_if_output_frames_round(self):
  asset={'original_name':'A.mp4'}
  first=self.make(41000,45266,0,128);second=self.make(45266,51366,128,311)
  a=app.source_overlap_interval(first,asset);b=app.source_overlap_interval(second,asset)
  self.assertEqual(a[1],b[0])
 def test_real_overlap_is_not_hidden(self):
  asset={'original_name':'A.mp4'}
  first=self.make(41000,45266,0,128);second=self.make(45000,51100,128,311)
  self.assertGreater(app.source_overlap_interval(first,asset)[1],app.source_overlap_interval(second,asset)[0])
 def test_edited_in_point_uses_actual_duration(self):
  s=self.make(41000,45266,0,128);s['in_s']=41.5
  self.assertAlmostEqual(app.source_overlap_interval(s,{'original_name':'A.mp4'})[1],41.5+128/30)

==================== FILE: tests/test_whisper_installer.py ====================
import hashlib
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
from tools import install_local_whisper as installer

class InstallerTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_verified_download_and_existing_destination(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            dest = Path(directory) / 'model'
            pieces = {f'model.bin.part-{i:02}': bytes([i]) * 3 for i in range(11)}
            pieces.update({'config.json': b'config', 'tokenizer.json': b'tokenizer', 'vocabulary.txt': b'vocab'})
            other = {k: hashlib.sha256(pieces[k]).hexdigest() for k in ('config.json','tokenizer.json','vocabulary.txt')}
            model = b''.join(pieces[f'model.bin.part-{i:02}'] for i in range(11))
            def download(name, out, h): out.write(pieces[name]);h.update(pieces[name])
            with patch.object(installer,'OTHER',other), patch.object(installer,'MODEL_HASH',hashlib.sha256(model).hexdigest()), patch.object(installer,'MODEL_SIZE',len(model)), patch.object(installer,'download',download):
                installer.install(dest)
                self.assertTrue(installer.valid(dest))
                installer.install(dest)
                (dest/'config.json').write_bytes(b'user content')
                with self.assertRaises(FileExistsError): installer.install(dest)
                self.assertEqual((dest/'config.json').read_bytes(),b'user content')

    def test_bad_download_leaves_no_partial_install(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            dest=Path(directory)/'model'
            def bad_download(name,out,h):out.write(b'bad');h.update(b'bad')
            with patch.object(installer,'download',bad_download):
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'Checksum'):installer.install(dest)
            self.assertFalse(dest.exists())
            self.assertEqual(list(Path(directory).iterdir()),[])

if __name__ == '__main__':unittest.main()

==================== END FILE: tests/test_whisper_installer.py ====================


==================== FILE: tests/test_yamnet_installer.py ====================
import hashlib
import io
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

from tools import install_local_yamnet as installer


class YamnetInstallerTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_verify_and_never_replace_destination(self):
        model=b'fake onnx model'
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, patch.object(installer,'SIZE',len(model)), patch.object(installer,'SHA256',hashlib.sha256(model).hexdigest()), patch.object(installer.urllib.request,'urlopen',return_value=io.BytesIO(model)):
            dest=Path(tmp)/'model.onnx'
            installer.install(dest)
            self.assertEqual(dest.read_bytes(),model)
            installer.install(dest)
            dest.write_bytes(b'different')
            with self.assertRaises(FileExistsError):installer.install(dest)
            self.assertEqual(dest.read_bytes(),b'different')

    def test_bad_checksum_leaves_no_model(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, patch.object(installer,'SIZE',3), patch.object(installer,'SHA256','0'*64), patch.object(installer.urllib.request,'urlopen',return_value=io.BytesIO(b'bad')):
            dest=Path(tmp)/'model.onnx'
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):installer.install(dest)
            self.assertFalse(dest.exists())
            self.assertEqual(list(Path(tmp).iterdir()),[])

==================== FILE: tools/ci_local.sh ====================
#!/usr/bin/env sh
# What CI runs, in one command. Starts the app, runs the acceptance sweep, stops it.
set -e
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
PY=.venv/bin/python
[ -x "$PY" ] || PY=python3
"$PY" -m compileall -q . -x '\.venv|__pycache__'
"$PY" -c "import app; assert app.app.test_client().get('/').status_code == 200; print('app serves')"
"$PY" -m unittest discover -s tests -v
PORT=${STORYROOM_PORT:-8123}
STORYROOM_URL="http://127.0.0.1:$PORT" "$PY" -c "
import os, app
app.app.run(host='127.0.0.1', port=$PORT, debug=False)
" &
SERVER=$!
trap 'kill $SERVER 2>/dev/null' EXIT
for i in $(seq 1 60); do
  curl -sf -o /dev/null "http://127.0.0.1:$PORT/" && break
  sleep 1
done
STORYROOM_URL="http://127.0.0.1:$PORT" "$PY" tools/runtime_sweep.py --runtimes 60 --work /tmp/ci-sweep --sources 8

==================== END FILE: tools/ci_local.sh ====================


==================== FILE: tools/install_local_whisper.py ====================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Install a pinned CTranslate2 Whisper small model; inference remains offline.

Fetches a public, MIT-licensed mirror of Systran's Whisper-small conversion from
GitHub, verifies pinned SHA-256 values, and installs outside version control.
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import os
import tempfile
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DEFAULT = ROOT / '.cache' / 'storyroom-models' / 'whisper-small'
REPO = 'YankS1/faster-whisper-mirror'
COMMIT = '40efd16c5a10f523b0eff5c6f6eca5cdd561e90d'
MODEL_SIZE = 483546902
MODEL_HASH = '3e305921506d8872816023e4c273e75d2419fb89b24da97b4fe7bce14170d671'
OTHER = {
    'config.json': 'b55496ac7940a7ae47d2c01eab40edfd8701feec1229d9cce3b40014383fb828',
    'tokenizer.json': 'fb7b63191e9bb045082c79fd742a3106a12c99513ab30df4a0d47fa6cb6fd0ab',
    'vocabulary.txt': '34ce3fe1c5041027b3f8d42912270993f986dbc4bb34cf27f951e34a1e453913',
}


def file_hash(path):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open('rb') as src:
        for block in iter(lambda: src.read(1024 * 1024), b''): h.update(block)
    return h.hexdigest()


def valid(dest):
    try:
        return (dest / 'model.bin').stat().st_size == MODEL_SIZE and file_hash(dest / 'model.bin') == MODEL_HASH and all(file_hash(dest / name) == digest for name, digest in OTHER.items())
    except OSError:
        return False


def download(name, out, digest):
    url = f'https://api.github.com/repos/{REPO}/contents/small/{name}?ref={COMMIT}'
    request = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={'Accept': 'application/vnd.github.raw+json', 'User-Agent': 'Storyroom-local-model-installer'})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=120) as source:
        while True:
            chunk = source.read(1024 * 1024)
            if not chunk: break
            out.write(chunk); digest.update(chunk)


def install(dest):
    dest = Path(dest).expanduser().resolve()
    if dest.exists():
        if valid(dest): print('Already installed and verified:', dest); return
        raise FileExistsError('Destination exists but does not match the pinned model; choose another destination. Nothing was replaced.')
    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='storyroom-whisper-download-', dir=dest.parent) as tmp:
        tmp = Path(tmp)
        for name, expected in OTHER.items():
            print('Downloading', name, flush=True)
            h = hashlib.sha256()
            with (tmp / name).open('wb') as out: download(name, out, h)
            if h.hexdigest() != expected: raise ValueError('Checksum mismatch for ' + name)
        h = hashlib.sha256()
        with (tmp / 'model.bin').open('wb') as out:
            for i in range(11):
                name = f'model.bin.part-{i:02}'
                print('Downloading', name, flush=True); download(name, out, h)
        if (tmp / 'model.bin').stat().st_size != MODEL_SIZE or h.hexdigest() != MODEL_HASH:
            raise ValueError('Model checksum or size mismatch; no model was installed.')
        # Rename the verified directory only after all files pass. Never replace an existing directory.
        os.rename(tmp, dest)
    print('Installed verified local model:', dest)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--dest', default=str(DEFAULT), help='Empty destination directory; defaults to the local model cache')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    install(args.dest)

==================== END FILE: tools/install_local_whisper.py ====================


==================== FILE: tools/install_local_yamnet.py ====================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Install an externally hosted YAMNet ONNX model for local CPU inference.

The model file is hosted by amplify-project/Physiological-Signals, whose
repository is AGPL-3.0. It identifies the file as YAMNet but does not document
its conversion provenance. See README for attribution and limitations.
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import os
import tempfile
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DEFAULT = ROOT / '.cache/storyroom-models/yamnet.onnx'
URL = 'https://api.github.com/repos/amplify-project/Physiological-Signals/git/blobs/15658631e3ab2705fbf72dc302b9df8a1304a8fa'
SHA256 = 'b96e5ca9359eb99ba3e8e372729bd61ef24274c8aa0d53022bbbfb04388a4527'
SIZE = 16093366


def install(dest):
    dest = Path(dest).expanduser().resolve()
    if dest.exists():
        if dest.is_file() and dest.stat().st_size == SIZE and hashlib.sha256(dest.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == SHA256:
            print('Already installed and verified:', dest); return
        raise FileExistsError('Destination exists but is not the pinned model; nothing was replaced.')
    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='storyroom-yamnet-', dir=dest.parent) as tmp:
        path = Path(tmp) / 'model.onnx'; digest = hashlib.sha256()
        request = urllib.request.Request(URL, headers={'Accept': 'application/vnd.github.raw+json', 'User-Agent': 'Storyroom-local-model-installer'})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=120) as src, path.open('wb') as out:
            while True:
                chunk = src.read(1024 * 1024)
                if not chunk: break
                out.write(chunk); digest.update(chunk)
        if path.stat().st_size != SIZE or digest.hexdigest() != SHA256:
            raise ValueError('Model checksum or size mismatch; nothing was installed.')
        # Use a hard link to avoid overwriting an existing destination in a race.
        os.link(path, dest)
    print('Installed verified local model:', dest)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--dest', default=str(DEFAULT))
    install(parser.parse_args().dest)

==================== END FILE: tools/install_local_yamnet.py ====================


==================== FILE: tools/runtime_sweep.py ====================
"""Prove that one code path serves every runtime.

The claim this script tests is narrow and checkable: a programme of any length is read,
planned, built, approved and exported by the same functions, and the output is exactly as
long as its own narration. Nothing here is sized per minute; the input is generated from a
runtime, and every assertion is derived from the result of that build.

It is a regression test, not a demo. Run it after touching the build path:

    python tools/runtime_sweep.py --runtimes 60,300,600,3600 --work /tmp/sweep

What it checks, for each runtime:

  * the SRT parses, at one or two digit hours and any millisecond precision
  * the built timeline starts at frame 0, is contiguous, and ends exactly on the SRT
  * every scene carries the SRT block its own plan row named
  * assembly reports no validation errors, and no repetition/capacity complaint
  * a block-range rebuild of one block leaves every other beat byte-identical
  * the total duration is unchanged by that rebuild

Fixtures are generated, never reused: narration is a tone with an amplitude envelope (so
pause detection has something real to find), SRT blocks are 3-9 s, and the block map places
every row in a source interval that is never reused and never runs past the clip.
"""
import argparse
import csv
import json
import math
import os
import random
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
import wave
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
BASE = os.environ.get('STORYROOM_URL', 'http://127.0.0.1:8003')
FPS = 30


def ffmpeg():
    import imageio_ffmpeg
    return imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()


def call(path, method='GET', data=None, ctype='application/json', raw=None):
    body = raw if raw is not None else (None if data is None else json.dumps(data).encode())
    request = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=body, headers={'Content-Type': ctype}, method=method)
    try:
        response = urllib.request.urlopen(request)
        text = response.read()
        return response.status, (json.loads(text) if text else {})
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
        text = error.read()
        try:
            return error.code, json.loads(text or b'{}')
        except ValueError:
            return error.code, {'error': text.decode()[:300]}


def upload(pid, path, kind, name=None):
    boundary = '----sweep'
    def part(key, value): return f'--{boundary}\r\nContent-Disposition: form-data; name="{key}"\r\n\r\n{value}\r\n'
    filename = name or os.path.basename(path)
    payload = (part('kind', kind) +
               f'--{boundary}\r\nContent-Disposition: form-data; name="file"; filename="{filename}"\r\n'
               'Content-Type: application/octet-stream\r\n\r\n').encode()
    payload += Path(path).read_bytes() + f'\r\n--{boundary}--\r\n'.encode()
    return call(f'/api/projects/{pid}/upload', 'POST', raw=payload,
                ctype='multipart/form-data; boundary=' + boundary)


def clock(seconds, digits=2, ms_digits=3):
    total = int(round(seconds * 1000))
    hours, rest = divmod(total, 3600000)
    minutes, rest = divmod(rest, 60000)
    secs, millis = divmod(rest, 1000)
    return '%0*d:%02d:%02d,%0*d' % (digits, hours, minutes, secs, ms_digits, millis)


def write_srt(path, runtime, rng, digits=2, ms_digits=3):
    blocks, cursor, number = [], 0.0, 1
    while cursor < runtime - 0.05:
        length = min(rng.choice([3.4, 4.2, 5.0, 6.1, 7.3, 8.0, 9.0]), runtime - cursor)
        if runtime - (cursor + length) < 3.0:
            length = runtime - cursor
        end = round(cursor + length, 3)
        blocks.append((number, round(cursor, 3), end))
        number += 1
        cursor = end
    cues = []
    for number, start, end in blocks:
        cues.append('%d\n%s --> %s\nNarration line %d.' % (number, clock(start, digits, ms_digits), clock(end, digits, ms_digits), number))
    Path(path).write_text('\n\n'.join(cues) + '\n')
    return blocks


def write_narration(path, runtime, rate=22050):
    with wave.open(str(path), 'wb') as handle:
        handle.setnchannels(1); handle.setsampwidth(2); handle.setframerate(rate)
        frames, chunk = int(runtime * rate), []
        for index in range(frames):
            moment = index / rate
            envelope = 0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * 0.31 * moment)
            chunk.append(struct.pack('<h', int(6000 * envelope * math.sin(2 * math.pi * 180 * moment))))
            if len(chunk) >= rate:
                handle.writeframes(b''.join(chunk)); chunk = []
        if chunk:
            handle.writeframes(b''.join(chunk))


def make_clips(directory, count, length, width=1280, height=720):
    directory = Path(directory)
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    existing = sorted(directory.glob('src*.mp4'))
    if len(existing) >= count:
        return existing[:count]
    for index in range(1, count + 1):
        target = directory / f'src{index}.mp4'
        if target.exists():
            continue
        # Each channel is masked to a byte: past about 40 clips the unmasked arithmetic
        # overflowed a channel into three hex digits and ffmpeg rejected the colour, which
        # only showed up when a sweep needed a large source pool.
        colour = '0x%02x%02x%02x' % ((20 + index * 6) % 256, (70 + index * 5) % 256, (130 + index * 4) % 256)
        subprocess.run([ffmpeg(), '-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'error', '-y', '-f', 'lavfi',
                        '-i', f'color=c={colour}:size={width}x{height}:rate={FPS}:duration={length}',
                        '-vf', ("drawbox=x='mod(t*40\\,900)':y='mod(t*25+40\\,500)':w=220:h=180:color=white@0.85:t=fill,"
                                f"drawtext=text='SOURCE {index}':x=40:y=40:fontsize=64:fontcolor=white@0.9"),
                        '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'veryfast', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p', '-g', '90', str(target)],
                       check=True)
    return sorted(directory.glob('src*.mp4'))[:count]


def write_map(path, blocks, sources, clip_length):
    """One beat per block, each in its own non-overlapping interval, never past the clip."""
    headroom = clip_length * 0.95
    cursor = {source.name: 0.0 for source in sources}
    rows = []
    for number, start, end in blocks:
        length = end - start
        chosen = next((s for s in sorted(sources, key=lambda s: cursor[s.name]) if cursor[s.name] + length <= headroom), None)
        if chosen is None:
            raise SystemExit(f'runtime needs more footage than {len(sources)} x {clip_length}s holds '
                             f'(failed at block {number}, a {length:.1f}s beat); raise --sources or --clip-seconds')
        offset = cursor[chosen.name]
        # A whole second of gutter between two uses of the same file. The 0.05 s that used
        # to be here was finer than the rounding the pipeline applies (source clocks are
        # written to the millisecond and beats are laid on whole frames), so consecutive
        # intervals occasionally overlapped by a quarter second and the engine correctly -
        # though confusingly - reported a genuine repeat in its own footage.
        cursor[chosen.name] = offset + length + 1.0
        rows.append({'block': number, 'start': start, 'end': end, 'beat': len(rows) + 1,
                     'media_file': chosen.name, 'source_in': round(offset, 3), 'source_out': round(offset + length, 3)})
    with open(path, 'w', newline='') as handle:
        writer = csv.writer(handle)
        writer.writerow(['block', 'start', 'end', 'beat', 'media_file', 'source_in', 'source_out'])
        for row in rows:
            def srt_clock(value):
                total = int(round(value * 1000))
                hours, rest = divmod(total, 3600000)
                minutes, rest = divmod(rest, 60000)
                secs, millis = divmod(rest, 1000)
                return '%02d:%02d:%02d.%03d' % (hours, minutes, secs, millis)
            writer.writerow([row['block'], srt_clock(row['start']), srt_clock(row['end']), row['beat'],
                             row['media_file'], srt_clock(row['source_in']), srt_clock(row['source_out'])])
    return rows


def check(name, runtime, work, clip_seconds=180, sources=8, narration=True):
    home = Path(work) / name
    home.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rng = random.Random(int(runtime))
    clips = make_clips(Path(work) / 'clips', sources, clip_seconds)
    # Exercise both cue dialects: standard for most, one-digit hours for a sub-hour piece.
    digits = 1 if runtime < 3600 and int(runtime) % 2 else 2
    blocks = write_srt(home / 'narration.srt', runtime, rng, digits=digits, ms_digits=3 if digits == 2 else 1)
    if narration:
        write_narration(home / 'voice.wav', runtime)
    write_map(home / 'map.csv', blocks, clips, clip_seconds)

    status, reply = call('/api/projects', 'POST', {'title': f'sweep {name}', 'picture_policy': 'clips_only'})
    project = reply.get('id') or (reply.get('project') or {}).get('id')
    if not project:
        raise SystemExit(f'{name}: could not create a project: {reply}')

    jobs = [(home / 'narration.srt', 'srt')]
    if narration:
        jobs.insert(0, (home / 'voice.wav', 'voiceover'))
    jobs += [(clip, 'asset') for clip in clips] + [(home / 'map.csv', 'srt_block_map')]
    for path, kind in jobs:
        status, reply = upload(project, path, kind)
        if status != 200:
            raise SystemExit(f'{name}: upload of {os.path.basename(str(path))} failed ({status}): {reply.get("error")}')

    started = time.time()
    status, reply = call(f'/api/projects/{project}/storyboard', 'POST', {'replace': True})
    build_seconds = time.time() - started
    if status != 200:
        raise SystemExit(f'{name}: build failed ({status}): {reply.get("error")}')
    scenes = reply['scenes']
    errors = list(reply['validation']['errors'])
    duration = scenes[-1]['frame_out'] / FPS

    problems = []
    if errors:
        problems.append(f'{len(errors)} validation error(s): {errors[:2]}')
        # A sweep with too few sources fails as a repetition complaint, which reads like an
        # engine bug. It is not: the cooldown is meant to stop a shot coming back within six
        # slots, so a runtime needing more than six distinct shots needs more than six files.
        # Say so, with the number that would fix it, instead of leaving it to be worked out.
        if any('cooldown' in str(e) for e in errors):
            needed = len(scenes)
            problems.append(f'this fixture has too few sources for {needed} beats: pass '
                            f'--sources {needed} or more (the cooldown is six slots)')
    if abs(duration - runtime) > 0.05:
        problems.append(f'duration {duration:.2f}s is not {runtime:.2f}s')
    if scenes[0]['frame_in'] != 0:
        problems.append(f'timeline does not start at frame 0 ({scenes[0]["frame_in"]})')
    gaps = [i + 1 for i in range(1, len(scenes)) if scenes[i]['frame_in'] != scenes[i - 1]['frame_out']]
    if gaps:
        problems.append(f'timeline is not contiguous at scene(s) {gaps[:5]}')
    mismatched = [i + 1 for i, scene in enumerate(scenes)
                  if scene.get('plan_row') and scene['plan_row'].get('srt_blocks')
                  and scene['srt_blocks'][0] != scene['plan_row']['srt_blocks'][0]]
    if mismatched:
        problems.append(f'scene(s) {mismatched[:5]} carry an SRT block their plan row did not name')

    # Rebuild one block: everything else must survive untouched, and the length must hold.
    # Keyed by the plan row, not the scene id: a rebuilt scene is a new record by design,
    # so comparing ids would report every beat as changed.
    def fingerprint(scene):
        return (scene.get('plan_row_id'), tuple(scene['srt_blocks']), scene['frame_in'], scene['frame_out'],
                round(float(scene['in_s']), 3), scene['asset_id'])
    before = {scene.get('plan_row_id'): fingerprint(scene) for scene in scenes}
    middle = blocks[len(blocks) // 2][0]
    started = time.time()
    status, rebuilt = call(f'/api/projects/{project}/storyboard', 'POST', {'replace': True, 'blocks': [middle]})
    rebuild_seconds = time.time() - started
    if status != 200:
        problems.append(f'block-range rebuild failed ({status}): {rebuilt.get("error")}')
    else:
        after = {scene.get('plan_row_id'): fingerprint(scene) for scene in rebuilt['scenes']}
        changed = [key for key in before if before[key] != after.get(key)]
        if len(changed) > 1:
            problems.append(f'a one-block rebuild changed {len(changed)} beats, not 1: {changed[:5]}')
        if rebuilt['scenes'][-1]['frame_out'] != scenes[-1]['frame_out']:
            problems.append('a one-block rebuild changed the total duration')

    verdict = 'PASS' if not problems else 'FAIL'
    print(f'{verdict}  {name:8s} {runtime:6.0f}s  blocks={len(blocks):3d} beats={len(scenes):3d} '
          f'build={build_seconds:6.1f}s rebuild={rebuild_seconds:5.2f}s duration={duration:8.2f}s '
          f'cue_digits={digits}')
    for problem in problems:
        print('       -', problem)
    return project, not problems


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    parser.add_argument('--runtimes', default='60,300,1080,3600',
                        help='comma-separated runtimes in seconds, e.g. 60,600,1800,3600')
    parser.add_argument('--work', default='/tmp/sweep', help='where generated fixtures and clips live')
    parser.add_argument('--clip-seconds', type=float, default=180)
    parser.add_argument('--sources', type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument('--no-narration', action='store_true', help='also prove the text-only path')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    failures = 0
    for value in [float(x) for x in args.runtimes.split(',') if x.strip()]:
        minutes = value / 60
        name = ('%02dmin' % int(minutes)) if minutes == int(minutes) else ('%03ds' % int(value))
        _, ok = check(name, value, args.work, args.clip_seconds, args.sources)
        failures += 0 if ok else 1
        if args.no_narration:
            _, ok = check(name + '_text', value, args.work, args.clip_seconds, args.sources, narration=False)
            failures += 0 if ok else 1
    print('\n%d failure(s)' % failures)
    sys.exit(1 if failures else 0)

==================== END FILE: tools/runtime_sweep.py ====================


==================== FILE: validate_production_package.py ====================
"""Validate a generated Storyroom production package before a new project is built.

Uses the editor's real SRT, Markdown beat-table and EDL parsers. This checks
documents/timing/source availability only; it is NOT face recognition, rights
clearance, or visual proof of what a shot depicts.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import json, sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import app as studio
from document_plan import rows_for
from production import matching_assets


def probe_duration(path):
    try:
        info = studio.probe(path)
        return float(info.get('duration') or 0), info.get('has_video', False)
    except Exception as exc:
        return 0.0, False


def load_sources(arg):
    path = Path(arg)
    if path.is_file() and path.suffix.lower() == '.json':
        data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        return {str(k): float(v) for k, v in data.items()}
    if not path.is_dir():
        raise RuntimeError('Source argument must be a directory of MP4s or a JSON map of filename -> duration.')
    result = {}
    for f in sorted(path.iterdir()):
        if f.is_file() and f.suffix.lower() in ['.mp4', '.mov']:
            duration, has_video = probe_duration(f)
            if has_video and duration > 0:
                result[f.name] = duration
    return result


def build_project(script_text, srt_text, source_durations):
    blocks = studio.parse_srt(srt_text)
    assets = []
    for i, (name, duration) in enumerate(source_durations.items(), 1):
        assets.append({
            'id': 'validation%03d' % i, 'name': name, 'original_name': name,
            'path': name, 'kind': 'video', 'duration': duration,
            'source': 'validation-only', 'people': [], 'category': 'general',
            'verified': True, 'rights': 'reviewed_other', 'notes': 'Validation placeholder.',
            'derived_from': None
        })
    return {
        'id': 'validation', 'schema_version': 1, 'title': 'Production package validation',
        'brief': '', 'people': [], 'assets': assets, 'scenes': [],
        'voiceover': {'name': 'narration', 'path': 'voiceover', 'duration': blocks[-1]['end_ms'] / 1000},
        'srt': {'blocks': blocks, 'name': 'captions.srt'},
        'production_script': {'name': 'production_script.md', 'text': script_text},
        'source_bindings': {}, 'picture_policy': 'clips_only',
        'transition_policy': 'clean_cuts_only',
        'repetition_policy': {'cooldown_slots': 6, 'source_interval_overlap_forbidden_within_window': True, 'visual_hash_max_distance': 4},
        'auto_assemble_uploads': True, 'manual_scene_edits': False
    }


def validate(script_path, srt_path, source_arg):
    script_text = Path(script_path).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
    srt_text = Path(srt_path).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
    source_durations = load_sources(source_arg)
    p = build_project(script_text, srt_text, source_durations)
    errors, warnings = [], []
    try:
        rows, mode = rows_for(p)
    except Exception as exc:
        return {'ready': False, 'errors': ['Parser rejected the production script: ' + str(exc)], 'warnings': []}
    if mode not in ['production_script_table', 'srt_block_map']:
        errors.append('No recognized Markdown beat table was found. Check the exact header row and the Unicode arrow →.')
    if not rows:
        errors.append('The beat table produced zero rows; a new project would build zero scenes.')
        return {'ready': False, 'errors': errors, 'warnings': warnings, 'mode': mode}
    blocks = p['srt']['blocks']
    expected_numbers = list(range(1, len(blocks) + 1))
    last_ms = blocks[-1]['end_ms']
    expected_frames = round(last_ms * .03)
    if rows[0]['timeline_in_ms'] != 0:
        errors.append('First beat does not start at 0 ms.')
    if rows[-1]['timeline_out_ms'] != last_ms:
        errors.append('Final beat OUT ' + str(rows[-1]['timeline_out_ms']) + ' ms does not equal final SRT end ' + str(last_ms) + ' ms.')
    if round(rows[-1]['timeline_out_ms'] * .03) != expected_frames:
        errors.append('Final 30 fps frame count does not match the SRT timeline.')
    covered = []
    for i, row in enumerate(rows, 1):
        pre = 'Beat ' + str(row.get('beat_id', i)) + ': '
        if i > 1 and row['timeline_in_ms'] != rows[i - 2]['timeline_out_ms']:
            errors.append(pre + 'timeline is not contiguous with the previous beat.')
        if row['timeline_out_ms'] <= row['timeline_in_ms']:
            errors.append(pre + 'empty or reversed timeline interval.')
        refs = row.get('srt_blocks') or []
        covered.extend(refs)
        if str(row.get('transition_out', 'cut')).lower() != 'cut':
            errors.append(pre + 'transition is not a clean cut.')
        if int(row.get('transition_handle_frames') or 0):
            errors.append(pre + 'clean cut has a transition handle.')
        source = str(row.get('source_file') or '').strip()
        if not source:
            errors.append(pre + 'no native MP4 filename.');continue
        matches = matching_assets(p, source)
        if len(matches) != 1:
            errors.append(pre + str(len(matches)) + ' uploaded files match ' + source + '; expected exactly one.');continue
        duration = float(matches[0]['duration'])
        sin = row.get('source_in_ms'); sout = row.get('source_out_ms')
        if sin is None or sout is None or sout <= sin:
            errors.append(pre + 'missing or invalid source IN/OUT milliseconds.');continue
        beat_duration = row['timeline_out_ms'] - row['timeline_in_ms']
        if abs((sout - sin) - beat_duration) > 1:
            errors.append(pre + 'source interval duration differs from the timeline beat by more than 1 ms.')
        if sin < 0 or sout / 1000 > duration + .034:
            errors.append(pre + 'source interval exceeds ' + source + ' (' + str(round(duration, 3)) + ' s).')
        if str(row.get('visual_status', '')).upper() == 'STILL':
            warnings.append(pre + 'document status says STILL; the clips-only builder converts it to a native MP4 interval. Verify that the MP4 actually shows the intended content.')
        for blocker in row.get('import_blockers') or []:
            errors.append(pre + 'import blocker: ' + str(blocker))
    if sorted(set(covered)) != expected_numbers or len(covered) != len(set(covered)):
        errors.append('SRT blocks are not covered exactly once in order. Covered=' + str(sorted(set(covered)))[:300])
    overlaps = []
    for i, row in enumerate(rows):
        source = str(row.get('source_file') or '')
        sin = row.get('source_in_ms') or 0
        sout = row.get('source_out_ms') or 0
        group = row.get('footage_group') or ''
        for j in range(max(0, i - 6), i):
            prev = rows[j]
            psource = str(prev.get('source_file') or '')
            pin = prev.get('source_in_ms') or 0
            pout = prev.get('source_out_ms') or 0
            pgroup = prev.get('footage_group') or ''
            same_group = bool(group and group == pgroup)
            same_overlap = source and source == psource and sin < pout - 1 and sout > pin + 1
            if same_group or same_overlap:
                overlaps.append({'beat': i + 1, 'repeats_beat': j + 1, 'source_file': source, 'footage_group': group})
                break
    if overlaps:
        errors.append('Six-slot rule: ' + str(len(overlaps)) + ' overlapping source interval / footage-group repeats found.')
    report = {
        'ready': not errors, 'mode': mode, 'beat_rows': len(rows), 'srt_blocks': len(blocks),
        'first_timeline_ms': rows[0]['timeline_in_ms'], 'last_timeline_ms': rows[-1]['timeline_out_ms'],
        'expected_30fps_frames': expected_frames, 'source_files': sorted(source_durations),
        'six_slot_overlap_violations': overlaps, 'warnings': warnings, 'errors': errors,
        'scope': 'Document, timing and source-availability validation only; not face recognition or visual identity verification.'
    }
    return report


def main():
    if len(sys.argv) != 4:
        print('Usage: python validate_production_package.py <production_script.md> <captions.srt> <source_dir_or_duration_map.json>')
        sys.exit(2)
    report = validate(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3])
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    sys.exit(0 if report['ready'] else 1)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

==================== END FILE: validate_production_package.py ====================
